# AI-VED (rastamojka) — the code map

Machine-rendered from six code readers + one completeness critic + the audit's findings on this path (2026-09-05). Companion to `docs/AI-VED-RASTAMOJKA.md`, which is the spec and the build plan. Paths are repo-relative unless absolute. Where a reader and the code disagree, the code wins — re-read before relying on a line number.



## bot-intake

### Summary
The staff bot's 🧮 Hisoblatish intake is a five-step in-memory conversation (platform/telegram/calc-intake.ts holds the state, staff-handlers.ts is the grammy shell, staff-bot.ts holds the testable decisions) that collects a section, a client hint, and a pile of text/photos/documents, then analyses it with claude-opus-5 (wms/calc/intake-ai.ts, structured JSON output, images sent as base64 blocks) merged with a narrow regex parser (intake-manual.ts) where TYPED facts always win, shows a summary with the section's missing REQUIRED_FIELDS (intake.ts), and on «✅ Tasdiqlash» lands through staff-bot.landCollectedIntake → intake-land.landIntake: a coded client (or an unambiguous phone) → the client's newest OPEN deal or a fresh one (dealFor); a stranger → the open lead already carrying that phone (openLeadForPhone) or a new lead; the AI's working plus the seller's verbatim material becomes ONE crm_activity note (id minted up front so photos are pre-bound to it while they arrive), then openCalcRequest (service.ts) inserts calc_requests + calc_request_items with source='bot', assigns via nextVedAssignee, mints a priority-1 task, and moveToCalcStage walks the card FORWARD to the crm_calc_stage/deal_calc_stage setting. The bot then enqueues JOB_CALC_PREFILL (pg-boss) carrying the request's rev so the AI VED hodimi can code/rate/baza it off the poller. Two other doors land in the SAME queue with the same item derivation (itemFacts/loneWeightKg): the desk card form (components/calc-send-form.tsx → hisoblash/actions.ts submitCalcAction, source='card', note written inside openCalcRequest with note_taken/note_foreign fences) and the CRM Telegram-thread door (from-thread.ts threadCalcAnalyze/threadCalcSend, material rebuilt server-side under the tg viewer fence). All bot texts are hard-coded Uzbek; the cabinet keyboard exists in uz/ru/en only; only the web form is four-locale. Registration order in bot.ts is /start → registerStaffBot → registerClientCabinet, and every staff handler calls next() for chats it does not own. The intake's conversation state machine, TTL and the c: callback shape have NO behavioural test — only source-shape pins.


### Files and exports


#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/calc-intake.ts` — The in-memory «Hisoblatish» collection: per-chat IntakeState with a 30-min TTL; the merge of manual+AI facts.

- **`IntakeStage`** `type IntakeStage = 'section' | 'client' | 'material' | 'review'` — 'section' is declared but never assigned — startIntake begins at 'client' (line 77).
- **`IntakeState`** `interface IntakeState { section: CalcSection; stage: IntakeStage; noteId: string; clientHintRaw: string; material: string[]; fileCount: number; facts: CalcFacts; steps: string[]; aiUsed: boolean; images: {data: Buffer; mediaType: 'image/jpeg'}[]; imagesSkipped: number; promptMessageId: number | null; expires: number }` — noteId is minted with uuidv4 at start so files can be stored against it before the note exists (#180 pattern). material = every typed text and photo caption in arrival order. images = downscaled copies for the model, capped by MAX_INTAKE_IMAGES; imagesSkipped counted, never silent. promptMessageId = the ONE live «Bo'ldi» prompt to edit in place. Comment (lines 15-19): «a bot restart mid-collection loses the typed text and the person starts again».
- **`MAX_INTAKE_IMAGES`** `const MAX_INTAKE_IMAGES = 6` — «At most this many photographs reach the model.»
- **`startIntake`** `startIntake(chatId: bigint, section: CalcSection): IntakeState` — Overwrites any live collection for the chat (Map.set, no guard); stage 'client'; expires = now + TTL_MS (30*60_000).
- **`activeIntake`** `activeIntake(chatId: bigint): IntakeState | null` — Expiry checked on read; an expired state is deleted and null returned.
- **`updateIntake`** `updateIntake(chatId: bigint, patch: Partial<IntakeState>): IntakeState | null` — Shallow merge + TTL refresh; null when no live collection (so a patch after expiry is silently dropped).
- **`endIntake`** `endIntake(chatId: bigint): void` — Deletes the state; files already stored against noteId are NOT touched.
- **`analyzeCollected`** `analyzeCollected(state: IntakeState): Promise<IntakeState>` — text = material.join('\n'); manual = parseManualFacts(text); ai = analyzeIntake({section,text,fileCount,images}) with a catch → null. Merge (lines 142-148): fromCity/toCity/weightKg/volumeM3 = manual ?? ai ?? null («Typed facts win over read ones»); goods = ai.goods if non-empty else manual.goods (always []). steps = ai.steps ?? []; aiUsed = Boolean(ai); stage → 'review'. Returns a NEW object; the caller must updateIntake with it.
- **`mayCollect`** `mayCollect(chatId: bigint): Promise<boolean>` — staffForChat(chatId) !== null — «Everyone is (owner's answer 3) — the only question is whether they are staff at all.»

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/staff-bot.ts` — Every DECISION the staff bot makes, integration-testable without Telegram; the platform→wms crossing for landing.

- **`staffPhonesMatch`** `staffPhonesMatch(a: string, b: string): boolean` — Digits only, compare last min(9,len) digits, both must have ≥7 digits.
- **`staffForChat`** `staffForChat(chatId: bigint): Promise<StaffChat | null>  // StaffChat = {id, fullName, locale}` — telegram_links row with status='linked' joined to an ACTIVE user; inactive → null.
- **`staffByPhone`** `staffByPhone(phone: string): Promise<StaffChat | null>` — Loads all active users and finds by staffPhonesMatch (full scan).
- **`linkStaffChat`** `linkStaffChat(userId: string, chatId: bigint): Promise<'linked' | 'chat_taken'>` — 'chat_taken' only when the chat's holder is a DIFFERENT user; own re-link updates the row (linkCode nulled); audits user/update {telegram:'linked_by_phone'}.
- **`isCabinetText`** `isCabinetText(text: string): boolean` — trim() and match against allLabelVariants of btnCargo/btnBalance/btnHistory/btnLanguage in every CLIENT_LOCALE — the cabinet labels ARE its router (#264).
- **`startMenuFor`** `startMenuFor(staff: StaffChat | null, clientCount: number): 'staff' | 'cabinet' | 'both' | 'entry'` — staff&&clients>0 → 'both'; staff → 'staff'; clients>0 → 'cabinet'; else 'entry'.
- **`BotCallback / parseCallback`** `type BotCallback = {kind:'task_done';taskId} | {kind:'approval';approvalId;verdict:'approved'|'refused'} | {kind:'entry';who:'staff'|'client'} | {kind:'calc';step:'yolkira'|'rastamojka'|'podklyuch'|'done'|'save'|'more'|'cancel'}; parseCallback(data: string): BotCallback | null` — Wire: 'e:s' / 'e:c'; /^c:(\w+)$/ restricted to CALC_STEPS; /^t:<uuid>$/; /^a:[01]:<uuid>$/. Telegram caps callback_data at 64 bytes. NO test exercises the 'c:' shape (staff-bot.integration.test.ts:128 «round-trips the three shapes»).
- **`buttonsFor`** `buttonsFor(type: string, payload: Record<string,unknown>): {text;callback_data}[][] | null` — TaskAssigned → «✅ Bajarildi» t:<taskId>; DebtApprovalRequested → «✅ Ruxsat» a:1:<id> / «⛔ Yo‘q» a:0:<id>; else null. Attached at SEND time by the notification drain.
- **`noteTaskPending / takeTaskPending / noteStaffEntry / takeStaffEntry`** `noteTaskPending(chatId,taskId):void; takeTaskPending(chatId):string|null; noteStaffEntry(chatId):void; takeStaffEntry(chatId):boolean` — In-memory Maps, PENDING_TTL_MS = 10*60_000, one-shot (deleted on take).
- **`botActorFor`** `botActorFor(chatId: bigint): Promise<(StaffChat & {permissions:Set<string>; roles:string[]; warehouseScoped:boolean; warehouseIds:string[]}) | null>` — getActor for a chat without a session; permissions union via user_roles→role_permissions; never invents an admin.
- **`lookupFromBot / assistantFromBot`** `lookupFromBot(chatId, query): Promise<string|null>; assistantFromBot(chatId, question): Promise<AskOutcome|null>` — Both null for a non-staff chat; wms/ai reached by dynamic import (platform must never import wms statically).
- **`landCollectedIntake`** `landCollectedIntake(chatId: bigint, staffId: string, staffName: string): Promise<{kind:'deal'|'lead'; id:string; label:string; queued?:boolean; requestId?:string|null} | null>` — null when no live intake. hint = parseClientHint(state.clientHintRaw); client = hint ? resolveIntakeClient(hint) : null; landIntake({noteId, section, facts, steps, material: state.material («Law 11: the words themselves go onto the card»), fileCount, collectedBy: staffId, collectedByName: staffName, client, leadName: state.clientHintRaw.trim() || 'Hisoblatish (nomsiz)', leadPhone: hint?.phone ?? null}). Pinned by tests/unit/ved-audit-wire.test.ts:86 (material passes through).
- **`completeTaskFromBot / decideApprovalFromBot`** `completeTaskFromBot(chatId, taskId, result): Promise<'done'|'not_linked'|'not_yours'|'already_closed'|'not_found'>; decideApprovalFromBot(chatId, approvalId, verdict): Promise<'decided'|'not_linked'|'forbidden'|'already_decided'|'not_found'>` — Not part of the intake; listed because they share the text handler's ordering. Approval needs finance.debt_override checked IN the bot.

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/staff-handlers.ts` — The grammy shell: keyboards, the whole calc conversation (handleCalcCallback), file capture, the edited-in-place prompt, the text router, and the off-poller analysis/prefill dispatch. All strings hard-coded Uzbek.

- **`staffKeyboard`** `staffKeyboard(): {keyboard:[[{text:'📋 Bugun'},{text:'🧮 Hisoblatish'}]], resize_keyboard:true, is_persistent:true}` — Constants BUGUN='📋 Bugun', HISOBLATISH='🧮 Hisoblatish' (lines 50-51). Uzbek only.
- **`bothKeyboard`** `bothKeyboard(locale?: string|null)` — Staff row above the cabinet rows [btnCargo,btnBalance],[btnHistory,btnLanguage] from clientLabels(locale) — reply keyboards are exclusive in Telegram (round 100, 13A).
- **`registerStaffBot`** `registerStaffBot(bot: Bot): void` — Registers, in order: callback_query:data (parseCallback null → next()); message:contact (only after takeStaffEntry, else next()); ['message:photo','message:document'] (only when activeIntake && stage !== 'client' && staff, else next()); message:text (see flow). Called BEFORE registerClientCabinet in bot.ts:158.
- **`entryKeyboard`** `entryKeyboard(): inline [[{text:'👨‍💼 Hodim',callback_data:'e:s'},{text:'📦 Mijoz',callback_data:'e:c'}]]` — The two doors an unknown chat is offered on /start.
- **`(internal) sectionKeyboard`** `inline [[🚚 Yo‘lkira c:yolkira],[🛃 Rastamojka c:rastamojka],[🔑 Podklyuch c:podklyuch]]` — Lines 82-90.
- **`(internal) doneKeyboard`** `inline [[✅ Bo‘ldi — tahlil qil c:done],[✖️ Bekor qilish c:cancel]]` — Lines 92-97.
- **`(internal) confirmKeyboard`** `inline [[✅ Tasdiqlash c:save],[➕ Yana ma’lumot c:more],[✖️ Bekor qilish c:cancel]]` — Lines 99-105.
- **`(internal) handleCalcCallback`** `handleCalcCallback(ctx, chatId, step): Promise<void>` — cancel → endIntake + «Bekor qilindi.»; section step → mayCollect else «Ulanmagan.», startIntake, reply «Mijozni yozing: **kodi** (GS777) yoki **telefon raqami**. Kod bo‘lmasa — ismini yozing.» (parse_mode Markdown); no activeIntake → «Bu so‘rov eskirgan. «🧮 Hisoblatish» tugmasidan qaytadan boshlang.»; more → stage 'material' + «Yana ma’lumot yuboring, tugagach «Bo‘ldi» ni bosing.» with doneKeyboard; done → if material.length===0 && fileCount===0 «Hali hech narsa yuborilmadi.» else «⏳ Tahlil qilinmoqda…» and `void analyseIntakeAndReply(ctx, chatId, state)` (OFF the sequential poller, #706); save → staffForChat else «Ulanmagan.»; landCollectedIntake (catch → null) → null: «Saqlab bo‘lmadi. Sistemadan qo‘lda kiriting.»; else endIntake, if target.queued && requestId → queuePrefill, reply «✅ Saqlandi — bitim|lead: <label>\n<APP_URL>/bitimlar|crm/leads/<id>\n\n🧮 Hisoblash navbatiga tushdi — VED xodimi javob beradi.» or «⚠️ Hisoblash navbatiga tushmadi — kartadan qo‘lda yuboring.» with reply_markup = await replyKeyboardFor(chatId).
- **`(internal) analyseIntakeAndReply`** `analyseIntakeAndReply(ctx, chatId, state): Promise<void>` — analysed = await analyzeCollected(state); updateIntake(chatId, analysed); reply intakeSummaryText({section, facts, clientLabel: clientHintRaw || null, fileCount}) + (aiUsed ? '' : '\n\n(AI mavjud emas — faqat yozilganidan o‘qildi)') with confirmKeyboard; any throw → «Tahlil qilib bo‘lmadi. Qaytadan urinib ko‘ring.» Pinned by tests/unit/bot-ai-dispatch.test.ts (awaited only inside this helper).
- **`(internal) queuePrefill`** `queuePrefill(requestId: string, staffId: string): Promise<void>` — rev = await prefillTicket(requestId); enqueue(JOB_CALC_PREFILL, {requestId, staffId, rev}); any throw is logged 'calc prefill could not be queued' — never costs the seller the confirmation.
- **`(internal) saveIntakeFile`** `saveIntakeFile(ctx, noteId, uploadedBy): Promise<{isPhoto:boolean; body:Buffer} | null>` — Needs TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN; ctx.getFile() then fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/file/bot<token>/<file_path>`) — NO timeout; empty body → null; saveAttachment({entityType:'crm_activity', entityId: noteId, fileName: photo ? `hisoblatish_<ts>.jpg` : document.file_name ?? `fayl_<ts>`, contentType: photo ? 'image/jpeg' : document.mime_type ?? 'application/octet-stream', body, uploadedBy}, {thumbnails:'skip'}) then generateThumbnails(id) inline (enqueue in the bot process would start the worker fleet, #253-261). Unsupported mime → FileValidationError('unsupported_type') → caught by caller → «Faylni saqlab bo‘lmadi — matn bilan yozib yuboring.»
- **`(internal) reduceForModel`** `reduceForModel(original: Buffer): Promise<Buffer|null>  // MODEL_IMAGE_PX = 1568` — sharp (lazy import) .rotate() .resize(1568,1568,{fit:'inside',withoutEnlargement:true}) .jpeg({quality:80}); result > 4_500_000 bytes → null (counted as skipped).
- **`(internal) showIntakePrompt`** `showIntakePrompt(ctx, chatId, state): Promise<void>` — Text: «Qabul qilindi: ✍️ N ta xabar · 📎 N ta fayl · 🖼 N ta rasm o‘qiladi · ⚠️ N ta rasm o‘qilmaydi\nYana yuboring yoki «Bo‘ldi» ni bosing.» (empty parts dropped). editMessageText on promptMessageId with doneKeyboard; on refusal (too old/deleted/identical) sends a fresh one and stores its message_id. Pinned by tests/unit/intake-images.test.ts:61-76.
- **`(internal) answerWithAssistant`** `answerWithAssistant(ctx, chatId, text): Promise<void>` — Not the intake; the free-lookup→AI fallback for plain staff text. Replies: not_configured «Topilmadi. Mijoz kodi (GS777), karobka kodi (YW26-000123), yashik (CR-…) yoki partiya kodini yozing.», limit «Bugungi AI savollar chegarasi tugadi — ertaga yana so‘rang.», error «AI javob berolmadi. Keyinroq urinib ko‘ring.», gave_up default «Oxirigacha yetolmadim — savolni soddaroq berib ko‘ring.»

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/keyboards.ts` — The one resolver for which reply keyboard a chat is owed (re-derived, never remembered).

- **`replyKeyboardFor`** `replyKeyboardFor(chatId: bigint, locale?: string|null): Promise<Keyboard | object | undefined>` — staff = staffForChat; linked = clientsForChat (wms, dynamic import, catch → []); menu = startMenuFor(staff, linked.length); 'both' → bothKeyboard(loc), 'staff' → staffKeyboard(), 'cabinet' → cabinetKeyboard(loc), 'entry' → undefined. loc = locale ?? first linked client's locale. Used by the calc-save reply, the staff-link success and the cabinet language switch (tests/unit/bot-both-modes.test.ts:69-79).

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/bot.ts` — Bot bootstrap: /start doors, registration order, polling with 409 retry.

- **`getBotUsername`** `getBotUsername(): Promise<string|null>` — Cached getMe; null without TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN.
- **`startTelegramBot`** `startTelegramBot(): void` — Returns early without TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN, when TELEGRAM_POLLING==='0', or if already started (globalThis latch). /start: ad payload (adSourceFromPayload) → rememberAdVisit + askPhone + phoneKeyboard; no code → startMenuFor: 'both' → «👋 <name>\n<yourCodes>: …» + bothKeyboard (+ Mini App inline button), 'staff' → «👋 <name>» + staffKeyboard(), 'cabinet' → cabinetKeyboard + app button, 'entry' → `${t.notLinked}\n\nKim sifatida kirasiz? / Кто вы?` + entryKeyboard(); with a code → telegram_links.linkCode match → linkStaffChat (chat_taken → «Bu Telegram boshqa xodimga ulangan. Adminga ayting.») else beginClientLink (ask_phone/no_phone/expired). Then registerStaffBot(bot); registerClientCabinet(bot); bot.catch logs 'telegram bot error'; bot.start({drop_pending_updates:true}) with exponential retry from 30 s to 300 s (409 = another poller holds the token).

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/client-cabinet.ts` — The customer cabinet; intersects the intake only via keyboards, the middleware that sets the menu button, and the contact handler that staff's phone-link falls through to.

- **`cabinetKeyboard`** `cabinetKeyboard(locale?: string|null): Keyboard` — [btnCargo,btnBalance],[btnHistory,btnLanguage], resized, persistent — labels from clientLabels(locale).
- **`phoneKeyboard`** `phoneKeyboard(locale?: string|null): Keyboard` — One requestContact button labelled clientLabels(locale).sharePhone, oneTime.
- **`registerClientCabinet`** `registerClientCabinet(bot: Bot): void` — bot.use middleware (line 318): first message per private chat per process sets the Mini App menu button for linked clients, then next(). message:contact (line 329): with no pending staff-minted code = the self-service door (own-number check → linkAllClientsForPhone; none found + ad visit → landInboundLead; none → phoneNotFound). bot.hears(allLabelVariants(btnCargo|btnBalance|btnHistory|btnLanguage)) at lines 466-552. No generic message:text handler — unknown customer text is unanswered.

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/client-labels.ts` — The cabinet's dictionary — three locales, not four.

- **`CLIENT_LOCALES`** `const CLIENT_LOCALES = ['uz','ru','en'] as const` — zh-CN is NOT a bot locale.
- **`clientLabels / allLabelVariants`** `clientLabels(locale?: string|null): ClientLabels; allLabelVariants(key: 'btnCargo'|'btnBalance'|'btnHistory'|'btnLanguage'): string[]` — btnCargo {uz:'📦 Yuklarim', ru:'📦 Мои грузы', en:'📦 My cargo'}; btnBalance {uz:'💰 Balans', ru:'💰 Баланс', en:'💰 Balance'}; btnHistory {uz:'🗄 Tarix', ru:'🗄 История', en:'🗄 History'}; btnLanguage {uz:'🌐 Til', ru:'🌐 Язык', en:'🌐 Language'}; sharePhone {uz:'📱 Telefon raqamimni yuborish', ru:'📱 Отправить мой номер', en:'📱 Share my phone number'}; notLinked uz 'GSR LOGISTICS. Kabinetga ulanish uchun menejeringizdan havola so‘rang.'; linkByPhone, askPhone, phoneNotFound, phoneMismatch (lines 137-206).

#### `src/modules/platform/ai/model.ts` — The assistant's injectable model client. NOT used by intake-ai (which builds its own Anthropic client); only aiConfigured() is used by the staff text router.

- **`aiConfigured`** `aiConfigured(): boolean` — Boolean(process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY). intake-ai.ts:90 tests the env var directly instead of calling this.
- **`ANALYST_MODEL / STAFF_MODEL / realCallModel`** `ANALYST_MODEL='claude-opus-5'; STAFF_MODEL='claude-sonnet-5'; realCallModel: CallModel` — CALL_TIMEOUT_MS=60_000, maxRetries 1. The intake uses 'claude-opus-5' literally in intake-ai.ts:104.

#### `src/modules/platform/ai/route-text.ts` — Free lookup before the paid model on plain staff text.

- **`codeCandidates`** `codeCandidates(text: string): string[]` — Tokens matching /^(?=.*[A-Za-z])(?=.*\d)[A-Za-z0-9-]{3,20}$/ after stripping .,!?:;()«»"' — uppercased, max 3.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/intake.ts` — Pure vocabulary and rules of the intake: sections, required fields, per-line measure, summary and note text, client-hint parsing.

- **`CalcSection / CALC_SECTIONS / SECTION_LABEL`** `type CalcSection = 'yolkira'|'rastamojka'|'podklyuch'; CALC_SECTIONS: CalcSection[]; SECTION_LABEL: Record<CalcSection,string>` — SECTION_LABEL = {yolkira:'🚚 Yo‘lkira', rastamojka:'🛃 Rastamojka', podklyuch:'🔑 Podklyuch (yo‘lkira + rastamojka)'} — Telegram strings, Uzbek only.
- **`REQUIRED_FIELDS`** `REQUIRED_FIELDS: Record<CalcSection, CalcField[]>` — yolkira: ['fromCity','toCity','weightKg','volumeM3','goods']; rastamojka: ['weightKg','volumeM3','goods','itemMeasure']; podklyuch: ['fromCity','toCity','weightKg','volumeM3','goods','itemMeasure']. Comment: «podklyuch is the sum of both, so it asks for everything».
- **`CalcField / FIELD_LABEL`** `type CalcField = 'fromCity'|'toCity'|'weightKg'|'volumeM3'|'goods'|'itemMeasure'; FIELD_LABEL` — FIELD_LABEL = {fromCity:'qaysi shahardan', toCity:'qaysi shaharga', weightKg:'og‘irligi (kg)', volumeM3:'hajmi (kub)', goods:'tovar nomi', itemMeasure:'tovarning soni yoki og‘irligi'}. itemMeasure is ONE field — the comment (lines 51-70) records that asking for count AND weight made a multi-line podklyuch from the seller's card form permanently incomplete (#649, found by CI).
- **`CalcFacts / CalcItemFact`** `interface CalcFacts { fromCity?; toCity?; weightKg?; volumeM3?; goods?: {name; quantity?; weightKg?; measureQty?; tnvedCode?; note?}[] }; interface CalcItemFact { name; quantity: number|null; weightKg: number|null; measureQty: number|null; tnvedCode: string|null; note: string|null }` — measureQty is typed by the VED in the workspace (0092), never at intake.
- **`loneWeightKg`** `loneWeightKg(itemCount: number, totalKg: number|null|undefined): number|null` — itemCount === 1 && Number(totalKg) > 0 ? total : null — «the one weight a single-line job never has to be asked for»; the one home for all THREE doors.
- **`itemFacts`** `itemFacts(facts: CalcFacts): CalcItemFact[]` — quantity/weightKg/measureQty > 0 else null; a line with no own weight takes loneWeightKg(goods.length, facts.weightKg).
- **`missingFields / isComplete`** `missingFields(section: CalcSection, facts: CalcFacts): CalcField[]; isComplete(section, facts): boolean` — goods → items.length===0; itemMeasure → items.some(quantity===null && weightKg===null && measureQty===null) (silent with no goods — `[].some()` is false, deliberately); weightKg/volumeM3 → !(Number > 0) («0 kg is not a weight»); strings → !trim().
- **`MATERIAL_NOTE_CAP`** `const MATERIAL_NOTE_CAP = 20_000` — Law 11's cap; past it the note appends '\n… (qisqartirildi)'.
- **`intakeSummaryText`** `intakeSummaryText({section, facts, clientLabel: string|null, fileCount}): string` — Lines: SECTION_LABEL; «Mijoz: <label | — (yangi)>»; «Yo‘nalish: from → to» (omitted for rastamojka); «Og‘irlik: N kg · Hajm: N kub» ('—' when ≤0); «Tovarlar (N):» up to 15 lines «· name — q dona · w kg · code» (+'…'); «Fayllar: N»; then «⚠️ Yetishmayapti: <FIELD_LABELs>\nYetishmaganini yozib yuboring yoki shundayligicha tasdiqlang.» or «✅ Ma’lumot to‘liq. Tasdiqlaysizmi?».
- **`intakeNoteText`** `intakeNoteText({section, facts, steps, collectedBy, via?, fileCount, material?}): string` — «🧮 Hisoblatish — <SECTION_LABEL>\nYig‘di: <name> (<via ?? 'Telegram bot'>)» + route (not for rastamojka) + weight/volume + «Fayllar: N» + «Tovarlar:» (with «TNVED: …» and note sub-lines) + «AI izohi:\n— step» + «Sotuvchi yuborgani (asl matn):\n<material joined, capped>» + «⚠️ Yetishmayotgan ma’lumot: …».
- **`parseClientHint`** `parseClientHint(raw: string): {code?: string; phone?: string} | null` — ≥7 digits anywhere → {phone: raw text}; else /^[A-Za-z]{1,4}\d{1,8}$/ → {code: upper}; else null (a name).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/intake-manual.ts` — The narrow no-model parser whose values WIN over the model's.

- **`parseManualFacts`** `parseManualFacts(text: string): CalcFacts` — weightKg via units ['kg','кг','kilo','килограмм'], volumeM3 via ['kub','куб','m3','м3','м³','m³']; regex `(\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)\s*<unit>(?![\p{L}\d])` with 'giu' (negative lookahead, not \b — «120кг» trap); reads EVERY occurrence and answers only when exactly ONE distinct value (several different numbers → null); first unit spelling that answers wins. Route: arrow forms «A → B / -> / =>» or «A dan|дан|из B ga|га|до|в»; hyphen deliberately NOT a route. goods always [] («guessing where one name ends … is exactly the job the model does better»).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/intake-ai.ts` — The model read of the material: extracts, explains, never prices.

- **`AiIntakeResult`** `interface AiIntakeResult { facts: CalcFacts; steps: string[] }` — steps = «short Uzbek lines about WHAT the model did», shown on the lenta.
- **`analyzeIntake`** `analyzeIntake({section, text, fileCount, images?: {data:Buffer; mediaType:'image/jpeg'|'image/png'|'image/webp'}[], timeoutMs?}): Promise<AiIntakeResult|null>` — null when !process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, when text is empty AND no images, on stop_reason==='refusal', or on ANY throw (logged 'calc intake AI failed'). new Anthropic({timeout: timeoutMs ?? 60_000, maxRetries: 1}); model 'claude-opus-5'; max_tokens 4096; system = Russian SYSTEM prompt (lines 36-57: extract cities, total kg/m³, goods with name/quantity/per-line weight_kg/tnved_code (10 digits, only when sure)/note, steps in Uzbek; «ЕСЛИ ПРИЛОЖЕНЫ ФОТО — читай их…»; «Ничего не выдумывай»); output_config.format = json_schema (factsSchema mirrored in zod); user content = image blocks FIRST (base64) then text `Раздел: <section>\nПрикреплено файлов: N\nФотографий для чтения: N\nМатериалы:\n<text.slice(0,20000)>` or 'Текста нет — читай фото.'; logs token usage '[calc intake] model tokens'; tnved_code kept only if /^\d{10}$/; steps.slice(0,20). No measureQty in the schema.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/intake-land.ts` — Where a confirmed intake lands: client resolution, deal-or-lead, the note, the queue, the stage move.

- **`IntakeTarget`** `interface IntakeTarget { kind:'deal'|'lead'; id; label; queued?: boolean; requestId?: string|null }` — queued=false means the note+files are on the card but nobody will calculate — the bot must say so.
- **`resolveIntakeClient`** `resolveIntakeClient(hint: {code?; phone?}): Promise<{id; clientCode; name} | null>` — code → clients.client_code = upper AND active=true; else phone → activeClientsByPhone; exactly ONE match else null («Ambiguity refuses … landing a quote on the wrong person's card is worse than asking»).
- **`dealFor`** `dealFor(client: {id; clientCode; name}, section: CalcSection, actorId: string): Promise<IntakeTarget>` — Newest OPEN deal (deal_stages.kind='open', ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1) or createDeal({clientId, title: `Hisoblatish — ${section}`, ownerId: actorId}). Never a won/lost deal. Also used by the thread door's client entity.
- **`landIntake`** `landIntake({noteId, section, facts, steps, fileCount, material?, collectedBy, collectedByName, client, leadName, leadPhone}): Promise<IntakeTarget>` — client → dealFor; else openLeadForPhone(leadPhone) or createLead({name: leadName, phone: leadPhone ?? '', note: `Telegram bot: hisoblatish (${section})`}, {actorId: collectedBy}) (owner defaults to the actor). addActivity({id: noteId, entityType, entityId, kind:'note', note: intakeNoteText(...)}). Then in its own try/catch: openCalcRequest({entityType, entityId, section, fromCity, toCity, weightKg, volumeM3, items: itemFacts(facts).map(...), noteId, source:'bot', hasMaterials: fileCount>0}, {actorId}) → queued=true, requestId; failure logged '[calc-intake] queue open failed' and queued stays false. Then moveToCalcStage in its own try/catch ('[calc-intake] stage move failed'). Returns {...target, queued, requestId}.
- **`openLeadForPhone`** `openLeadForPhone(phone: string): Promise<{id; name} | null>` — Last-9 digits (≥7 required) against the newest 500 leads WITHOUT a client_id — a converted lead is not reused.
- **`(internal) calcStageMove / moveToCalcStage`** `calcStageMove(board:'lead'|'deal', currentStageId): Promise<string|null>; moveToCalcStage(target, actorId)` — Reads setting crm_calc_stage / deal_calc_stage (getSetting on the pool, outside any tx); target must exist, be active and kind='open'; FORWARD only by sort_order (#392); moves through moveLead(id, to, '', ctx) / moveDeal(id, to, ctx) so audit + stage event + rules fire.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/from-thread.ts` — The THIRD door: «Hisoblatishga yuborish» from a CRM Telegram thread.

- **`ThreadCalcEntity / ThreadMaterial / ThreadCalcPreview`** `type ThreadCalcEntity = {kind:'client'|'lead'|'deal'; id}; interface ThreadMaterial {lines: string[]; fileCount; clientId}; interface ThreadCalcPreview {facts; steps; missing: string[]; lines; fileCount}` — lines are «Mijoz: …» / «<manager name | Biz>: …» in sent order plus «(N ta fayl suhbatda — kartadagi chat panelida)».
- **`threadMaterial`** `threadMaterial(actor: Actor, entity, messageIds: string[]): Promise<ThreadMaterial>` — CalcError('empty') on none, ('too_many') over 200, ('not_found') when a deal has no client or threadClientFor returns null, ('not_yours') when rows.length !== ids.length (refuse, never trim). Viewer fence = tgViewerFor(actor): own manager_user_id unless viewer.all.
- **`threadCalcAnalyze`** `threadCalcAnalyze(actor, {entity, section, messageIds}): Promise<ThreadCalcPreview>` — Same manual-wins merge as the bot; analyzeIntake with timeoutMs 20_000 and NO images; missing = missingFields.
- **`threadCalcSend`** `threadCalcSend(actor, {entity, section, messageIds, noteId, facts?, steps?}, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<{kind; id; queued: true}>` — CalcError('bad_section') unless in CALC_SECTIONS; material REBUILT server-side; posted facts sanitised by cleanFacts (numbers 0..1e6, strings capped 80/200/10/300, goods ≤1000); steps ≤20 × 300 chars; client entity → dealFor; note via intakeNoteText({via:'suhbatdan', fileCount:0, material: lines}); openCalcRequest({..., items: itemFacts(facts)..., note:{id: noteId, text}, source:'card', hasMaterials: fileCount>0}). Files deliberately NOT copied (stated cut, lines 32-36).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/labels.ts` — Bundle-key maps for the bot's vocabulary so the web form is translatable.

- **`SECTION_LABELS / FIELD_LABELS`** `SECTION_LABELS: Record<CalcSection,string> = {yolkira:'sections.yolkira', rastamojka:'sections.rastamojka', podklyuch:'sections.podklyuch'}; FIELD_LABELS: Record<CalcField,string> = {fromCity:'fields.fromCity', …, itemMeasure:'fields.itemMeasure'}` — Keys under the `calc` namespace. Anchored by tests/unit/calc-queue.test.ts:78-85 (NOT locales.test.ts as the file's comment says).
- **`isCalcSection`** `isCalcSection(value: string|null|undefined): value is CalcSection` — CALC_SECTIONS.includes — every form/action validates the section through it.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/service.ts` — The queue: CalcError, the request insert, assignment, the task, the stored-request checklist adapter.

- **`CalcError`** `class CalcError extends Error { constructor(public readonly code: string, public readonly seq?: number) }` — Codes thrown on the intake path: 'unauthenticated', 'too_many_items', 'not_found', 'too_many_open', 'note_taken', 'note_foreign', 'bad_section', 'empty', 'too_many', 'not_yours'.
- **`MAX_CALC_ITEMS / MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER / calcDueMinutes`** `MAX_CALC_ITEMS = 1000; MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER = 20; calcDueMinutes(itemCount: number, hasMaterials = false): number` — itemCount ≤ 0 → hasMaterials ? 60 : 30; else min(30*itemCount, 120). MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER is «a runaway guard: the bot mints a fresh LEAD for every stranger with no phone».
- **`CalcItemInput / CalcRequestInput`** `interface CalcItemInput { name; quantity?; unit?; weightKg?; volumeM3?; amount?; currency?; tnvedCode?; note? }; interface CalcRequestInput { entityType:'deal'|'lead'; entityId; section: CalcSection; fromCity?; toCity?; weightKg?; volumeM3?; items: CalcItemInput[]; note?: {id; text}|null; noteId?: string|null; source:'card'|'bot'; hasMaterials?: boolean }` — note = a note to WRITE (card/thread path); noteId = a note already written (bot path).
- **`openCalcRequest`** `openCalcRequest(input: CalcRequestInput, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<{id: string; assigneeId: string|null}>` — Refuses: no actorId ('unauthenticated'), items > 1000 ('too_many_items'), missing deal/lead ('not_found'), ≥20 open requests by this requester ('too_many_open'), note.id already existing ('note_taken'), note not on the same entity ('note_foreign'). Pooled reads (label, count, note, tnvedFor TNVED memory, nextVedAssignee) BEFORE the transaction (#714); items get seq = i+1, name.slice(300), unit 20, currency 8, note 500, tnvedCode = given || TNVED memory. Tx: insert calc_requests {…, source, noteId, takenAt: assignee ? now : null, dueAt} + calc_request_items + audit(entity, 'update', {calcRequested, section, items, dueAt}). After tx: createTask({title: `Hisoblash: <label> (<n>)`, priority 1, dueAt, entity link}) and stamp task_id (failure logged); no assignee → notifyStaffTelegram super_admins type 'CalcRequested' «🧮 Hisoblash so'rovi keldi: <label>\n⚠️ VED xodimi topilmadi — navbatda turibdi».
- **`missingFor`** `missingFor(section: string|null, facts: CalcFacts): string[]` — null section → [] («a row written before 0085 has no section and no checklist»); else missingFields — the screen and the bot ask the same question.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/jobs.ts` — pg-boss queue names and the owned AI prefill worker the bot enqueues into.

- **`JOB_CALC_PREFILL / CalcPrefillJob`** `JOB_CALC_PREFILL = 'calc.prefill'; interface CalcPrefillJob { requestId; staffId; rev?: number|null }` — Also JOB_CALC_OVERDUE='calc.overdue' (*/5 * * * *), JOB_CALC_REVIEW='calc.dict-review' (0 6 * * *).
- **`registerCalcPrefillWorker`** `registerCalcPrefillWorker(boss: PgBoss): Promise<void>` — Per job: prefillStanding(requestId, rev) !== 'ok' → stand down (logged); else aiPrefill(requestId, {actorId: null}) and notifyStaffTelegram({userIds:[staffId], type:'CalcPrefilled', text}); failures logged, never rethrown.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/prefill.ts` — (post-landing) the rev ticket and standing check the bot's queuePrefill uses.

- **`prefillTicket`** `prefillTicket(requestId: string): Promise<number|null>` — calc_requests.rev at queue time.
- **`prefillStanding / PrefillStand`** `type PrefillStand = 'ok'|'touched'|'confirmed'|'closed'|'not_found'; prefillStanding(requestId, expectRev: number|null): Promise<PrefillStand>` — closed when completed_at set; touched when rev moved; confirmed when any group confirmed.

#### `src/components/calc-send-form.tsx` — The desk door: «Hisoblatishga yuborish» on a lead/deal card (hidden below sm; phone users are pointed at the bot).

- **`CalcSendForm`** `CalcSendForm({entityType:'deal'|'lead'; entityId; revalidate}): JSX` — Controlled state, no <form action> (#377/#419/#463). noteId minted with uuidv4 and rotated after success. Files: POST /api/files/upload with entityType 'crm_activity', entityId noteId, AbortSignal.timeout(120_000). parseGoods: one line per product split on [,;\t] → {name, quantity, unit}. submitCalcAction({entityType, entityId, section, fromCity, toCity, weightKg: num, volumeM3: num, goods, noteId: (note || files>0) ? noteId : '', noteText: note || t('panelTitle'), revalidate}). testids: calc-send-form, calc-section-<yolkira|rastamojka|podklyuch>, calc-from-city, calc-to-city, calc-weight, calc-volume, calc-goods, calc-note, calc-files, calc-send, calc-send-error. i18n calc.sectionLabel/sections.*/fields.*/goods/goodsHint/answerNote/send/deadlineHint/errors.<code>. Default section 'podklyuch'; route inputs hidden for rastamojka.

#### `src/components/calc-panel.tsx` — Server panel that mounts the form and gates it.

- **`CalcPanel`** `CalcPanel({entityType, entityId, revalidate, clientName?, clientLocale?}): Promise<JSX|null>` — null unless getActor() && canWriteDeal(actor.permissions); lead also needs 'crm.leads'; catches isServerBehind. Form under `hidden sm:block`; below sm prints t('sendOnPhone') («Telegram bot orqali yuborish»).

#### `src/components/thread-calc.tsx` — The thread door's client island.

- **`ThreadCalc`** `ThreadCalc(props incl. entity/calcTarget): JSX` — testids thread-calc-start, thread-calc-bar, thread-calc-count, thread-calc-preview, thread-calc-analyze, thread-calc-send, thread-calc-done, thread-calc-error; noteId = crypto.randomUUID() on start; capture-phase document click listener selects bubbles by data-msg-id; i18n calc.threadStart/threadPickHint/threadRead/threadMissing/threadPicked/threadReading/threadAnalyze/threadSendBtn/threadCancel/threadLands/threadSent/threadOpenCard.

#### `src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/actions.ts` — Server actions for the card and thread doors.

- **`submitCalcAction / SubmitCalcInput / CalcFormState`** `submitCalcAction(input: {entityType:'deal'|'lead'; entityId; section: string; fromCity; toCity; weightKg: number|null; volumeM3: number|null; goods: CalcItemInput[]; noteId; noteText; revalidate}): Promise<{ok?: boolean; error?: string}>` — Errors: 'unauthenticated' (no actor), 'forbidden' (!canWriteDeal, or lead without 'crm.leads'), 'bad_section' (!isCalcSection), 'validation' (bad entityType), any CalcError.code, 'server_behind' (isServerBehind → logs 'migration 0085 not applied'). items = goods.map(g => ({...g, weightKg: g.weightKg ?? loneWeightKg(goods.length, weightKg)})); note = noteId ? {id, text: noteText} : null; source 'card'. revalidatePath(input.revalidate) + '/hisoblash'.
- **`threadCalcAnalyzeAction / threadCalcSendAction`** `threadCalcAnalyzeAction({entity, section, messageIds}): Promise<CalcFormState & {preview?}>; threadCalcSendAction({entity, section, messageIds, noteId, facts?, steps?}): Promise<CalcFormState & {landed?: {kind; id}}>` — threadCalcGate: 'forbidden' unless canReadTg(actor) AND canWriteDeal; lead needs 'crm.leads'; 'bad_section' via isCalcSection. Pinned by tests/unit/thread-calc-wire.test.ts:58-66.

#### `src/modules/platform/db/schema/wms.ts` — Drizzle schema for calc_requests (line 1911) and calc_request_items (line 2017).

- **`calcRequests / calcRequestItems`** `pgTable('calc_requests', …) / pgTable('calc_request_items', …)` — See dataModel.

#### `src/modules/platform/settings/service.ts` — Settings registry.

- **`crm_calc_stage / deal_calc_stage`** `defaults '' (lines 128-130)` — «Empty means leave the card where it is»; stored as a stage id; set from /crm/settings and /bitimlar/etaplar via crm/actions.ts:516.

#### `src/modules/wms/client-cabinet/service.ts` — Phone → client resolution used by resolveIntakeClient and clientsForChat used by replyKeyboardFor.

- **`activeClientsByPhone`** `activeClientsByPhone(phone: string): Promise<Client[]>` — <7 digits → []; SQL prefilter on last-7 digits over jsonb phones, then JS phoneBelongsToClient (last-9) as arbiter; active clients only.
- **`clientsForChat`** `clientsForChat(chatId: bigint)` — client_telegram_links status='linked' joined to clients, ordered.

#### `src/modules/wms/crm/service.ts` — createLead / addActivity consumed by landIntake.

- **`createLead`** `createLead(input: LeadInput, ctx: AuditContext, opts?: SystemOpts)` — Does NOT run leadSchema.parse (line 479-500) — the bot's leadName/phone reach the row unvalidated; stageId defaults to defaultStageId(); throws CrmError('unauthenticated') without actorId unless opts.system.
- **`addActivity`** `addActivity(input: z.infer<typeof activitySchema>, ctx, opts?)` — Does NOT run activitySchema.parse (line 1158-1175) — input.id honoured when given (pre-binding); audits crm_<entity>_activity/create with note.slice(0,200).

#### `src/modules/platform/files/service.ts` — Attachment storage the bot's saveIntakeFile calls.

- **`saveAttachment`** `saveAttachment({entityType; entityId; fileName; contentType; body: Buffer; uploadedBy}, {thumbnails?: 'enqueue'|'skip'} = {}): Promise<{id}>` — PHOTO_TYPES jpeg/png/webp (MAX_PHOTO_BYTES 15 MiB); DOC_TYPES gif/heic/heif/pdf/xlsx/docx/xls/doc/txt/csv/zip/rar/7z (MAX_DOC_BYTES 25 MiB); video/audio sets; else FileValidationError('unsupported_type'). 'application/octet-stream' (the bot's fallback for a document with no mime) is NOT in any set.

#### `src/modules/platform/notifications/mutes.ts` — Per-user mute groups.

- **`MUTE_GROUPS.tasks`** `['TasksDue','TaskAssigned','TaskDone','CalcRequested','CalcTaken','CalcDone','CalcReturned','CalcPrefilled']` — CalcOverdue is in `alerts` (line 63).

### Flows


**/start doors and which keyboard a chat gets**

1. bot.ts:56 bot.command('start') — code = ctx.match
2. bot.ts:65 adSourceFromPayload(code) (ad-intake.ts) → rememberAdVisit + clientLabels(tg).askPhone + phoneKeyboard — return
3. bot.ts:77-79 no code → staffForChat + clientsForChat → startMenuFor(staff, n) (staff-bot.ts:138)
4. 'both' → «👋 name\nyourCodes: …» + bothKeyboard(locale) (staff-handlers.ts:70) + cabinetInlineKeyboard(APP_URL) prompt
5. 'staff' → «👋 name» + staffKeyboard() (staff-handlers.ts:53)
6. 'cabinet' → yourCodes + cabinetKeyboard(locale) (client-cabinet.ts:44) + app prompt
7. 'entry' → `${notLinked}\n\nKim sifatida kirasiz? / Кто вы?` + entryKeyboard() (staff-handlers.ts:667) → e:s → noteStaffEntry + «Hodim sifatida ulanish uchun telefon raqamingizni yuboring 👇» + phoneKeyboard('uz'); e:c → notLinked + linkByPhone + phoneKeyboard(tg)
8. message:contact (staff-handlers.ts:162): only if takeStaffEntry; contact.user_id !== from.id → «Faqat o‘zingizning raqamingizni yuboring.»; staffByPhone null → «Bu raqam xodimlar ro‘yxatida topilmadi. Sistemaga kirib Profil → Telegram ulash orqali urinib ko‘ring, yoki adminga ayting.»; linkStaffChat 'chat_taken' → «Bu Telegram boshqa xodimga ulangan. Adminga ayting.»; else «✅ Ulandi: <name>. Xabarnomalar shu yerga keladi.» with replyKeyboardFor(chatId)
9. bot.ts:120 with a code → telegram_links.link_code → linkStaffChat → «✅ Telegram подключён: <name>. Уведомления будут приходить сюда.»; else beginClientLink (cabinet)

**Bot 🧮 Hisoblatish conversation (state machine)**

1. message:text === '🧮 Hisoblatish' or '/hisoblatish' (staff-handlers.ts:274): mayCollect(chatId) else next(); reply «Nimani hisoblatamiz?» + sectionKeyboard (c:yolkira / c:rastamojka / c:podklyuch)
2. callback c:<section> → handleCalcCallback (staff-handlers.ts:585): mayCollect else «Ulanmagan.»; startIntake(chatId, section) [calc-intake.ts:71 — stage 'client', noteId = uuidv4, expires now+30 min; OVERWRITES any live collection]; reply «Mijozni yozing: **kodi** (GS777) yoki **telefon raqami**. Kod bo‘lmasa — ismini yozing.» (Markdown)
3. STAGE client — next text (staff-handlers.ts:259): updateIntake({clientHintRaw: text.trim(), stage:'material'}); reply «Endi hamma narsani yuboring: tovar ro‘yxati, fayllar, rasmlar, kub/kg, yo‘nalish.\nTugagach «Bo‘ldi» ni bosing.» + doneKeyboard. A photo/document in this stage falls through to the cabinet (line 210 next()) — ignored.
4. STAGE material — text (staff-handlers.ts:267): material.push(text), stage 'material', showIntakePrompt (edit-in-place «Qabul qilindi: ✍️ N ta xabar · 📎 N ta fayl · 🖼 N ta rasm o‘qiladi · ⚠️ N ta rasm o‘qilmaydi\nYana yuboring yoki «Bo‘ldi» ni bosing.» + doneKeyboard). ALL text except the literal '🧮 Hisoblatish' is swallowed as material (line 256), including «📋 Bugun», cabinet buttons, codes and '/hisoblatish'.
5. STAGE material — photo/document (staff-handlers.ts:205): saveIntakeFile → attachments row entityType 'crm_activity' entityId noteId (thumbnails inline); if photo and images.length < MAX_INTAKE_IMAGES → reduceForModel (1568 px JPEG q80, >4.5 MB → skipped) else imagesSkipped++; updateIntake({stage:'material', fileCount+1 if saved, material += caption, images, imagesSkipped}); save failure → «Faylni saqlab bo‘lmadi — matn bilan yozib yuboring.» (caption and counters still recorded); else showIntakePrompt
6. c:done (staff-handlers.ts:614): no activeIntake → «Bu so‘rov eskirgan. «🧮 Hisoblatish» tugmasidan qaytadan boshlang.»; material.length===0 && fileCount===0 → «Hali hech narsa yuborilmadi.»; else «⏳ Tahlil qilinmoqda…» and void analyseIntakeAndReply (off the sequential poller)
7. analyseIntakeAndReply (staff-handlers.ts:359) → analyzeCollected (calc-intake.ts:125): parseManualFacts(material.join('\n')) + analyzeIntake({section, text, fileCount, images}) [intake-ai.ts: claude-opus-5, 60 s timeout, images first as base64 blocks, text.slice(0,20000), json_schema output; null on no key/refusal/error]; merge manual ?? ai; goods = ai.goods || []; stage 'review'; updateIntake; reply intakeSummaryText (intake.ts:203) + «(AI mavjud emas — faqat yozilganidan o‘qildi)» when !aiUsed, with confirmKeyboard (c:save / c:more / c:cancel); throw → «Tahlil qilib bo‘lmadi. Qaytadan urinib ko‘ring.»
8. STAGE review — c:more (staff-handlers.ts:606): stage 'material' + «Yana ma’lumot yuboring, tugagach «Bo‘ldi» ni bosing.» + doneKeyboard. A plain text in review also drops stage to 'material' but keeps the OLD facts/steps until c:done is pressed again.
9. c:cancel (any stage): endIntake + «Bekor qilindi.» (stored files stay orphaned on the never-written noteId)
10. c:save (staff-handlers.ts:630): staffForChat else «Ulanmagan.»; landCollectedIntake(chatId, staff.id, staff.fullName) (staff-bot.ts:310) → parseClientHint → resolveIntakeClient → landIntake (see next flow); null/throw → «Saqlab bo‘lmadi. Sistemadan qo‘lda kiriting.»; else endIntake; if queued && requestId → queuePrefill (prefillTicket rev → enqueue JOB_CALC_PREFILL {requestId, staffId, rev}); reply «✅ Saqlandi — bitim|lead: <label>\n<APP_URL>/bitimlar|crm/leads/<id>\n\n🧮 Hisoblash navbatiga tushdi — VED xodimi javob beradi.» or «⚠️ Hisoblash navbatiga tushmadi — kartadan qo‘lda yuboring.» with replyKeyboardFor(chatId)
11. Any stage: TTL 30 min refreshed on every updateIntake; expiry is checked on read (activeIntake) and answered by the «eskirgan» message on the next button.

**landIntake — where a confirmed intake lands (bot door)**

1. staff-bot.ts:327 hint = parseClientHint(clientHintRaw): ≥7 digits → {phone: raw}; /^[A-Za-z]{1,4}\d{1,8}$/ → {code}; else null (a name)
2. intake-land.ts:40 resolveIntakeClient: code → clients where client_code = UPPER and active; phone → activeClientsByPhone (client-cabinet/service.ts:77, last-7 SQL prefilter + last-9 JS) — exactly one match or null (ambiguous phone refuses)
3. intake-land.ts:155 client → dealFor: newest OPEN deal (deals ⋈ deal_stages kind='open' ORDER BY created_at DESC) or createDeal({clientId, title `Hisoblatish — <section>`, ownerId: collectedBy}) (deals/service)
4. intake-land.ts:160 no client → openLeadForPhone(leadPhone) (leads without client_id, newest 500, last-9 match) or createLead({name: leadName (= raw hint or 'Hisoblatish (nomsiz)'), phone: leadPhone ?? '', note: `Telegram bot: hisoblatish (<section>)`}, {actorId}) (crm/service.ts:479; owner = actor; no zod parse)
5. intake-land.ts:173 addActivity({id: noteId, entityType: target.kind, entityId, kind:'note', note: intakeNoteText({section, facts, steps, collectedBy: name, fileCount, material})}) (crm/service.ts:1158; crm_activities row with the pre-bound attachments now attached)
6. intake-land.ts:199 try openCalcRequest({entityType, entityId, section, fromCity, toCity, weightKg, volumeM3, items: itemFacts(facts) → {name, quantity, weightKg, tnvedCode, note}, noteId, source:'bot', hasMaterials: fileCount>0}, {actorId}) (service.ts:167) → queued=true, requestId; catch → log '[calc-intake] queue open failed', queued=false
7. intake-land.ts:239 try moveToCalcStage: getSetting('crm_calc_stage'|'deal_calc_stage') → forward-only target → moveLead/moveDeal; catch logged
8. return {kind, id, label, queued, requestId}

**openCalcRequest — the one queue insert (all three doors)**

1. service.ts:171 ctx.actorId required ('unauthenticated'); items.length > MAX_CALC_ITEMS (1000) → 'too_many_items'
2. service.ts:176 label = deals.code or leads.name ('not_found' when missing)
3. service.ts:186 count of this requester's open requests (openRequests = completed_at IS NULL) ≥ MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER (20) → 'too_many_open'
4. service.ts:196 input.note (card/thread) → crm_activities.id must NOT exist ('note_taken') then addActivity; input.noteId (bot) reused
5. service.ts:216 note must sit on the same entity ('note_foreign') — the attachment gate widens for ved.docs on exactly this link
6. service.ts:227 tnvedFor(item names) — TNVED memory fills tnved_code before any model; items normalised (seq i+1, caps 300/20/8/500)
7. service.ts:241 assigneeId = nextVedAssignee() (fewest open over usersWithPermission('ved.docs')); dueAt = now + calcDueMinutes(items.length, Boolean(noteId) || hasMaterials) min
8. service.ts:245 tx: INSERT calc_requests {entity_type, entity_id, requested_by, assignee_id, item_count, section, from_city(120), to_city(120), weight_kg, volume_m3, source, note_id, taken_at (now if assigned), due_at}; INSERT calc_request_items; writeAudit(entity,'update',{calcRequested,section,items,dueAt})
9. service.ts:281 assignee → createTask({title `Hisoblash: <label> (<n>)`, priority 1, dueAt, entity link}) and UPDATE calc_requests.task_id (failure logged: «Claim first, task second»); no assignee → notifyStaffTelegram(super_admins, 'CalcRequested', «🧮 Hisoblash so'rovi keldi: <label>\n⚠️ VED xodimi topilmadi — navbatda turibdi», exceptUserId actor)
10. return {id, assigneeId}

**Card form door — «Hisoblatishga yuborish» on a lead/deal card**

1. calc-panel.tsx:47 CalcPanel renders only for getActor() with canWriteDeal (lead also 'crm.leads'); form under `hidden sm:block`, phones see calc.sendOnPhone
2. calc-send-form.tsx:41 noteId = uuidv4(); files POST /api/files/upload {entityType:'crm_activity', entityId: noteId} (route handler, #291; 120 s AbortSignal)
3. calc-send-form.tsx:99 submitCalcAction({entityType, entityId, section, fromCity, toCity, weightKg, volumeM3, goods: parseGoods() [name, quantity, unit per line], noteId: (note||files) ? noteId : '', noteText: note || t('panelTitle'), revalidate})
4. hisoblash/actions.ts:172 submitCalcAction: getActor ('unauthenticated'); canWriteDeal ('forbidden'); isCalcSection ('bad_section'); entityType ('validation'); lead needs crm.leads ('forbidden'); items weightKg = g.weightKg ?? loneWeightKg(goods.length, weightKg); note = noteId ? {id, text} : null; source 'card'
5. service.ts openCalcRequest (writes the note itself, note_taken/note_foreign fences)
6. actions.ts:212 revalidatePath(revalidate) + '/hisoblash'; form shows ✅, clears goods/note/files, rotates noteId; error → t(`errors.<code>`) in calc-send-error

**Thread door — «Hisoblatishga yuborish» from a CRM Telegram thread**

1. thread-calc.tsx thread-calc-start → noteId = crypto.randomUUID(); capture-phase click listener selects bubbles by data-msg-id (telegram-bubble.tsx)
2. thread-calc-analyze → threadCalcAnalyzeAction (actions.ts:684): threadCalcGate = canReadTg + canWriteDeal + isCalcSection + lead needs crm.leads
3. from-thread.ts:147 threadCalcAnalyze → threadMaterial (tg viewer fence: own manager_user_id unless viewer.all; lead → tg_messages.lead_id; client/deal → threadClientFor(clientId, viewer) phone-sibling; rows.length !== ids.length → 'not_yours') → lines «Mijoz: …»/«<name>: …» + «(N ta fayl suhbatda — kartadagi chat panelida)» → parseManualFacts + analyzeIntake(timeoutMs 20_000, no images) → preview {facts, steps, missing, lines, fileCount}
4. thread-calc-send → threadCalcSendAction (actions.ts:704) → from-thread.ts:215 threadCalcSend: 'bad_section' unless CALC_SECTIONS; material REBUILT; cleanFacts sanitises the browser's facts; lead → itself, deal → itself, client → dealFor(client, section, actor.id); intakeNoteText({via:'suhbatdan', material: lines, fileCount: 0}); openCalcRequest({…, items: itemFacts(facts), note:{id: noteId, text}, source:'card', hasMaterials: fileCount>0}) — a retried confirm hits 'note_taken'
5. revalidatePath('/hisoblash'); island shows thread-calc-done with threadOpenCard link

Mijoz kodi (GS777), karobka kodi (YW26-000123), yashik (CR-…) yoki partiya kodini yozing.»; else «🤖 O‘ylayapman…» + void answerWithAssistant (delivered via ctx.api.sendMessage)

**After landing — the queued AI prefill (boundary of this map)**

1. staff-handlers.ts:388 queuePrefill: prefillTicket(requestId) (calc_requests.rev) → enqueue('calc.prefill', {requestId, staffId, rev}) (platform/jobs/boss.ts enqueue; ensureListening)
2. jobs.ts:93 registerCalcPrefillWorker: prefillStanding(requestId, rev) must be 'ok' (not 'touched'/'confirmed'/'closed'/'not_found') → aiPrefill(requestId, {actorId: null}) → notifyStaffTelegram({userIds:[staffId], type:'CalcPrefilled', text}) through the notification drain; failures logged, never rethrown

### Data model

- **`calc_requests`** (migration 0085 calc_requests (VED phase A); 0086 (current_version_no, supersedes, freight_zone), 0091 (has_certificate, fee_override_usd), 0092 (rev); schema at src/modules/platform/db/schema/wms.ts:1911): id uuid PK; entity_type text NOT NULL; entity_id uuid NOT NULL; requested_by uuid NOT NULL → users; assignee_id uuid → users; item_count int NOT NULL; task_id uuid → tasks; section text; from_city text; to_city text; weight_kg numeric(12,3); volume_m3 numeric(12,3); source text; note_id uuid → crm_activities ON DELETE SET NULL; requested_at timestamptz NOT NULL default now; due_at timestamptz NOT NULL; taken_at; completed_at; completed_by → users; completed_via text; return_reason text; answer_amount numeric(14,2); answer_currency text; answer_note text; overdue_notified_at; current_version_no int NOT NULL default 0; supersedes_request_id uuid → calc_requests SET NULL; freight_zone text; ai_proposal_started_at; has_certificate bool NOT NULL default true; fee_override_usd numeric(12,2); rev int NOT NULL default 0; updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL default now — CHECKs: calc_requests_entity_check entity_type IN ('deal','lead'); calc_requests_items_check item_count BETWEEN 0 AND 1000; calc_requests_via_check completed_via IN ('lines','task','returned','sealed') or NULL; calc_requests_section_check section IN ('yolkira','rastamojka','podklyuch') or NULL; calc_requests_source_check source IN ('card','bot') or NULL; calc_requests_fee_override_check ≥0 and <> NaN; indexes calc_requests_note_idx(note_id), calc_requests_assignee_idx(assignee_id, requested_at), calc_requests_entity_idx(entity_type, entity_id)
- **`calc_request_items`** (migration 0085; 0086 (group/baza), 0092 (measure pair), 0094 (import_row_id); schema wms.ts:2017): id uuid PK; request_id uuid NOT NULL → calc_requests ON DELETE CASCADE; seq int NOT NULL; name text NOT NULL; quantity numeric(12,3); unit text; weight_kg numeric(12,3); volume_m3 numeric(12,3); amount numeric(14,2); currency text; tnved_code text; note text; group_id uuid → calc_groups SET NULL; baza_usd numeric(14,4); baza_basis text; baza_source text; measure_unit text; measure_qty numeric(14,4); import_row_id bigint → customs_import_rows SET NULL — CHECKs: UNIQUE calc_request_items_seq_idx(request_id, seq); calc_items_baza_basis_check IN ('unit','kg','juft','litr','m2'); calc_items_baza_source_check IN ('dictionary','typed','import') — never 'ai'; calc_items_measure_unit_check IN ('juft','litr','m2','sm3'); calc_items_measure_qty_check >0 and <> NaN; calc_items_measure_pair_check (measure_unit IS NULL) = (measure_qty IS NULL)
- **`crm_activities`** (migration pre-existing (CRM phases); nullable created_by from 0066): id uuid (client-minted when files were pre-bound: activitySchema.id optional); entity_type ('lead'|'client'|'deal'); entity_id; kind ('call'|'meeting'|'message'|'note'); note text; happened_at; created_by uuid nullable (0066) — CHECKs: The intake's note is kind='note', id = the bot's/form's pre-minted noteId; openCalcRequest refuses note_taken (id exists) and note_foreign (entity mismatch)
- **`attachments`** (migration pre-existing): entity_type 'crm_activity'; entity_id = noteId; file_name `hisoblatish_<ts>.jpg` or the document's name; content_type; uploaded_by = staff id — CHECKs: Type allowlist in files/service.ts (PHOTO_TYPES / DOC_TYPES / VIDEO / AUDIO); 15 MiB photo / 25 MiB doc caps; thumbnails generated inline by the bot ({thumbnails:'skip'} + generateThumbnails)
- **`telegram_links`** (migration spec 4.5): user_id; telegram_chat_id bigint; status ('linked'|'revoked'|…); link_code; linked_at — CHECKs: staffForChat requires status='linked' AND users.active; linkStaffChat refuses a chat held by a different user ('chat_taken'); UNIQUE on telegram_chat_id (bot.ts comment lines 140-145)
- **`client_telegram_links`** (migration phase 2.2 / 0042): client_id; telegram_chat_id; status 'linked' — CHECKs: clientsForChat — decides 'both'/'cabinet' menus
- **`leads`** (migration pre-existing): name; phone; stage_id; owner_id (defaults to actor); client_id (NULL = not converted); note; created_by — CHECKs: openLeadForPhone matches only rows with client_id IS NULL by last-9 digits; leadSchema (name min 2, phone max 40) is NOT applied by createLead
- **`deals`** (migration pre-existing): code; client_id; stage_id; title; owner_id — CHECKs: dealFor picks newest deal whose deal_stages.kind='open'; new deal title `Hisoblatish — <section>`
- **`settings`** (migration round 84 (settings registry)): crm_calc_stage ('' default); deal_calc_stage ('' default) — CHECKs: A stage id; ignored when the stage is missing, inactive or not kind='open'; move is FORWARD only by sort_order
- **`tasks`** (migration phase 3): title `Hisoblash: <label> (<n>)`; assignee_id; due_at = request.due_at; priority 1; entity link — CHECKs: Created AFTER the request tx; failure only logged
- **`notifications`** (migration 0082 notify_claim (drain)): type 'CalcRequested' (no assignee → super_admins), 'CalcPrefilled' (the AI pass's answer), 'CalcOverdue' — CHECKs: MUTE_GROUPS.tasks carries CalcRequested/CalcPrefilled; alerts carries CalcOverdue

### Invariants (and the fence that enforces each)

- Typed facts beat the model's reading: calc-intake.ts:141-148 and from-thread.ts:160-167 merge `manual ?? ai`; fenced by tests/unit/intake-facts.test.ts:22-55 (a typed number wins only when the text states ONE number — several different numbers → null so the model's total is not overwritten).
- The model proposes words, never a number a customer pays: intake-ai.ts SYSTEM «Твоя задача — ИЗВЛЕЧЬ факты, а не считать цену»; tnved_code kept only when /^\d{10}$/ (intake-ai.ts:191); calc_items_baza_source_check has no 'ai' (schema wms.ts CHECK).
- Podklyuch = rastamojka + yolkira: REQUIRED_FIELDS (intake.ts:39-43); fenced by tests/integration/calc-intake.integration.test.ts:92-104 and tests/unit/calc-queue.test.ts:35-56 (through missingFor).
- itemMeasure is ONE per-line question (count OR weight OR law unit), silent when there are no goods, absent for yolkira: intake.ts:164-185; fenced by intake-facts.test.ts:114-151.
- A single line's weight is DERIVED (loneWeightKg) and every landing door applies it: intake.ts:126-157; fenced by intake-facts.test.ts:77-107 and the DERIVED walk in tests/unit/intake-items-wire.test.ts (every openCalcRequest caller must use itemFacts( or loneWeightKg( and never `.goods ??`).
- A zero is a blank («0 kg is not a weight»): intake.ts:181-182 and itemFacts; fenced by calc-intake.integration.test.ts:106-117, intake-facts.test.ts:109-112, calc-queue.test.ts:65-75.
- Law 11 — the seller's words land verbatim on the note, capped at MATERIAL_NOTE_CAP with a visible «… (qisqartirildi)»: intake.ts:279-285; fenced by calc-intake.integration.test.ts:161-199 and tests/unit/ved-audit-wire.test.ts:86 (landCollectedIntake passes state.material).
- Coded client → DEAL (newest open, reused), stranger → LEAD (open lead on the same phone reused), ambiguous phone → nobody: intake-land.ts:40-98, 155-171, 278-292; fenced by calc-intake.integration.test.ts:230-319, 396-404.
- The stage move is FORWARD only and a machine never walks a card backwards; an empty setting leaves the card alone: intake-land.ts:110-128; fenced by calc-intake.integration.test.ts:321-394 (snapshots/restores crm_calc_stage — CONFIGURATION, #183).
- The note+files survive a queue failure, and the bot says which half landed: intake-land.ts:191-232 try/catch → queued:false → «⚠️ Hisoblash navbatiga tushmadi — kartadan qo‘lda yuboring.» (staff-handlers.ts:654-660). No test asserts queued/requestId (gap).
- The model call never holds grammy's sequential poller and carries its own deadline: `void analyseIntakeAndReply(` and `new Anthropic({timeout:` — fenced by tests/unit/bot-ai-dispatch.test.ts:65-95 (analyzeCollected may be awaited only inside the helper's body).
- Photos reach the model, reduced and capped, with the overflow COUNTED: MAX_INTAKE_IMAGES=6, reduceForModel, imagesSkipped — fenced (source-shape) by tests/unit/intake-images.test.ts:27-59; a photo-only collection is still analysed (`if (!material && images.length === 0) return null;`).
- ONE live «Bo‘ldi» control edited in place with a fresh one only when the edit is refused: showIntakePrompt — fenced (source-shape) by intake-images.test.ts:61-76.
- A cabinet button belongs to the cabinet even in a both-chat and is checked BEFORE the task-result capture: staff-handlers.ts:286 — fenced by tests/unit/bot-both-modes.test.ts:53-62 and the isCabinetText derivation over CLIENT_LOCALES (lines 14-37).
- Every keyboard-bearing reply re-derives the keyboard (replyKeyboardFor) instead of naming one: fenced by bot-both-modes.test.ts:69-79 (`await replyKeyboardFor(chatId)` in staff-handlers.ts).
- A stranger's text never reaches the model or the question ledger: staffForChat gate at staff-handlers.ts:318 before the lookup/AI; every staff handler calls next() for non-staff chats (registered BEFORE the cabinet, bot.ts:155-159).
- Only the sender's OWN verified number can staff-link: contact.user_id === from.id (staff-handlers.ts:167) — same rule as the cabinet; the «Hodim» intent is one-shot (staff-bot.integration.test.ts:118-124).
- The section posted by any form is validated, never defaulted: isCalcSection in submitCalcAction/threadCalcGate/threadCalcSend — fenced by calc-queue.test.ts:98-106 and thread-calc-wire.test.ts:59-66.
- No door accepts an assignee — the queue decides (nextVedAssignee); fenced by calc-queue.test.ts:108.
- A pre-minted note id cannot be reused or point at another card: 'note_taken' / 'note_foreign' in openCalcRequest (service.ts:196-224); the thread door's double press is fenced by thread-calc.integration.test.ts:233.
- The thread door refuses, never trims, a selection outside the viewer's fence: `if (rows.length !== ids.length) throw new CalcError('not_yours')` — fenced by thread-calc.integration.test.ts:173-193 and thread-calc-wire.test.ts:68-73.
- Everything pooled happens BEFORE openCalcRequest's transaction (#714): service.ts:160-166 comment; guarded by tests/unit/tx-pool.test.ts (repo-wide fence).

- `/home/user/fullprompt/tests/unit/intake-facts.test.ts` — A typed number wins only when the text states ONE number (several distinct → null; repeats = one; one unit spelling per fact; the «120кг» ASCII-\b trap); the checklist keeps ⚠ on a refused weight; one line takes the shipment's weight and two lines take nothing; a stated per-line weight wins; zero is a blank; a line needs ONE measure (count | weight | measureQty), never both; no goods = ONE absence; freight asks for no per-line measure; the summary prints '100 dona' and '250 kg'. · fixture: Pure calls to parseManualFacts / missingFields / itemFacts / loneWeightKg / intakeSummaryText, no db.

### Gaps (not built / cut / owed / suspect)

- NO behavioural test of the bot conversation itself: nothing calls startIntake/activeIntake/updateIntake/endIntake/analyzeCollected/mayCollect, and parseCallback's 'c:' shape is uncovered (staff-bot.integration.test.ts:128 says «the three shapes»; grep of tests/ for c:yolkira|kind: 'calc'|startIntake returns nothing). The TTL, the stage transitions, the «eskirgan» path and the edit-in-place prompt are pinned only as source shape (intake-images.test.ts, bot-ai-dispatch.test.ts).
- calc-intake.integration.test.ts never asserts landIntake's `queued`/`requestId` (it only cleans calc_requests in afterAll) — the bot→queue half is proven by intake-items-wire.test.ts's source walk, not by a row read back.
- Documents (PDF/XLSX/DOCX) are stored against the note but NEVER read by the model: only photos are reduced and sent (staff-handlers.ts:224-233); intake-ai receives just `Прикреплено файлов: N`. docs/VED.md:164 «goods (manual / Excel import / files)» reads as if files were read.
- saveIntakeFile's `fetch('https://api.telegram.org/file/bot…')` (staff-handlers.ts:482) has no timeout/AbortSignal — round 97's rule («an un-deadlined network call is the failure that looks like a hang»); it runs INSIDE the sequential poller's handler, so a hung download holds every customer.
- A photo/document sent while stage === 'client' (before the client name is typed) falls through to the cabinet via next() (staff-handlers.ts:210) and is silently ignored — no reply tells the person.
- During a live collection EVERY text except the literal '🧮 Hisoblatish' is swallowed as material (staff-handlers.ts:256): «📋 Bugun», '/bugun', cabinet buttons, '/hisoblatish', codes. A staff member cannot use any other bot function for up to 30 min without pressing ✖️.
- Pressing a section button while a collection is live calls startIntake, which overwrites the state without warning (calc-intake.ts:90); files already stored against the old noteId become orphan attachments on a crm_activity id that is never written (same for ✖️ Bekor and TTL expiry — endIntake never deletes them).
- After the review, a further text/photo drops stage to 'material' but keeps the PREVIOUS facts/steps; the old «✅ Tasdiqlash» button still works and lands the stale facts (the new text reaches the note verbatim only). Re-analysis needs another «Bo‘ldi».
- analyseIntakeAndReply is fire-and-forget with no claim: two «Bo‘ldi» presses in flight start two opus calls; if the TTL expires mid-analysis, updateIntake returns null and the summary + confirm keyboard are still shown, whose «Tasdiqlash» then answers «eskirgan».
- When openCalcRequest refuses (too_many_open, note_foreign, not_found…), the bot only says «⚠️ Hisoblash navbatiga tushmadi — kartadan qo‘lda yuboring.»; the CalcError code is logged ('[calc-intake] queue open failed') and never shown — the collector cannot tell a 20-open-requests cap from a schema-behind server.
- A typed phone that matches no client becomes the lead's NAME (leadName = raw hint, intake-land via staff-bot.ts:343); only an empty hint gets 'Hisoblatish (nomsiz)'. An INACTIVE client's code resolves to nobody (intake-land.ts:48 active=true) and lands as a lead named by the code.
- A document with no Telegram mime_type is stored as 'application/octet-stream' (staff-handlers.ts:460), which is in none of files/service.ts's type sets → FileValidationError → «Faylni saqlab bo‘lmadi» — the caption is still recorded and fileCount not incremented.
- The staff row of the merged keyboard and every bot sentence are Uzbek only (staff-handlers.ts:42-45 states the choice); the cabinet dictionary has uz/ru/en (CLIENT_LOCALES) — zh-CN exists only in the web bundles. The task's «four languages» applies solely to the card form (messages/*.json calc.*).
- The AI schema has no measureQty: the law's own unit (m²/juft/litr) can only be typed by the VED in the workspace (intake.ts:92-100), so a rastamojka request whose only per-line measure is m² arrives from the bot with the itemMeasure chip standing.
- Stated cuts in the code: the thread door copies no files (from-thread.ts:32-36 «STATED CUT»); voice notes are not transcribed (docs/VED.md:161); the model's pick reason for a baza is logged and shown nowhere (docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md §6); freight is not quoted without a zone (§6).
- IntakeStage 'section' (calc-intake.ts:22) is dead — startIntake begins at 'client'; the section is chosen by the callback that CREATES the state.
- MATERIAL_NOTE_CAP = 20_000 (intake.ts:248) exceeds activitySchema.note.max(4000) (crm/service.ts:1152); harmless today only because addActivity does not run the schema (line 1158-1175) — a later `.parse` would make every long bot note fail with «Saqlab bo‘lmadi».
- intake-ai.ts:90 tests process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY directly instead of aiConfigured() (platform/ai/model.ts:42) — two definitions of «configured» that happen to agree.
- The docs' «asks follow-ups until complete» loop (docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md §3 collection-loop bullet; docs/VED.md:159-160 «asks ONLY for what is missing («Shahar qaysi?»)») is not built: the bot prints the whole missing list once in the summary and offers «➕ Yana ma’lumot»/«shundayligicha tasdiqlang».
