## queue-lifecycle

### Summary
The calc REQUEST lifecycle is VED phase A (docs/VED.md; migration 0085) reopening round 28's `calc_requests` table as a JOB queue: one row per consignment (section yolkira|rastamojka|podklyuch, route, kg, m³, goods in `calc_request_items`, materials = one `crm_activities` note the files are pre-bound to). Three doors land a request through ONE writer, `openCalcRequest` in /home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/service.ts: the card form (`submitCalcAction`, gated `canWriteDeal` + `crm.leads` for leads), the staff bot's 🧮 (`landIntake` in intake-land.ts, which then enqueues `JOB_CALC_PREFILL` for the AI VED hodimi), and a CRM Telegram thread (`threadCalcSend`). Assignment is the QUEUE's: `nextVedAssignee()` picks the active `ved.docs` holder with the fewest open requests (never-had-one first), stamps `assignee_id`/`taken_at`, and mints a priority-1 timed task titled `Hisoblash: ${label} (${n})` whose `due_at` = now + `calcDueMinutes(itemCount, hasMaterials)` (30 min/line, cap 120; 0 lines = 30, or 60 with materials). Nobody holding `ved.docs` → request stored unassigned and super_admins get `CalcRequested`. The clock has FOUR ends, all through `endRequest` (or the seal's own UPDATE): `'task'` (`finishCalcRequest` writes `answer_amount/currency/note`, sends `CalcDone` to the requester — but throws `seal_instead` when `canSeal(workspace)` is true), `'returned'` (`returnCalcRequest`, reason ≥3 chars mandatory, `CalcReturned`), `'lines'` (`completeCalcForDeal`, hooked AFTER `saveLines`' transaction), and `'sealed'` (`sealCalc`'s closing UPDATE in workspace.ts, which also bumps `current_version_no` and sends `CalcSealed`). `completeCalcForTask`/`releaseCalcForTask` are the task-side hooks (a hand-closed task ends the request via 'task' with no figure; a cancelled task releases it back to the queue). `takeCalcRequest` is a single UPDATE with `assignee_id IS DISTINCT FROM me … RETURNING` so two «Olaman» presses mint one task; `releaseCalcRequest` nulls assignee/task/taken_at and cancels the task. The overdue sweep `notifyOverdueCalcs` (pg-boss `calc.overdue`, every 5 min) CLAIMS rows by stamping `overdue_notified_at` in the same UPDATE before sending `CalcOverdue` to requester + super_admins (+ the whole ved.docs pool when untaken). A correction is never a re-open: `recalcFromSealed` mints a NEW request with `supersedes_request_id`, copying groups/items/extras but not confirmations, refusing `not_closed`/`not_sealed`/`recalc_open`/`recalc_superseded`; the printed «V2» is DERIVED by chain.ts (`chainOf`/`quoteNoFor`: rank among the chain's sealed versions by seal time, superseded decided by the EDGE), while the stored `current_version_no` stays 1 on every correction. Screens: `/hisoblash` (queue + 30-day speed table; `ved.docs` only; lead rows print «Hisoblash» not the lead name and link the card only under `crm.leads`, #514), `/hisoblash/[id]` (facts + checklist + `CargoFactsForm`, materials fold, workspace, read-only goods fallback under the same `calc-items` testid, linked receipts, `CalcActions` take/release/return/finish), `/hisoblash/tarix` (registry of sealed versions, door `mayReadCalcRegistry`), and the VED home flow (`vedFlowCounts` reading `calcQueueCounts()` over the same `openRequests` fragment the queue filters by, #513). Every action runs through `run('ved.docs', …)` which maps `CalcError.code` → `{error}` and `isServerBehind` → `'server_behind'`; no door accepts an `assigneeId`.


### Files and exports


#### `src/modules/wms/calc/service.ts` — The request lifecycle: open, assign, take, release, end (task/returned/lines), overdue sweep, queue/detail/card/speed reads, rekey on win. Runs on the POOL (no tx except the INSERT in openCalcRequest); header comment (23-46) states the three rules: a request is a JOB not a card state; the queue owns assignment; every speed figure excludes 'returned'.

- **`CalcError`** `class CalcError extends Error { constructor(public readonly code: string, public readonly seq?: number) }  (48-57)` — Every refusal in the calc module. `seq` names the ROW a table refusal is about. Actions map `.code` to `{error: code}`; the screen prints `t('errors.<code>')`.
- **`MAX_CALC_ITEMS`** `export const MAX_CALC_ITEMS = 1000  (60)` — «1 dan 1000 tagacha tovar» — openCalcRequest throws `too_many_items` above it; DB CHECK `calc_requests_items_check` (item_count BETWEEN 0 AND 1000) is the fence.
- **`MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER`** `export const MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER = 20  (70)` — Runaway guard, «not a security fence»: openCalcRequest counts the actor's open requests (`requested_by = actor AND completed_at IS NULL`) and throws `too_many_open` at ≥20.
- **`calcDueMinutes`** `export function calcDueMinutes(itemCount: number, hasMaterials = false): number  (81-84)` — Owner's scale verbatim: `itemCount <= 0 ? (hasMaterials ? 60 : 30) : min(30*itemCount, 120)`. Pinned by tests/unit/calc-queue.test.ts.
- **`openRequests`** `export const openRequests = isNull(calcRequests.completedAt)  (91)` — «the ONE fragment the queue, its counters and the VED home all filter by … cannot disagree (#513)». Used by nextVedAssignee, openCalcRequest's cap, take/release/endRequest WHERE, completeCalcFor*, notifyOverdueCalcs, calcQueueCounts, openCalcFor.
- **`nextVedAssignee`** `export async function nextVedAssignee(): Promise<string | null>  (102-120)` — Pool = `usersWithPermission('ved.docs')`; LEFT JOIN open calc_requests; `WHERE users.active AND id IN pool`; ORDER BY `count(...) asc, max(requested_at) asc nulls first`; LIMIT 1. Null when nobody holds ved.docs. Restates crm/routing.ts's rota rather than sharing it (comment 97-100).
- **`CalcItemInput`** `interface { name: string; quantity?; unit?; weightKg?; volumeM3?; amount?; currency?; tnvedCode?; note? }  (122-132)` — One goods line as submitted; truncated on write (name 300, unit 20, currency 8, note 500).
- **`CalcRequestInput`** `interface { entityType: 'deal'|'lead'; entityId; section: CalcSection; fromCity?; toCity?; weightKg?; volumeM3?; items: CalcItemInput[]; note?: {id; text}|null; noteId?: string|null; source: 'card'|'bot'; hasMaterials?: boolean }  (134-150)` — `note` = a note to WRITE (card path, id pre-bound to uploads); `noteId` = a note already written (bot path). `hasMaterials` set by the bot; card path derives it from noteId.
- **`openCalcRequest`** `export async function openCalcRequest(input: CalcRequestInput, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<{ id: string; assigneeId: string | null }>  (167-330)` — Refuses: `unauthenticated` (no ctx.actorId), `too_many_items` (>1000), `not_found` (card missing), `too_many_open` (≥MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER), `note_taken` (input.note.id already exists in crm_activities), `note_foreign` (note not on the same card). Everything pooled BEFORE the tx (note write via addActivity, TNVED memory via tnvedFor/productKey, rota); tx INSERTs calc_requests (takenAt = now if assignee else null, dueAt = now + calcDueMinutes) + items + writeAudit(entityType=card, action 'update', after {calcRequested, section, items, dueAt}); AFTER the tx: createTask (title `Hisoblash: ${label} (${n})`, priority 1, dueAt ISO, entity = the card) then UPDATE task_id — task failure only logged '[calc] task creation failed' («Claim first, task second»); no assignee → notifyStaffTelegram to usersWithRoles(['super_admin']) type 'CalcRequested' text «🧮 Hisoblash so'rovi keldi: ${label}\n⚠️ VED xodimi topilmadi — navbatda turibdi». Callers: actions.ts submitCalcAction, intake-land.ts landIntake, from-thread.ts threadCalcSend.
- **`takeCalcRequest`** `export async function takeCalcRequest(id: string, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<void>  (340-418)` — ONE UPDATE `SET assignee_id=me, taken_at=now WHERE id AND completed_at IS NULL AND assignee_id IS DISTINCT FROM me RETURNING …`; no row → re-reads: `not_found` / `already_closed` / `already_yours`. Existing task re-pointed directly (`UPDATE tasks SET assignee_id WHERE status='open'`, «not through reassignTask»); no task → createTask under the taker. Audits {calcTakenBy, calcRequest}; notifies requester type 'CalcTaken' («🧮 Hisoblash olindi: label\n👤 name + link»), exceptUserId = taker. Previous holder is NOT told.
- **`releaseCalcRequest`** `export async function releaseCalcRequest(id: string, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<void>  (424-455)` — UPDATE `SET assignee_id=null, task_id=null, taken_at=null WHERE id AND open RETURNING taskId (the previous one)`; no row → `already_closed`. Cancels the old task (`status='cancelled', result='Navbatga qaytarildi'` where open). Audits {calcReleased}. No notification.
- **`returnCalcRequest`** `export async function returnCalcRequest(id: string, reason: string, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<void>  (518-546)` — `reason.trim().length < 3` → `reason_required`; endRequest via 'returned' with return_reason (≤500); `already_closed` if not open; audits {calcReturned, reason}; notifies requester 'CalcReturned' («↩️ Hisoblash qaytarildi: label\n📝 reason(≤300) + link»).
- **`finishCalcRequest`** `export async function finishCalcRequest(id: string, answer: { amount?: number|null; currency?: string|null; note?: string|null }, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<void>  (549-591)` — Round 112: dynamic-imports workspace `loadWorkspace(id)`; if `canSeal(workspace)` → throws `seal_instead` (562-564). Else endRequest via 'task' with answerAmount, answerCurrency (= currency||'USD' only when amount != null), answerNote (≤2000); `already_closed` if not open. Audits {calcDone, amount}; notifies requester 'CalcDone' («✅ Hisoblash tayyor: label\n💵 amount cur\n📝 note(≤300) + link»).
- **`completeCalcForDeal`** `export async function completeCalcForDeal(dealId: string, actorId: string): Promise<void>  (601-612)` — Finds the OLDEST open request on the deal (orderBy requestedAt asc) and endRequest via 'lines'. Called from deals/service.ts saveLines AFTER its tx, only when lines.length > 0 (deals/service.ts:402-413).
- **`completeCalcForTask`** `export async function completeCalcForTask(taskId: string, actorId: string): Promise<void>  (615-621)` — Open request with this task_id → endRequest via 'task' (NO answer figure). Called from platform/tasks/service.ts completeTask (346-351) — the bot's «✅ Bajarildi» door reaches here through completeTaskFromBot → completeTask.
- **`releaseCalcForTask`** `export async function releaseCalcForTask(taskId: string, actorId: string): Promise<void>  (628-638)` — Open request with this task_id → assignee/task/taken_at nulled (no audit, logger.info '[calc] request released by task cancel'). Called from tasks/service.ts cancelTask (434-441).
- **`rekeyLeadCalcRequests`** `export async function rekeyLeadCalcRequests(leadId: string, dealId: string): Promise<number>  (647-679)` — UPDATE EVERY calc_requests row (open AND closed/sealed — comment 651-659: a sealed one carries the LOCK) from ('lead', leadId) to ('deal', dealId); ALSO calc_offers (663-676, #789). Returns moved request count. Called from crm/service.ts winLead (884-893) inside try/catch.
- **`notifyOverdueCalcs`** `export async function notifyOverdueCalcs(now = new Date()): Promise<number>  (711-790)` — Claim = `UPDATE … SET overdue_notified_at = now WHERE open AND due_at < now AND overdue_notified_at IS NULL RETURNING`. Recipients per row: requestedBy + usersWithRoles(['super_admin']) + (unassigned ? usersWithPermission('ved.docs') : []); exceptUserId = assignee. Type 'CalcOverdue', text «🔴 Hisoblash kechikdi: label (n)\n👤 name | ⚠️ Hech kim olmagan\n⏱ N daqiqa kechikdi». Returns count sent. Run by JOB_CALC_OVERDUE.
- **`CalcQueueRow`** `interface { id; entityType; entityId; label; section; fromCity; toCity; weightKg; volumeM3; itemCount; requestedAt: Date; dueAt: Date; requesterName; assigneeId; assigneeName; missing: string[]; late: boolean }  (792-810)` — Queue/panel row. `label` = deal code or lead name (coalesce(d.code, l.name)) — the SCREEN decides whether to print it.
- **`calcQueue`** `export async function calcQueue(now = new Date()): Promise<CalcQueueRow[]>  (818-883)` — Raw SQL, LEFT JOINs on requester/assignee/deal/lead, `WHERE completed_at IS NULL ORDER BY (assignee_id IS NOT NULL), requested_at ASC LIMIT 200`; timestamps coerced from TEXT (866-870); `missing` via missingFor over goodsByRequest facts (name/quantity/weightKg/measureQty).
- **`calcQueueCounts`** `export async function calcQueueCounts(now = new Date()): Promise<{ open: number; late: number }>  (890-901)` — count(*) and count FILTER (due_at < now) over `openRequests`. Uncapped. Read by role-flows.ts vedFlowCounts.
- **`missingFor`** `export function missingFor(section: string | null, facts: CalcFacts): string[]  (956-959)` — Adapter: null section → [] («a row written before 0085 has no section and therefore no checklist»); else intake.ts `missingFields(section, facts)`.
- **`isComplete`** `export { isComplete }  (961)` — Re-export of intake.ts isComplete.
- **`CalcRequestDetail`** `interface extends CalcQueueRow { noteId; source; completedAt; completedVia; returnReason; answerAmount; answerCurrency; answerNote; items: {seq; name; quantity; unit; weightKg; volumeM3; amount; currency; tnvedCode; note}[] }  (963-984)` — The [id] page's row. Item projection carries NO measure pair; the checklist reads the ROWS (1040-1049).
- **`calcRequestDetail`** `export async function calcRequestDetail(id: string, now = new Date()): Promise<CalcRequestDetail | null>  (987-1062)` — No access gate here (the page gates on ved.docs). `late` = !completedAt && dueAt < now.
- **`openCalcFor`** `export async function openCalcFor(entityType: 'deal'|'lead', entityId: string, now = new Date()): Promise<CalcQueueRow[]>  (1065-1115)` — Open requests on ONE card, LEFT JOIN assignee, oldest first; returns rows with `label: ''`, `requesterName: ''`, `missing: []`. Read by components/calc-panel.tsx.
- **`lastCalcAnswerFor`** `export async function lastCalcAnswerFor(entityType, entityId): Promise<{ amount; currency; note; at: Date } | null>  (1118-1137)` — Newest row with `completed_via = 'task'` (by completed_at desc) — includes a hand-closed task with NULL amount.
- **`calcSpeed`** `export async function calcSpeed(since: Date): Promise<CalcSpeedRow[]>  (1155-1186)` — Per assignee: done/avg_minutes/on_time all FILTER `completed_via <> 'returned'`; open = completed_at IS NULL; WHERE requested_at >= since OR open. Comment 1151-1153 states why returns are excluded.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/jobs.ts` — pg-boss workers for the calc module.

- **`JOB_CALC_OVERDUE`** `export const JOB_CALC_OVERDUE = 'calc.overdue'  (6)` — Queue name; scheduled `'*/5 * * * *'` in registerCalcWorker (32-44), handler = notifyOverdueCalcs, rethrows on failure.
- **`JOB_CALC_REVIEW`** `export const JOB_CALC_REVIEW = 'calc.dict-review'  (7)` — Scheduled DAILY `'0 6 * * *'` (11:00 Tashkent), claimed monthly by review.ts notifyDictionaryReview (58-70).
- **`JOB_CALC_PREFILL`** `export const JOB_CALC_PREFILL = 'calc.prefill'  (8)` — The AI VED hodimi's pass, OWNED by pg-boss. Handler (95-134): `prefillStanding(requestId, job.data.rev ?? null)` !== 'ok' → stand down (logged); else `aiPrefill(requestId, { actorId: null })` and notifyStaffTelegram to staffId type 'CalcPrefilled' with out.text. Failures logged, NOT rethrown («best-effort by construction»).
- **`CalcPrefillJob`** `interface { requestId: string; staffId: string; rev?: number | null }  (10-18)` — `rev` = the revision at queue time; absent on old jobs → falls back to the confirmed/closed checks alone.
- **`registerCalcWorker / registerCalcReviewWorker / registerCalcPrefillWorker`** `(boss: PgBoss) => Promise<void>` — Registered in platform/jobs/boss.ts WORKER_REGISTRATIONS under names 'calc-overdue' (81), 'calc-review' (82), 'calc-prefill' (83-86).

Comment 9-18: deliberately not upsaleScopeFor nor seesAllMoney. Gates /hisoblash/nazorat.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/chain.ts` — The correction CHAIN — what «V2» counts (derived, never stored) and the sealed-versions registry.

- **`ChainVersion`** `interface { versionId; requestId; quoteNo: number; sealedAt: Date; sealedByName; section: CalcSectionName; totalUsd; superseded: boolean; supersededByNo: number|null; recalcOpen: boolean; expired: boolean }  (96-112)` — quoteNo = `row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY root_id ORDER BY sealed_at, id)` over calc_versions joined to the recursive tree (treeSql 47-94, depth cap 64); superseded = a child request EXISTS (edge, not rank); recalcOpen = a child exists with no version; expired = valid_until < now.
- **`chainOf`** `export async function chainOf(requestId: string, now = new Date()): Promise<ChainVersion[]>  (160-177)` — Walks UP to the root then lists every sealed version under it, oldest seal first. Same answer from any link (integration test).
- **`quoteNoFor`** `export async function quoteNoFor(versionId: string): Promise<number | null>  (180-186)` — null for a version that does not exist.
- **`RegistryFilters / RegistryRow / REGISTRY_CAP`** `RegistryFilters { from?; to?; section?; sealerId?; q?; leadsReadable: boolean }; REGISTRY_CAP = 200  (188-216)` — `leadsReadable` = actor has `crm.leads`; a lead's name is neither printed (cardLabel null) nor searchable without it (#514).
- **`registryRows / registryCounts / registrySealers`** `registryRows(f, now?): Promise<RegistryRow[]>; registryCounts(f): Promise<{versions; jobs}>; registrySealers(): Promise<{id; name}[]>  (260-330)` — ONE row per sealed version, newest first, LIMIT REGISTRY_CAP; rows and counts share `registryWhere` (#513); jobs = count(DISTINCT root_id).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/intake.ts` — The pure checklist the queue reuses (bot intake, round 37).

- **`CalcSection / CALC_SECTIONS`** `type 'yolkira'|'rastamojka'|'podklyuch'; const CALC_SECTIONS = ['yolkira','rastamojka','podklyuch']  (21-23)` — Mirrored by DB CHECK calc_requests_section_check.
- **`REQUIRED_FIELDS`** `Record<CalcSection, CalcField[]> = { yolkira: [fromCity,toCity,weightKg,volumeM3,goods], rastamojka: [weightKg,volumeM3,goods,itemMeasure], podklyuch: [fromCity,toCity,weightKg,volumeM3,goods,itemMeasure] }  (39-43)` — Podklyuch «is the sum of both, so it asks for everything».
- **`CalcField`** `'fromCity'|'toCity'|'weightKg'|'volumeM3'|'goods'|'itemMeasure'  (45-70)` — `itemMeasure` = ONE field (a count OR a weight OR the law's unit), per-item, customs sections only (comment 51-69, #649 twice).
- **`loneWeightKg`** `export function loneWeightKg(itemCount: number, totalKg): number | null  (126-132)` — `itemCount === 1 && total > 0 ? total : null` — the ONE home for «a single line's weight IS the shipment's», applied by all three landing doors.
- **`missingFields`** `export function missingFields(section: CalcSection, facts: CalcFacts): CalcField[]  (164-185)` — goods → items.length === 0; itemMeasure → some item with quantity, weightKg AND measureQty all null (silent when no goods at all, deliberate); weightKg/volumeM3 → !(Number > 0) («0 kg is a blank»); cities → empty string.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts` — Phase B+ workspace; the parts that touch the REQUEST lifecycle: canSeal, the seal's closing UPDATE, recalcFromSealed, lockRequestInTx/mutateRequest, setCargoFacts, lastAnswerAnchorFor.

- **`canSeal`** `export function canSeal(workspace: { blockers: unknown[]; totals: { ok: boolean } | null }): boolean  (1752-1757)` — `blockers.length === 0 && totals?.ok`. ONE predicate for three doors (#513): sealCalc's `not_ready`, the seal button, and finishCalcRequest's `seal_instead`.
- **`sealCalc (closing UPDATE)`** `export async function sealCalc(requestId, input: SealInput, ctx): Promise<SealedVersion>  (1398; UPDATE 1541-1560)` — `lockRequestInTx` then `if (locked.rev !== workspace.rev) throw 'conflict'`; UPDATE calc_requests SET completed_at=now, completed_by, completed_via='sealed', current_version_no+1, rev+1 WHERE id AND completed_at IS NULL RETURNING versionNo…; no row → `already_closed`; INSERT calc_versions. announceSeal (1668-1716): requester gets 'CalcSealed' («🧮 Hisob tayyor: $total\nlink»); discount or band override → 'CalcDiscounted' to discountAudience (super_admin+admin roles ∪ accountant ∪ requestedBy ∪ superseded request's requestedBy, 1718-1743).
- **`recalcFromSealed`** `export async function recalcFromSealed(requestId: string, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<string>  (1803-1953)` — Guards in order: `not_found` (1808), `not_closed` (1809), `not_sealed` (no calc_versions row, 1819), `recalc_superseded` (a child exists and is completed) / `recalc_open` (a child exists and is open) (1832). Tx: INSERT calc_requests copying entity/requestedBy(`ctx.actorId ?? old.requestedBy`)/assigneeId/itemCount/section/route/kg/m³/source/noteId/freightZone/hasCertificate/feeOverrideUsd, `supersedesRequestId: old.id`, `dueAt: now + 2h` (fixed); copies groups (NOT confirmations, 1902-1904), items (incl. bazas, importRowId, measure pair), extras; writeAudit(entityType 'calc_request', action 'create', after {supersedes}). Mints NO task, leaves taken_at NULL. Gated in actions.ts recalcAction on `admin.settings.manage`.
- **`lockRequestInTx (private)`** `async function lockRequestInTx(tx, requestId, opts: { ignoreAiClaim?: boolean } = {}): Promise<{ rev: number }>  (2396-2422)` — `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`; `not_found`; `already_closed` when completed_at set; `ai_running` when ai_proposal_started_at within AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS (10 min, 2050) unless ignoreAiClaim. Every workspace mutator goes through it (mutateRequest 2440-2450 = lock → fn → bumpRevInTx). Fenced by tests/unit/calc-clock.test.ts.
- **`setCargoFacts`** `export async function setCargoFacts(requestId, input: { fromCity; toCity; weightKg; volumeM3 }, ctx): Promise<void>  (703-737)` — The VED's door onto the request's own kg/m³/route (owner's report, comment 706-722); `bad_number` (NaN / ≥1e9), `measure_positive` (≤0); via mutateRequest (moves rev, not a confirmation clear); audit entityType 'calc_request' after {cargoFacts}.
- **`assertOpen (private)`** `async function assertOpen(requestId): Promise<calcRequests row>  (664-669)` — `not_found` / `already_closed` — «a check, not a lock» (mutateRequest comment 2432-2436).
- **`lastAnswerAnchorFor`** `export async function lastAnswerAnchorFor(entityType, entityId): Promise<{ requestId; amountUsd; currency; completedAt: Date; stands: boolean; expired: boolean } | null>  (3780-3822)` — Newest completed request with answer_amount NOT NULL; `stands` via answerFloorStandsSql; expired = completedAt + setting `quote_valid_days` (default QUOTE_VALID_DAYS_DEFAULT = 30, line 99) < now. Phase 4's «Готово answer as an offer anchor».
- **`loadWorkspace`** `export async function loadWorkspace(requestId: string, opts?): Promise<Workspace | null>  (317)` — Pool read; the [id] page and finishCalcRequest call it. Workspace carries `blockers: SealBlocker[]` (249-259: section_missing | no_groups | ungrouped_items | groups_unconfirmed | customs | freight | fee | totals | customs_on_yolkira), `sealedVersion`, `completedAt`, `rev`.

- **`landIntake (openCalcRequest call site)`** `(196-232)` — After addActivity(noteId): openCalcRequest with items via `itemFacts(facts)`, noteId, source 'bot', hasMaterials = fileCount > 0, ctx {actorId: collectedBy}; caught → `queued=false`; returns `{ ...target, queued, requestId }`; then moveToCalcStage (settings `crm_calc_stage`/`deal_calc_stage`).

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/staff-handlers.ts` — grammy shell for the 🧮 flow.

- **`c:save handler + queuePrefill`** `(620-668; queuePrefill 397-411)` — landCollectedIntake → endIntake → if `target.queued && target.requestId` → queuePrefill: `rev = prefillTicket(requestId)`, `enqueue(JOB_CALC_PREFILL, { requestId, staffId, rev })` (warn-only on failure); reply «✅ Saqlandi — …» + «🧮 Hisoblash navbatiga tushdi — VED xodimi javob beradi.» or «⚠️ Hisoblash navbatiga tushmadi — kartadan qo‘lda yuboring.»

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/prefill.ts` — The AI VED hodimi pass (adjacent; its stand-down reads the request's rev).

- **`PrefillStand / prefillStanding / prefillTicket`** `type PrefillStand = 'ok'|'touched'|'confirmed'|'closed'|'not_found' (59); prefillStanding(requestId, expectRev: number|null): Promise<PrefillStand> (61-78); prefillTicket(requestId): Promise<number|null> (81-87)` — closed when completed_at set; touched when rev moved; confirmed when any group has confirmed_at.

#### `src/modules/wms/attachments/access.ts` — File read gate; the calc widening.

- **`decideAttachmentRead case 'crm_activity'`** `(198-222)` — Deal note → crm.leads|clients.manage|ved.docs; lead note → crm.leads|clients.manage, OR `ved.docs` when a calc_requests row has `note_id = attachment.entityId` → rule 'crm-activity-calc' (law 11, 0085's calc_requests_note_idx).

#### `src/modules/platform/db/schema/wms.ts` — Drizzle tables calcRequests (1911-2015) and calcRequestItems (2017-).

- **`calcRequests / calcRequestItems`** `pgTable('calc_requests', …) / pgTable('calc_request_items', …)` — See dataModel.

#### `src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/tarix/page.tsx` — «Muhrlangan hisob-kitoblar» registry.

- **`CalcRegistryPage (default)`** `searchParams { dan?; gacha?; bolim?; ved?; q? }` — Gate `mayReadCalcRegistry` (68-70); dates via isoDay round-trip; bolim ∈ CALC_SECTIONS; ved must match UUID; q ≤80 chars; leadsReadable = crm.leads.

### Flows


**Open a request from a card (desk form)**

1. components/calc-send-form.tsx: files POST /api/files/upload {entityType:'crm_activity', entityId: noteId(uuid minted in browser)}; submitCalcAction({section, cities, kg, m³, goods parsed one-per-line, noteId|'' , noteText, revalidate})
2. app/(protected)/hisoblash/actions.ts submitCalcAction: getActor → canWriteDeal → isCalcSection → entityType check → lead needs crm.leads; items.weightKg ?? loneWeightKg(); note {id,text} when noteId
3. modules/wms/calc/service.ts openCalcRequest: card exists (deals/leads) → open-count cap (MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER) → note_taken/note_foreign checks → addActivity(crm_activities, kind 'note') → tnvedFor() memory fills items.tnvedCode → nextVedAssignee() → dueAt = now + calcDueMinutes(items.length, Boolean(noteId)||hasMaterials)
4. tx: INSERT calc_requests (taken_at = now iff assignee) + INSERT calc_request_items + writeAudit(card, 'update', {calcRequested,…})
5. after tx: createTask(title 'Hisoblash: <label> (<n>)', priority 1, dueAt, entity=card) → 'TaskAssigned' to assignee with extra.taskId → UPDATE calc_requests.task_id; OR no assignee → 'CalcRequested' to super_admins
6. action revalidates card path + '/hisoblash'; panel (calc-panel.tsx) shows the row via openCalcFor

**Open a request from the staff bot 🧮 and hand it to the AI VED hodimi**

1. telegram/staff-handlers.ts callback c:save → staffForChat → landCollectedIntake → intake-land.ts landIntake: resolve target (deal via dealFor / lead) → addActivity(noteId) → openCalcRequest({items: itemFacts(facts), noteId, source:'bot', hasMaterials: fileCount>0}, {actorId: collectedBy}) → queued/requestId → moveToCalcStage (settings crm_calc_stage / deal_calc_stage via moveLead/moveDeal)
2. staff-handlers.ts queuePrefill: prefill.ts prefillTicket(requestId) → rev; jobs/boss.ts enqueue(JOB_CALC_PREFILL='calc.prefill', {requestId, staffId, rev})
3. calc/jobs.ts registerCalcPrefillWorker: prefillStanding(requestId, rev) !== 'ok' → stand down; else aiPrefill(requestId, {actorId:null}) → notifyStaffTelegram(staffId, 'CalcPrefilled', out.text)
4. request stays OPEN in the queue, groups unconfirmed; the VED takes it over on /hisoblash/[id]

**Open a request from a CRM Telegram thread**

1. components/thread-calc.tsx → actions.ts threadCalcAnalyzeAction (canReadTg + canWriteDeal + isCalcSection + lead→crm.leads) → from-thread.ts threadCalcAnalyze (preview)
2. actions.ts threadCalcSendAction → from-thread.ts threadCalcSend: deal → its own deal; client card → dealFor(); note text via intakeNoteText(via 'suhbatdan'); openCalcRequest({note:{id,text}, source:'card', hasMaterials, items: itemFacts(facts)})

**Take / release**

1. calc-actions.tsx calc-take → actions.ts takeCalcAction → run('ved.docs') → service.ts takeCalcRequest: UPDATE calc_requests … assignee_id IS DISTINCT FROM me RETURNING → loser gets not_found|already_closed|already_yours
2. won.taskId → UPDATE tasks.assignee_id (status='open'); else createTask under taker + UPDATE task_id
3. writeAudit(card, {calcTakenBy, calcRequest}); notifyStaffTelegram(requestedBy, 'CalcTaken')
4. calc-release → releaseCalcRequest: UPDATE assignee/task/taken_at NULL RETURNING old taskId → UPDATE tasks status 'cancelled', result 'Navbatga qaytarildi' → audit {calcReleased}

**Overdue sweep**

1. platform/jobs/boss.ts WORKER_REGISTRATIONS 'calc-overdue' → calc/jobs.ts registerCalcWorker: boss.schedule('calc.overdue', '*/5 * * * *')
2. service.ts notifyOverdueCalcs: UPDATE calc_requests SET overdue_notified_at=now WHERE completed_at IS NULL AND due_at < now AND overdue_notified_at IS NULL RETURNING (the CLAIM)
3. hoisted: usersWithRoles(['super_admin']), usersWithPermission('ved.docs') only if any row unassigned, deal codes / lead names in two IN queries
4. per row: notifyStaffTelegram({userIds: requester+owners+(unassigned? ved pool), type:'CalcOverdue', exceptUserId: assignee})

**Ending: finish with a typed answer («Bajarildi»)**

1. calc-actions.tsx calc-finish-open → calc-finish → finishCalcAction(id, {amount (null when canSeal), currency, note})
2. service.ts finishCalcRequest: dynamic import workspace → loadWorkspace(id) → canSeal → throw 'seal_instead'
3. endRequest(id, {via:'task', answerAmount, answerCurrency, answerNote}): UPDATE calc_requests SET completed_at, completed_by, completed_via='task', answer_* WHERE open RETURNING; null → 'already_closed'; task → status 'done', result 'Hisoblandi'
4. writeAudit(card, {calcDone, amount}); notifyStaffTelegram(requestedBy, 'CalcDone', «✅ Hisoblash tayyor: label 💵 amount cur 📝 note link»)
5. calc-panel.tsx lastCalcAnswerFor shows «Javob: amount cur date»; lastAnswerAnchorFor opens the phase-4 offer door when USD and within quote_valid_days

**Ending: return (bounce back)**

1. calc-actions.tsx calc-return-open → calc-return-reason → calc-return → returnCalcAction(id, reason)
2. service.ts returnCalcRequest: reason.trim().length<3 → 'reason_required'; endRequest via 'returned' with return_reason(≤500); task result 'Qaytarildi'
3. audit {calcReturned, reason}; 'CalcReturned' to requester with the reason

**Ending: the seal (workspace)**

1. calc-workspace.tsx seal → actions.ts sealAction → workspace.ts sealCalc: compute on pool → tx lockRequestInTx → rev CAS ('conflict') → UPDATE calc_requests SET completed_at, completed_by, completed_via='sealed', current_version_no+1, rev+1 WHERE completed_at IS NULL RETURNING → INSERT calc_versions → adopt superseded request's receipts (E1)
2. announceSeal: 'CalcSealed' to requestedBy; discount/band override → 'CalcDiscounted' to discountAudience()
3. the task minted at open is NOT closed by the seal path (endRequest is not called; sealCalc closes only calc_requests) — see gaps

**Ending: deal lines saved / task closed by hand / task cancelled**

1. deals/service.ts saveLines (after tx, lines.length>0) → completeCalcForDeal(dealId): OLDEST open request on the deal → endRequest via 'lines'
2. tasks/service.ts completeTask (after audit) → completeCalcForTask(taskId): request with this task_id → endRequest via 'task' (no figure) — the bot's «✅ Bajarildi» (staff-bot.ts completeTaskFromBot → completeTask) ends here
3. tasks/service.ts cancelTask → releaseCalcForTask(taskId): assignee/task/taken_at NULL — back to the queue

**Correction («Qayta hisoblash») and the V2 chain**

1. calc-workspace.tsx calc-recalc (only when canRecalc = admin.settings.manage) → actions.ts recalcAction → authorize('admin.settings.manage')
2. workspace.ts recalcFromSealed: not_found → not_closed → not_sealed (no calc_versions) → child exists ? (completed ? recalc_superseded : recalc_open)
3. tx: INSERT calc_requests {…copied, supersedes_request_id: old.id, requested_by: ctx.actorId ?? old.requestedBy, assignee_id: old.assigneeId, due_at: now+2h}; copy calc_groups (no confirmations), calc_request_items, calc_extras; writeAudit('calc_request','create',{supersedes})
4. calc-panel.tsx / calc-workspace.tsx / tarix: chain.ts chainOf(requestId) → quoteNo (rank by sealed_at within root), superseded (edge), recalcOpen; ChainStateChip prints the standing; stored current_version_no stays 1

**Won lead → deal re-key**

1. crm/service.ts winLead → createDeal → rekeyLeadCalcRequests(leadId, dealId): UPDATE calc_requests (ALL, incl. sealed) entity → deal; UPDATE calc_offers entity → deal
2. later saveLines on the deal can stop the clock via completeCalcForDeal

**Queue screen and VED home**

1. app/(protected)/page.tsx → role-flows.ts buildHomeFlow → ved_manager → vedFlowCounts → service.ts calcQueueCounts (openRequests) → VedFlow rows ved-flow-hero (/hisoblash, count calcOpen, warn calcLate)
2. app/(protected)/hisoblash/page.tsx: ved.docs gate → calcQueue (LIMIT 200, unclaimed first then oldest; missingFor via goodsByRequest) + calcSpeed(30 d) → rows calc-queue-row (lead rows print «Hisoblash», card link only under crm.leads) + calc-late / calc-unassigned / calc-missing chips + calc-speed table

### Data model

- **`calc_requests`** (migration 0052_calc_requests.sql (round 28), 0085_calc_queue.sql (phase A), 0086_calc_pricing.sql (276-291), 0088_calc_upsale.sql (136-137), 0089 (66-67), 0091_calc_law.sql (69-75), 0092_calc_measure.sql (59-60), 0093_calc_offer_answer.sql (21-22)): id uuid PK; entity_type text NOT NULL; entity_id uuid NOT NULL; requested_by uuid NOT NULL FK users; assignee_id uuid FK users (nullable since 0085); item_count integer NOT NULL; task_id uuid FK tasks (nullable since 0085); section text; from_city text; to_city text; weight_kg numeric(12,3); volume_m3 numeric(12,3); source text; note_id uuid FK crm_activities ON DELETE SET NULL; requested_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(); due_at timestamptz NOT NULL; taken_at timestamptz; completed_at timestamptz; completed_by uuid FK users; completed_via text; return_reason text; answer_amount numeric(14,2); answer_currency text; answer_note text; overdue_notified_at timestamptz; current_version_no integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 (0086); supersedes_request_id uuid FK calc_requests ON DELETE SET NULL (0086); freight_zone text (0086); ai_proposal_started_at timestamptz (0086); has_certificate boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true (0091); fee_override_usd numeric(12,2) (0091); rev integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 (0092); updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now() (0085) — CHECKs: calc_requests_entity_check entity_type IN ('deal','lead'); calc_requests_items_check item_count BETWEEN 0 AND 1000 (0085 relaxed from 1..500); calc_requests_via_check completed_via IS NULL OR IN ('lines','task','returned','sealed') (0086 added 'sealed'); calc_requests_section_check IN ('yolkira','rastamojka','podklyuch'); calc_requests_source_check IN ('card','bot'); calc_requests_fee_override_check (>=0, <> NaN); calc_requests_answer_amount_check answer_amount IS NULL OR (>0 AND <> 'NaN') NOT VALID (0093). Indexes: calc_requests_assignee_idx (assignee_id, requested_at) (0052); calc_requests_note_idx (note_id) (0085); calc_requests_entity_idx (entity_type, entity_id) (0089); calc_requests_supersedes_idx partial (0088). DROPPED: calc_requests_open_entity_idx (0085 §2). 0085 §8 backfill: `UPDATE calc_requests SET overdue_notified_at = now() WHERE completed_at IS NULL AND overdue_notified_at IS NULL`.
- **`calc_request_items`** (migration 0085 §9, 0086, 0092, 0094): id uuid PK; request_id uuid NOT NULL FK calc_requests ON DELETE CASCADE; seq integer NOT NULL (seller's ordering, never moves); name text NOT NULL; quantity numeric(12,3); unit text; weight_kg numeric(12,3); volume_m3 numeric(12,3); amount numeric(14,2); currency text; tnved_code text (TNVED memory at intake); note text; group_id uuid FK calc_groups SET NULL (0086); baza_usd numeric(14,4); baza_basis text; baza_source text ('dictionary'|'typed'|'import', never 'ai'); measure_unit text + measure_qty numeric(14,4) (0092 pair); import_row_id bigint FK customs_import_rows SET NULL (0094) — CHECKs: calc_request_items_seq_idx UNIQUE (request_id, seq); calc_request_items_group_idx; calc_items_baza_basis_check IN ('unit','kg','juft','litr','m2'); calc_items_baza_source_check
- **`tasks (the clock's carrier)`** (migration phase 3 tasks): Minted by openCalcRequest/takeCalcRequest: title `Hisoblash: ${label} (${itemCount})`, note '', typeId null, assigneeId, dueAt (ISO of request.due_at), priority 1, entityType/entityId = the card, repeatUnit null. Closed by endRequest: status 'done', done_at, done_by, result 'Hisoblandi'|'Qaytarildi'; by releaseCalcRequest: status 'cancelled', result 'Navbatga qaytarildi' — CHECKs: tasks.status must be 'open' for any calc-side UPDATE (WHERE eq(tasks.status,'open'))
- **`notifications (via notifyStaffTelegram)`** (migration 0082): user_id, channel 'telegram', type ∈ {CalcRequested, CalcTaken, CalcReturned, CalcDone, CalcOverdue, CalcPrefilled, CalcSealed, CalcDiscounted, TaskAssigned, TaskDone}, payload {text, …extra}, status 'pending'|'muted' — CHECKs: 0082 notify_claim status CHECK (claimed_at/'sending')
- **`crm_activities (the materials)`** (migration 0085 §4 (note_id FK + index)): id = the pre-bound noteId; entity_type/entity_id = the card; kind 'note'; note = seller's text or intakeNoteText(); attachments rows with entity_type 'crm_activity', entity_id = noteId — CHECKs: openCalcRequest: note must not pre-exist (note_taken) and must sit on the same card (note_foreign); attachment gate rule 'crm-activity-calc' widens ved.docs onto a LEAD note only when calc_requests.note_id points at it
- **`calc_versions (read by chain.ts)`** (migration 0086): id, request_id FK calc_requests CASCADE, version_no, sealed_at, sealed_by, valid_until, section, total_usd, per_m3_usd, per_kg_usd, discount_usd, band_override_min, … — CHECKs: immutable («never updated after it is written», 0086 §7)
- **`calc_offers (re-keyed on win)`** (migration 0087, 0088, 0093): entity_type/entity_id denormalised from the card; version_id | request_id (one-anchor CHECK 0093) — CHECKs: calc_offers_one_anchor_check
- **`receipts.calc_request_id (E1 link, read on the [id] page)`** (migration 0089): uuid FK calc_requests ON DELETE SET NULL; calc_link_confirmed_at — CHECKs: —

### Invariants (and the fence that enforces each)

- ONE fragment for «still waiting» — `openRequests = isNull(completedAt)` (service.ts:91) filters the queue, the counters and the VED home; fence: tests/integration/calc-queue.integration.test.ts «the counters and the rows answer the same question» (291-295).
- The queue owns assignment: fewest-open active `ved.docs` holder, never-had-one first (nextVedAssignee 102-120); fence: integration «assigns on open» (250-254) asserts the assignee is in the ved.docs pool.
- No door anywhere accepts an assigneeId; fence: tests/unit/calc-queue.test.ts 108-122 greps actions.ts and calc-send-form.tsx (comments stripped) for /assigneeId/.
- The take is decided by the UPDATE (`assignee_id IS DISTINCT FROM me … RETURNING`), never a read-then-write; fences: unit 124-134 (source order) + integration race 268-289 (exactly one fulfilled, exactly one new task).
- The overdue sweep CLAIMS (`overdue_notified_at = now` in the UPDATE) before anything is sent; fences: unit 136-145 (claim index < notify index) + integration 331-349.
- The 'lines' clock stop runs OUTSIDE saveLines' transaction and only on a non-empty save; fences: unit 147-157 + tests/unit/tx-pool.test.ts (pool inside tx = whole-app freeze, #714).
- A cancelled task releases its request instead of parking it; fence: unit 159-165 (cancelTask body contains releaseCalcForTask).
- A won lead's calc requests (ALL, sealed included) AND its calc_offers follow onto the deal; fences: unit 167-170, integration 352-374, tests/unit/calc-rekey.test.ts (derived over every calc_* table carrying both entity columns).
- A return needs a reason of ≥3 chars after trim (`reason_required`); fence: integration 299-309.
- A closed request cannot be closed twice — endRequest's WHERE carries `openRequests` and returns null → `already_closed`; fence: integration 321-327.
- «Готово» on a job that could be SEALED is refused (`seal_instead`), and still accepted when the workspace cannot price; fence: tests/integration/calc-finish.integration.test.ts (both directions).
- Section is validated against CALC_SECTIONS (`bad_section`) and by DB CHECK calc_requests_section_check; item_count 0..1000 (MAX_CALC_ITEMS → `too_many_items`, CHECK calc_requests_items_check); completed_via ∈ lines|task|returned|sealed (CHECK); answer_amount > 0 and not NaN (0093 CHECK, NOT VALID); fence for the section wire: unit 98-106.
- The materials note must be minted under the pre-bound id (`note_taken` when it already exists) and must sit on the same card (`note_foreign`); fence: integration 377-410.
- Every workspace mutator locks the request row (mutateRequest/lockRequestInTx) and bumps `rev`; the seal and both confirm doors CAS on it (`conflict`); fences: tests/unit/calc-clock.test.ts (derived from workspace.ts's export list — an unclassified export is red) + tests/integration/calc-clock.integration.test.ts (deterministic FOR UPDATE holder, observed via pg_stat_activity).
- A correction is a NEW request, never a re-open: recalcFromSealed refuses not_closed/not_sealed/recalc_open/recalc_superseded; fence: tests/integration/calc-chain.integration.test.ts 244-267.
- «V2» is DERIVED (rank among the chain's sealed versions by seal time, superseded by the EDGE); the stored current_version_no stays 1 on a correction — pinned as a PREMISE; fence: calc-chain.integration 184-241.
- A lead's name is printed/searchable in the registry only under `crm.leads` (#514); fence: calc-chain.integration 297-310. The queue page applies the same rule in JSX (page.tsx:92, 131-135) — no test found for that half.
- Read doors enumerated over every seeded role: calcControlScopeFor (all/own/none) and mayReadCalcRegistry (= control scope !== 'none'); fence: tests/unit/calc-control-scope.test.ts over ROLE_MATRIX.
- The checklist is the bot's own `missingFields` (service.ts missingFor is an adapter; null section → []); a stored row with no section has no checklist; fences: unit 32-76 + integration «the checklist can be SATISFIED» (200-246) reading quantity/weightKg/measureQty off the ROWS.
- Every speed figure excludes `completed_via = 'returned'` (calcSpeed 1166-1170, migration 0085 §5 comment) — stated in code and migration; no dedicated test found.
- Pre-0085 open rows were stamped `overdue_notified_at = now()` in the migration (0085 §8) so the reopened sweep does not announce year-old work.
- Every VED action re-derives ids in the service and runs under `run('ved.docs')`; server-behind (`isServerBehind`) is caught to `'server_behind'` at every action (#472); no test found beyond the pattern.

### Vocabulary (codes, keys, testids, settings, env, jobs, constants)

- CalcError codes (service.ts): unauthenticated, too_many_items, not_found, too_many_open, note_taken, note_foreign, already_closed, already_yours, reason_required, seal_instead
- CalcError codes (workspace.ts, request-level): not_closed, not_sealed, recalc_open, recalc_superseded, conflict, ai_running, zone_unknown, measure_positive, bad_number, not_ready
- Action-level codes (actions.ts): forbidden, server_behind, bad_section, validation, unauthenticated
- i18n `calc.errors.*` (messages/{uz,ru,zh-CN,en}.json) present for: validation, forbidden, not_found, unauthenticated, too_many_items, too_many_open, note_taken, note_foreign, already_closed, already_yours, reason_required, bad_section, failed, not_ready, not_closed, server_behind, conflict, ai_running, seal_instead, not_sealed, recalc_open, recalc_superseded, measure_positive, bad_number (+ workspace/offer codes)
- i18n `calc.*` keys read by these screens: title, queueTitle, myQueue, historyTitle, dictTitle, registryTitle, controlTitle, openNone, items, late, takenBy, unassigned, requester, dueBy, openCard, missingLabel, speedTitle, doneCol, minutes {n}, onTimeShort, openCol, askedAt, route, complete, materials, goods, returned, finish, answered, take, release, returnBtn, returnReasonLabel, answerAmount, answerNote, sealInstead, linkedReceipts, linkNoneOnCard, panelTitle, sendOnPhone, sectionLabel, goodsHint, send, deadlineHint, sealExpired, sealExpiredHint, validUntil, discount, chainPrev, chainTitle, chainCurrent, supersededBy {no}, supersededPlain, chainRecalcOpen, registryNav, registryCounts, registryCapped, registryNone, registryLead, filterSealer, editCargoFacts, sections.yolkira|rastamojka|podklyuch, fields.fromCity|toCity|weightKg|volumeM3|goods|itemMeasure
- i18n `home.*` for the VED flow: flowTitle, flowCalcOpen («Navbatdagi hisoblashlar»), flowCalcLate («kechikkan»), flowDocsPending, flowTnvedMissing
- Notification event types emitted by this subsystem: CalcRequested (no assignee → super_admins), CalcTaken (→ requester), CalcReturned (→ requester), CalcDone (→ requester), CalcOverdue (→ requester + super_admins [+ ved pool when untaken], except assignee), CalcPrefilled (→ the staff who sent, from the prefill job), CalcSealed (→ requester, workspace.ts:1679), CalcDiscounted (→ discountAudience, workspace.ts:1709), TaskAssigned (createTask/reassign), TaskDone (completeTask)
- MUTE_GROUPS (platform/notifications/mutes.ts): tasks = [TasksDue, TaskAssigned, TaskDone, CalcRequested, CalcTaken, CalcDone, CalcReturned, CalcPrefilled]; alerts ∋ CalcOverdue, CalcBelowFloor; operations ∋ CalcDictReview, CalcOffer
- pg-boss job names (calc/jobs.ts): JOB_CALC_OVERDUE='calc.overdue' (cron '*/5 * * * *'), JOB_CALC_REVIEW='calc.dict-review' (cron '0 6 * * *'), JOB_CALC_PREFILL='calc.prefill' (enqueued, payload CalcPrefillJob {requestId, staffId, rev?}); boss.ts WORKER_REGISTRATIONS names 'calc-overdue', 'calc-review', 'calc-prefill'
- Constants: MAX_CALC_ITEMS=1000, MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER=20, calcDueMinutes (30/line, cap 120; 0 lines → 30 or 60 with materials), calcQueue LIMIT 200, REGISTRY_CAP=200, AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS=600000, QUOTE_VALID_DAYS_DEFAULT=30, recalcFromSealed dueAt = +2 h, calcSpeed window on /hisoblash = 30 days, chain recursion depth cap 64
- Settings keys: quote_valid_days (lastAnswerAnchorFor, workspace.ts), crm_calc_stage / deal_calc_stage (intake-land.ts moveToCalcStage — round 84)
- Env vars: APP_URL (cardLink in notifications/links.ts; staff-handlers reply link); ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (prefill's aiConfigured, adjacent)
- Permissions: ved.docs (queue, detail, all four VED actions, workspace writes, nav /hisoblash), crm.leads (lead card link/name, lead-side submit), admin.settings.manage (recalcAction), finance.reports (calcControlScopeFor 'all', mayReadCalcRegistry, nav /hisoblash/tarix), admin.dictionaries.manage (tariff only), canWriteDeal(actor.permissions) = DEAL_WRITE_PERMISSIONS (submitCalcAction, CalcPanel, makeOfferAction)
- Roles named in code: ved_manager (buildHomeFlow, MENU_BY_ROLE), super_admin (CalcRequested/CalcOverdue audience), admin + accountant (discountAudience)
- Audit `after` keys written on the CARD (entityType deal|lead, action 'update'): calcRequested, section, items, dueAt (open); calcTakenBy, calcRequest (take); calcReleased (release); calcReturned, reason (return); calcDone, amount (finish). On entityType 'calc_request': freightZone (setFreightZone), cargoFacts (setCargoFacts), action 'create' after {supersedes} (recalcFromSealed)
- Task strings: title `Hisoblash: ${label} (${n})`, results 'Hisoblandi' | 'Qaytarildi' | 'Navbatga qaytarildi'
- Telegram texts (hard-coded Uzbek): «🧮 Hisoblash so'rovi keldi: … ⚠️ VED xodimi topilmadi — navbatda turibdi», «🧮 Hisoblash olindi: … 👤 name», «↩️ Hisoblash qaytarildi: … 📝 reason», «✅ Hisoblash tayyor: … 💵 amount cur 📝 note», «🔴 Hisoblash kechikdi: label (n) / 👤 name | ⚠️ Hech kim olmagan / ⏱ N daqiqa kechikdi», «🧮 Hisob tayyor: $total», «⚠️ Hisobda chegirma: …»; bot: «🧮 Hisoblash navbatiga tushdi — VED xodimi javob beradi.» / «⚠️ Hisoblash navbatiga tushmadi — kartadan qo‘lda yuboring.»

### Gaps (not built / cut / owed / suspect)

- `CalcSealed` (workspace.ts:1679) and `CalcDiscounted` (workspace.ts:1709) are in NO `MUTE_GROUPS` list (mutes.ts 7-126): only a user's `'all'` silences them and the profile's group checkboxes cannot; the mutes tripwire (tests/unit/notification-mutes.test.ts:45-60) walks `buildRecipients` cases only, so direct `notifyStaffTelegram` callers are outside its fence. Evidence: `grep -rn CalcSealed src` hits only workspace.ts.
- The SEAL closes calc_requests but never closes the TASK minted at open: sealCalc's UPDATE (workspace.ts:1541-1560) touches calc_requests only and `endRequest` (which closes the task) is not called on the 'sealed' path — the VED's timed «Hisoblash: …» task stays `open` on their /bugun after sealing (unverified in a browser; inferred from code — no test asserts either way).
- `recalcFromSealed` (workspace.ts:1838-1860) copies `assignee_id` but mints NO task and leaves `taken_at` NULL; `due_at` is a fixed `now + 2 h` regardless of item count (not `calcDueMinutes`); `requested_by` becomes `ctx.actorId` (the admin who pressed recalc) so `CalcSealed` on a correction goes to that admin and NOT the original seller — only the CONCESSION message widens to the superseded request's requester via `discountAudience` (1718-1743). The overdue sweep will flag every correction after 2 h with nobody's task red.
- `completeCalcForDeal` (service.ts:601-612) ends only the OLDEST open request on the deal via 'lines' — since 0085 allows several open requests per card, a `saveLines` closes one arbitrarily and leaves the siblings open with their clocks running.
- `lastCalcAnswerFor` (service.ts:1118-1137) takes the newest `completed_via='task'` row including a task closed by hand with no figure (`completeCalcForTask`), so the card panel then prints «Javob: —» (calc-panel.tsx:211-222 renders whenever `last` is non-null).
- Audit rows written with `entityType: 'calc_request'` (setFreightZone workspace.ts:683, setCargoFacts :731, recalcFromSealed :1947) have no screen: History panels live on lead/deal/client cards, and `grep -rn calc_request src/modules/platform/entities src/modules/platform/audit` is empty. The card-side keys `calcRequested/calcTakenBy/calcReleased/calcReturned/calcDone` also have no `AUDIT_FIELD_LABELS` entry (grep empty) — the card history prints the raw key name (#62's fallback).
- `takeCalcRequest` re-points the previous holder's open task to the taker (service.ts:373-376) with no notification to the loser and no audit on the task — the comment (369-372) states «Nobody needs telling either — the new assignee is the presser», which is true of the taker only.
- `notifyOverdueCalcs` addresses `usersWithRoles(['super_admin'])` (service.ts:743) — the `admin` ROLE is excluded, unlike `discountAudience` (super_admin + admin) and unlike CLAUDE.md's «his own accounts carry «bir admin va sotuvchi», not necessarily super_admin».
- The queue is `LIMIT 200` (service.ts:851) with no «capped» sentence: page.tsx's subtitle prints `rows.length` while `calcQueueCounts` (the home number) is uncapped — the two disagree past 200 open requests. (Comment 13-17 says the engine is «one screen away».)
- No ownership gate on the request: /hisoblash/[id] (page.tsx:42-44) and all four actions gate on `ved.docs` alone; any holder can take/return/finish/set cargo facts on anybody's request. By design per service.ts:41-42 («Any VED person may take it over, and that is audited») — stated, not defective.
- `openCalcFor` (service.ts:1065-1115) returns `CalcQueueRow` with `label: ''`, `requesterName: ''`, `missing: []` — the type promises facts the panel never gets; the checklist is not shown on the card.
- `calcQueue` page header comment (page.tsx:19-24) says a lead's «card is not linked», but lines 131-135 DO link it for a `crm.leads` holder — the code is the narrower, correct statement.
- mutes.ts:18-21 comment says «The calc queue's three everyday messages ride here» while the list carries FOUR (CalcPrefilled was added, explained at 22-24) — stale wording only.
- Several `calc.*` i18n keys look like round-28 leftovers with no observed reader in this subsystem's files (inQueue, lateBy, leftMin, inProgress, reportTitle, lastDays, employee, avgCol, maxCol, onTimeCol, noData, checklist, goodsFile, sent, allQueue) — NOT grepped exhaustively across src; treat as unverified.
- The e2e finish test (m9zo, 120-140) types 480 and expects success — it depends on `canSeal` being false, i.e. empty dictionaries on the CI database; on a database whose dictionaries price a 2-line podklyuch it would be refused `seal_instead` (CLAUDE.md #880 records the dependency).
- CLAUDE.md sub-round B «STATED, not built: the model's REASON for a pick is logged and shown nowhere» — still true; the prefill job logs `{codesStamped, picked, aiUsed}` only (jobs.ts:125-128).
- `CalcPrefillJob.rev` is optional; a job queued before the field existed falls back to the confirmed/closed checks alone (jobs.ts:14-17) — a deliberately weaker guard for old rows.
- `MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER` counts by `requested_by`; the bot path stamps the collecting STAFF member as requester (intake-land.ts ctx {actorId: collectedBy}), so one busy seller's bot submissions and card submissions share one cap of 20 — a refusal reads `too_many_open` («Ochiq so‘rovlar juda ko‘p»).

### Docs-vs-code disagreements

- docs/VED.md:171-172 («The ANSWER returns to the seller's bot chat: price, breakdown, validity — plus a ready-to-forward client offer text») vs the code: `finishCalcRequest` sends `CalcDone` = amount + note + card link only (service.ts:582-590) and `announceSeal` sends `CalcSealed` = «🧮 Hisob tayyor: $total + link» (workspace.ts:1677-1682) — no breakdown, no validity, no offer text; the offer text is a separate SELLER door (`recordOffer`/`CalcOffer`, actions.ts:579-644).
- CLAUDE.md («`recalcFromSealed` refuses `not_sealed` / `recalc_open` / `recalc_superseded`») omits two guards the code has first: `not_found` (workspace.ts:1808) and `not_closed` (1809).
- DECISIONS #748 («The clock's three ends are 'lines', 'task', 'returned'») vs the schema CHECK `calc_requests_via_check` which has FOUR values incl. 'sealed' (wms.ts ~1973-1976; migration 0086:288-291) — phase B added the fourth; the decision is historically accurate, CLAUDE.md's phase-A paragraph does not restate the fourth end.
- docs/VED.md «What already exists» table (row «Calc request + SLA clock … door closed — REOPEN») and «VED queue: … reopen the round-28 machinery» describe the pre-phase-A state; the doors are reopened (service.ts:23-30 comment says so). Spec text, not updated.
- service.ts:1126 `lastCalcAnswerFor` reads `completed_via = 'task'`; CLAUDE.md phase A says «the finish records the ANSWER (amount + note, pushed to the seller)» — true of `finishCalcRequest`, but the same `'task'` ending is also written by `completeCalcForTask` with no answer (service.ts:615-621), which the doc does not distinguish.
- hisoblash/page.tsx:19-24 header comment («A lead's NAME is not printed and its card is not linked») vs lines 131-135 which link the card when `actor.permissions.has('crm.leads')`.
- CLAUDE.md phase-A paragraph says assignment is «fewest-open-first over `ved.docs`»; the code additionally requires `users.active = true` and breaks ties by `max(requested_at) asc nulls first` (service.ts:113-117) — a refinement the doc does not mention.
- mutes.ts:18-21 comment («three everyday messages») vs the four calc entries in the `tasks` group (lines 29-33).


## pricing-law-engine

### Summary
The pricing + law engine is `src/modules/wms/calc/pricing.ts` — a zero-import, browser-safe pure module whose governing rule (its own header) is that it "never returns a number it had to invent": every entry point answers `{ok:true,…}` or `{ok:false, reason}`. It prices customs per GROUP over per-ITEM bazas (`customsFor`), in PP-3818's four duty shapes (advalor/specific/max/plus) with the 28.02.2026 additional duty by band when no certificate stands (`addDutyBand`, inclusive-high per guide example 9.2), excise in the VAT base, a per-DECLARATION BHM fee (`customsFeeFor`, VMQ-55 step scale converted through the day's UZS rate, refusing `fee_fx_missing`), the freight band by WHOLE kg/m³ (`bandFor`, refusing `band_missing`/`band_ambiguous`), and totals by section (`totalsFor`). The dictionaries (`dictionaries.ts`) are date-versioned like `fx_rates` but with NO earliest-row fallback; PP-3818's 1,489 rates enter through `rates-seed.ts` → `scripts/seed.ts` `seedPp3818Rates()` (ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING on (code, '2026-01-01'), vat_pct 12 on every row) and the owner's contiguous 12-band tariff through `tariff-seed.ts` → `seedFreightTariff()` (only into an EMPTY table). `workspace.ts` (3,820 lines) drives the engine: `loadWorkspace` recomputes a DRAFT from the dictionaries on every render (pool reads only, #714), `saveTable` is the ONE table write door (validate → pre-tx dictionary/import reads → FOR UPDATE lock → edits by immutable id → ghost inserts → regroup+SWEEP by typed code → prune → merge identical-rate duplicates → measure pass → import auto-fill → unconfirm touched → recount → ONE audit row), every mutator runs through `mutateRequest`/`lockRequestInTx` and bumps the integer `calc_requests.rev` clock, both confirm doors record `confirmed_warnings`/`confirm_via` and CAS the rev, and `sealCalc` is "the one door between" draft and FACT: it CASes the rev under FOR UPDATE, closes the request via `UPDATE … RETURNING current_version_no+1`, inserts an immutable `calc_versions` row with the whole `breakdown` snapshot plus phase-E1's three counters, adopts the superseded request's receipts, and writes the price LOCKED onto the lead/deal card. Law 1 (the model proposes words, never a number) is structural: `rate_source` CHECK admits only 'dictionary'|'typed', `baza_source` only 'dictionary'|'typed'|'import', `applyProposal` writes only `ai_*` columns, and `tests/unit/ai-advisory.test.ts` pins all three fences as source shape. `proposeGroups` claims the request (`ai_proposal_started_at`, 10-min stale window shared with the lock), lands the proposal, RELEASES the claim, then `priceProposedGroups` pulls the book's rates per coded group and stamps item codes through `saveTable`. Screens: `/hisoblash/[id]` (gate `ved.docs`), whose `items-table.tsx` re-runs `customsFor`/`requestCustomsFor`/`totalsFor` in the browser per keystroke so the bar and the seal cannot disagree; every server action in `hisoblash/actions.ts` gates on `ved.docs` and maps `CalcError.code` to `calc.errors.*`. Settings read: `bhm_uzs`, `quote_valid_days`, `import_baza_min_sim`. Tests: 388-line `calc-pricing.test.ts`, 325-line `calc-law.test.ts` (guide §9.1–9.3 verbatim), `calc-warnings`, `calc-basis`, `calc-grouping`, `calc-clock` (derived mutator fence), `proposal-wire`, `pricing-pure`; integration `calc-seal` (888 lines) and `calc-table` (822 lines) on a real database with run-tagged fixtures deleted in `afterAll`.


### Files and exports


#### `src/modules/wms/calc/pricing.ts` — The pure arithmetic engine (docs/VED.md phase B). Zero imports, runs in the browser too (items-table.tsx). Header: «it never returns a number it had to invent … Every entry point answers {ok:true,…} or {ok:false, reason}, and the caller has to look.»

- **`CalcSectionName`** `type CalcSectionName = 'yolkira' | 'rastamojka' | 'podklyuch'` — The three request types (law 9). L15.
- **`BazaBasis`** `type BazaBasis = 'unit' | 'kg' | 'juft' | 'litr' | 'm2'` — What a baza is PER. 'unit' KEEPS meaning dona (stored spelling for ever). sm3 deliberately absent: «nothing is VALUED per cm³ of displacement — a vehicle's baza is its invoice price per dona (0092)». L22.
- **`BazaSource`** `type BazaSource = 'dictionary' | 'typed' | 'import' | null` — «There is deliberately no 'ai': the model proposes words and can never reach a number (law 3, phase B)». L30.
- **`sectionParts`** `sectionParts(section: CalcSectionName): { customs: boolean; freight: boolean; extras: boolean }` — customs = section !== 'yolkira'; freight = section !== 'rastamojka'; extras always true. Consumed by the seal, loadWorkspace and calc-workspace.tsx. L33-43.
- **`PricedItem`** `interface PricedItem { seq; label; quantity: number|null; weightKg: number|null; bazaUsd: number|null; bazaBasis: BazaBasis|null; measureUnit: MeasureUnit|null; measureQty: number|null }` — The ITEM is the priced unit (owner's law 5: one TNVED code holds several products with different bazas). measureUnit/measureQty are required-nullable «every constructor must answer (#790)». L58-73.
- **`MeasureUnit`** `type MeasureUnit = 'juft' | 'litr' | 'm2' | 'sm3'` — «The four units that need their own column — the rest live on kg/quantity.» L76.
- **`itemMeasure`** `itemMeasure(item: PricedItem, unit: BazaBasis | DutyUnit): number | null` — ONE resolver for «how much of this item, in that unit»: kg→weightKg; unit/dona/1000_dona→quantity; else measureQty only when item.measureUnit === unit. Both the value loop and the specific-duty half call it. L84-88.
- **`DutyMode`** `type DutyMode = 'advalor' | 'specific' | 'max' | 'plus'` — PP-3818's four shapes: advalor BQ×%; specific Miqdor×T; max = greater of the two (198 rows); plus = their sum (41 vehicle rows). «A max read as its percentage alone silently loses the floor — hence a mode and not a flag.» L97.
- **`DutyUnit`** `type DutyUnit = 'kg' | 'dona' | 'litr' | 'juft' | '1000_dona' | 'sm3' | 'm2'` — The law's seven units; since phase 3 the engine prices ALL seven, refusing `measure_missing` naming the item when it cannot answer in the law's unit. L106.
- **`PricedGroup`** `interface PricedGroup { seq; label; tnvedCode: string|null; dutyPct: number|null; vatPct: number|null; feeUsd: number|null; dutyMode: DutyMode; dutySpecific: number|null; dutyUnit: DutyUnit|null; excisePct: number|null; hasCertificate: boolean; dutyFree: boolean; vatFree: boolean }` — dutyMode/dutySpecific/dutyUnit all REQUIRED («null duty_mode in the db reads as 'advalor' — the mapping layer resolves it»). hasCertificate is the RESOLVED answer (group's own else the request's). dutyFree/vatFree = the per-CALC lgota (law 7). L108-130.
- **`CustomsRefusal`** `type CustomsRefusal = 'group_empty' | 'baza_missing' | 'measure_missing' | 'rates_missing' | 'not_a_number'` — Complete union of customsFor's refusal reasons. L132-137.
- **`isNumber`** `const isNumber = (n: number|null|undefined): n is number => typeof n === 'number' && Number.isFinite(n)` — The NaN fence: «Number('1 000') … NaN passes every comparison guard … Postgres then stores 'NaN'::numeric happily — and 'NaN'::numeric >= 0 is TRUE there». Used by dictionaries.ts/workspace.ts `mustBeNumber` → CalcError('bad_number'). L152-153.
- **`CustomsBreakdown / CustomsResult`** `{ ok:true; valueUsd; dutyUsd; addDutyUsd; addDutyPct; exciseUsd; vatUsd; feeUsd; customsUsd } | { ok:false; reason: CustomsRefusal; itemSeq?: number; itemLabel?: string }` — addDutyPct = BK 300-1 band (0 when a certificate stands). L157-175.
- **`customsFor`** `customsFor(group: PricedGroup, items: PricedItem[]): CustomsResult` — Order of refusals: items.length===0 → group_empty; dutyPct/vatPct null (after lgota zeroing) → rates_missing; non-finite dutyPct/vatPct/feeUsd/excisePct → not_a_number; non-advalor mode (and not dutyFree) missing dutySpecific/dutyUnit → rates_missing (non-finite → not_a_number); per item: bazaUsd/bazaBasis null → baza_missing{itemSeq,itemLabel}; itemMeasure(item, bazaBasis) null or ≤0 → measure_missing; non-finite → not_a_number. Arithmetic (rounded so «every figure the screen prints must be reproducible from the figures printed beside it»): valueUsd = round2(Σ bazaUsd×measure); advalorUsd = round2(value×duty%/100); specific half sums itemMeasure(item, dutyUnit) (÷1000 for '1000_dona'), refusing measure_missing per item; dutyUsd = specific | max(advalor,specific) | round2(advalor+specific); addDutyPct = (!hasCertificate && !dutyFree) ? addDutyBand(dutyPct) : 0; exciseUsd = round2(value×(excisePct??0)/100) (SK 285 base); vatUsd = round2((value+duty+addDuty+excise)×vat%/100) (SK 254); feeUsd = round2(group.feeUsd??0) («per-group column survives for rows a person typed one into» — the declaration fee lives in customsFeeFor); customsUsd = round2(duty+addDuty+excise+vat+fee). L186-286.
- **`addDutyBand`** `addDutyBand(advalorPct: number): 5 | 10 | 15 | 20` — <10→5, <20→10, <30→15, else 20. «inclusive-HIGH — exactly 20 % lands in the «20 dan 30 gacha» band — because the guide's own worked example 9.2 prices a 20 % idish-tovoq at +15 %». L297-302.
- **`FreightBand`** `interface FreightBand { zone: string; minDensity: number; maxDensity: number|null; priceUsd: number; perKg: boolean }` — maxDensity inclusive; null = open-ended top row (per kg). L308-315.
- **`BandRefusal / BandResult`** `type BandRefusal = 'band_missing' | 'band_ambiguous'; BandResult = {ok:true; band} | {ok:false; reason: BandRefusal}` — L317-319.
- **`bandDensityOf`** `const bandDensityOf = (density: number) => Math.round(density)` — «The whole kg/m³ a band is looked up by» — 100.4 must find «1–100», not fall in a one-wide hole; also what every screen prints. L337.
- **`bandFor`** `bandFor(rows: FreightBand[], zone: string, density: number): BandResult` — Filters rows by zone and d ≥ min && (max null || d ≤ max) on the ROUNDED density; 0 hits → band_missing, >1 → band_ambiguous. «Both refusals are his table's, not ours» — a property of a tariff a person edits on /admin/tarif. L343-352.
- **`FreightRefusal`** `type FreightRefusal = 'zone_required' | 'measure_missing' | 'not_a_number' | BandRefusal` — Complete union of freightFor's reasons. L354.
- **`SMALL_FREIGHT_USD`** `const SMALL_FREIGHT_USD = 50` — Law 8's «no minimum charge — very small cargo gets only a warning»; MONEY not volume. L365.
- **`freightFor`** `freightFor(rows: FreightBand[], input: { zone: string|null; weightKg: number|null; volumeM3: number|null; overrideDensity?: number|null }): FreightResult` — !zone → zone_required; kg/m3 null or ≤0 → measure_missing; non-finite (incl. override) → not_a_number; density = kg/m3 (raw, kept for the record); bandDensity = bandDensityOf(override ?? density); band via bandFor; listUsd = round2(perKg ? price×kg : price×m3); small = listUsd < 50. FreightBreakdown = {ok:true; density; bandDensity; band; listUsd; small}. «overrideDensity is the VED's judgement … not a discount, and the two are kept apart all the way into the seal». L390-423.
- **`FEE_TIERS`** `const FEE_TIERS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [maxValueUsd, bhm]> = [[10_000,1],[20_000,1.5],[40_000,2.5],[60_000,4],[100_000,7],[200_000,10],[500_000,15],[1_000_000,20]]` — VMQ-55 2026 step scale «value up to and including this → this many BHM»; ≥1,000,000 → 25. L437-446.
- **`FeeRefusal / FeeResult`** `type FeeRefusal = 'fee_fx_missing' | 'not_a_number'; FeeBreakdown = {ok:true; feeUsd; bhmCoefficient; overridden}` — L448-458.
- **`customsFeeFor`** `customsFeeFor(input: { valueUsd: number; bhmUzs: number; fxUzsPerUsd: number|null; overrideUsd: number|null }): FeeResult` — overrideUsd !== null → (non-finite or <0 → not_a_number) else {feeUsd: round2(override), bhmCoefficient: 0, overridden: true}; valueUsd/bhmUzs non-finite or bhm ≤0 → not_a_number; fx null or ≤0 → fee_fx_missing; tier = value ≥ 1_000_000 ? 25 : first FEE_TIERS row with value ≤ max; feeUsd = round2(bhm×bhmUzs/fx). «exactly a million pays 25 … this off-by-one shipped and its boundary test caught it». The fee prices a DECLARATION, never per group. L474-506.
- **`pricedGroupOf`** `pricedGroupOf(g: {…PricedGroup fields but `effectiveCertificate: boolean` instead of hasCertificate}): PricedGroup` — The SHARED mapper server and browser both use; the parameter name `effectiveCertificate` «cannot be handed the raw field by accident — picking the wrong one silently drops the 5-20 % add-duty». L517-546.
- **`RequestCustoms / requestCustomsFor`** `requestCustomsFor(input: { customs: CustomsResult[]; bhmUzs: number|null; fxUzsPerUsd: number|null; feeOverrideUsd: number|null }): { valueUsd: number|null; fee: FeeResult|null; customsUsd: number|null }` — «no partial sums, ever»: any group !ok → all three null; fee only when customs.length>0 (bhmUzs null passed as NaN → fee not_a_number); fee !ok → customsUsd null; else Σ group.customsUsd + fee. ONE definition consumed by loadWorkspace AND items-table.tsx. L548-595.
- **`TotalsRefusal / Totals / totalsFor`** `totalsFor(input: { section; customsUsd; freightUsd; extrasUsd; discountUsd; weightKg: number|null; volumeM3: number|null }): {ok:true; customsUsd; freightUsd; extrasUsd; discountUsd; totalUsd; perM3Usd: number|null; perKgUsd: number|null} | {ok:false; reason: 'discount_exceeds_total' | 'not_a_number'}` — Any of the four money inputs non-finite → not_a_number; parts by sectionParts zero the parts the section lacks; gross = round2(c+f+e); discount > gross → discount_exceeds_total; totalUsd = round2(gross−discount); perM3 = round2(total/m3) when m3>0 else null; perKg = round4(total/kg) when kg>0 else null. L601-659.
- **`densityOf`** `densityOf(weightKg: number|null, volumeM3: number|null): number|null` — Unrounded kg/m3; null without a positive volume. L662-665.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/dictionaries.ts` — The four dictionaries: product baza (calc_bazas), code rates (calc_rates, longest-prefix lookup), freight tariff (calc_freight_tariffs), selling price book (calc_price_book). BazaRow = {id; productKey; label; tnvedCode; bazaUsd; basis; effectiveDate; note}. L85-101.

L144-200.

- **`ratesForCodes / ratesFor`** `ratesForCodes(codes: string[], date: string): Promise<Map<code, RatesRow>>; ratesFor(code, date)` — One query over all prefixes ≤ date, newest per stored code; per typed code the longest stored prefix wins («6403120000's own 5 % beats heading 6403's 20 %, min $3/juft»). RatesRow = {id; tnvedCode (the STORED prefix that answered); dutyPct; vatPct; feeUsd; dutyMode; dutySpecific; dutyUnit; effectiveDate; source: 'manual'|'correction'|'pp3818'; note}. L224-251.
- **`listRates`** `listRates(search?): Promise<(RatesRow & {future})[]>` — Unfiltered shows only source <> 'pp3818' (DOM-crush lesson); a typed code searches both ways (`code LIKE q% OR q LIKE code||'%'`), limit 300. L261-275.
- **`saveRates`** `saveRates(input: { tnvedCode; dutyPct; vatPct; feeUsd; effectiveDate; source?: 'manual'|'correction'; note?; dutyMode?; dutySpecific?; dutyUnit? }, ctx): Promise<id>` — Refuses code_required, bad_number, rate_range (pct outside 0..100, fee<0, non-advalor without specific ≥0 + unit). dutyMode absent = CARRY the in-force EXACT-code row's shape forward («a person correcting the percentage of a MAX code must not silently strip its per-piece floor»); explicit 'advalor' removes it. - **`tariffZones / listTariff / saveTariffBand`** `tariffZones(date): string[]; listTariff(); saveTariffBand(input: { zone; minDensity; maxDensity; priceUsd; perKg; effectiveDate }, ctx)` — saveTariffBand refuses zone_required, bad_number, price_positive, band_range (min<0 or max<min); zone lowercased; upsert on (zone, min_density, effective_date); audit 'calc_tariff'. Gate is admin.dictionaries.manage (actions.ts L534), NOT ved.docs — «the person giving the discount cannot move the list price it is measured against». L454-531.

Refuses code_required, bad_number, price_positive, label_required; audit 'calc_price'. L588-672.

After: audit, announceSeal → notifyStaffTelegram CalcSealed to requestedBy; CalcDiscounted to discountAudience (super_admin+admin roles ∪ accountant ∪ requestedBy ∪ superseded request's requestedBy) when discount>0 or band override. L1382-1730.
- **`canSeal`** `canSeal(workspace: { blockers: unknown[]; totals: {ok}|null }): boolean` — blockers.length===0 && totals.ok. ONE predicate for the seal (not_ready), the seal button, and finishCalcRequest's `seal_instead` refusal. L1733-1740.
- **`currentVersion / currentSealFor`** `currentVersion(requestId): SealedVersion|null; currentSealFor(entityType: 'deal'|'lead', entityId): SealedVersion|null` — «The card's price is the VERSION, not answer_*». L1742-1790.
- **`recalcFromSealed`** `recalcFromSealed(requestId, ctx): Promise<newRequestId>` — Refuses not_found, not_closed, not_sealed (completed but no version — a 'returned' ending), recalc_open (an open child exists), recalc_superseded (a sealed child exists). Inserts a NEW request (supersedes_request_id = old, dueAt now+2h, inherits section/route/kg/m3/zone/hasCertificate/feeOverrideUsd), copies groups incl. law shape + ai_proposal (NOT confirmations), items incl. baza triple + importRowId + measure pair, extras. Gate in actions.ts: admin.settings.manage. L1800-1960.
- **`AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS`** `= 10 * 60_000` — ONE constant for the claim that TAKES the request and the lock that REFUSES while somebody holds it (#513). L2050.
- **`proposeGroups`** `proposeGroups(requestId, ctx): Promise<{ groups; batches; failed; ratesPulled; codesStamped; importFilled }>` — assertOpen; CAS claim on ai_proposal_started_at (NULL or older than 10 min) else ai_running; no_items; planBatches → proposeGoodsGrouping per slice (a failed batch costs its own goods; all failed → ai_failed); mergeProposals → applyProposal; THEN releaseAiClaim (must precede the tail — «the whole pricing half was dead in production» when held) → priceProposedGroups; finally releaseAiClaim again (idempotent). L2065-2175.
- **`priceProposedGroups`** `priceProposedGroups(requestId, ctx): Promise<{ ratesPulled; codesStamped; importFilled }>` — For each coded group with null duty/vat: pullRatesFromDictionary, swallowing ONLY rates_not_in_dictionary/code_required; then stamps each item's tnved_code from its group's code through saveTable (never a bare UPDATE). Rates BEFORE codes because duty_unit decides unitsForRow. L2200-2275.
- **`TableItemEdit / TableNewItem / TableSaveResult`** `TableItemEdit = { id; seq; name?; quantity?; weightKg?; volumeM3?; tnvedCode?; note?; measureQty?; bazaUsd?; bazaBasis?; importRowId?: string|null }; TableNewItem = { name; quantity?; unit?; weightKg?; volumeM3?; tnvedCode?; note?; measureQty?; bazaUsd?; bazaBasis? }; TableSaveResult = { minted: string[]; swept; added; merged: string[]; measuresCleared: number[]; measuresDropped: number[]; basisSuspect: number[]; importFilled: number[] }` — The measure UNIT is never posted — the server stamps it from the group's dutyUnit. importRowId is a CLAIM re-read from the file. L2290-2345.
- **`saveTable`** `saveTable(requestId, input: { items: TableItemEdit[]; adds: TableNewItem[] }, ctx): Promise<TableSaveResult>` — Row-naming refusals via CalcError(code, seq) (ghost rows named by NEGATIVE seq): bad_code (not /^\d{4,10}$/), bad_number, measure_positive, name_required, baza_positive, bad_basis, import_row_missing, not_found, too_many_items (> MAX_CALC_ITEMS 1000), ai_running, already_closed. PRE-TX (pool): standing items; tnvedFor() memory for adds; ONE ratesForCodes over edited ∪ sweep ∪ add codes; importRowForCode per picked id (price+basis REPLACE the browser's); suggestImportFills (newestReadyBatchId, getSetting('import_baza_min_sim'), unitsForRow, suggestImportBaza per distinct code|units|name|perPiece memo). TX: lockRequestInTx → (1) edits by id — measures/bazas mark the group touched, name/note do not; baza triple + importRowId written together, bazaSource = importRowId ? 'import' : 'typed' → (2) insert ghosts with bazaSource 'typed' → (3) regroup: typed codes + THE SWEEP (every coded-but-ungrouped standing item), autoGroupInTx find-or-create by code with the book's rates at mint (rateSource 'dictionary' or NULL rates + NULL source) → (4) pruneEmptyGroupsInTx → (5) merge same-code duplicates only when sameGroupRates() → (6) measure pass: required unit = group.dutyUnit ∈ juft/litr/m2/sm3; posted qty dropped+named when none required; standing pair cleared+named on a recode; written as {unit, qty} together → (7) import auto-fill where still coded, still empty, and unitsForRow of the FINAL group allows the basis → basisSuspect (basis 'unit' under a m2/juft/litr law) → unconfirmInTx(touched) (4 columns) → recountItemsInTx (count(*), bumps rev) → ONE writeAudit(tx). L2680-3200.
- **`deleteItem`** `deleteItem(requestId, itemId, ctx)` — Own tx + lock; unconfirms and prunes the emptied group; recount. L3248-3275.
- **`recordOffer / releaseOffer / offersFor / releasedOfferWhere / offerStandsSql / releasedPriceFor / lastAnswerAnchorFor`** `recordOffer(anchor: {versionId}|{requestId}, input: { clientPriceUsd; locale: 'uz'|'ru'|'en'; clientName?; belowFloorReason?; mayApprove?; expect? }, ctx): Promise<OfferResult>` — Phase C/D/4 (adjacent subsystem; lives in this file). Refuses price_positive, unauthenticated, not_found, discounted_no_upsale (price above a DISCOUNTED sealed floor), answer_missing, answer_not_usd, superseded, answer_expired, below_floor_reason_required, not_pending. Note `superseded` has NO calc.errors key (see gaps). L3300-3820.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/service.ts` — Phase A queue + the error class the whole module throws. (Queue itself is the intake subsystem; listed for CalcError/MAX_CALC_ITEMS/finishCalcRequest.)

- **`CalcError`** `class CalcError extends Error { constructor(public readonly code: string, public readonly seq?: number) }` — `seq` names the ROW a table refusal is about. Every action maps `err.code` → `calc.errors.<code>` (actions.ts L99/205/277). L46-54.
- **`MAX_CALC_ITEMS / MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER`** `1000 / 20` — Mirrors calc_requests_items_check BETWEEN 0 AND 1000. L57-68.
- **`openCalcRequest`** `openCalcRequest(input: CalcRequestInput, ctx): Promise<{ id; assigneeId }>` — Prefills item tnved_code from the TNVED memory (tnvedFor/productKey) BEFORE any model; refuses unauthenticated, too_many_items, not_found, too_many_open, note_taken, note_foreign. L173-330.
- **`finishCalcRequest`** `finishCalcRequest(id, answer: { amount?; currency?; note? }, ctx)` — Dynamic-imports workspace: if canSeal(loadWorkspace(id)) → CalcError('seal_instead') (round 112). L~560.
- **`rekeyLeadCalcRequests`** `rekeyLeadCalcRequests(leadId, dealId): Promise<number>` — Moves EVERY request (sealed included) and calc_offers to the deal on a won lead; fenced by tests/unit/calc-rekey.test.ts (derived from schema). L~690.

- **`currentVersionSql`** `currentVersionSql(): SQL` — v.version_no = max version_no of its request. L17-22.
- **`notSupersededSql`** `notSupersededSql(): SQL` — NOT EXISTS r2 with supersedes_request_id = r.id. L25-29.
- **`answerFloorStandsSql`** `answerFloorStandsSql(): SQL` — completed, answer_amount>0, currency 'USD', not superseded, no newer USD answer on the card, no version sealed after it. No expiry clause (the DOOR gates expiry). L50-71.
- **`measurableRequestSql`** `measurableRequestSql(): SQL` — Not superseded by a SEALED child — deliberately NOT payable's clause. L86-92.

Imports customsFor/pricedGroupOf/requestCustomsFor/totalsFor from pricing.ts and defaultBasisFor/uniformBazaOf from basis.ts; recomputes liveCustomsByGroup / liveBazaByGroup / liveTotals per keystroke over drafts keyed by item ID. Dirty law: «while anything is dirty, every OTHER mutating control (✅, confirm-all, propose, pull-bazas, certificate — and the seal, gated in SealPanel) is off, replaced by «Avval saqlang»». Client refuses NaN/bad_code before the wire; server stays the authority.

L57-176.
