#### `src/modules/platform/db/migrations/` — Hand-written SQL migrations + meta/_journal.json (96 entries, idx 0..95). 0085_calc_queue (when 1785190000055), 0086_calc_pricing (…056), 0087_calc_price_book (…057), 0088_calc_upsale (…058), 0089_calc_actuals (…059), 0090_notice_staff (…060), 0091_calc_law (…070), 0092_calc_measure (…071), 0093_calc_offer_answer (…072), 0094_customs_import (…073), 0095_customs_import_heartbeat (…074). drizzle.config.ts: out './src/modules/platform/db/migrations', casing 'snake_case'.

- **`0086_calc_pricing.sql`** `291 lines` — CREATE calc_bazas, calc_rates, calc_freight_tariffs, calc_groups, calc_extras, calc_versions; ALTER calc_request_items (group_id, baza_usd, baza_basis, baza_source + 3 CHECKs); ALTER calc_requests (current_version_no, supersedes_request_id, freight_zone, ai_proposal_started_at, via CHECK + 'sealed').
- **`0091_calc_law.sql`** `112 lines` — calc_rates duty_mode/duty_specific/duty_unit + 4 CHECKs + source 'pp3818'; calc_requests has_certificate default true + fee_override_usd; calc_groups duty_mode/duty_specific/duty_unit/excise_pct/has_certificate + 5 CHECKs (mode pair via coalesce).
- **`0092_calc_measure.sql`** `60 lines` — calc_request_items measure_unit/measure_qty + unit/qty/pair CHECKs; baza_basis and calc_bazas.basis widened to unit/kg/juft/litr/m2; calc_requests.rev integer default 0.
- **`0089_calc_actuals.sql`** `141 lines` — receipts.calc_request_id/calc_link_source/calc_link_confirmed_at/_by + source CHECK + partial index; calc_requests_entity_idx; calc_groups ai_proposal/confirmed_warnings/confirm_via + confirm_via/confirm_pair CHECKs; calc_versions warned_groups/ai_blind_groups/ai_rate_taken_groups + e_counts CHECK.

### Flows


**Render the workspace (a DRAFT price)**

1. GET /hisoblash/[id] (page.tsx) — getActor, redirect without ved.docs
2. workspace.ts loadWorkspace(requestId, {overrideDensity?, discountUsd?}) — first query reads calc_requests (incl. rev)
3. Promise.all: calc_groups, calc_request_items, calc_extras, cost_types(active), currentVersion(calc_versions newest)
4. Promise.all: bazasFor(item names) [calc_bazas ≤ today], ratesForCodes(group codes) [calc_rates longest prefix ≤ today], tariffFor/tariffZones [calc_freight_tariffs], namesOf(confirmers), lgotaLastByCode [calc_groups⋈calc_versions], getSetting('bhm_uzs'), uzsPerUsd [fx_rates UZS]
5. Per group: pricedGroupOf(effectiveCertificate = g.has_certificate ?? request.has_certificate) → customsFor(group, items) ; warningsForGroup(facts) ; groupQuantity/groupMeasure
6. freightFor(tariff, {zone, kg, m3, overrideDensity}) only when parts.freight
7. requestCustomsFor({customs[], bhmUzs, fxUzsPerUsd, feeOverrideUsd}) only when parts.customs — fee = customsFeeFor per DECLARATION
8. totalsFor when section && customsUsd !== null && (!freight part || freight.ok)
9. blockersFor → SealBlocker[]; reconcile mismatch only when every item grouped+measured
10. calc-workspace.tsx / items-table.tsx render; items-table re-runs customsFor/requestCustomsFor/totalsFor in the BROWSER over drafts (bhmUzs/fxUzsPerUsd shipped in Workspace)

**Save the table (saveTable — the ONE write door)**

1. items-table.tsx save(): builds TableItemEdit[] (by item id) + TableNewItem[]; client refuses bad_code/NaN first
2. actions.ts saveTableAction → runTable (authorize ved.docs) → workspace.saveTable
3. Validate every cell BEFORE any write: tableCode (bad_code), tableMeasure (bad_number/measure_positive/≥1e9), checkBazaPair (baza_positive/bad_basis/bad_number), name_required; ghost rows named by NEGATIVE seq
4. POOL reads: standing items; tnvedFor(adds) fills codes from tnved_assignments; ONE ratesForCodes over edited ∪ sweep ∪ add codes; importRowForCode per posted importRowId (import_row_missing) — file's price+basis REPLACE the browser's; suggestImportFills (newestReadyBatchId, getSetting('import_baza_min_sim'), unitsForRow, suggestImportBaza)
5. db.transaction: lockRequestInTx (FOR UPDATE; already_closed; ai_running within AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS); too_many_items > 1000
6. 1. edits by id → calc_request_items (baza triple + import_row_id together; source 'import'|'typed'); measures/bazas mark group touched
7. 2. insert ghosts (bazaSource 'typed')
8. 3. regroup: moves = typed codes + SWEEP of coded-ungrouped items → autoGroupInTx find-or-create calc_groups by code with ratesForCodes hit (rate_source 'dictionary', fee_usd NULL, duty_mode NULL when advalor) or NULL rates + NULL source
9. 4. pruneEmptyGroupsInTx
10. 5. merge same-code duplicate groups only when sameGroupRates()
11. 6. measure pass: required unit = group.duty_unit ∈ EXT_UNITS; write/clear measure_unit+measure_qty TOGETHER (measuresCleared/measuresDropped named)
12. 7. import auto-fill where still coded+empty and unitsForRow(final group) allows the basis → baza_source 'import', import_row_id
13. basisSuspect (basis 'unit' under m2/juft/litr law); unconfirmInTx(touched) (4 columns); recountItemsInTx (count(*), rev+1); ONE writeAudit(tx, entityType 'calc_request')
14. TableFormState {ok, minted, swept, added, merged, measuresCleared, measuresDropped, basisSuspect, importFilled} → bar sentences (calc.table.minted/swept/importFilled/…)

**Confirm a group (✅ / Hammasini tasdiqlash)**

1. items-table.tsx (calc-confirm-group / calc-confirm-all, disabled while dirty>0) → confirmGroupAction/confirmAllAction (ved.docs)
2. workspace.warningsNow(requestId) = loadWorkspace on the POOL → {byGroup: warningsForGroup lists, rev}
3. mutateRequest: lockRequestInTx FOR UPDATE → locked.rev !== warnings.rev → CalcError('conflict')
4. UPDATE calc_groups SET confirmed_by, confirmed_at, confirm_via 'single'|'bulk', confirmed_warnings=jsonb WHERE id AND confirmed_at IS NULL (re-press = no-op)
5. writeAudit(tx, 'calc_group'|'calc_request'); bumpRevInTx

**Seal (Muhrlash) — draft becomes FACT**

1. calc-workspace.tsx SealPanel (calc-do-seal enabled only when blockers=0 && dirty=0) → sealAction(id, {discountUsd, discountReason, bandOverrideMin, bandOverrideReason}) (ved.docs)
2. sealCalc: reason rules (discount_reason_required / band_reason_required); loadWorkspace(id, {overrideDensity: bandOverrideMin, discountUsd}) on the pool; canSeal else not_ready; freight_zone_required / freight_missing / section_required
3. getSetting('quote_valid_days') (before the tx) → validUntil; read supersedes_request_id; build breakdown snapshot; sealCounters + unchangedFromProposal over calc_groups.ai_proposal
4. db.transaction: lockRequestInTx → locked.rev !== workspace.rev → conflict
5. UPDATE calc_requests SET completed_at, completed_by, completed_via 'sealed', current_version_no+1, rev+1 WHERE completed_at IS NULL RETURNING version_no (none → already_closed)
6. INSERT calc_versions (section, kg, m3, density, customs/freight/extras/total/perM3/perKg, freight_zone/band_min/rate/per_kg/list_usd, band_override_*, discount_*, ai_groups_sealed, low_confidence_sealed, warned_groups, ai_blind_groups, ai_rate_taken_groups, breakdown)
7. if superseded: UPDATE receipts SET calc_request_id = new WHERE calc_request_id = old (E1 cargo adoption)
8. UPDATE leads (quoted_amount/currency 'USD'/volume/weight) or deals (+quoted_at/quoted_by) — the card LOCK (law 2; updateLead/updateDeal refuse a changed quote via quoteLockedFor)
9. writeAudit(db); announceSeal → notifyStaffTelegram 'CalcSealed' to requested_by; 'CalcDiscounted' to discountAudience when discount>0 or band override

**Correction (Qayta hisoblash)**

1. calc-workspace.tsx calc-recalc → recalcAction (authorize admin.settings.manage)
2. recalcFromSealed: not_closed / not_sealed / recalc_open / recalc_superseded
3. INSERT calc_requests (supersedes_request_id = old, due_at +2h, inherits section/route/kg/m3/freight_zone/has_certificate/fee_override_usd)
4. copy calc_groups (law shape, ai_proposal, NOT confirmations) → copy calc_request_items (baza triple, import_row_id, measure pair) → copy calc_extras
5. writeAudit create; redirect to /hisoblash/<newId>; chain.ts chainOf derives V1/V2 by seal time

**AI proposal → priced calculation (✨)**

1. items-table.tsx calc-propose → proposeAction (ved.docs) → proposeGroups
2. assertOpen; CAS UPDATE calc_requests SET ai_proposal_started_at WHERE NULL or older than AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS → else ai_running
3. items; planBatches(200) → tnved/service.proposeGoodsGrouping (ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, claude-opus-5, 60 s timeout) per slice; failed batches skipped; all failed → ai_failed
4. grouping.mergeProposals → applyProposal (own tx, ignoreAiClaim; groups_confirmed refusal; deletes+recreates calc_groups writing only ai_* columns; bumpRev)
5. releaseAiClaim (BEFORE the tail — both tail doors take lockRequestInTx)
6. priceProposedGroups: pullRatesFromDictionary per coded rate-less group (swallow rates_not_in_dictionary/code_required only) → saveTable(items: code stamps) (sweep/measure/import fill ride along)
7. finally releaseAiClaim (idempotent)

**Dictionary pulls**

1. calc-pull-rates → pullRatesAction(id, groupId) → pullRatesFromDictionary: ratesForCodes([code], today) SERVER-side → setGroupRates(source 'dictionary', feeUsd null, book's dutyMode/specific/unit) → four-column unconfirm inline
2. calc-pull-bazas → pullBazasAction → pullBazasFromDictionary: bazasFor(item names) → fill only NULL bazas whose basis resolves (unit/kg, or group.duty_unit === basis) → bazaSource 'dictionary', importRowId null → unconfirmInTx

**Seeding the law and the tariff**

1. pnpm db:seed → scripts/seed.ts main()
2. seedFreightTariff(): if calc_freight_tariffs has any row → return; else insert tariff-seed.ownerTariffRows() (24 rows) dated OWNER_TARIFF_FROM '2026-01-01'
3. seedPp3818Rates(): rates-seed.pp3818Rows() (1,489 rows from pp3818-rates.json, vat_pct '12.000', fee '0.00', source 'pp3818', dated PP3818_FROM) in batches of 500, onConflictDoNothing (tnved_code, effective_date)
4. Running app reads calc_rates via ratesForCodes (longest prefix ≤ date); person corrections = newer dated rows source 'manual'|'correction'

**Browser live recompute (phase 3's live-equals-saved invariant)**

1. items-table.tsx liveItem(item, requiredUnit, group) merges drafts over stored values (bazaBasis ?? defaultBasisFor(group))
2. liveCustomsByGroup = customsFor(pricedGroupOf(g), liveItems) per group (useMemo on [workspace, drafts])
3. liveBazaByGroup = uniformBazaOf(liveItems) → block footer «База $2/kg»
4. liveTotals = requestCustomsFor({customs, bhmUzs, fxUzsPerUsd, feeOverrideUsd}) → totalsFor(section, …, discountUsd 0) — null (blocked) while any block or the fee refuses
5. TotalsPanel shows calc-total / calc-total-blocked; dims (opacity-60, calc-totals-stale) while dirty>0

### Data model

- **`calc_requests`** (migration 0085_calc_queue (+0086 current_version_no/supersedes_request_id/freight_zone/ai_proposal_started_at; 0091 has_certificate/fee_override_usd; 0092 rev; 0093 answer_amount CHECK)): id uuid PK; entity_type text NOT NULL ('deal'|'lead'); entity_id uuid NOT NULL; requested_by uuid→users NOT NULL; assignee_id uuid→users; item_count int NOT NULL; task_id uuid→tasks; section text; from_city text; to_city text; weight_kg numeric(12,3); volume_m3 numeric(12,3); source text ('card'|'bot'); note_id uuid→crm_activities ON DELETE SET NULL; requested_at timestamptz default now(); due_at timestamptz NOT NULL; taken_at; completed_at; completed_by uuid→users; completed_via text; return_reason text; answer_amount numeric(14,2); answer_currency text; answer_note text; overdue_notified_at; current_version_no int NOT NULL default 0; supersedes_request_id uuid→calc_requests ON DELETE SET NULL; freight_zone text; ai_proposal_started_at timestamptz; has_certificate boolean NOT NULL default true; fee_override_usd numeric(12,2); rev int NOT NULL default 0; updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL default now() — CHECKs: calc_requests_entity_check entity_type IN ('deal','lead'); calc_requests_items_check item_count BETWEEN 0 AND 1000; calc_requests_via_check completed_via IN ('lines','task','returned','sealed'); calc_requests_section_check section IN ('yolkira','rastamojka','podklyuch'); calc_requests_source_check source IN ('card','bot'); calc_requests_fee_override_check (NULL or ≥0 and <> 'NaN'); calc_requests_answer_amount_check (0093, NOT VALID) answer_amount NULL or (>0 and <> NaN). Indexes: calc_requests_note_idx(note_id), calc_requests_assignee_idx(assignee_id, requested_at), calc_requests_entity_idx(entity_type, entity_id) [0089], calc_requests_supersedes_idx [0088]
- **`calc_request_items`** (migration 0085 (+0086 group_id/baza_*; 0092 measure_*; 0094 import_row_id + widened baza_source)): id uuid PK; request_id uuid→calc_requests ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL; seq int NOT NULL; name text NOT NULL; quantity numeric(12,3); unit text; weight_kg numeric(12,3); volume_m3 numeric(12,3); amount numeric(14,2); currency text; tnved_code text; note text; group_id uuid→calc_groups ON DELETE SET NULL; baza_usd numeric(14,4); baza_basis text; baza_source text; measure_unit text; measure_qty numeric(14,4); import_row_id bigint→customs_import_rows ON DELETE SET NULL — CHECKs: UNIQUE calc_request_items_seq_idx (request_id, seq); index calc_request_items_group_idx(group_id); calc_items_baza_basis_check baza_basis IN ('unit','kg','juft','litr','m2') [0092]; calc_items_baza_value_check baza_usd NULL or (>0 and <> NaN) [0086 — absent from the drizzle mirror]; calc_items_baza_source_check baza_source IN ('dictionary','typed','import') [0094 — no 'ai']; calc_items_measure_unit_check measure_unit IN ('juft','litr','m2','sm3'); calc_items_measure_qty_check (>0 and <> NaN); calc_items_measure_pair_check (measure_unit IS NULL) = (measure_qty IS NULL). Deliberately NO CHECK mentions import_row_id (#809: a CHECK spanning an ON DELETE SET NULL column cannot coexist with it)
- **`calc_groups`** (migration 0086 (+0087 calc_groups_code_idx partial; 0089 ai_proposal/confirmed_warnings/confirm_via; 0091 duty_mode/duty_specific/duty_unit/excise_pct/has_certificate)): id uuid PK; request_id uuid→calc_requests CASCADE NOT NULL; seq int NOT NULL; label text NOT NULL; tnved_code text; duty_pct numeric(6,3); vat_pct numeric(6,3); fee_usd numeric(12,2); rate_source text; duty_mode text (NULL reads 'advalor'); duty_specific numeric(12,4); duty_unit text; excise_pct numeric(6,3); has_certificate boolean (NULL inherits request); duty_free boolean NOT NULL default false; vat_free boolean NOT NULL default false; ai_proposed boolean NOT NULL default false; ai_confidence text; ai_duty_pct numeric(6,3) («Recorded, never multiplied»); ai_proposal jsonb {tnvedCode, aiDutyPct, itemSeqs}; confirmed_warnings jsonb (CalcWarningKind[]); confirm_via text; confirmed_by uuid→users; confirmed_at timestamptz; note text; created_at — CHECKs: UNIQUE calc_groups_seq_unique (request_id, seq); calc_groups_rate_source_check rate_source NULL or IN ('dictionary','typed'); calc_groups_rates_check [0086, not in drizzle mirror] duty/vat 0..100 <> NaN, fee ≥0 <> NaN; calc_groups_confidence_check IN ('high','medium','low'); calc_groups_duty_mode_check IN ('advalor','specific','max','plus'); calc_groups_duty_unit_check IN ('kg','dona','litr','juft','1000_dona','sm3','m2'); calc_groups_specific_check ≥0 <> NaN; calc_groups_excise_check 0..100; calc_groups_mode_pair_check ((coalesce(duty_mode,'advalor')='advalor') = (duty_specific IS NULL)) AND (… = (duty_unit IS NULL)); calc_groups_confirm_via_check IN ('single','bulk'); calc_groups_confirm_pair_check confirm_via IS NULL OR confirmed_at IS NOT NULL («TWO writers clear it — unconfirm() and the clear setGroupRates inlines»)
- **`calc_extras`** (migration 0086): id; request_id→calc_requests CASCADE; seq int; cost_type_id uuid→cost_types; label text NOT NULL; amount_usd numeric(14,2) NOT NULL; note; created_at — CHECKs: calc_extras_amount_check amount_usd ≥ 0 (SQL adds <> NaN); UNIQUE calc_extras_seq_unique (request_id, seq)
- **`calc_versions`** (migration 0086 (+0088 calc_versions_request_no_idx; 0089 warned_groups/ai_blind_groups/ai_rate_taken_groups)): id; request_id→calc_requests CASCADE; version_no int; sealed_at timestamptz default now(); sealed_by uuid→users NOT NULL; valid_until timestamptz NOT NULL; section text NOT NULL; weight_kg numeric(12,3); volume_m3 numeric(12,4); density numeric(12,4); customs_usd/freight_usd/extras_usd numeric(14,2) NOT NULL default 0; total_usd numeric(14,2) NOT NULL; per_m3_usd numeric(14,2); per_kg_usd numeric(14,4); freight_zone text; freight_band_min numeric(10,2); freight_rate numeric(10,4); freight_per_kg boolean; freight_list_usd numeric(14,2); band_override_min numeric(10,2); band_override_reason text; discount_usd numeric(14,2) NOT NULL default 0; discount_reason text; ai_groups_sealed int default 0; low_confidence_sealed int default 0; warned_groups int default 0; ai_blind_groups int default 0; ai_rate_taken_groups int default 0; breakdown jsonb NOT NULL default '{}' — CHECKs: calc_versions_section_check IN ('yolkira','rastamojka','podklyuch'); calc_versions_total_check total_usd ≥ 0 (SQL: AND <> NaN); calc_versions_discount_check ≥0 (SQL adds NaN); calc_versions_parts_check [0086 only] customs/freight/extras <> NaN; calc_versions_e_counts_check all three ≥ 0; UNIQUE calc_versions_request_no_unique (request_id, version_no); index calc_versions_sealed_idx(sealed_at). NEVER UPDATEd.
- **`calc_bazas`** (migration 0086 (+0092 widened basis)): id; product_key text NOT NULL (= productKey(name)); label text NOT NULL; tnved_code text; baza_usd numeric(14,4) NOT NULL; basis text NOT NULL; effective_date date NOT NULL; note; entered_by uuid→users; created_at — CHECKs: calc_bazas_basis_check basis IN ('unit','kg','juft','litr','m2') (no sm3); calc_bazas_value_check baza_usd > 0 (SQL: AND <> NaN); UNIQUE calc_bazas_key_date_unique (product_key, effective_date). Read with NO earliest-row fallback.
- **`calc_rates`** (migration 0086 (+0091 duty_mode/duty_specific/duty_unit + source 'pp3818')): id; tnved_code text NOT NULL (a PREFIX at the law's grain: 4/6/8/9/10 digits); duty_pct numeric(6,3) NOT NULL default 0; vat_pct numeric(6,3) NOT NULL default 0; fee_usd numeric(12,2) NOT NULL default 0; duty_mode text NOT NULL default 'advalor'; duty_specific numeric(12,4); duty_unit text; effective_date date NOT NULL; source text NOT NULL default 'manual'; note text; entered_by; created_at — CHECKs: calc_rates_source_check IN ('manual','correction','pp3818'); calc_rates_pct_check duty/vat 0..100, fee ≥0 (SQL 0086 adds <> NaN on all three; drizzle mirror omits NaN); calc_rates_duty_mode_check IN ('advalor','specific','max','plus'); calc_rates_duty_unit_check IN ('kg','dona','litr','juft','1000_dona','sm3','m2'); calc_rates_specific_check ≥0 <> NaN; calc_rates_mode_pair_check ((duty_mode='advalor') = (duty_specific IS NULL)) AND ((duty_mode='advalor') = (duty_unit IS NULL)); UNIQUE calc_rates_code_date_unique (tnved_code, effective_date). Seeded with 1,489 PP-3818 rows dated 2026-01-01.
- **`calc_freight_tariffs`** (migration 0086): id; zone text NOT NULL ('cn'|'kashgar' seeded); min_density numeric(10,2) NOT NULL; max_density numeric(10,2) (NULL = open top, per kg); price_usd numeric(10,4) NOT NULL; per_kg boolean NOT NULL default false; effective_date date NOT NULL; entered_by; created_at — CHECKs: calc_freight_price_check price_usd > 0 (SQL: <> NaN); calc_freight_density_check min ≥ 0 AND (max NULL OR max ≥ min); UNIQUE calc_freight_zone_band_date_unique (zone, min_density, effective_date). Seeded only into an EMPTY table (24 rows).
- **`calc_price_book`** (migration 0087): id; tnved_code text NOT NULL; label text NOT NULL; price_usd numeric(14,4) NOT NULL; unit text NOT NULL default 'm3'; effective_date date NOT NULL; note; entered_by; created_at — CHECKs: calc_price_book_unit_check IN ('m3','kg'); calc_price_book_value_check > 0 AND <> NaN; UNIQUE (tnved_code, effective_date). Stores the CLIENT price, never the sealed floor.
- **`calc_offers`** (migration 0087 (+0088 below_floor_reason/approved_*/payout_*; 0093 request_id anchor, version_id nullable)): id; version_id uuid→calc_versions CASCADE (nullable); request_id uuid→calc_requests CASCADE (nullable); entity_type; entity_id; client_price_usd numeric(14,2) NOT NULL; below_floor boolean NOT NULL default false; below_floor_reason text; approved_at; approved_by→users; locale text NOT NULL; text text NOT NULL; offered_by→users NOT NULL; offered_at default now(); payout_expense_id→expenses; payout_at; payout_by; payout_usd numeric(14,2) — CHECKs: calc_offers_entity_check; calc_offers_locale_check IN ('uz','ru','en'); calc_offers_one_anchor_check (version_id IS NULL) <> (request_id IS NULL); calc_offers_price_check >0 <> NaN; calc_offers_below_floor_reason_check btrim <> ''; calc_offers_below_floor_only_check; calc_offers_approval_pair_check; calc_offers_payout_amount_check; calc_offers_payout_pair_check (all four payout columns together); indexes version_idx, entity_idx(entity_type, entity_id, offered_at), pending_idx, unpaid_idx, payout_expense_idx, request_idx (partial)
- **`receipts (columns added by 0089_calc_actuals — there is NO calc_actuals table; the migration is named for the phase)`** (migration 0089): calc_request_id uuid→calc_requests ON DELETE SET NULL; calc_link_source text; calc_link_confirmed_at timestamptz; calc_link_confirmed_by uuid→users — CHECKs: receipts_calc_link_source_check calc_link_source NULL or IN ('auto','person'); partial index receipts_calc_request_idx(calc_request_id) WHERE NOT NULL. sealCalc re-points these rows from the superseded request to the new seal.
- **`customs_import_batches / customs_import_rows`** (migration 0094 (+0095 heartbeat_at)): rows: id bigint; batch_id; tnved_code; name; unit IN ('kg','dona','m2','juft','litr'); price_per_unit_usd (>0, <> NaN); weight_per_unit_kg; declared_at; sender … GIN trigram index customs_import_rows_name_trgm_idx — CHECKs: Referenced by calc_request_items.import_row_id (the «📥 taxmin» provenance); status IN ('processing','ready','failed')

### Invariants (and the fence that enforces each)

- The engine never invents a number: every pricing.ts entry point returns {ok:false, reason} for a missing baza/measure/rate/zone/band/fx — fenced by tests/unit/calc-pricing.test.ts (baza_missing names the item; measure_missing; rates_missing; group_empty; zone_required; band_missing/ambiguous) and calc-law.test.ts.
- NaN is a refusal at all three layers: pricing.ts isNumber → 'not_a_number'; dictionaries.ts/workspace.ts mustBeNumber → CalcError('bad_number'); SQL CHECKs `<> 'NaN'::numeric` on every money column (0086 L40,66-70,101,148-152,179,203,253-260; 0091; 0092). Tests: calc-pricing «refuses NaN», calc-law «NaN anywhere is a refusal», calc-seal «a typo is refused before it is stored, never sealed as NaN».
- pricing.ts stays pure (no imports, no require, no server-only/next/headers/process.env/Date.now) because items-table.tsx runs it in the browser — tests/unit/pricing-pure.test.ts.
- Law 1 — the model never reaches a sealed number: pricing.ts mentions no `ai*` identifier; the calc_versions INSERT mentions exactly {aiGroupsSealed, aiBlindGroups, aiRateTakenGroups}; applyProposal writes no dutyPct/vatPct/feeUsd/bazaUsd/rateSource and DOES write aiDutyPct + aiProposal; CHECKs calc_groups_rate_source_check ('dictionary','typed') and calc_items_baza_source_check ('dictionary','typed','import') have no 'ai'; import-baza.ts names no ai identifier; prefill.ts writes no bazaSource/rateSource — tests/unit/ai-advisory.test.ts (comments stripped, #725).
- Every workspace mutator moves the rev clock under the request row's FOR UPDATE: mutateRequest = lockRequestInTx + fn + bumpRevInTx; the table doors and the seal call lockRequestInTx in their own tx and bump via recountItemsInTx / the closing UPDATE — tests/unit/calc-clock.test.ts DERIVES the export list of workspace.ts and demands every export be classified MUTATOR (must match /mutateRequest(|lockRequestInTx(/) or EXEMPT with a stated reason; also pins `locked.rev !== workspace.rev` in sealCalc, `locked.rev !== warnings.rev` in both confirm doors, and `rev: w?.rev ?? -1` in warningsNow.
- No pooled call inside a transaction (#714): loadWorkspace/getSetting/ratesForCodes/bazasFor/suggestImportBaza all run BEFORE the tx; audit rides the tx handle — tests/unit/tx-pool.test.ts (transitive, comment-stripped) plus the explicit comments at sealCalc («getSetting is read BEFORE the transaction opens») and saveTable's TX LAW.
- A ✅ must not outlive the numbers it was about: setGroupRates inlines the four-column clear; setItemBaza, pullBazasFromDictionary, moveItemToGroup (both ends), setRequestCertificate (inheriting groups only), saveTable (touched groups), deleteItem all call unconfirmInTx — CHECK calc_groups_confirm_pair_check (confirm_via IS NULL OR confirmed_at IS NOT NULL) makes forgetting loud; integration calc-seal «changing a rate un-confirms», «changing a baza under a confirmed group takes the ✅ away», calc-table «moving a coded item unconfirms BOTH ends — all four columns», «a measure edit unconfirms; a rename does not».
- A re-press of ✅ is a no-op, never a re-stamp: `WHERE confirmed_at IS NULL` in confirmGroup/confirmAllGroups — calc-table «a re-press of ✅ is a no-op … the first record is the record E1 reads».
- The seal is single-shot: `UPDATE calc_requests … WHERE completed_at IS NULL RETURNING version_no`; a second press gets already_closed — calc-seal «a second seal on the same request is refused, not a second version»; UNIQUE (request_id, version_no).
- The tariff has no holes and no overlaps: tests/unit/calc-pricing.test.ts walks every whole kg/m³ 1..1500 in both zones over ownerTariffRows() and asserts exactly one band claims each, plus 700→$300, 900/950/999→$320, 1000→per-kg $0.55; the engine's band_missing/band_ambiguous refusals stay for the editable table (HOLEY fixture).
- Fee boundaries pinned: ≤10,000→1; 10,000.01→1.5; 20,000→1.5; 40,000→2.5; 60,000→4; 100,000→7; 200,000→10; 500,000→15; 999,999.99→20; 1,000,000→25; FEE_TIERS length 8 — calc-law.test.ts «pins every boundary of the law's own table».
- Additional-duty bands inclusive-HIGH (0→5, 9.99→5, 10→10, 19.99→10, 20→15, 29.99→15, 30→20, 70→20); zero-duty code without certificate still pays +5 %; a lgota that frees the duty frees the add-duty — calc-law.test.ts.
- Guide examples are permanent engine tests: §9.1 (6102 MAX 20 %/min $3/dona, 1000 dona, BQ 13,500 → duty 3,000, VAT 1,980, fee 1.5 BHM $52.37), §9.2 (3924 20 % no certificate, BQ 32,500 → add 15 % 4,875, VAT 5,265, fee 2.5 BHM $87.28), §9.3 (5703 MAX 30 %/min $0.7/kg, 5,000 kg → 3,500, VAT 1,524, fee 1 BHM $34.91) at FX 11,801.23 and BHM 412,000 — calc-law.test.ts.
- One measure per fact: measure_unit CHECK admits only juft/litr/m2/sm3; kg lives on weight_kg, dona/1000_dona on quantity; the pair is written/cleared TOGETHER (calc_items_measure_pair_check; saveTable step 6 is the ONE writer) — calc-table «the measure pair … stamped together», «a recode … CLEARS the pair and NAMES the row», «a measure posted for a code that needs none is DROPPED with a named note».
- Provenance quadruple moves together (0094): every writer of bazaUsd also writes bazaBasis, bazaSource and importRowId (setItemBaza/pullBazasFromDictionary null it; saveTable sets 'import' iff importRowId posted) — a DERIVED source-shape test finds every `.set()` writing bazaSource (#896).
- The ✨ proposal is wired to its pricing tail and the claim is released before it: proposeGroups contains applyProposal( < releaseAiClaim( < priceProposedGroups(; the tail calls pullRatesFromDictionary( before saveTable( and never `.set(`/`.insert(` — tests/unit/proposal-wire.test.ts (comments stripped).
- canSeal is ONE predicate for three doors (seal → not_ready, the seal button's disabled state, finishCalcRequest → seal_instead).
- Dictionaries read «newest row on or before the date, no earliest-row fallback» — calc-seal «a baza dated TOMORROW prices nothing today», «the newest row on or before today wins», «the same rate corrected twice in one day is ONE row» (unique (key, effective_date) + upsert).
- Longest stored prefix answers a typed code (6403120000 → its own 5 %; 6403520000 → heading 6403's MAX) — calc-seal «the LONGEST stored prefix answers a typed 10-digit code»; saveRates carries an EXACT-code in-force row's law shape forward — «a rate correction CARRIES the law shape forward unless told otherwise».
- Freight is never allocated per group and the fee is per DECLARATION: pullRatesFromDictionary and autoGroupInTx write fee_usd NULL; requestCustomsFor computes one customsFeeFor over Σ valueUsd; no partial sums — «fee only when every group prices».
- The freight columns on a version are ONE value (freight_usd = round2(listUsd) = freight_list_usd); the discount and the band override are separate columns («two concessions, two columns»).

### Vocabulary (codes, keys, testids, settings, env, jobs, constants)

- CustomsRefusal (pricing.ts L132): group_empty | baza_missing | measure_missing | rates_missing | not_a_number
- FreightRefusal (pricing.ts L354): zone_required | measure_missing | not_a_number | band_missing | band_ambiguous
- FeeRefusal (pricing.ts L448): fee_fx_missing | not_a_number
- TotalsRefusal (pricing.ts L601): discount_exceeds_total | not_a_number
- SealBlocker.kind (workspace.ts L268): section_missing | no_groups | ungrouped_items | groups_unconfirmed | customs | freight | fee | totals | customs_on_yolkira
- CalcWarningKind (warnings.ts L16): rate_off_dictionary | baza_off_dictionary | ai_low_confidence | ai_rate_taken | rate_noted | baza_from_import
- CalcError codes thrown by workspace.ts: not_found, already_closed, ai_running, conflict, bad_number, measure_positive, zone_unknown, group_foreign, rate_range, baza_positive, code_required, rates_not_in_dictionary, amount_range, label_required, groups_confirmed, discount_reason_required, band_reason_required, not_ready, section_required, freight_zone_required, freight_missing, not_closed, not_sealed, recalc_open, recalc_superseded, no_items, ai_failed, bad_code, name_required, bad_basis, import_row_missing, too_many_items, price_positive, unauthenticated, discounted_no_upsale, answer_missing, answer_not_usd, superseded, answer_expired, below_floor_reason_required, not_pending
- CalcError codes thrown by dictionaries.ts: bad_number, product_required, baza_positive, bad_basis, code_required, rate_range, zone_required, price_positive, band_range, label_required
- CalcError codes thrown by service.ts: unauthenticated, too_many_items, not_found, too_many_open, note_taken, note_foreign, already_yours, already_closed, reason_required, seal_instead
