### Gaps (not built / cut / owed / suspect)

- `superseded` is thrown by workspace.ts recordOffer (answer branch) and releaseOffer but has NO `calc.errors.superseded` key in any bundle (messages/ru.json has only recalc_superseded / supersededBy / supersededPlain); components/calc-offer.tsx:164 guards with t.has so the screen prints the bare code «superseded» instead of a sentence (no crash).
- Stale i18n keys with no thrower: calc.errors.stale_baza (phase-3 retired the fan-out fence), calc.errors.group_gone, calc.errors.save_failed, calc.errors.failed (used only as a TS cast at calc-actions.tsx:219), calc.errors.ai_not_configured (TnvedError, caught in proposeGroups and surfaced as ai_failed); calc.refusals.unit_unsupported (messages/ru.json:2228) — pricing.ts no longer emits it (DECISIONS #868 retired it for measure_missing).
- calc-actions.tsx:219 renders `t(\`errors.${error}\`)` WITHOUT a t.has guard (every other surface guards) — safe today because run()'s reachable codes all have keys, but a new CalcError code on the queue actions would throw at render (CLAUDE.md footgun 1).
- The drizzle schema (wms.ts) is a documentation MIRROR and omits several CHECKs/indexes the SQL has: calc_groups_rates_check (0086 L148-152), calc_items_baza_value_check (0086 L176-179), calc_versions_parts_check (0086 L257-260), the NaN clauses of calc_rates_pct_check (0086 L66-70), calc_requests_answer_amount_check (0093 L21-22, NOT VALID), calc_groups_code_idx (0087 L86), calc_offers_pending/unpaid/payout_expense/request idx, calc_versions_request_no_idx, calc_requests_supersedes_idx. The SQL is the truth; anyone generating from the TS schema would lose them.
- The PP-3818 JSON contains NO 'specific'-mode rows (1,250 advalor / 198 max / 41 plus) — DutyMode 'specific' is engine/CHECK-legal but only reachable through a person's saveRates/setGroupRates.
- Phase E2 (AI control: history-dependent suspicious rules, per-worker ranking, actuals column, Telegram digest) is NOT built — docs/VED.md L245-262 states the precondition (≥20 sealed versions with confirmed links, non-empty calc_price_book); per-TNVED calc-vs-actual and the nightly AI pass are CUT.
- docs/VED.md law 5 promises «A monthly task opens for the VED to review the baza dictionary» — built as a Telegram NOTIFICATION (review.ts, JOB_CALC_REVIEW daily cron claimed monthly via calc_review_notified_month), not a task.
- Dictionaries ship EMPTY except calc_rates (PP-3818 seed) and calc_freight_tariffs (owner's table): calc_bazas and calc_price_book must be filled by the VED at /hisoblash/lugatlar before anything can seal («nothing can be sealed until then — by design», CLAUDE.md); the customs IMPORT suggestion answers nothing until a quarterly file is uploaded at /admin/bojxona-import.
- The model's REASON for an import-row pick is logged and shown nowhere (owed as a column + chip per docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md §6, CLAUDE.md #906-909).
- The `pullRatesFromDictionary` path keeps the group's stored lgota flags but deliberately writes feeUsd: null — a per-group fee a person typed earlier is erased by a pull (by design: «copying a dictionary fee here would pay it once per GROUP on top»).
- labels.ts L10-13/L37-41 claim WARNING_LABELS/REFUSAL_LABELS/BAND_REFUSAL_LABELS are anchored in tests/unit/locales.test.ts — grep of tests/unit finds no test importing those three maps (only SECTION_LABELS/FIELD_LABELS in calc-queue.test.ts), so a renamed warnings.* key would not be caught by a map-walking test.

### Docs-vs-code disagreements

- src/modules/platform/db/schema/wms.ts:2163-2165 (calc_rates.dutyUnit comment) says «The engine prices kg/dona/1000_dona and refuses the rest (`unit_unsupported`)» — pricing.ts L98-104 and DECISIONS #868 (DECISIONS.md:1445) say the engine prices ALL seven units since phase 3 and `unit_unsupported` is RETIRED for `measure_missing`. Same stale sentence in migrations 0091_calc_law.sql:25-27 (historical, expected) and the live key messages/ru.json:2228 calc.refusals.unit_unsupported.
- CLAUDE.md phase-B paragraph («`rate_source`/`baza_source` allow 'dictionary'|'typed' and there is no 'ai'») vs wms.ts:2091-2096 / 0094_customs_import.sql:84-85: baza_source now also admits 'import' (rate_source unchanged). CLAUDE.md's later 0094 paragraph and pricing.ts L24-30 say so; the phase-B sentence is stale.
- docs/VED.md:68-72 (law 5) «A monthly task opens for the VED to review the baza dictionary» vs calc/jobs.ts:46-70 + review.ts: a daily-cron Telegram notification claimed monthly (setting calc_review_notified_month), no task row.
- src/modules/wms/calc/labels.ts:10-13 and :37-41 say the label maps are «anchored the same way in tests/unit/locales.test.ts» — no test under tests/unit/ imports WARNING_LABELS / REFUSAL_LABELS / BAND_REFUSAL_LABELS (grep), and locales.test.ts contains no calc anchor; only SECTION_LABELS/FIELD_LABELS are walked (tests/unit/calc-queue.test.ts:82-84).
- Drizzle schema vs hand-written SQL (the SQL is authoritative): wms.ts calc_rates checks (~L2180-2200) omit the `<> 'NaN'::numeric` clauses 0086_calc_pricing.sql:66-70 adds; wms.ts calc_groups (~L2310-2345) omits calc_groups_rates_check (0086:148-152); wms.ts calc_request_items omits calc_items_baza_value_check (0086:176-179); wms.ts calc_versions omits calc_versions_parts_check (0086:257-260); wms.ts calc_requests omits calc_requests_answer_amount_check (0093:21-22).
- CLAUDE.md «Latest migration on this branch: 0089 … ledger must reach 90» (older State paragraph) vs the journal: 96 entries, last 0095_customs_import_heartbeat (ledger must reach 96) — the newer «Latest migration: 0095» paragraph is the current one; the 0089 sentence is a dated leftover.
- CLAUDE.md says migration 0089 is `calc_actuals` — there is no calc_actuals TABLE; 0089_calc_actuals.sql adds receipts.calc_request_id/calc_link_* (L21-35), calc_groups.ai_proposal/confirmed_warnings/confirm_via (L80-95) and the three calc_versions counters (L106-113).


## ai-prefill-import

### Summary
The AI VED hodimi is a best-effort pg-boss pass (`JOB_CALC_PREFILL` = 'calc.prefill') over a calc request the staff bot has just landed: the ONLY enqueuer is the bot's «✅ Tasdiqlash» (`c:save`) door in `src/modules/platform/telegram/staff-handlers.ts` (`queuePrefill`, line 397; called at line 649 only when `landIntake` answered `queued && requestId`), which stamps the request's current `calc_requests.rev` on the job (`prefillTicket`) and enqueues `{requestId, staffId, rev}` with boss defaults (retryLimit 5, retryBackoff). The worker (`registerCalcPrefillWorker` in `src/modules/wms/calc/jobs.ts`) first asks `prefillStanding(requestId, rev)` and stands down on anything but 'ok' (touched/confirmed/closed/not_found), runs `aiPrefill(requestId, {actorId: null})` (the machine writes as NOBODY), and delivers `out.text` via `notifyStaffTelegram({userIds:[staffId], type:'CalcPrefilled'})` — i.e. through the notifications drain (`JOB_SEND_TELEGRAM`), never a grammy ctx; failures are logged and NOT rethrown. `aiPrefill` (`src/modules/wms/calc/prefill.ts`) does four things in order and only for sections whose `sectionParts(section).customs` is true: (1) `proposeGroups` (the ✨ door — claim `ai_proposal_started_at`, Opus `proposeGoodsGrouping` in 200-item batches, `applyProposal` (deletes+reinserts every group, writes only `aiDutyPct`/`aiProposal`, never a rate), `releaseAiClaim` BEFORE the tail, then `priceProposedGroups` = `pullRatesFromDictionary` per coded group (PP-3818 book, source 'dictionary') THEN `saveTable` stamping the group's code onto each item); (2) an empty `saveTable(requestId,{items:[],adds:[]})` SWEEP that groups memory-coded items and fires 0094's deterministic import auto-fill (`suggestImportFills` → `suggestImportBaza` with `unitsForRow`, threshold setting `import_baza_min_sim` 0.45, applied in-tx with three re-checks); (3) `pickBazas` — for still-empty coded rows (cap `MAX_PICK_ROWS` 40) the model (`pickImportRows`, `src/modules/wms/calc/prefill-ai.ts`, claude-opus-5, 60 s timeout, json_schema `{picks:[{row,candidate|null,reason}]}`) chooses an INDEX into real `customs_import_rows` candidates, every index re-checked, basis fence `BASIS_FOR_UNIT[chosen.unit] === BASIS_FOR_UNIT[unit]`, «still empty» re-read after the model call (overtaken rows dropped), written through `saveTable`'s `importRowId` edit path so the PRICE is re-read from the FILE and `baza_source` lands 'import' — prefill.ts writes no `bazaSource` of its own; (4) `loadWorkspace` → pure `prefillReplyText` (`prefill-reply.ts`): «🧮 Tahminiy: rastamojka ~$X (N ta tovarsiz) · yo‘lkira ~$Y» + «⚠️ Rasmiy emas — VED xodimi tasdiqlaydi.» on its own line, `customsUsd` forced null when the section has no customs half or has zero groups (so never «~$0.00»), freight = `freight.listUsd` (tariff list price, never a discount), blockers in Uzbek words via `Record<Union,string>` maps capped at 6 with «; …», counts of what the machine did and what it did not (capped/refused/overtaken). The request STAYS in the VED queue with every group unconfirmed (`groups_unconfirmed` is a seal blocker) — the official price is still the VED's ✅ + seal. The quarterly customs import (`src/modules/wms/customs/*`) is the baza source: admin uploads at `/admin/bojxona-import` (gate `admin.dictionaries.manage`) through route handler `POST /api/admin/customs-import` (ZIP-magic check, 200 MB cap, storage-first, batch row 'processing', `enqueue(JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT, …, {expireInSeconds: 7200, retryLimit: 2})`); the worker streams the file (exceljs stream reader / csv), matches columns by HEADER NAME, beats `heartbeat_at` every 10 s (first beat = the CLAIM, `WHERE status='processing' RETURNING`), settles 'ready' with period_from/to, or 'failed' with a sentence (`importFailureText`); a 5-min sweep fails a beating batch quiet 15 min (`IMPORT_STALE_MS`) or a never-started one older than 3 h (`IMPORT_NEVER_STARTED_MS`); `deleteImportBatch` refuses `in_use` for a non-failed batch any `calc_request_items.import_row_id` points into; `newestReadyBatchId()` is what every suggestion reads. Owed and stated in docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md §6: the model's per-pick REASON is only logged ('[calc-prefill] the model chose'), never stored or shown, so a pick and the deterministic auto-fill are indistinguishable on screen.


### Files and exports


#### `src/modules/wms/calc/prefill.ts` — The AI VED hodimi's pass (docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md §3). Orchestrates proposeGroups → sweep saveTable → pickBazas → loadWorkspace → prefillReplyText. Every write goes through existing doors (`proposeGroups`, `saveTable`); «There is no second writer.» Model injectable via deps.

- **`PrefillStand`** `export type PrefillStand = 'ok' | 'touched' | 'confirmed' | 'closed' | 'not_found'` — The worker's stand-down verdict.
- **`prefillStanding`** `export async function prefillStanding(requestId: string, expectRev: number | null): Promise<PrefillStand>` — 'not_found' if no row; 'closed' if calc_requests.completed_at set; 'touched' if expectRev !== null && rev !== expectRev; 'confirmed' if any calc_groups.confirmed_at IS NOT NULL for the request; else 'ok'. Comment: «a pass killed half-way is not retried, because its own first half has already moved the clock … The refusal is logged, never silent.» Called by registerCalcPrefillWorker before aiPrefill.
- **`prefillTicket`** `export async function prefillTicket(requestId: string): Promise<number | null>` — Reads calc_requests.rev (null if row missing). Called by the bot's queuePrefill at enqueue time so the rev travels ON the job (calc-prefill-wire.test pins `enqueue(JOB_CALC_PREFILL, {… rev …})`).
- **`PrefillDeps`** `export interface PrefillDeps { propose?: typeof proposeGroups; pick?: (rows: PickRequest[]) => Promise<PickAnswer[] | null>; configured?: boolean }` — Injection points for tests (container has no key): propose defaults to proposeGroups, pick to pickImportRows, configured to aiConfigured().
- **`PrefillOutcome`** `export interface PrefillOutcome { text: string; customsUsd: number | null; freightUsd: number | null; codesStamped: number; ratesPulled: number; importFilled: number; picked: number; aiUsed: boolean }` — `text` is the Telegram message; `importFilled` in the reply = importFilled + picked.
- **`aiPrefill`** `export async function aiPrefill(requestId: string, ctx: AuditContext, deps: PrefillDeps = {}): Promise<PrefillOutcome>` — Reads calc_requests.section → `sectionParts(section)`; if parts.customs is false NOTHING is proposed/swept/picked (yolkira left alone — no model call, no groups, no $0). Step 1 (configured && parts.customs): `propose(requestId, ctx)` in try/catch (logs '[calc-prefill] propose failed'), accumulates codesStamped/ratesPulled/importFilled, aiUsed=true. Step 2 (parts.customs): `saveTable(requestId,{items:[],adds:[]},ctx)` — the SWEEP, model-free half; logs '[calc-prefill] sweep failed'. Step 3 (configured && parts.customs): `pickBazas` → picked/capped/refused/overtaken. Step 4: `loadWorkspace`; `customsUsd = ws && ws.parts.customs && ws.groups.length > 0 ? ws.customsUsd : null` (comment: «NOTHING TO PRICE» IS NOT «PRICED AT ZERO» — requestCustomsFor([]) is vacuously ok and answers 0); `freightUsd = freight.ok ? freight.listUsd : null` («A prefill states the tariff, never a discount»). Returns prefillReplyText({... link: null, aiConfigured: configured, pickCapped, pickRefused, pickOvertaken}). Side effects: audit rows via saveTable/applyProposal under ctx (worker passes actorId null). Called by registerCalcPrefillWorker and the integration test.
- **`MAX_PICK_ROWS (module const)`** `const MAX_PICK_ROWS = 40` — «How many rows are ever sent to the pick — a wall, not a silent truncation.» Rows past it are counted as `capped` and named in the reply.
- **`pickBazas (module-private)`** `async function pickBazas(requestId, ctx, pick): Promise<{ picked; capped; refused; overtaken }>` — Returns zeros if newestReadyBatchId() is null (comment: «inventing a price is the one thing this module may never do»). Candidates = rows with bazaUsd===null && tnvedCode; per row `unitsForRow({dutyUnit of its group, hasWeight, hasQuantity})[0]`; skips rows with no unit; `suggestImportBaza({tnvedCode,name,unit,weightPerUnitKg: kg/qty}, {batchId})` — NON-picker mode (up to 10 candidates, incl. unit mismatches labelled); skips rows with zero candidates. After `pick(asking)`: null answer → nothing written; each answer's `candidate` looked up in the asked list; REFUSED if `BASIS_FOR_UNIT[chosen.unit] !== BASIS_FOR_UNIT[unit]` («the model is choosing a row, not a basis»); edits carry `bazaUsd: chosen.pricePerUnitUsd, bazaBasis: chosen.basis, importRowId: chosen.id` («The amount is re-read from the FILE by saveTable»). Logs picks+reasons as '[calc-prefill] the model chose'. Re-reads bazaUsd of the edited ids; rows now non-null are DROPPED as `overtaken` ('[calc-prefill] a person filled these while the model was thinking — left alone'). Writes through `saveTable(requestId,{items: live, adds: []}, ctx)`.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/prefill-ai.ts` — The model PICKS A ROW out of the customs file — never a price (law 1 at its narrowest). ONE call per request.

- **`PickRequest`** `export interface PickRequest { seq: number; name: string; tnvedCode: string; candidates: ImportBazaRow[] }` — seq = the item's display order — how the answer is matched back.
- **`PickAnswer`** `export interface PickAnswer { seq: number; candidate: number | null; reason: string }` — candidate = index into that row's candidates or null («none of these»); reason ≤300 chars, Uzbek one-liner («Это читает человек»).
- **`pickImportRows`** `export async function pickImportRows(rows: PickRequest[], opts: { timeoutMs?: number } = {}): Promise<PickAnswer[] | null>` — Returns null when `!process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, on stop_reason 'refusal', or any throw (logged 'calc prefill pick failed'); `[]` when no row has candidates. `new Anthropic({ timeout: opts.timeoutMs ?? 60_000, maxRetries: 1 })`, model 'claude-opus-5', max_tokens 4096, output_config.format json_schema `{picks:[{row:integer, candidate:integer|null, reason:string}]}` (additionalProperties false), user content = listing `Строка ${seq}: «name» (код X)` + `[i] name(≤200) — $price/basis · w kg/dona (единица не совпадает)` sliced to 40_000 chars. Logs '[calc-prefill pick] model tokens' {in,out}. Validation: an answer about an unasked seq is dropped; a non-integer / out-of-range candidate is dropped («the model's output is data, not a command»). zod `picksSchema` mirrors the wire schema; ai-advisory.test pins that neither schema nor `out.push` carries price/usd/amount/baza/summa.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/prefill-reply.ts` — PURE reply shaping for the staff chat (Uzbek strings, bot convention). Law 6: never a $0, never a silence; owner's answer 6: figure is TAHMINIY.

- **`blockerText`** `export function blockerText(b: SealBlocker): string` — section_missing→'bo‘lim tanlanmagan'; no_groups→'hali birorta guruh yo‘q'; ungrouped_items→`${count} ta tovarga TNVED kod qo‘yilmagan`; groups_unconfirmed→`${count} ta guruh tasdiqlanmagan (VED ✅ qiladi)`; customs→`${groupLabel}: ${CUSTOMS_REASON[reason] ?? reason}` + ` — ${itemLabel}`; freight→`yo‘lkira: …`; fee→`bojxona yig‘imi: …`; totals→`jami: …`; customs_on_yolkira→'bu bo‘limda rastamojka hisoblanmaydi'; default 'noma’lum'. Maps are `Record<CustomsRefusal|FreightRefusal|FeeRefusal|TotalsRefusal, string>` (compile-time drift fence); unknown reason prints the raw code.
- **`PrefillReplyInput`** `export interface PrefillReplyInput { customsUsd: number|null; freightUsd: number|null; hasFreight: boolean; hasCustoms: boolean; blockers: SealBlocker[]; codesStamped: number; ratesPulled: number; importFilled: number; link: string|null; aiConfigured: boolean; pickCapped?: number; pickRefused?: number; pickOvertaken?: number }` — hasCustoms exists because loadWorkspace answers customsUsd 0 (not null) for a section with no customs half.
- **`prefillReplyText`** `export function prefillReplyText(input: PrefillReplyInput): string` — priced = (hasCustoms && customsUsd!==null) || freightUsd!==null. Line 1: `🧮 Tahminiy: rastamojka ~$X.XX[ (N ta tovarsiz)] · yo‘lkira ~$Y.YY` (partial suffix from an `ungrouped_items` blocker); line 2: '⚠️ Rasmiy emas — VED xodimi tasdiqlaydi.'; else '🧮 Hozircha hisoblab bo‘lmadi.'. If hasFreight && freightUsd===null && priced: 'Yo‘lkira hali hisoblanmadi.'. `AI qo‘ydi: N ta kod · N ta stavka · N ta baza (import)` only when nonzero. `!aiConfigured` → 'AI sozlanmagan — faqat yozilganidan o‘qildi.'. `Bazasi qo‘yilmadi: N ta qator ko‘rilmadi (juda ko‘p) · N ta taklif o‘lchovi mos kelmadi · N tasini o‘zingiz to‘ldirdingiz`. `Yetishmayapti: ` + blockerText ×≤6 joined '; ' + '; …' when more. money = `$${n.toFixed(2)}`. Link appended if given (aiPrefill passes null).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/review.ts` — Monthly dictionary-review reminder (not part of the prefill pass; lives beside it in calc/jobs).

- **`reviewMonth`** `export function reviewMonth(now: Date, timeZone = 'Asia/Tashkent'): string` — YYYY-MM in office zone.
- **`claimReviewMonth`** `export async function claimReviewMonth(month: string): Promise<boolean>` — Atomic INSERT…ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE with setWhere `value IS DISTINCT FROM month::jsonb` on settings key 'calc_review_notified_month'; RETURNING decides the winner.
- **`notifyDictionaryReview`** `export async function notifyDictionaryReview(now = new Date()): Promise<number>` — staleDictionaryCounts().total===0 → 0 (month NOT claimed); else claim, then notifyStaffTelegram({userIds: usersWithPermission('ved.docs'), type:'CalcDictReview', text}). Scheduled by registerCalcReviewWorker at '0 6 * * *'.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/intake-ai.ts` — The intake's EXTRACTION half: reads forwarded material (text + downscaled photos) into CalcFacts. «It EXTRACTS and it EXPLAINS; it does not price anything.»

- **`AiIntakeResult`** `export interface AiIntakeResult { facts: CalcFacts; steps: string[] }` — steps = the model's own Uzbek description of what it did, shown on the card's lenta (≤20).
- **`analyzeIntake`** `export async function analyzeIntake(input: { section: CalcSection; text: string; fileCount: number; images?: { data: Buffer; mediaType: 'image/jpeg'|'image/png'|'image/webp' }[]; timeoutMs?: number }): Promise<AiIntakeResult | null>` — null when no ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, when text AND images are both empty, on stop_reason 'refusal', or any throw (logged 'calc intake AI failed'). `new Anthropic({ timeout: input.timeoutMs ?? 60_000, maxRetries: 1 })`, model 'claude-opus-5', max_tokens 4096, json_schema `{from_city, to_city, weight_kg, volume_m3, goods:[{name, quantity, weight_kg, tnved_code, note}], steps:[string]}` all nullable except name/steps; images FIRST as base64 blocks then text `Раздел: …\nПрикреплено файлов: N\nФотографий для чтения: N\nМатериалы:` (text sliced 20 000). A tnved_code not /^\d{10}$/ is blanked to null (#378). Logs '[calc intake] model tokens'. Caller: telegram/calc-intake.ts analyzeCollected (manual typed facts WIN over the model's).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts (proposal + claim + saveTable parts)` — The workspace's one write door and the ✨ proposal; holds the AI claim and the rev clock.

- **`AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS`** `export const AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS = 10 * 60_000` — «ONE constant for the two places that ask (#513): the claim that TAKES the request and the workspace lock that REFUSES while somebody holds it.»
- **`proposeGroups`** `export async function proposeGroups(requestId: string, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<{ groups: number; batches: number; failed: number; ratesPulled: number; codesStamped: number; importFilled: number }>` — assertOpen (CalcError 'not_found'/'already_closed'); CLAIM = UPDATE calc_requests SET ai_proposal_started_at=now() WHERE id AND (ai_proposal_started_at IS NULL OR < now()-AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS) RETURNING — none → CalcError('ai_running'). Loads items ordered by seq ('no_items' if none); `planBatches(items.length)` (AI_BATCH_SIZE 200) × `proposeGoodsGrouping` from ../tnved/service (per-batch failures counted; all failed → 'ai_failed'); `mergeProposals` → `applyProposal`; then `releaseAiClaim` (idempotent UPDATE to null) BEFORE `priceProposedGroups` (both tail doors take lockRequestInTx which refuses on the claim — #906); `finally` releases again.
- **`applyProposal`** `export async function applyProposal(requestId: string, drafts: DraftGroup[], ctx: AuditContext): Promise<void>` — One tx: lockRequestInTx(tx, requestId, { ignoreAiClaim: true }); refuses CalcError('groups_confirmed') if any group confirmed; DELETES all calc_groups for the request and inserts drafts with label/tnvedCode/aiProposed/aiConfidence/aiDutyPct (toFixed(3))/aiProposal {tnvedCode, aiDutyPct, itemSeqs}/note, sets items' group_id by seq. Writes NO dutyPct/vatPct/feeUsd/bazaUsd/rateSource (ai-advisory.test pins).
- **`priceProposedGroups`** `export async function priceProposedGroups(requestId: string, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<{ ratesPulled: number; codesStamped: number; importFilled: number }>` — For every coded group with dutyPct or vatPct null → pullRatesFromDictionary(group.id, ctx); swallows ONLY CalcError codes 'rates_not_in_dictionary' and 'code_required' (logged '[calc] proposed code has no dictionary rate'), rethrows everything else. Then items whose group code differs from their tnvedCode are edited via `saveTable(requestId, {items:[{id,seq,tnvedCode}], adds:[]}, ctx)`; codesStamped = edits.length; importFilled = saved.importFilled.length. Order pinned by proposal-wire.test: rates BEFORE codes (the group's duty_unit drives unitsForRow).
- **`pullRatesFromDictionary`** `export async function pullRatesFromDictionary(groupId: string, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<void>` — 'not_found' / 'code_required' (blank group code) / 'rates_not_in_dictionary' (ratesForCodes([code], onDate()) miss); delegates to setGroupRates with source 'dictionary', feeUsd null, dutyMode/dutySpecific/dutyUnit from the book. Goes through mutateRequest → lockRequestInTx → may throw 'ai_running'.
- **`lockRequestInTx (private)`** `async function lockRequestInTx(tx, requestId, opts: { ignoreAiClaim?: boolean } = {}): Promise<{ rev: number }>` — SELECT … FROM calc_requests WHERE id FOR UPDATE; 'not_found'; 'already_closed' if completed_at; 'ai_running' if ai_proposal_started_at within AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS unless ignoreAiClaim («exists for exactly one caller: applyProposal»).
- **`mutateRequest (private)`** `async function mutateRequest<T>(requestId, fn: (tx, locked: {rev}) => Promise<T>): Promise<T>` — lock → fn → bumpRevInTx (rev+1, updated_at). calc-clock.test.ts derives the mutator list.
- **`TableItemEdit`** `export interface TableItemEdit { id: string; seq: number; name?; quantity?; weightKg?; volumeM3?; tnvedCode?; note?; measureQty?; bazaUsd?: number|null; bazaBasis?: BazaBasis|null; importRowId?: string | null }` — importRowId is the picker's/pick's claim; the server re-reads price+basis from the file.
- **`TableSaveResult`** `export interface TableSaveResult { minted: string[]; swept: number; added: number; merged: string[]; measuresCleared: number[]; measuresDropped: number[]; basisSuspect: number[]; importFilled: number[] }` — importFilled = seqs whose EMPTY baza this save filled from the customs import — «Named, never silent».
- **`saveTable`** `export async function saveTable(requestId: string, input: { items: TableItemEdit[]; adds: TableNewItem[] }, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<TableSaveResult>` — Pre-tx on the POOL (#714): validation (CalcError 'name_required'/bad number with seq, checkBazaPair), ratesForCodes over the union of edited+sweep+add codes, `importRowForCode(importRowId, code)` per picked id → CalcError('import_row_missing', seq) if absent/other code/non-ready batch; picked price REPLACES the posted bazaUsd/bazaBasis; `suggestImportFills` computes the auto-fill plan. In tx: lockRequestInTx; edits by id; baza triple (`patch.bazaSource = e.importRowId ? 'import' : 'typed'`, `importRowId = BigInt(...)`), null clears amount/basis/source/importRowId; adds inserted; regroup+sweep; merge; measure pass; step 7 import auto-fill applied only where `item.bazaUsd === null`, `item.tnvedCode === fill.code`, and `unitsForRow(group.dutyUnit,…)` admits `fill.basis` — writes bazaSource 'import' + importRowId; unconfirmInTx(touched groups); recount; ONE audit row (entityType 'calc_request', action 'update', after.importFilled…). 'too_many_items' (MAX_CALC_ITEMS 1000).
- **`suggestImportFills (private)`** `async function suggestImportFills(input: { standing; itemEdits; withCodes; rates: Map<string, RatesRow> }): Promise<ImportFillPlan>` — Only rows ending the save CODED and BAZA-LESS; newestReadyBatchId() null → empty plan; minSim = Number(getSetting('import_baza_min_sim')); memo per `${code}|${units}|${name}|${perPiece}`; first unit in `unitsForRow` order whose suggestImportBaza(...).auto answers wins.
- **`Workspace / SealBlocker`** `export interface Workspace { rev; section; parts:{customs,freight,extras}; groups; ungrouped; freight: FreightResult|null; customsUsd: number|null; blockers: SealBlocker[]; sealedVersion; completedAt; … }; export type SealBlocker = section_missing | no_groups | ungrouped_items{count} | groups_unconfirmed{count} | customs{groupSeq,groupLabel,reason,itemLabel?} | freight{reason} | fee{reason} | totals{reason} | customs_on_yolkira` — `blockersFor` (line 616): customs half only when parts.customs; a yolkira request carrying groups → customs_on_yolkira; `groups_unconfirmed` = «Law 1: an unconfirmed group is still the model's opinion.»

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/grouping.ts` — Pure proposal folding.

- **`AI_BATCH_SIZE`** `export const AI_BATCH_SIZE = 200` — proposeGoodsGrouping's own cap.
- **`planBatches`** `export function planBatches(total: number, size = AI_BATCH_SIZE): { offset: number; count: number }[]` — Slices.
- **`mergeProposals`** `export function mergeProposals(batches: { offset: number; groups: ProposedGroup[] | null }[], itemSeqs: number[]): DraftGroup[]` — Failed batch (null) contributes nothing; same-code groups merged with WORST confidence; blank (invalid) code groups stay loose; out-of-slice indexes dropped; DraftGroup = {label, tnvedCode, itemSeqs, confidence, aiDutyPct (duty_rate_pct), aiProposed: true, note (reasoning)}. (CLAUDE.md: orphan groups minted with aiProposed:false.)

#### `src/modules/wms/tnved/service.ts` — Model grouping/classification (the ✨ half) — pre-existing round 17 code.

- **`proposeGoodsGrouping`** `export async function proposeGoodsGrouping(goods: { name: string; quantity: number | null; unit: string | null }[]): Promise<TnvedGrouping>` — TnvedError('ai_not_configured') without key; 'ai_failed' on 0 or >200 goods, refusal, parse failure or no valid groups. `new Anthropic({ timeout: 60_000, maxRetries: 1 })`, 'claude-opus-5', max_tokens 8192, json_schema `{groups:[{tnved_code, name_ru, item_indexes:int[], confidence:'high'|'medium'|'low', reasoning, duty_rate_pct:number|null}]}`; invalid codes blanked + demoted to 'low'; indexes ≥ goods.length dropped. duty_rate_pct is ADVISORY only (lands in calc_groups.ai_duty_pct).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/jobs.ts` — pg-boss registrations for the calc module.

- **`JOB_CALC_OVERDUE / JOB_CALC_REVIEW / JOB_CALC_PREFILL`** `'calc.overdue' | 'calc.dict-review' | 'calc.prefill'` — Queue names.
- **`CalcPrefillJob`** `export interface CalcPrefillJob { requestId: string; staffId: string; rev?: number | null }` — «rev … Absent on a job queued before this field existed, which then falls back to the confirmed/closed checks alone.»
- **`registerCalcPrefillWorker`** `export async function registerCalcPrefillWorker(boss: PgBoss): Promise<void>` — createQueue(JOB_CALC_PREFILL); boss.work<CalcPrefillJob>(JOB_CALC_PREFILL, async (jobs) => …): per job dynamic-imports prefill + notifications/staff; `prefillStanding(requestId, job.data.rev ?? null)` !== 'ok' → log '[calc-prefill] stood down — the request has moved' and `continue`; `aiPrefill(requestId, { actorId: null })`; `notifyStaffTelegram({ userIds: [staffId], type: 'CalcPrefilled', text: out.text })`; log '[calc-prefill] pass finished'; catch logs '[calc-prefill] pass failed — the VED still has the job' and does NOT rethrow. Registered in boss.ts WORKER_REGISTRATIONS under name 'calc-prefill'.
- **`registerCalcWorker`** `export async function registerCalcWorker(boss: PgBoss): Promise<void>` — '*/5 * * * *' → notifyOverdueCalcs().
- **`registerCalcReviewWorker`** `export async function registerCalcReviewWorker(boss: PgBoss): Promise<void>` — '0 6 * * *' → notifyDictionaryReview().

#### `src/modules/platform/jobs/boss.ts` — pg-boss singleton, named worker registrations, sender-only enqueue.

- **`WORKER_REGISTRATIONS`** `export const WORKER_REGISTRATIONS: [string, (boss: PgBoss) => Promise<void>][]` — Includes ['calc-overdue'], ['calc-review'], ['calc-prefill' → registerCalcPrefillWorker], ['customs-import' → registerCustomsImportWorker], ['customs-import-sweep' → registerCustomsImportSweep]. Each registered at most once per process (registerAllWorkers with a done-set; boss.work is NOT idempotent).
- **`EnqueueOptions`** `export interface EnqueueOptions { expireInSeconds?: number; retryLimit?: number }` — «The customs import is the first that is neither [seconds long nor safe on 5 retries]».»
- **`enqueue`** `export async function enqueue<T extends object>(name: string, data: T, options: EnqueueOptions = {}): Promise<void>` — ensureListening (boss.start only — never registers workers); createQueue upsert once per process; `boss.send(name, data, { retryLimit: 5, retryBackoff: true, ...options })`. So JOB_CALC_PREFILL gets 5 retries / default 15-min expiry; JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT gets expireInSeconds 7200, retryLimit 2.
- **`isBossStarted / startBoss / getBoss`** `export function isBossStarted(): boolean; export async function startBoss(): Promise<PgBoss>; export function getBoss(): PgBoss` — Latch set LAST after all registrations.

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/staff-handlers.ts` — grammy shell for the staff bot; the ONLY place a prefill is enqueued.

- **`queuePrefill (private, line 397)`** `async function queuePrefill(requestId: string, staffId: string): Promise<void>` — Dynamic-imports enqueue/JOB_CALC_PREFILL/prefillTicket; `rev = await prefillTicket(requestId)`; `enqueue(JOB_CALC_PREFILL, { requestId, staffId, rev })`; errors logged 'calc prefill could not be queued' (the seller's confirmation must not be lost). Called at line 649 in the `c:save` callback branch only if `target.queued && target.requestId`, AFTER `landCollectedIntake` and `endIntake(chatId)`; the reply then says '🧮 Hisoblash navbatiga tushdi — VED xodimi javob beradi.' or '⚠️ Hisoblash navbatiga tushmadi — kartadan qo‘lda yuboring.'
- **`callback vocabulary`** `c:yolkira | c:rastamojka | c:podklyuch | c:done | c:more | c:save | c:cancel | e:s | e:c` — Section pick, «Bo‘ldi» (→ '⏳ Tahlil qilinmoqda…' + `void analyseIntakeAndReply` OFF the poller), more, «✅ Tasdiqlash», cancel; entry buttons. Text triggers: HISOBLATISH button / '/hisoblatish' → sectionKeyboard() (only if mayCollect(chatId) = staffForChat !== null).
- **`reduceForModel (private)`** `async function reduceForModel(original: Buffer): Promise<Buffer | null>` — sharp rotate, resize inside MODEL_IMAGE_PX 1568, jpeg q80; >4_500_000 bytes → null (counted imagesSkipped). MAX_INTAKE_IMAGES 6 reach the model.

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/calc-intake.ts` — In-memory «Hisoblatish» collection state (30-min TTL, lost on restart — stated).

- **`IntakeState / IntakeStage`** `export type IntakeStage = 'section'|'client'|'material'|'review'; export interface IntakeState { section; stage; noteId; clientHintRaw; material: string[]; fileCount; facts: CalcFacts; steps: string[]; aiUsed; images; imagesSkipped; promptMessageId; expires }` — noteId minted up front (uuid) so photos are stored pre-bound (#180).
- **`startIntake / activeIntake / updateIntake / endIntake`** `startIntake(chatId: bigint, section): IntakeState; activeIntake(chatId): IntakeState|null; updateIntake(chatId, patch): IntakeState|null; endIntake(chatId): void` — TTL_MS 30*60_000; expiry checked on read; MAX_INTAKE_IMAGES = 6.

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/staff-bot.ts` — Pure-ish decisions behind the bot.

- **`analyzeCollected`** `export async function analyzeCollected(state: IntakeState): Promise<IntakeState>` — parseManualFacts(text) + analyzeIntake({section,text,fileCount,images}) (errors → null); «Typed facts win over read ones»: fromCity/toCity/weightKg/volumeM3 = manual ?? ai; goods = ai.goods if non-empty else manual.goods; steps = ai.steps ?? []; aiUsed = Boolean(ai); stage 'review'.
- **`landCollectedIntake`** `export async function landCollectedIntake(chatId: bigint, staffId: string, staffName: string): Promise<{ kind:'deal'|'lead'; id; label; queued?; requestId?: string|null } | null>` — parseClientHint(clientHintRaw) → resolveIntakeClient; landIntake({noteId, section, facts, steps, material, fileCount, collectedBy, collectedByName, client, leadName: clientHintRaw || 'Hisoblatish (nomsiz)', leadPhone}).
- **`mayCollect`** `export async function mayCollect(chatId: bigint): Promise<boolean>` — «Everyone is (owner's answer 3)» — staff only.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/intake-land.ts` — Where a confirmed intake lands (deal for a coded client, lead otherwise) and opens the calc request.

- **`IntakeTarget`** `export interface IntakeTarget { kind: 'deal'|'lead'; id: string; label: string; queued?: boolean; requestId?: string|null }` — queued=false means «send it from the card»; requestId is what the prefill works on.
- **`landIntake`** `export async function landIntake(input: { noteId; section; facts; steps; fileCount; material?; collectedBy; collectedByName; client; leadName; leadPhone }): Promise<IntakeTarget>` — dealFor (newest OPEN deal or createDeal) / openLeadForPhone (last-9 match, no clientId) / createLead; addActivity note (intakeNoteText); `openCalcRequest({ …, items: itemFacts(facts).map(...), noteId, source: 'bot', hasMaterials })` in try/catch (queued/requestId) — the items go through `itemFacts` so a single line's weight is derived (loneWeightKg); moveToCalcStage via settings crm_calc_stage/deal_calc_stage, forward only. Does NOT enqueue the prefill itself.
- **`resolveIntakeClient / dealFor / openLeadForPhone`** `resolveIntakeClient(hint:{code?,phone?}); dealFor(client, section, actorId); openLeadForPhone(phone)` — Ambiguous phone → nobody.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/intake.ts` — Pure intake rules: sections, required fields, checklist, summary/note text.

- **`REQUIRED_FIELDS`** `export const REQUIRED_FIELDS: Record<CalcSection, CalcField[]> = { yolkira: ['fromCity','toCity','weightKg','volumeM3','goods'], rastamojka: ['weightKg','volumeM3','goods','itemMeasure'], podklyuch: ['fromCity','toCity','weightKg','volumeM3','goods','itemMeasure'] }` — itemMeasure = ONE per-line measure (count OR weight OR measureQty) — «never both» (#910).
- **`loneWeightKg / itemFacts / missingFields / isComplete`** `loneWeightKg(itemCount: number, totalKg): number|null; itemFacts(facts: CalcFacts): CalcItemFact[]; missingFields(section, facts): CalcField[]; isComplete(section, facts): boolean` — One line takes the shipment's weight; ≥2 lines take nothing. itemMeasure missing when some item has quantity===null && weightKg===null && measureQty===null. All three landing doors must use itemFacts/loneWeightKg (intake-items-wire.test).
- **`FIELD_LABEL`** `Record<CalcField,string>: itemMeasure → 'tovarning soni yoki og‘irligi'` — Checklist wording.

#### `src/modules/wms/customs/import-baza.ts` — The customs dump answering the VED's baza question (§2.3). CODE narrows exactly, NAME ranks (word_similarity), WEIGHT re-ranks dona goods, below threshold nothing fills.

- **`BASIS_FOR_UNIT / UNIT_FOR_BASIS`** `BASIS_FOR_UNIT: Record<ImportUnit,'kg'|'unit'|'m2'|'juft'|'litr'> = {kg:'kg', dona:'unit', m2:'m2', juft:'juft', litr:'litr'}; UNIT_FOR_BASIS the inverse` — Bijection pinned by customs-import-parse.test.
- **`unitsForRow`** `export function unitsForRow(input: { dutyUnit: string|null|undefined; hasWeight: boolean; hasQuantity: boolean }): ImportUnit[]` — m2/juft/litr → [that]; 'kg' → ['kg']; else kg-first ([...kg,...dona]) unless dutyUnit is dona/1000_dona/sm3 (then dona-first); neither weight nor count → []. Comment: «74 % of his file is declared per kilogram, while defaultBasisFor answers 'unit' for every ordinary advalor code.»
- **`ImportBazaRow / ImportBazaSuggestion`** `ImportBazaRow { id; name; unit; basis; pricePerUnitUsd; weightPerUnitKg; declaredAt; sender; nameSim; score; unitMatches }; ImportBazaSuggestion { auto: ImportBazaRow|null; candidates: ImportBazaRow[]; batchId: string|null; total: number }` — unitMatches: «Only a match may auto-fill.»
- **`suggestImportBaza`** `export async function suggestImportBaza(input: { tnvedCode: string; name: string; unit: ImportUnit; weightPerUnitKg?: number|null }, opts: { batchId?: string|null; minSim?: number; picker?: boolean } = {}): Promise<ImportBazaSuggestion>` — batchId defaults to newestReadyBatchId(); null → empty. needle = normalizeName(name); usable = needle.length >= MIN_NEEDLE (4); !usable && !picker → empty candidates; limit = picker ? PICKER_LIMIT 50 : CANDIDATE_LIMIT 10; queryCandidates ORDER BY (unit match) DESC, score DESC, declared_at DESC; picker with short needle → candidates with nameSim 0, auto null. minSim = opts.minSim ?? Number(getSetting('import_baza_min_sim') ?? 0.45); auto = best && best.unitMatches && best.nameSim >= minSim. Score: `word_similarity(needle, r.name_norm)`; for unit 'dona' with weightPerUnitKg>0: 0.7*sim + 0.3*(1 - LEAST(1, |w - weight_per_unit_kg| / GREATEST(w, 0.01))); `count(*) OVER ()` = total.
- **`importRowForCode`** `export async function importRowForCode(rowId: string, tnvedCode: string): Promise<ImportBazaRow | null>` — Row must exist, carry the SAME tnved_code and belong to a batch with status 'ready' (id-teleport fence #864); returns nameSim 1/score 1/unitMatches true.

#### `src/modules/wms/customs/import-parse.ts` — Pure parsing rules of the customs dump (zero imports).

- **`ImportUnit / mapUnit`** `export type ImportUnit = 'kg'|'dona'|'m2'|'juft'|'litr'; export function mapUnit(raw: unknown): ImportUnit | null` — UNIT_WORDS кг/kg/килограмм→kg; шт/штук/дона/pcs→dona; м2/м²/m2/кв м→m2; пар/пара/juft→juft; л/литр/l→litr; anything else null (skipped, «never guessed»).
- **`REQUIRED_FIELDS / HEADER_WORDS / readHeader / normalizeHeader / FIELD_LABELS`** `REQUIRED_FIELDS: ImportField[] = ['tnvedCode','name','pricePerUnit','unit']; readHeader(cells: unknown[]): { index: Partial<Record<ImportField,number>>; missing: ImportField[] }` — Columns by NORMALISED HEADER NAME (his 1b); first match wins for duplicated «Страна происхождения»; customsValue matches only dollar spellings ('там стоим $' …) — «Божхона киймати» is the contract currency. FIELD_LABELS gives the file's own words for the refusal.
- **`normalizeName / parseNumber / parseDate / fitNumeric`** `normalizeName(raw): string; parseNumber(raw): number|null; parseDate(raw): string|null; fitNumeric(value: number|null, precision: number, scale: number): number|null` — normalizeName lowercases, strips «^\d+[.)]», collapses whitespace (whole ru+en string kept). parseDate handles Date, Excel serial 20_000..60_000 (stream reader hands serials), ISO and dd.mm.yyyy. fitNumeric rounds to the column's scale first and refuses overflow (#918).
- **`parseRow / SkipReason / priceDrift`** `parseRow(cells, header): {ok:true,row:ParsedImportRow} | {ok:false, reason: SkipReason}; type SkipReason = 'bad_code'|'no_name'|'bad_price'|'unknown_unit'|'rejected'; priceDrift(row): number|null` — CODE_SHAPE /^\d{4,10}$/; price fitNumeric(…,14,4) must be > 0; weight fitNumeric(…,12,4) kept only if > 0; name ≤500, sender ≤300, origin ≤120. priceDrift = |customs_value/netto − price| / expected on kg rows (sampled ≤100, logged).

#### `src/modules/wms/customs/import-service.ts` — Streaming ingest of the quarterly dump, batch lifecycle, sweep, delete.

- **`IMPORT_STALE_MS / IMPORT_NEVER_STARTED_MS`** `export const IMPORT_STALE_MS = 15 * 60_000; export const IMPORT_NEVER_STARTED_MS = 3 * 60 * 60_000` — Beating batch dead after 15 min silence; never-started batch lost after 3 h (the job's 2 h + margin). HEARTBEAT_MS 10_000, CHUNK 1_000, SPOOL_DRAIN_MS 3_000 are module-private.
- **`BatchGoneError / CustomsImportError`** `export class BatchGoneError extends Error {}; export class CustomsImportError extends Error { constructor(readonly reason: 'missing_columns'|'no_rows'|'unreadable'|'in_use', readonly detail?: string) }` — BatchGoneError = «swept, or deleted from the screen, while it was reading» — not retried, nothing written. CustomsImportError = a refusal the admin can act on (not retried).
- **`runCustomsImport`** `export async function runCustomsImport(input: { batchId: string; storageKey: string; fileName: string }): Promise<ImportOutcome { rowCount; skipped; skipReasons; periodFrom; periodTo }>` — Deletes the batch's rows first (wipe-and-redo idempotence); first `beat(batchId,0,0)` is the CLAIM (UPDATE … WHERE status='processing' RETURNING; none → BatchGoneError); streams rows (xlsx via ExcelJS.stream.xlsx.WorkbookReader over storage.getStream, csv by line; only sheet 1's rows yielded but the reader RUNS OUT to release the /tmp spool; source errors raced per row with a re-armed gate); header found by name — a partial header row → CustomsImportError('missing_columns', labels); flush 1000-row chunks, falling back row-by-row and counting `rejected`; beat every 10 s; settle 'ready' (rowCount, skippedRows, periodFrom/To, error null, heartbeatAt) with the same status fence else BatchGoneError; 'missing_columns'/'no_rows' if no header / zero rows.
- **`failCustomsImport`** `export async function failCustomsImport(batchId: string, message: string): Promise<void>` — status 'failed', error = message.slice(0,500).
- **`sweepStuckImports`** `export async function sweepStuckImports(staleMs = IMPORT_STALE_MS, neverStartedMs = IMPORT_NEVER_STARTED_MS): Promise<number>` — UPDATE customs_import_batches SET status='failed', error='to‘xtab qoldi — faylni qayta yuklang' WHERE status='processing' AND ((heartbeat_at IS NOT NULL AND heartbeat_at < now-stale) OR (heartbeat_at IS NULL AND uploaded_at < now-neverStarted)) RETURNING id; logs count.
- **`listImportBatches / newestReadyBatchId / importBatchUsage / deleteImportBatch / batchCodeCount`** `listImportBatches(limit = 20); newestReadyBatchId(): Promise<string|null>; importBatchUsage(batchId): Promise<number>; deleteImportBatch(batchId): Promise<void>; batchCodeCount(batchId, tnvedCode): Promise<number>` — newestReadyBatchId = newest uploaded_at with status 'ready' (his 2b). deleteImportBatch: missing → no-op; status !== 'failed' && usage>0 → CustomsImportError('in_use'); else DELETE (rows CASCADE; calc_request_items.import_row_id SET NULL).

#### `src/modules/wms/customs/jobs.ts` — pg-boss worker + sweep for the import.

- **`JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT / JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT_SWEEP`** `'customs.import' | 'customs.import-sweep'` — Queue names.
- **`CustomsImportJob`** `export interface CustomsImportJob { batchId: string; storageKey: string; fileName: string }` — Job payload.
- **`importFailureText`** `export function importFailureText(err: CustomsImportError): string` — missing_columns → `Faylda kerakli ustun topilmadi (detail). Sarlavha qatorini tekshiring.`; no_rows → "Faylda o'qiladigan bironta qator yo'q — bo'sh varaqmi yoki boshqa fayl?"; unreadable → `Fayl o'qilmadi(detail). Excel'da qayta saqlab ko'ring.`; in_use → "Bu bazadan narx olingan — o'chirib bo'lmaydi." (unreachable from the parse).
- **`registerCustomsImportWorker`** `export async function registerCustomsImportWorker(boss: PgBoss): Promise<void>` — boss.work<CustomsImportJob>(JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT, { includeMetadata: true }, …): BatchGoneError → log + continue; CustomsImportError → failCustomsImport(importFailureText) + continue (never `return`); other errors → on the LAST attempt (`retryCount >= retryLimit`) failCustomsImport(`Xatolik: ${message}`), then rethrow for retry.
- **`registerCustomsImportSweep`** `export async function registerCustomsImportSweep(boss: PgBoss): Promise<void>` — schedule '*/5 * * * *' → sweepStuckImports().

#### `src/app/api/admin/customs-import/route.ts` — The admin's upload (route handler, #291).

- **`POST`** `export async function POST(request: Request): Promise<Response>` — authorize('admin.dictionaries.manage') (403 'forbidden'); formData 'file' → 400 'no_file' / 'empty', 413 'too_large' (MAX_BYTES 200 MB), 400 'not_xlsx' unless .csv or ZIP magic 50 4B 03 04; storage key `customs-import/<uuid>.xlsx|.csv`; INSERT customs_import_batches status 'processing'; audit entityType 'customs_import' action 'create'; `enqueue(JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT, {batchId, storageKey, fileName}, { expireInSeconds: 2*60*60, retryLimit: 2 })` — on failure failCustomsImport('navbatga qo‘shilmadi — qayta yuklang') and 500 'enqueue_failed'; else `{ ok: true, batchId }`.

#### `src/app/api/calc/import-baza/route.ts` — «Bu kod uchun importda nima bor?» — the picker behind the 📥 chip.

- **`GET`** `export async function GET(request: Request): Promise<Response>` — authorize('ved.docs') (403 'forbidden'); `?item=<uuid>` else 400 'bad_item'; item read with its group's dutyUnit; 404 'not_found'; no code → `{state:'no_code'}`; basis = defaultBasisFor({dutyUnit}); unit = UNIT_FOR_BASIS[basis]; perPiece only for basis 'unit'; suggestImportBaza(…, { picker: true }); no batch → `{state:'no_batch'}`; else `{state:'ok', candidates, batchId, total, itemName, tnvedCode, basis, source: 'periodFrom … periodTo' | fileName}` with cache-control private,no-store; isServerBehind → `{state:'behind'}`.

#### `src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/import-baza-dialog.tsx` — The picker dialog (portal Overlay, kept MOUNTED — #684).

- **`ImportBazaDialog`** `export function ImportBazaDialog({ target: PickerTarget | null; onClose: () => void; onPick: (itemId, row: { id; pricePerUnitUsd; basis: BazaBasis }) => void })` — Fetches /api/calc/import-baza?item=…; PickerAnswer.state 'ok'|'no_batch'|'no_code'|'behind'; testids calc-import-title/-search/-count/-failed/-nobatch/-empty/-nomatch/-candidate/-unitwarn/-name-more/-cancel; opened from items-table's ⋯ button `calc-import-pick` (items-table.tsx line 1592); a pick drafts bazaValue+basis+importRowId → posted as TableItemEdit.importRowId on save.
- **`ImportCandidate / PickerTarget`** `ImportCandidate { id; name; basis; pricePerUnitUsd; weightPerUnitKg; unit; declaredAt; sender; unitMatches }; PickerTarget { itemId; name; tnvedCode }` — Shapes.

#### `src/app/(protected)/admin/bojxona-import/page.tsx + upload-form.tsx + delete-button.tsx + actions.ts` — The admin screen (list of FACTS: file, period, rows, skipped, status, last beat, error).

- **`CustomsImportPage`** `default async function CustomsImportPage()` — redirect('/login') without actor; redirect('/') without 'admin.dictionaries.manage'; listImportBatches() with isServerBehind catch; AutoRefresh 5 s while any batch processing; testids customs-import, import-batch, import-ready, import-failed; delete button hidden on processing rows. Hub tile in admin/hub-doors.ts: href '/admin/bojxona-import', label 'customsImport.title', allow ['admin.dictionaries.manage'].
- **`ImportUploadForm`** `export function ImportUploadForm()` — POST /api/admin/customs-import via fetch; testids import-file (accept .xlsx,.csv), import-upload, import-queued, import-error; error text via `customsImport.errors.<code>` if present.
- **`deleteImportBatchAction`** `export async function deleteImportBatchAction(batchId: string): Promise<ImportActionState { ok?: boolean; error?: string }>` — authorize('admin.dictionaries.manage') → 'forbidden'; deleteImportBatch; CustomsImportError → error = reason ('in_use'); isServerBehind → 'server_behind'; audit entityType 'customs_import' action 'void'; revalidatePath('/admin/bojxona-import'). DeleteBatchButton (testid import-delete) confirms with t('deleteConfirm') and prints `customsImport.errors.<code>`.

#### `src/modules/platform/ai/model.ts` — The assistant's model adapter — shared config flag, tiers, timeout.

- **`aiConfigured`** `export function aiConfigured(): boolean` — Boolean(process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY). Used by aiPrefill's `configured` default and by the bot's fallback.
- **`ANALYST_MODEL / STAFF_MODEL`** `export const ANALYST_MODEL = 'claude-opus-5'; export const STAFF_MODEL = 'claude-sonnet-5'` — Assistant tiers only; the VED code paths hard-code 'claude-opus-5' themselves.
- **`realCallModel / CallModel / ModelRequest / ModelResponse`** `export const realCallModel: CallModel = async (req) => …` — CALL_TIMEOUT_MS 60_000, maxRetries 1. Not used by prefill/intake (they build their own Anthropic clients with the same 60 s / maxRetries 1 shape).

#### `src/modules/platform/ai/assistant.ts / sql-runner.ts / vet.ts / route-text.ts / tools.ts / schema-card.ts` — The staff AI assistant (round 100) — NOT part of the VED prefill, but it owns the daily cap and the gsr_ai_reader fence.

- **`askAssistant`** `export async function askAssistant(input: AskInput, deps: { callModel?: CallModel } = {}): Promise<AskOutcome>` — AskOutcome status 'ok'|'gave_up'|'not_configured'|'limit'|'error'; reserves a row in `ai_questions` under pg_advisory_xact_lock per user vs setting `ai_daily_limit` (40) on the Tashkent day — the ONLY AI cap in the system; MAX_ROUNDS 6, WALL_CLOCK_MS 120_000. The prefill/intake/pick calls are NOT counted here (spec §3: «no new cap in v1, but log token use»).
- **`runAnalystSql`** `export async function runAnalystSql(raw: string, opts: { timeoutMs?: number } = {}): Promise<AnalystSqlResult>` — vetAnalystSql (VetRefusal 'empty'|'too_long'|'multi_statement'|'comment'|'not_select'), BEGIN READ ONLY on ONE connection, SET LOCAL ROLE gsr_ai_reader (0080), LIMIT bound as ANALYST_ROW_CAP+1 (200); fails CLOSED if the role did not engage.
- **`codeCandidates`** `export function codeCandidates(text: string): string[]` — Code-shaped tokens tried by the free lookup before any paid model call.

#### `src/modules/platform/notifications/staff.ts + mutes.ts` — How the answer reaches Telegram.

- **`notifyStaffTelegram`** `export async function notifyStaffTelegram(input: { userIds: string[]; type: string; text: string; exceptUserId?: string|null; extra?: Record<string,unknown> }): Promise<number>` — Inserts one `notifications` row per active user (channel 'telegram', payload {text}), status 'pending' or 'muted' ('muted by user'); kicks enqueue(JOB_SEND_TELEGRAM). The drain (0082 claim, round 101 transient/permanent budget) sends it. 'CalcPrefilled' sits in MUTE_GROUPS.tasks beside CalcRequested/CalcTaken/CalcDone/CalcReturned; 'CalcDictReview' is used by review.ts (not listed in the mutes excerpt read).

#### `src/modules/platform/settings/service.ts` — SETTING_DEFAULTS.

- **`import_baza_min_sim`** `import_baza_min_sim: 0.45` — «the line between 'fill it and mark it 📥' and 'leave it empty and offer the list'»; read via getSetting on the POOL in suggestImportFills (pre-tx) and suggestImportBaza. Described in all four bundles under settings.
- **`ai_daily_limit / bhm_uzs / calc_review_notified_month`** `ai_daily_limit: 40; bhm_uzs: 412000; calc_review_notified_month: ''` — Assistant cap; BHM fee base; review claim.

#### `src/modules/platform/db/schema/wms.ts` — Drizzle tables (see dataModel).

- **`calcRequests / calcRequestItems / calcGroups / customsImportBatches / customsImportRows`** `pgTable definitions` — customsImportRows.id is bigint identity (`mode: 'bigint'`), calcRequestItems.importRowId bigint FK ON DELETE SET NULL.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/warnings.ts` — Group warning codes the ✅ records.

- **`WarningCode 'baza_from_import'`** `| 'baza_from_import'` — Pushed when `facts.items.some(i => i.bazaSource === 'import' && i.bazaUsd !== null)` — deliberately NO dictionary clause («it means nobody stated the price at all»). 'baza_off_dictionary' also fires for source 'import' when the value differs from the book.

### Flows


**«Request landed» → bot reply (the AI VED hodimi pass)**

1. Staff presses 🧮 Hisoblatish → section (c:yolkira/c:rastamojka/c:podklyuch) → client hint text → material (text/photos/documents; photos stored pre-bound to state.noteId, downscaled copies ≤6 kept in IntakeState.images) → «Bo‘ldi» (c:done) [src/modules/platform/telegram/staff-handlers.ts]
2. c:done → `void analyseIntakeAndReply` OFF grammy's sequential poller → staff-bot.ts `analyzeCollected`: intake-manual `parseManualFacts` + intake-ai `analyzeIntake` (claude-opus-5, 60 s) — typed facts WIN; reply = intake.ts `intakeSummaryText` (+ '(AI mavjud emas — faqat yozilganidan o‘qildi)' when aiUsed false) with confirmKeyboard
3. c:save → staff-bot.ts `landCollectedIntake` → intake-land.ts `landIntake`: dealFor/createLead, addActivity note, `openCalcRequest({… items: itemFacts(facts) …, source:'bot'})` [calc/service.ts] inserting calc_requests + calc_request_items (tnved_code prefilled from the TNVED memory), queued=true/requestId; moveToCalcStage (settings crm_calc_stage/deal_calc_stage)
4. staff-handlers.ts: `endIntake`; if target.queued && requestId → `queuePrefill(requestId, staff.id)`: prefill.ts `prefillTicket` reads calc_requests.rev; boss.ts `enqueue(JOB_CALC_PREFILL='calc.prefill', {requestId, staffId, rev})` (retryLimit 5, retryBackoff); reply '✅ Saqlandi … 🧮 Hisoblash navbatiga tushdi — VED xodimi javob beradi.'
5. calc/jobs.ts `registerCalcPrefillWorker` (app process only — WORKER_REGISTRATIONS 'calc-prefill'): `prefillStanding(requestId, job.data.rev)` — reads calc_requests.rev/completed_at + calc_groups.confirmed_at; anything but 'ok' → log '[calc-prefill] stood down' and skip
6. prefill.ts `aiPrefill(requestId, { actorId: null })`: reads calc_requests.section → pricing.ts `sectionParts` (customs = section !== 'yolkira'); if no customs half, steps 1-3 are skipped entirely
7. Step 1 (configured && customs): workspace.ts `proposeGroups` → claim UPDATE calc_requests.ai_proposal_started_at (else CalcError 'ai_running') → tnved/service.ts `proposeGoodsGrouping` per 200-item batch (claude-opus-5, json_schema groups) → grouping.ts `mergeProposals` → `applyProposal` (lock with ignoreAiClaim; refuses 'groups_confirmed'; DELETE+INSERT calc_groups with ai_proposed/ai_confidence/ai_duty_pct/ai_proposal, items.group_id) → `releaseAiClaim` → `priceProposedGroups`: `pullRatesFromDictionary` per coded group (calc_rates via ratesForCodes → setGroupRates source 'dictionary'; only 'rates_not_in_dictionary'/'code_required' swallowed) THEN `saveTable` with {id,seq,tnvedCode} edits (items' tnved_code = group code) → sweep/regroup + 0094 import auto-fill inside that save
8. Step 2 (customs): `saveTable(requestId, {items:[], adds:[]})` — the SWEEP: coded-but-ungrouped items placed in code groups with rates at mint; `suggestImportFills` (pool) → customs/import-baza.ts `suggestImportBaza` per distinct (code|units|name|perPiece) using `unitsForRow(rates.get(code).dutyUnit, hasWeight, hasQuantity)`, threshold getSetting('import_baza_min_sim'); applied in-tx step 7 only if still baza-less / same code / group's unitsForRow admits the basis → calc_request_items.baza_usd/baza_basis/baza_source='import'/import_row_id; touched groups unconfirmed; rev+1; ONE audit row
9. Step 3 (configured && customs): `pickBazas` → `newestReadyBatchId()` (customs_import_batches status 'ready' newest) — none → nothing; rows with baza_usd IS NULL and a code, first 40 (`MAX_PICK_ROWS`), each `suggestImportBaza(..., {batchId})` (≤10 candidates, unit mismatches included+labelled) → prefill-ai.ts `pickImportRows` (ONE Opus call, json_schema picks[{row,candidate|null,reason}], indexes re-checked) → basis fence (`BASIS_FOR_UNIT[chosen.unit] === BASIS_FOR_UNIT[unit]`, else `refused`) → log '[calc-prefill] the model chose' with reasons → re-read baza_usd, drop `overtaken` → `saveTable(requestId, {items: [{id, seq, bazaUsd: chosen.pricePerUnitUsd, bazaBasis: chosen.basis, importRowId: chosen.id}]})` → saveTable's `importRowForCode` re-reads price/basis from customs_import_rows (ready batch, same code) and stamps baza_source 'import'
10. Step 4: `loadWorkspace(requestId)` → customsUsd null unless parts.customs && groups.length>0; freightUsd = freight.ok ? freight.listUsd : null; prefill-reply.ts `prefillReplyText({..., blockers: ws.blockers, aiConfigured: configured, pickCapped/Refused/Overtaken})`
11. jobs.ts: `notifyStaffTelegram({ userIds: [staffId], type: 'CalcPrefilled', text })` → `notifications` row (pending/muted) + enqueue(JOB_SEND_TELEGRAM) → the drain sends to the seller's linked staff chat; log '[calc-prefill] pass finished' {codesStamped, picked, aiUsed}; any throw → '[calc-prefill] pass failed — the VED still has the job', not rethrown
12. The request stays in the VED queue (/hisoblash) with every group unconfirmed; the VED opens /hisoblash/[id], sees 📥 chips (testid calc-baza-import, `baza_from_import` warning), ✅ confirms (confirmGroup records confirmed_warnings/confirm_via) and seals; the official price is the seal or «Готово»

**Quarterly customs import lifecycle**

1. Admin at /admin/bojxona-import (gate admin.dictionaries.manage; hub tile label customsImport.title) picks a file (testid import-file) → upload-form.tsx fetch POST /api/admin/customs-import
2. route.ts: authorize → size/magic checks → getStorage().put('customs-import/<uuid>.xlsx|csv') → INSERT customs_import_batches (status 'processing', uploaded_by) → audit ('customs_import','create') → enqueue(JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT='customs.import', {batchId, storageKey, fileName}, {expireInSeconds: 7200, retryLimit: 2}); enqueue failure → failCustomsImport('navbatga qo‘shilmadi — qayta yuklang') + 500
3. customs/jobs.ts registerCustomsImportWorker (includeMetadata) → import-service.ts `runCustomsImport`: DELETE own customs_import_rows; first `beat(batchId,0,0)` claims (`WHERE status='processing' RETURNING`, else BatchGoneError); `streamRows` (storage.getStream → xlsxRows via ExcelJS.stream.xlsx.WorkbookReader {entries:'emit', sharedStrings:'cache', worksheets:'emit'} or csvRows; per-row race against a re-armed source-error gate); import-parse.ts `readHeader` by name (missing required → CustomsImportError 'missing_columns' naming FIELD_LABELS); `parseRow` per row (skipReasons bad_code/no_name/bad_price/unknown_unit); `flush` every 1000 (row-by-row fallback → 'rejected'); `priceDrift` sampled ≤100 and logged; `beat` every 10 s (returns false → BatchGoneError)
4. Settle: UPDATE status 'ready', row_count, skipped_rows, period_from/to (min/max declared_at), error null, heartbeat_at — same status fence; log '[customs-import] batch ready'. Refusal: failCustomsImport(importFailureText(err)) + continue; other error on last attempt: failCustomsImport('Xatolik: …') then rethrow
5. customs/jobs.ts registerCustomsImportSweep every '*/5 * * * *' → `sweepStuckImports()`: status 'processing' AND (heartbeat_at older than 15 min OR heartbeat_at IS NULL and uploaded_at older than 3 h) → 'failed' + 'to‘xtab qoldi — faylni qayta yuklang'
6. page.tsx lists batches (AutoRefresh 5 s while processing; chips import-ready/import-failed; lastBeat); DeleteBatchButton (import-delete, window.confirm) → deleteImportBatchAction → `deleteImportBatch`: failed → delete; ready with `importBatchUsage` > 0 → CustomsImportError('in_use') → 'customsImport.errors.in_use'; delete CASCADEs rows and SET NULLs calc_request_items.import_row_id
7. Every suggestion (saveTable auto-fill, pickBazas, GET /api/calc/import-baza) reads `newestReadyBatchId()` only; a processing batch never answers

**The VED's manual picker (📥 Importdan tanlash)**

1. items-table.tsx row ⋯ (calc-item-menu) → button calc-import-pick (line 1592) → ImportBazaDialog opens with PickerTarget {itemId, name, tnvedCode}
2. Dialog fetches GET /api/calc/import-baza?item=<uuid> (ved.docs) → suggestImportBaza(..., {picker:true}) → ≤50 candidates ordered unit-match, score, date; state ok/no_batch/no_code/behind; candidates list (calc-import-candidate, calc-import-unitwarn for mismatches)
3. Tap → onPick(itemId, {id, pricePerUnitUsd, basis}) drafts the baza pair + importRowId → calc-unsaved → «Saqlash» posts TableItemEdit.importRowId → saveTable `importRowForCode` (exists, same code, ready batch) or CalcError('import_row_missing', seq) → price/basis REPLACED from the file → baza_source 'import', import_row_id set → 📥 chip (calc-baza-import, t('calc.importGuess') 'из импорта (оценка)') → group warning baza_from_import; a hand-typed amount later posts source 'typed' and clears import_row_id

**The ✨ button on the workspace (same door the prefill uses)**

1. items-table.tsx calc-propose → action → workspace.ts proposeGroups (claim; refuses 'ai_running' if another pass holds the claim < 10 min; 'groups_confirmed' if any ✅ stands) → applyProposal → releaseAiClaim → priceProposedGroups (rates then codes via saveTable) → the same import auto-fill fires inside that save
2. Any concurrent saveTable/mutateRequest during the claim window throws CalcError('ai_running') ('ИИ уже работает'); a dead process's claim heals after AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS (10 min) on both the claim and the lock

### Data model

- **`calc_requests (src/modules/platform/db/schema/wms.ts ~line 1910)`** (migration 0085_calc_requests (queue), 0086_calc_pricing (ai_proposal_started_at), 0092_calc_measure (rev)): id uuid; entity_type/entity_id (deal|lead card); requested_by; assignee_id; item_count; section text (yolkira|rastamojka|podklyuch); from_city/to_city; weight_kg/volume_m3; source ('bot'|'card'|thread); note_id; requested_at; due_at; taken_at; completed_at (closed = sealed/finished); overdue_notified_at; supersedes_request_id; freight_zone; ai_proposal_started_at timestamptz (0086 line 285 — the AI CLAIM); has_certificate bool default true; rev integer NOT NULL default 0 (0092 — the revision clock); updated_at — CHECKs: lockRequestInTx refuses completed_at ('already_closed') and a live claim ('ai_running' within AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS); rev bumped by every mutator (calc-clock.test derives the list)
- **`calc_request_items`** (migration 0086, 0092, 0094): id uuid; request_id FK cascade; seq int; name; quantity numeric(12,3); unit; weight_kg numeric(12,3); volume_m3; amount/currency; tnved_code (prefilled from the TNVED memory at intake); note; group_id FK calc_groups ON DELETE SET NULL; baza_usd numeric(14,4); baza_basis ('unit'|'kg'|'juft'|'litr'|'m2'); baza_source ('dictionary'|'typed'|'import'|NULL — never 'ai'); measure_unit/measure_qty numeric(14,4) (pair, 0092); import_row_id bigint FK customs_import_rows(id) ON DELETE SET NULL (0094) — CHECKs: calc_items_baza_source_check: baza_source IS NULL OR IN ('dictionary','typed','import') (0094 DROP+ADD); measure pair CHECKs (0092); deliberately NO CHECK mentions import_row_id (#809)
- **`calc_groups`** (migration 0086, 0089 (confirm record), 0091 (duty shapes)): id; request_id; seq; label; tnved_code; duty_pct numeric(6,3); vat_pct; fee_usd; rate_source ('dictionary'|'typed'); duty_mode/duty_specific/duty_unit; has_certificate (nullable override); ai_proposed bool default false; ai_confidence text; ai_duty_pct numeric(6,3) (advisory, never multiplied); ai_proposal jsonb {tnvedCode, aiDutyPct, itemSeqs}; confirmed_warnings jsonb; confirm_via ('single'|'bulk'); confirmed_by; confirmed_at — CHECKs: calc_groups_rate_source_check IN ('dictionary','typed') — no 'import', no 'ai'; calc_groups_confirm_via_check; calc_groups_confirm_pair_check (confirm_via IS NULL OR confirmed_at IS NOT NULL)
- **`customs_import_batches (0094 + 0095)`** (migration src/modules/platform/db/migrations/0094_customs_import.sql, 0095_customs_import_heartbeat.sql): id uuid PK default gen_random_uuid(); file_name text NOT NULL; uploaded_by uuid FK users; uploaded_at timestamptz default now(); status text default 'processing'; row_count int default 0; skipped_rows int default 0; period_from date; period_to date; error text; heartbeat_at timestamptz (0095, nullable) — CHECKs: customs_import_batches_status_check: status IN ('processing','ready','failed'); partial index customs_import_batches_processing_idx ON (heartbeat_at) WHERE status='processing'
- **`customs_import_rows (0094)`** (migration 0094_customs_import.sql): id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PK; batch_id uuid FK batches ON DELETE CASCADE; tnved_code text NOT NULL; name text NOT NULL (≤500); name_norm text NOT NULL (lowercased, «1. » stripped, whitespace collapsed); unit text NOT NULL; price_per_unit_usd numeric(14,4) NOT NULL; weight_per_unit_kg numeric(12,4); netto_kg numeric(14,3); customs_value_usd numeric(14,2); declared_at date; sender text; origin_country text — CHECKs: customs_import_rows_unit_check IN ('kg','dona','m2','juft','litr'); customs_import_rows_price_check (> 0 AND <> 'NaN'); customs_import_rows_weight_check (NULL OR (> 0 AND <> 'NaN')); indexes customs_import_rows_batch_idx (batch_id), customs_import_rows_code_idx (batch_id, tnved_code), customs_import_rows_name_trgm_idx GIN (name_norm gin_trgm_ops)
- **`notifications (existing)`** (migration 0082_notify_claim): user_id; channel 'telegram'; type ('CalcPrefilled', 'CalcDictReview', …); payload jsonb {text}; status pending|sending|sent|failed|muted; claimed_at; error — CHECKs: 0082 claim (FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED) in the drain; muted rows = 'muted by user' / 'telegram not linked'
- **`settings`** (migration —): key; value jsonb — import_baza_min_sim (0.45), ai_daily_limit (40), bhm_uzs (412000), calc_review_notified_month ('') — CHECKs: SETTING_DEFAULTS in src/modules/platform/settings/service.ts; settings tripwire demands a description ×4 locales
- **`ai_questions (0080)`** (migration 0080_ai_assistant): id; user_id; surface ('bot'|'web'); question; answer; ok; tool_log jsonb; created_at — CHECKs: Daily cap for the ASSISTANT only (reserveQuestion under pg_advisory_xact_lock); prefill/intake/pick model calls are NOT ledgered here
- **`pgboss queues`** (migration pg-boss owned): calc.prefill (data {requestId, staffId, rev?}); customs.import (data {batchId, storageKey, fileName}); customs.import-sweep (cron */5); calc.overdue (cron */5); calc.dict-review (cron 0 6 * * *); notify.telegram — CHECKs: enqueue defaults retryLimit 5 + retryBackoff; customs.import overrides expireInSeconds 7200 / retryLimit 2

### Invariants (and the fence that enforces each)

- The model proposes WORDS/ROWS, never a number a customer pays: applyProposal writes no dutyPct/vatPct/feeUsd/bazaUsd/rateSource (fence: tests/unit/ai-advisory.test.ts 'a proposal lands nothing in a rate or baza column'); pricing.ts mentions no `ai*` identifier (same file); the seal row carries only aiGroupsSealed/aiBlindGroups/aiRateTakenGroups (same file); DB CHECKs calc_groups_rate_source_check ('dictionary','typed') and calc_items_baza_source_check ('dictionary','typed','import') — asserted WHOLE with no 'ai' (same file, reading 0086 and 0094 SQL).
- The pick answers with an INDEX into real declarations; its schema (zod + wire) carries only row/candidate/reason and no price/usd/amount/baza/summa; every `out.push` is {seq, candidate, reason} (ai-advisory.test 'the answer the model may give carries no amount at all'); indexes re-checked against the asked row in pickImportRows (code); the written edit carries `importRowId: chosen.id` and prefill.ts never mentions bazaSource/rateSource (ai-advisory.test 'the prefill writes no provenance of its own'); saveTable re-reads the price from the FILE via importRowForCode (customs-import.integration 'a picked row is read from the FILE, not from the browser'; 'refuses an import row that does not price this row’s code').
- A per-kg declaration may not price a per-dona row: pickBazas refuses when BASIS_FOR_UNIT differs (calc-prefill.integration 'a per-kg declaration may not price a per-dona row'); suggestImportBaza auto requires unitMatches (customs-import.integration 'never auto-fills across a UNIT'); the in-tx fill re-checks unitsForRow against the group's duty_unit (workspace.ts step 7).
- Auto-fill only above the threshold and with a usable (≥4 char) name; picker still lists with nameSim 0 (customs-import.integration 'a name too short to mean anything never auto-fills' / 'a SHORT product name still lists the code'); a typed baza is never second-guessed ('never touches a baza a person typed'); the pick never overwrites a baza a person typed while the model was thinking (calc-prefill.integration 'a baza the VED types WHILE the model thinks is left alone').
- The machine STOPS before money: every group stays unconfirmed (groups_unconfirmed blocker) and nothing is sealed (calc-prefill.integration 'the request STAYS in the queue — nothing is confirmed or sealed').
- A freight-only (yolkira) job gets no model call, no groups, no $0 (calc-prefill.integration 'a freight-only job is left ALONE'; sectionParts gate in aiPrefill).
- Never a $0, never a silence: customsUsd null when no groups or no customs half (calc-prefill.integration 'a customs job with NOTHING classified refuses in words'; prefill-reply.test 'never invents a zero', 'a yolkira job never leads with «rastamojka ~$0.00»'); every engine refusal union member has a sentence that does not contain the code (prefill-reply.test 'every refusal the engine can make has a sentence' — reads unions out of pricing.ts); capped/refused/overtaken picks are said out loud ('the pass names what it did NOT do'); a partial figure names the subset '(N ta tovarsiz)' ('a figure over PART of the cargo says so').
- The pass is OWNED by pg-boss, not a void promise: bot enqueues JOB_CALC_PREFILL with the rev; worker registered; answer via notifyStaffTelegram type 'CalcPrefilled' in MUTE_GROUPS; prefillStanding asked before aiPrefill; actorId null; failure not rethrown (tests/unit/calc-prefill-wire.test.ts, all six).
- The machine stands down once a person has been here: any saveTable moves rev → 'touched'; a confirmed group → 'confirmed' (calc-prefill.integration 'the machine stands down once a person has been here').
- The AI claim is released BEFORE the pricing tail and AFTER applyProposal; the tail writes only through pullRatesFromDictionary and saveTable (no .set/.insert), rates before codes (tests/unit/proposal-wire.test.ts); claim and lock heal on the same AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS (code comment #513).
- Three landing doors build items through itemFacts/loneWeightKg and never read `.goods ??` directly (tests/unit/intake-items-wire.test.ts — derived over every openCalcRequest caller: intake-land.ts, from-thread.ts, hisoblash/actions.ts); one measure per line, never both (tests/unit/intake-facts.test.ts 'a line needs ONE measure, never both').
- Import columns are found by HEADER NAME, never position; unknown units are skipped, never guessed; missing required headers are named in the file's own words (tests/unit/customs-import-parse.test.ts).
- 'failed' is terminal and 'ready' is what every suggestion reads: beat and settle both fence on status='processing' with RETURNING (tests/unit/customs-import-wire.test.ts 'the beat and the settle both fence on «still processing»'); BatchGoneError stands the parse down without a write or a retry (same file + customs-import.integration 'a parse whose batch was swept writes nothing more'); the sweep never touches a ready batch and asks TWO clocks (customs-import.integration sweep block; wire test 'the sweep asks TWO questions').
- A ready batch may be deleted only while nothing is priced off it — `in_use` otherwise — and losing a batch drops only the provenance (SET NULL), never the price (customs-import.integration 'a ready batch nobody has priced off may go…', 'losing the import leaves the PRICE and drops only the provenance').
- Nothing pooled inside a transaction (#714): the fill and the rates are computed on the POOL before saveTable's tx (tests/unit/tx-pool.test.ts derives the pooled set transitively).
- Every refusal the parser can raise has an admin-readable sentence not containing the code (customs-import-wire.test 'every refusal the parser can raise has a sentence — derived from the union'); the worker asks includeMetadata and writes failCustomsImport on the last attempt (same file).

- JOB_CALC_PREFILL = 'calc.prefill' — src/modules/wms/calc/jobs.ts
- JOB_CALC_OVERDUE = 'calc.overdue', JOB_CALC_REVIEW = 'calc.dict-review' — calc/jobs.ts
- JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT = 'customs.import', JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT_SWEEP = 'customs.import-sweep' — src/modules/wms/customs/jobs.ts
- WORKER_REGISTRATIONS names: 'calc-overdue','calc-review','calc-prefill','customs-import','customs-import-sweep' — src/modules/platform/jobs/boss.ts
- JOB_SEND_TELEGRAM = 'notify.telegram' (the drain) — boss.ts
- Notification types: 'CalcPrefilled' (MUTE_GROUPS.tasks), 'CalcDictReview' — src/modules/platform/notifications/mutes.ts, calc/jobs.ts, calc/review.ts
- PrefillStand: 'ok'|'touched'|'confirmed'|'closed'|'not_found' — calc/prefill.ts
- MAX_PICK_ROWS = 40 — calc/prefill.ts
- AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS = 10*60_000 — calc/workspace.ts
- AI_BATCH_SIZE = 200 — calc/grouping.ts
- CalcError codes touched here: 'ai_running','groups_confirmed','no_items','ai_failed','already_closed','not_found','code_required','rates_not_in_dictionary','import_row_missing','name_required','too_many_items','conflict','zone_unknown','unauthenticated' — calc/service.ts (class), calc/workspace.ts (throwers); sentences under `calc.errors.*` in messages/*.json (e.g. ai_running 'ИИ уже работает', import_row_missing 'Выбранная строка импорта не найдена…')
- TnvedError codes: 'ai_not_configured','ai_failed' — src/modules/wms/tnved/service.ts
- CustomsImportError.reason: 'missing_columns'|'no_rows'|'unreadable'|'in_use' — customs/import-service.ts; BatchGoneError
- SkipReason: 'bad_code'|'no_name'|'bad_price'|'unknown_unit'|'rejected' — customs/import-parse.ts

- Engine refusal unions: CustomsRefusal 'group_empty'|'baza_missing'|'measure_missing'|'rates_missing'|'not_a_number'; FreightRefusal 'zone_required'|'measure_missing'|'not_a_number'|BandRefusal('band_missing'|'band_ambiguous'); FeeRefusal 'fee_fx_missing'|'not_a_number'; TotalsRefusal 'discount_exceeds_total'|'not_a_number' — calc/pricing.ts lines 130/318/360/452/600
