### Gaps (not built / cut / owed / suspect)

- OWED (docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md §6, verbatim): «**The model's REASON for a pick is logged and not shown.** `pickImportRows` returns a one-line reason per row and nothing on any screen can display it: there is no column for it and sub-round B mints no migration. So a pick and the deterministic ≥0.45 auto-fill land identically (`baza_source='import'` + `import_row_id`, one 📥 chip, one `baza_from_import` warning), and the VED reviewing a number cannot tell which of the two put it there. Owed: a column and a chip that separate «the file matched this name» from «the model chose between five candidates», in a round that may mint one.» Evidence: prefill.ts lines 359-372 log `picks: [{seq, candidate, reason}]` under '[calc-prefill] the model chose' and nothing else reads it.
- OWED (§6): «A pass killed half-way is NOT retried — its own first half has moved the rev clock, and `prefillStanding` then reads `touched`. Deliberate … and logged, never silent.» (prefill.ts lines 53-57).
- CUT (§6): «Prefix fallback for prices: CUT in v1 (exact code only)»; «Sub-round A's «bu kod avvalgi chorakda qancha edi» (the per-code history across quarters) was designed and is NOT built — owed to him as a «yo'q» or a later round»; «Auto-fill threshold 0.45 is a first guess — tune on the real file and record the measurement»; «The full 500k file is NOT yet delivered» (CLAUDE.md: «After the 0094 deploy he owes the FULL quarterly customs file»).
- ONLY THE BOT DOOR ENQUEUES THE PREFILL: `queuePrefill` is called solely from staff-handlers.ts's c:save branch (line 649). The other two landing doors — `src/modules/wms/calc/from-thread.ts` (suhbatdan hisoblatish) and `src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/actions.ts` (the seller's card form) — call openCalcRequest but never enqueue JOB_CALC_PREFILL (grep: no 'prefill' in either). Spec §3 says «After landing (the request already lands in the queue via intake-land): run aiPrefill(requestId)» — as built, a card-form or thread-landed job gets no machine pass unless somebody presses ✨ on the workspace.
- NO DAILY CAP on the prefill/intake/pick model calls: `ai_daily_limit`/`ai_questions` gate only `askAssistant`; spec §3 «no new cap in v1, but log token use» — token use is logged ('[calc-prefill pick] model tokens', '[calc intake] model tokens'), but `proposeGoodsGrouping` in tnved/service.ts logs NO token usage (no logger.info of response.usage there).
- The pick is offered ≤10 candidates (CANDIDATE_LIMIT, non-picker suggestImportBaza), not «TOP-5» as §3 wrote; the listing sent to the model is sliced at 40_000 chars with no per-row accounting, so on a 40-row × 10-candidate × 200-char request the tail rows can be silently cut from the prompt (prefill-ai.ts line 123) — the reply's `capped` count only knows about rows past MAX_PICK_ROWS, not rows lost to the slice.
- `pickBazas` issues one `suggestImportBaza` query per empty row (up to 40 trigram scans per pass) — acceptable per the code, but note it is NOT the memoised `suggestImportFills` path (#432's discipline is applied only in saveTable).
- `priceProposedGroups`'s `pullRatesFromDictionary` runs `setGroupRates` per group (one tx + rev bump each) rather than one batched write — N model-proposed groups = N locks; not a defect, but the rev moves N times and any concurrent VED save in that window is refused/interleaved.
- The intake collection is in-memory (calc-intake.ts TTL 30 min): a bot restart mid-collection loses typed text — stated in code and CLAUDE.md.
- `proposeGoodsGrouping` refuses > 200 goods per call; `planBatches` slices at AI_BATCH_SIZE 200 so requests up to MAX_CALC_ITEMS 1000 are 5 calls; a batch that fails costs only its goods (code comment) but `failed === batches.length` throws 'ai_failed' (all-fail only).
- The customs worker never validates `unreadable` from the parser: CustomsImportError('unreadable') is in the union and has a sentence, but nothing in import-service.ts raises it (grep: only the constructor union and importFailureText mention it) — a corrupt zip surfaces via the stream-error race as a generic `Xatolik: …` on the last attempt instead.
- `customs_import_batches.uploaded_by` FK has no ON DELETE rule (0094 line 14) — deactivation is the house rule, so fine, but a hard user delete would be refused by this FK.
- m9zu leaves the created lead behind if the funnel card is not found (`if ((await card.count()) === 0) return;`, line 138) — best-effort cleanup for that half; the batch deletion itself is asserted.
- docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md line 3 still reads «**The agreed spec, NOT yet built.**» while §2 and §3 carry SHIPPED banners — the header is stale.

### Docs-vs-code disagreements

- docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md line 3: «The agreed spec, NOT yet built.» vs §2 (line 102: «SHIPPED 2026-09-04 (DECISIONS #889-895)») and §3 (line 312: «SHIPPED 2026-09-04») and CLAUDE.md («sub-rounds A AND B SHIPPED») — the doc's own header is stale.
- docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md §2.3 heading names the service `src/modules/wms/calc/import-baza.ts`; the code lives at `src/modules/wms/customs/import-baza.ts` (the §2 SHIPPED banner itself records the move).
- §2.3 says «Rank in SQL: `similarity(name_norm, …)` … LIMIT 10 by score»; code uses `word_similarity(${needle}, r.name_norm)` (import-baza.ts line 208) with CANDIDATE_LIMIT 10 for auto and PICKER_LIMIT 50 for the picker (lines 106/117), and MIN_NEEDLE 4.
- §2.2 says «cap ~150 MB»; route.ts line 22 `MAX_BYTES = 200 * 1024 * 1024`.
- §2.2 says «Update `row_count` every ~20k so the screen's AutoRefresh shows progress» and «delete button for failed batches only»; code beats on a WALL CLOCK every 10 s (import-service.ts HEARTBEAT_MS line 38, #916) and `deleteImportBatch` (line 620) allows deleting a READY batch while `importBatchUsage` is 0 (refuses `in_use` otherwise); page.tsx line 167 shows the button on every non-processing row.
- §2.4 names the warning kind `baza_import`; code is `baza_from_import` (warnings.ts line 35).
- §3 says bazas «give the model the TOP-5 rows»; code offers `sug.candidates` from the non-picker path = up to CANDIDATE_LIMIT 10 (prefill.ts line 305-316, import-baza.ts line 106).
- §3 says codes come from «TNVED memory first (existing), then the model's suggestTnved for the rest»; the shipped pass uses `proposeGroups` → `proposeGoodsGrouping` (whole-list grouping, tnved/service.ts line 192), not per-item `suggestTnved` (prefill.ts line 119/156).
- §3 says «After landing … run aiPrefill(requestId)» as if every landing triggers it; only the staff bot's c:save enqueues JOB_CALC_PREFILL (staff-handlers.ts line 649); from-thread.ts and hisoblash/actions.ts do not.
- CLAUDE.md «the import alone takes 2 hours / 2 attempts»; route.ts line 81 passes `retryLimit: 2`, and pg-boss counts retries AFTER the first attempt (boss.ts EnqueueOptions comment «Retries after the first attempt»), and the route's own comment says «three attempts heal a storage blip» — so it is 3 attempts, not 2.
- CLAUDE.md says `enqueue` «re-delivers it up to five times» for the prefill — true for JOB_CALC_PREFILL (boss default retryLimit 5), but the prefill worker never rethrows (jobs.ts line 129-131), so pg-boss re-delivery only happens when the process dies mid-job (expiry), not on a caught failure — consistent with the code comment, worth stating precisely.
- §2.1 says indexes «(tnved_code) btree»; 0094 line 63 creates the composite `customs_import_rows_code_idx` ON (batch_id, tnved_code) instead (the SQL comment explains why).
- CLAUDE.md (round 100 paragraph) says «Tier 1 runs claude-sonnet-5, tier 2 claude-opus-5» for the assistant — matches model.ts; but the VED intake/pick/grouping calls all hard-code 'claude-opus-5' in their own files and do not use platform/ai/model.ts at all (prefill-ai.ts line 95, intake-ai.ts line 104, tnved/service.ts line 129/208).


## money-after-seal

### Summary
This subsystem is everything downstream of the seal in the VED calc module. `sealCalc` (src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts:1398) is the one door between a DRAFT (recomputed from dictionaries every render) and a FACT: it inserts an immutable `calc_versions` row, closes the `calc_requests` row (`completed_via='sealed'`, `current_version_no+1`, `rev+1` under `lockRequestInTx`), writes the FLOOR onto the card (`leads/deals.quoted_amount`, LOCKED by `quoteLockedFor`), re-points `receipts.calc_request_id` from a superseded request, and announces `CalcSealed` to the requester plus `CalcDiscounted` to the owner/accountant/seller on any concession. A seller then makes an OFFER (`recordOffer`) anchored on EXACTLY ONE of a version or a completed request's Готово answer (`calc_offers_one_anchor_check`); the client price is theirs, the text (`offerLines`/`offerText`, one shape for Telegram and PDF, no emoji) never prints or decomposes the sealed total; below the floor is recorded but the PROMISE waits on `approved_at` (law 4, admin-only = `mayApproveBelowFloor` = `finance.reports`); above a DISCOUNTED floor is refused (`discounted_no_upsale`); a released offer writes the CLIENT price onto the card and the lock follows it (later-writer-wins by clock). The upsale is DERIVED, never stored: `payableOffersSql()` (upsale.ts:198) is the single home for five rules (one payable per request via row_number, current version, not superseded, released, eligible+positive), embedded by `upsaleRows`, `upsaleLiability`, `payUpsale`'s own claim; payout is an `expenses` row in the mandatory `upsale_expense_category_id` category with a server-derived amount, all four `payout_*` columns set in one UPDATE, and `voidExpense` re-opens the offers. Which standing clauses apply is written once in version-set.ts (`currentVersionSql`, `notSupersededSql`, `answerFloorStandsSql`, `measurableRequestSql`) and composed by `offerStandsSql`/`releasedOfferWhere` for the lock, the claim, the cash screen and the PDF routes. Who sees what is three different predicates over the same role matrix: `upsaleScopeFor` (finance.reports→all; crm.leads|clients.manage→own; else none — the VED is NONE), `calcControlScopeFor` (finance.reports→all; ved.docs→own; else none — sellers NONE), `mayReadCalcRegistry` (finance.reports||ved.docs), all fenced by per-role enumeration tests; round 91's `seesAllMoney` is deliberately NOT used because `ved_manager` holds `finance.manage`. The cargo join is at the RECEIPT grain (`receipts.calc_request_id`, 0089): `stampCalcLink` is the only writer (called from `confirmReceipt` and `linkReceipt`), an `auto` link is a suggestion, only a person's confirmation (`calc_link_confirmed_at`, `measurableLinkSql`) is scored, and `calcActuals` compares CUSTOMS ONLY (freight gets a band check) with nine named refusals and two clocks. A correction is a NEW request (`recalcFromSealed`, gated `admin.settings.manage`); «V2» is DERIVED by `chain.ts` as rank among sealed versions of the `supersedes_request_id` chain, and `/hisoblash/tarix` lists one row per sealed version. The price book (`calc_price_book`, keyed on TNVED code, fx_rates-style no-fallback read) and `quoteHistoryFor`/`lastQuotesByCode` give law 10's history in two views (VED: cost side, no client price; seller: prices, no floor). Won leads move every `calc_*` row carrying (entity_type, entity_id) onto the deal (`rekeyLeadCalcRequests`), fenced by a schema-derived test.


### Files and exports


#### `src/modules/wms/calc/version-set.ts` — The four 'which calculation stands' SQL clauses, written once (#513) and embedded by upsale.ts, workspace.ts (offerStandsSql, recordOffer's answer branch, lastAnswerAnchorFor) and actuals.ts.

- **`currentVersionSql`** `currentVersionSql(): SQL` — Predicate over alias `v` (calc_versions): `v.version_no = (SELECT max(v2.version_no) FROM calc_versions v2 WHERE v2.request_id = v.request_id)`. 'A corrected job must not be measured on the figure it was corrected away from.' Consumers: payableOffersSql, offerStandsSql; actuals.ts restates it privately as currentVersion().
- **`notSupersededSql`** `notSupersededSql(): SQL` — Predicate over alias `r` (calc_requests): NOT EXISTS a request whose supersedes_request_id = r.id. 'Nobody has superseded this request — a correction is a NEW request.' Consumers: payableOffersSql, offerStandsSql, answerFloorStandsSql, stampCalcLink restates it inline.
- **`answerFloorStandsSql`** `answerFloorStandsSql(): SQL` — Predicate over alias `r`: completed_at NOT NULL AND answer_amount NOT NULL AND > 0 AND answer_currency='USD' AND notSupersededSql AND no NEWER completed USD answer on the same (entity_type, entity_id) AND no calc_versions sealed on the same card after r.completed_at. 'Deliberately NO expiry clause: like the version anchor, the clock gates the DOOR (recordOffer refuses `answer_expired`), never a promise already made.' Consumers: payableOffersSql answer branch, offerStandsSql answer branch, recordOffer answer branch, lastAnswerAnchorFor.
- **`measurableRequestSql`** `measurableRequestSql(): SQL` — NOT EXISTS a child request that ALSO has a calc_versions row. Deliberately not notSupersededSql: 'the money side must stop paying on the old promise at once … The measurement side must NOT drop the shipment' — an abandoned (unsealed) correction still lets the old version be measured. Consumers: actuals.ts calcActuals `want` CTE + main WHERE, calcCoverage.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/upsale.ts` — The upsale arithmetic (pure) and THE payable predicate (SQL fragment).

- **`MONEY_EPSILON`** `const MONEY_EPSILON = 0.009` — Money never compares exactly (numeric(14,2) vs a float); below this there is no upsale and no below-floor either. Used by payableOffersSql, upsaleRows state ladder, recordOffer's belowFloor test (literal 0.009 there).
- **`upsaleEligible`** `upsaleEligible(facts: { discountUsd: number; density: number | null; bandOverrideMin: number | null }): boolean` — Law 4: ANY concession kills it. false if discountUsd non-finite or > MONEY_EPSILON; true if no band override; false (fails closed) if override with null/non-finite density; else `bandOverrideMin >= density - 0.0001` (an override BELOW real density = cheaper band = concession; above = VED charging more = fine). Code comment: '`freight_usd <> freight_list_usd` is NOT one of them, and testing it is a trap in both directions' (seal writes both from one value; rastamojka zeroes freight).
- **`upsaleOf`** `upsaleOf(input: { clientPriceUsd: number; totalUsd: number }): number | null` — round2(client − total) if > MONEY_EPSILON else null; null on non-finite input.
- **`payableOffersSql`** `payableOffersSql(): SQL` — CTE `ranked` over calc_offers o LEFT JOIN calc_versions v ON v.id=o.version_id JOIN calc_requests r ON r.id=COALESCE(v.request_id,o.request_id), WHERE (o.version_id NOT NULL AND currentVersionSql AND notSupersededSql) OR (o.version_id NULL AND answerFloorStandsSql); total_usd = COALESCE(v.total_usd, r.answer_amount); rn = row_number() PARTITION BY COALESCE(v.request_id,o.request_id) ORDER BY o.offered_at DESC, o.id DESC. Outer: rn=1 AND (NOT below_floor OR approved_at NOT NULL) AND (version_id NULL OR discount_usd <= 0.009) AND (version_id NULL OR NOT (band_override_min NOT NULL AND (density NULL OR band_override_min < density-0.0001))) AND client_price_usd - total_usd > 0.009; emits `upsale_usd = round(client_price_usd - total_usd, 2)` plus id, version_id, offered_by, offered_at, client_price_usd, below_floor, approved_at, payout_expense_id, payout_at, payout_usd, request_id, section, entity_type, entity_id. 'Every version-only clause is GUARDED by `o.version_id IS NOT NULL` — unguarded, each evaluates NULL on a request-anchored row and silently drops it.' Consumers: upsaleRows, payUpsale (quote read + claim UPDATE FROM).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/upsale-scope.ts` — Law 4 + law 10 resolved as three predicates over the permission set; no new permission code (#170).

- **`upsaleScopeFor`** `upsaleScopeFor(actor: { permissions: { has(code: string): boolean } }): 'all' | 'own' | 'none'` — 'finance.reports' → 'all' (owner, admin, accountant); else 'crm.leads' || 'clients.manage' → 'own' (sales_manager, logist); else 'none' (ved_manager, warehouse_*, viewer). Comment: '`finance.reports` and NOT round 91's `seesAllMoney`: that predicate is `finance.manage || clients.manage`, and **`ved_manager` holds `finance.manage`**'. Consumers: /upsale page+actions, calc-panel, narxlar page, both PDF routes, finance/[clientId] (bothFigures gate), quoteHistoryFor (required arg).
- **`mayOffer`** `mayOffer(actor): boolean` — upsaleScopeFor(actor) !== 'none'. Asked by makeOfferAction on top of canWriteDeal, because DEAL_WRITE_PERMISSIONS carries ved.docs.
- **`mayApproveBelowFloor`** `mayApproveBelowFloor(actor): boolean` — actor.permissions.has('finance.reports'). 'and not `finance.debt_override` alone: that grant is held by the logist, the warehouse manager and every sales manager'. Consumers: makeOfferAction (handed down as `mayApprove`), releaseOfferAction, /upsale page (pending list), calc-panel.
- **`approverIds`** `approverIds(): Promise<string[]>` — usersWithPermission('finance.reports') via dynamic import of notifications/service; used by notifyApprovers in workspace.ts (CalcBelowFloor).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/control-scope.ts` — The THIRD door (phase E1 control screen) and the registry's boolean door.

- **`calcControlScopeFor`** `calcControlScopeFor(actor): 'all' | 'own' | 'none'` — 'finance.reports' → 'all'; 'ved.docs' → 'own' (the calculations they sealed); else 'none' (sellers included — law 10: 'sellers read prices, never the cost breakdown'). Consumers: nazorat page + actions run(), receipts/[id]/page.tsx `mayLinkCalc`, link.ts assertMine takes the resolved scope.
- **`mayReadCalcRegistry`** `mayReadCalcRegistry(actor): boolean` — has('finance.reports') || has('ved.docs'). Owner's answer 2A: himself, accountant, VED — not sellers ('every figure on that screen is a FLOOR'). A boolean, not the control scope, because 'own' there means the calculations you sealed and a history must answer about the company's. Consumers: tarix page, narxlar header link, calc-panel (`readsHistory` decides whether previous version's money prints).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/upsale-service.ts` — Reading, allowing and paying the upsale; every read embeds payableOffersSql().

- **`upsaleRows`** `upsaleRows(scope: UpsaleScope, actorId: string, opts?: { from?: string; to?: string; sellerId?: string }): Promise<{ rows: UpsaleRow[]; truncated: boolean }>` — scope 'none' → empty without querying. 'own' → p.offered_by = actorId; 'all' + opts.sellerId → that seller. from/to inclusive to end of day. Joins users, deals (entity_type='deal'), clients, LATERAL sum of non-void 'charge' client_transactions on the deal. LIMIT UPSALE_CAP+1 (300). Second query `balancesForClients`. State ladder: payout_at → 'paid'; entity_type≠'deal' → 'no_deal'; charged < clientPrice−ε → 'no_invoice'; balance−deferred > ε → 'awaiting_payment'; else 'payable'. UpsaleRow carries floorUsd even under 'own' (the page hides it).
- **`bySeller`** `bySeller(rows: UpsaleRow[]): { sellerId; sellerName; jobs; earnedUsd; paidUsd; waitingUsd }[]` — Pure grouped totals, sorted by earnedUsd desc. Rendered only under scope 'all'.
- **`upsaleLiability`** `upsaleLiability(): Promise<{ payableUsd: number; accruedUsd: number }>` — upsaleRows('all','',{}); payable = state 'payable' only ('a real liability against real money'); accrued = everything else unpaid, kept OFF the balance sheet.
- **`payUpsale`** `payUpsale(offerIds: string[], input: { accountId: string; currency: string; expenseDate: string; note?: string }, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<{ expenseId: string; paidUsd: number; count: number }>` — Refusals in order: 'unauthenticated', 'no_offers', 'upsale_category_unset' (setting empty), 'fx_missing' (rateFor null), 'not_found' (account), 'account_currency_mismatch', 'offer_not_payable' (payable rows ≠ ids), 'one_seller_at_a_time', 'nothing_to_pay'. Amount = Σ upsale_usd / rate rounded 2. All reads on the pool BEFORE db.transaction (#714). In tx: addExpenseTx(categoryId, amount, currency, expenseDate, accountId, employeeId, note default `Upsale · N`), then the CLAIM = one UPDATE calc_offers SET payout_expense_id, payout_at=now(), payout_by, payout_usd=p.upsale_usd FROM (payableOffersSql()) p WHERE o.id=p.id AND o.id IN ids AND o.payout_expense_id IS NULL RETURNING; 'offer_already_paid' if fewer rows, 'amount_moved' if Σ differs → whole tx rolls back. Audit entityType 'expense', action 'update'.
- **`reopenUpsaleForExpense`** `reopenUpsaleForExpense(expenseId: string): Promise<number>` — Nulls all four payout columns where payout_expense_id = expenseId (#528 pair rule). Called best-effort from accounting/service.ts voidExpense (line 260-261).
- **`pendingBelowFloor`** `pendingBelowFloor(): Promise<calcOffers row[]>` — below_floor AND approved_at IS NULL, ordered by offered_at. Rendered on /upsale only for mayApproveBelowFloor.
- **`setUpsaleCategory`** `setUpsaleCategory(categoryId: string, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<void>` — Non-empty id re-checked against expense_categories ACTIVE only → 'category_not_found'; empty = 'nobody has chosen'. Writes setting `upsale_expense_category_id` and audits under settings entity SETTINGS_AUDIT_ID.
- **`bothFiguresForDeals`** `bothFiguresForDeals(dealIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, { floorUsd: number; clientPriceUsd: number }>>` — Newest standing RELEASED offer per deal (releasedOfferWhere + offerStandsSql, ordered asc, last write wins); floor = COALESCE(version total_usd, request answer_amount). CALLER gates on upsaleScopeFor==='all' (finance/[clientId]/page.tsx line ~48).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts` — The seal, the correction, the offer record/release, the card-price and lock readers (lines 1353-2035 and 3268-3820 are this subsystem).

- **`QUOTE_VALID_DAYS_DEFAULT`** `const QUOTE_VALID_DAYS_DEFAULT = 30` — Fallback when setting `quote_valid_days` is null; the owner's 'bir oy'.
- **`sealCalc`** `sealCalc(requestId: string, input: { discountUsd: number; discountReason: string | null; bandOverrideMin: number | null; bandOverrideReason: string | null }, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<{ versionNo: number; totalUsd: number }>` — Refusals: 'bad_number', 'amount_range' (discount<0), 'discount_reason_required', 'band_reason_required', 'not_found', 'already_closed', 'not_ready' (!canSeal), 'section_required', 'freight_zone_required', 'freight_missing', 'conflict' (rev moved). Loads workspace WITH the override+discount (so an override can rescue an uncovered density). Computes validUntil = now + quote_valid_days. Builds `breakdown` jsonb (groups incl. dutyMode/dutySpecific/dutyUnit/excisePct/hasCertificate/rateSource/aiProposed/aiConfidence/items with volumeM3/bazaUsd/bazaBasis/bazaSource/measureUnit/measureQty; extras; tariffRow; density; hasCertificate; fee). Counters via sealCounters (warned_groups, ai_blind_groups, ai_rate_taken_groups) + ai_groups_sealed + low_confidence_sealed (aiProposed && 'low'). In tx: lockRequestInTx CAS on rev; UPDATE calc_requests SET completed_at, completed_by, completed_via='sealed', current_version_no+1, rev+1 WHERE completed_at IS NULL RETURNING → 'already_closed' if none; INSERT calc_versions; if supersedes: UPDATE receipts SET calc_request_id=requestId WHERE calc_request_id=superseded (a correction ADOPTS cargo at SEAL time); write floor onto card: leads.quoted_amount/currency='USD'/volume/weight, or deals + quoted_at/quoted_by. After: audit calc_request 'update' {sealed, totalUsd, discountUsd, bandOverrideMin}; announceSeal → notifyStaffTelegram type 'CalcSealed' to requestedBy; on discount>0 or band override → type 'CalcDiscounted' to discountAudience = usersWithRoles(['super_admin','admin']) ∪ accountant ∪ requestedBy ∪ superseded request's requestedBy. Does NOT call stampCalcLink.
- **`canSeal`** `canSeal(workspace: { blockers: unknown[]; totals: { ok: boolean } | null }): boolean` — blockers.length===0 && totals.ok. ONE predicate for three doors: sealCalc ('not_ready'), the seal button, finishCalcRequest ('seal_instead').
- **`currentVersion`** `currentVersion(requestId: string): Promise<SealedVersion | null>` — Newest version_no of one request.
- **`currentSealFor`** `currentSealFor(entityType: 'deal' | 'lead', entityId: string): Promise<SealedVersion | null>` — Newest calc_versions by sealed_at across ALL requests of the card. 'The card's price is the VERSION, not `answer_*`'. SealedVersion.expired = valid_until < now() decided at READ time. Consumers: quoteLockedFor (crm/service.ts:362), calc-panel.
- **`recalcFromSealed`** `recalcFromSealed(requestId: string, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<string /* new request id */>` — Refusals: 'not_found', 'not_closed' (no completed_at), 'not_sealed' (no version — 'a handed-back request was a legal parent by URL'), 'recalc_open' (child exists with no completed_at), 'recalc_superseded' (child completed) — 'two children off one parent both stand … payableOffersSql pays the seller's commission on BOTH'. In tx inserts new calc_requests (same entity, requestedBy=ctx.actorId, assignee, section, cities, kg/m3, freightZone, hasCertificate, feeOverrideUsd, supersedes_request_id=old.id, due_at=now+2h), copies calc_groups (all law columns + aiProposal, NOT confirmed_*), calc_request_items (incl. importRowId, measureUnit/Qty), calc_extras. Audit calc_request 'create' {supersedes}. Action gate: admin.settings.manage.
- **`recordOffer`** `recordOffer(anchor: { versionId: string } | { requestId: string }, input: { clientPriceUsd: number; locale: 'uz'|'ru'|'en'; clientName?: string|null; belowFloorReason?: string|null; mayApprove?: boolean; expect?: { entityType: 'deal'|'lead'; entityId: string } }, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<{ id; text: string|null; belowFloor; delivered; pending }>` — 'bad_number' (mustBeNumber), 'price_positive', 'unauthenticated'. Version branch: 'not_found' (missing or expect mismatch); floor = v.total_usd; 'discounted_no_upsale' if v.discount_usd > 0 AND price > floor+0.009 (round 112); text = offerText from version's volume/weight/section/validUntil + request cities. Answer branch: plain read (NOT lockRequestInTx); 'not_found'; 'answer_missing' (completed_at null or amount null/≤0); 'answer_not_usd'; 'superseded' (answerFloorStandsSql false); 'answer_expired' (completed_at + quote_valid_days < now); floor = answer_amount; section coalesced to 'podklyuch'. belowFloor = price < floor − 0.009; 'below_floor_reason_required'; approved = belowFloor ? Boolean(mayApprove) : true. INSERT calc_offers (version_id XOR request_id, entity from the request, client_price_usd toFixed(2), below_floor, below_floor_reason, approved_at/by only when approved&&belowFloor, locale, text, offered_by). Audit 'calc_offer' create. If NOT approved: notifyApprovers (type 'CalcBelowFloor' to approverIds(), exceptUserId actor, text «⚠️ Tannarxdan past narx ruxsat kutmoqda …») and return {text:null, pending:true, delivered:false}. Else applyOfferToCard (leads/deals.quoted_amount=client price, quoted_currency='USD', updated_at) and push type 'CalcOffer' to the seller only if hasLinkedChat (telegram_links status 'linked'); delivered reflects that honestly.
- **`offersFor`** `offersFor(entityType, entityId): Promise<calcOffers row[]>` — Every offer on the card, newest first, LIMIT 10, both anchors. Panel filters to own unless scope 'all'.
- **`releaseOffer`** `releaseOffer(offerId: string, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<OfferResult>` — Claim IS the UPDATE: SET approved_at=now(), approved_by WHERE id AND below_floor AND approved_at IS NULL AND offerStandsSql() RETURNING. No row → 'superseded' if still pending (correction replaced it) else 'not_pending'. Then resolves request from the offer's OWN anchor, applyOfferToCard, audit 'calc_offer' update {approved:true}, push 'CalcOffer' to offered_by if linked.
- **`releasedOfferWhere`** `releasedOfferWhere(): SQL` — `(NOT calc_offers.below_floor OR calc_offers.approved_at IS NOT NULL)` — the promise lock's one home for offer-shaped reads (releasedPriceFor, bothFiguresForDeals, both PDF routes).
- **`offerStandsSql`** `offerStandsSql(): SQL` — (version_id NOT NULL AND EXISTS version with currentVersionSql AND notSupersededSql) OR (request_id NOT NULL AND EXISTS request with answerFloorStandsSql). Consumers (three): releaseOffer's claim, releasedPriceFor, bothFiguresForDeals. NOT used by the PDF routes ('A SUPERSEDED offer's sheet deliberately still prints').
- **`releasedPriceFor`** `releasedPriceFor(entityType, entityId): Promise<{ price: number; at: Date } | null>` — Newest released+standing offer on the card; `at` = approved_at ?? offered_at (the moment the price reached the CARD). Consumer: quoteLockedFor.
- **`lastAnswerAnchorFor`** `lastAnswerAnchorFor(entityType, entityId): Promise<{ requestId; amountUsd: number|null; currency: string|null; completedAt: Date; stands: boolean; expired: boolean } | null>` — Newest completed request with answer_amount; stands = answerFloorStandsSql; expired = completed_at + quote_valid_days < now. Advisory only (panel decides which door to draw); recordOffer re-derives.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/offer.ts` — The client-facing offer TEXT, pure. Rule 1: seller's price not sealed; rule 2: never decompose; rule 3: no emoji (NotoSansSC glyph 0).

- **`offerLines`** `offerLines(input: OfferInput, locale: ClientLocale): { title: string; rows: { label: string; value: string }[]; footer: string }` — Rows: route (if any city), volume (`n m3`, only >0), weight (`n kg`), includes (offerIncludes by section: yolkira→offerSecFreight, rastamojka→offerSecCustoms, else offerSecAll), total (offerMoney), per m3 / per kg DERIVED FROM CLIENT PRICE, valid until (offerDate in Asia/Tashkent). Labels from clientLabels(locale) (platform/telegram/client-labels.ts). ONE shape rendered twice (Telegram text + PDF).
- **`offerText`** `offerText(input: OfferInput, locale: ClientLocale): string` — `${clientName}\n\n${title}` (name optional), then `label: value` lines, then footer.
- **`offerLocaleFor`** `offerLocaleFor(clientLocale: string | null | undefined): 'uz' | 'ru' | 'en'` — Returns the value if one of the three, else 'uz' (clients.locale is NULL for every client; the seller chooses).
- **`OFFICE_TIME_ZONE / offerDate / offerMoney`** `OFFICE_TIME_ZONE='Asia/Tashkent'; offerDate(at: Date, timeZone?): string 'dd.MM.yyyy'; offerMoney(usd: number): string '$1 234.56'` — Date formatted in the office zone so a 19:00 seal is not one day short; money with thin-space thousands.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/offer-pdf.ts` — The A4 offer sheet (pdf-lib + subsetted NotoSansSC).

- **`buildOfferPdf`** `buildOfferPdf(input: OfferSheetInput /* OfferInput & { clientCode; clientPhone; docNo; offeredAt: Date; managerName; managerPhone; items: OfferSheetItem[] } */, locale: ClientLocale): Promise<Uint8Array>` — Prints company_name/company_address/company_phone settings + public/logo-full.png, doc no (deal code) + OFFER date, client name/code/phone, goods table from `items` (seq,label,quantity,unit,weightKg,volumeM3 — NO price, NO TNVED, #781), offerLines rows verbatim, manager name·phone, signature, page numbers if >1. Every string goes through pdfTextCleaner() and into cjkSubsetFor(); embedFont subset:false (#881). OfferSheetItem is a pure projection pinned by tests/unit/offer-sheet.test.ts.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/history.ts` — Law 10 price history: per-section, per-code, exact-or-refuse.

- **`groupPerUnit`** `groupPerUnit(group: BreakdownGroup, measure: 'volumeM3' | 'quantity'): number | null` — customs/total rounded 2 only when customs finite, total>0, items non-empty and EVERY item carries the measure >0; an OLD breakdown without item volume reads as missing.
- **`quoteHistoryFor`** `quoteHistoryFor(tnvedCode: string, opts: { scope: UpsaleScope; limit?: number; section?: CalcSectionName }): Promise<QuoteHistoryRow[]>` — EXISTS over calc_groups (indexed by 0087 calc_groups_code_idx), never a JOIN; ORDER sealed_at DESC LIMIT (default 5); offersByVersion = newest version-anchored offer per version in one query. scope REQUIRED: 'own' → hideCost (totalUsd, perM3Usd, perKgUsd, groupCustoms* all null, cardReadable=false); 'none' → hidePrice (clientPriceUsd null, belowFloor false). Freight is NEVER allocated per product.
- **`codesInVersion`** `codesInVersion(requestId: string): Promise<{ code: string; label: string }[]>` — Codes on calc_groups of the request.
- **`lastQuotesByCode`** `lastQuotesByCode(codes: string[], perCode = 3): Promise<Map<string, LastQuote[]>>` — ONE query: DISTINCT (code, version) pairs, row_number() PARTITION BY code ORDER BY sealed_at DESC, rn <= perCode. Consumer: hisoblash/[id]/last-quotes.tsx (perCode 5) beside priceBookForCodes.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/chain.ts` — The correction chain: derived «V2», the registry and its counts.

- **`chainOf`** `chainOf(requestId: string, now = new Date()): Promise<ChainVersion[]>` — Recursive walk up supersedes_request_id to the root, then treeSql() down (depth cap 64); quote_no = row_number() PARTITION BY root ORDER BY sealed_at, id; superseded = a child request exists (edge, not rank); recalc_open = child with no version; superseded_by_no = min child quote_no; expired = valid_until < now. db.execute hands timestamps back as TEXT → parsed with new Date(). Consumers: calc-panel (V{quoteNo}, 'Oldingi: V1'), hisoblash/[id]/page.tsx → CalcWorkspace chain list.
- **`quoteNoFor`** `quoteNoFor(versionId: string): Promise<number | null>` — The printed number for one version.
- **`registryRows / registryCounts / registrySealers / REGISTRY_CAP`** `registryRows(f: RegistryFilters, now?): Promise<RegistryRow[]>; registryCounts(f): Promise<{ versions: number; jobs: number }>; registrySealers(): Promise<{ id; name }[]>; REGISTRY_CAP = 200` — One row per SEALED version, newest first, LIMIT 200; filters (from/to on sealed_at, section, sealerId, q ILIKE over client_code/client name/deal code and lead name ONLY when leadsReadable) in ONE predicate shared with the counts (#513); jobs = count(DISTINCT root_id). cardLabel for a lead is null unless leadsReadable.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/link.ts` — The calc↔prixod join (receipt grain), its one writer and its one readable-predicate.

- **`CalcLinkError`** `class CalcLinkError extends Error { code: 'receipt_not_found' | 'request_not_found' | 'request_foreign' | 'no_deal' | 'not_mine' }` — Thrown by confirmCalcLink/setCalcLink; nazorat actions return { error: code }.
- **`stampCalcLink`** `stampCalcLink(tx: Tx, receiptId: string, dealId: string | null): Promise<string | null>` — tx-only (#714). Finds sealed, non-superseded requests on the deal (limit 3, de-duped per request, latest valid_until wins); requires EXACTLY ONE; requires receipt.confirmed_at within [request.requested_at, valid_until]; UPDATE receipts SET calc_request_id, calc_link_source='auto' WHERE calc_request_id IS NULL (never over a person's link). Called from receipts/service.ts confirmReceipt (line 416) and deals/service.ts linkReceipt (line 469).
- **`confirmCalcLink`** `confirmCalcLink(receiptId: string, scope: 'all' | 'own', ctx: AuditContext): Promise<void>` — Re-proves request.entity is the receipt's deal ('request_foreign'), assertMine (sealed_by must be actor under 'own' → 'not_mine'), sets calc_link_source='person', calc_link_confirmed_at/by; audit receipt update.
- **`setCalcLink`** `setCalcLink(receiptId: string, requestId: string | null, scope: 'all' | 'own', ctx: AuditContext): Promise<void>` — assertMine on BOTH the old and the new request; null clears all four columns; non-null needs receipt.deal_id ('no_deal') and request on that deal ('request_foreign'), writes source 'person' + confirmed stamp.
- **`measurableLinkSql`** `measurableLinkSql(alias = 'r'): SQL` — calc_request_id NOT NULL AND calc_link_confirmed_at NOT NULL AND status='confirmed' AND voided_at IS NULL. 'an `auto` link is a suggestion and never scores anybody.' Consumers: calcActuals `linked` CTE.
- **`LINK_IMPLAUSIBLE_FACTOR / calcLinkOptions / linkedReceipts`** `LINK_IMPLAUSIBLE_FACTOR = 3; calcLinkOptions(dealId: string | null): Promise<CalcLinkOption[]>; linkedReceipts(requestId: string): Promise<LinkedReceipt[]>` — Options = sealed requests on the deal (newest first, ≤20 versions de-duped); linkedReceipts = non-void receipts pointing at the request with Σ lot volume/weight and linkConfirmed flag, LIMIT 50 (workspace page 'Linked receipts' section).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/actuals.ts` — «Hisob vs haqiqat» — CUSTOMS ONLY comparison, band check for freight, nothing stored.

- **`ActualRefusal`** `type ActualRefusal = 'customs_by_client' | 'section_has_no_customs' | 'unconverted' | 'unallocated' | 'no_actual_yet' | 'no_actual_cost' | 'cargo_incomplete' | 'link_implausible' | 'not_linked'` — 'A bucket that cannot be compared is never scored.' Labelled by REFUSAL_LABELS → calc.refusal.*.
- **`bucketRefusal`** `bucketRefusal(facts: { section; linkedReceipts; anyCustomsByClient; cargoIncomplete; linkImplausible; entriesFound; mappedEntries; unconvertedEntries; unallocatedEntries }): ActualRefusal | null` — Ladder in order: not_linked → link_implausible → section_has_no_customs → customs_by_client → cargo_incomplete → no_actual_yet → no_actual_cost → unconverted → unallocated → null.
- **`calcActuals`** `calcActuals(who: { scope: 'all' | 'own'; actorId: string }, opts?: { since?: Date; limit?: number; settledOnly?: boolean }): Promise<CalcActualRow[]>` — Reads settings calc_customs_cost_type_codes (JSON list, default ["customs"]), calc_actual_settle_days (7), deal_deviation_threshold_pct (10), calc_customs_deviation_pct (15), and the whole tariffHistory(). One grouped query: want (currentVersion + measurableRequestSql + sealed_at ≥ since(90d) + own filter on v.sealed_by), linked (measurableLinkSql, by_client three-state: receipt's explicit answer wins else bool_or of departed batches' customs_by_client), cargo Σ, arrival (max box_movements.created_at INTO a UZ warehouse with cause IN ARRIVED_ON_A_TRUCK and from IS DISTINCT FROM to), entries/scoped/money over cost_allocations + receipt-scope and unconverted batch-scope entries, settledFilter in SQL before LIMIT when settledOnly. JS: cargoIncomplete on volumePct ?? weightPct < −threshold; linkImplausible actual > quoted×3; customsPct = (actual−quoted)/quoted ×100 (1 decimal); customsOffThreshold = |pct| > calc_customs_deviation_pct; band = freightBandCheck with bandsAsOf(tariff, sealed date).
- **`freightBandCheck / settledFor / customsCostCodes`** `freightBandCheck(input: { section; tariff: FreightBand[]; zone; quotedMin; quotedRate; actualWeightKg; actualVolumeM3 }): FreightBandCheck; settledFor(arrivedAt: Date | null, settleDays: number, now?): boolean; customsCostCodes(): Promise<string[]>` — Band check re-looks up freightFor at arrived density; ok = |arrivedMin − quotedMin| < 0.005; refusal 'band_missing' | 'band_ambiguous' surfaced (BAND_REFUSAL_LABELS). settledFor: arrived AND now − arrived ≥ days.
- **`calcCoverage / linkSuggestions / warnedGroups`** `calcCoverage(who, since: Date): Promise<{ sealed; linked; suggested }>; linkSuggestions(who, limit = 50): Promise<LinkSuggestion[]>; warnedGroups(who, since: Date, limit = 100): Promise<WarnedGroup[]>` — Coverage over currentVersion+measurableRequestSql on the sealed-at clock; suggestions = confirmed non-void receipts with calc_request_id and NO calc_link_confirmed_at; warned = calc_groups with jsonb_array_length(confirmed_warnings)>0 on the current version, carrying calc_groups.note and confirm_via.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/labels.ts` — Literal i18n lookup maps (#163) so runtime keys are walkable by tests/unit/locales.test.ts.

- **`SECTION_LABELS / FIELD_LABELS / WARNING_LABELS / REFUSAL_LABELS / BAND_REFUSAL_LABELS / isCalcSection`** `Record<string,string> maps → 'sections.*', 'fields.*', 'warnings.{rateOffDictionary,bazaOffDictionary,aiLowConfidence,aiRateTaken,rateNoted,bazaFromImport}', 'refusal.{customsByClient,sectionHasNoCustoms,unconverted,unallocated,noActualYet,noActualCost,cargoIncomplete,linkImplausible,notLinked}', 'bandRefusal.{missing,ambiguous}'; isCalcSection(v): v is CalcSection` — All keys live under the `calc` namespace.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/dictionaries.ts` — Dictionary 4 (price book) lives here beside bazas/rates/tariff (lines 560-715).

- **`priceBookForCodes / priceBookAt`** `priceBookForCodes(codes: string[], date: string): Promise<Map<string, PriceBookRow>>; priceBookAt(code: string, date: string): Promise<PriceBookRow | null>` — Newest row with effective_date <= date per code, NO earliest-row fallback (fx_rates' rule). PriceBookRow = { id; tnvedCode; label; priceUsd; unit: 'm3'|'kg'; effectiveDate; note }.
- **`listPriceBook / savePriceBook`** `listPriceBook(): Promise<(PriceBookRow & { stale: boolean; future: boolean })[]>; savePriceBook(input: { tnvedCode; label; priceUsd; unit: 'm3'|'kg'; effectiveDate; note? }, ctx): Promise<string>` — save refuses 'code_required', 'bad_number', 'price_positive', 'label_required'; UPSERT on (tnved_code, effective_date) — 'the last word that day wins'; audit entityType 'calc_price'. stale = effective_date <= today − BAZA_STALE_DAYS.
- **`staleDictionaryCounts`** `staleDictionaryCounts(): Promise<{ bazas; rates; prices; total }>` — Distinct keys whose newest effective_date <= cutoff; feeds notifyDictionaryReview.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/review.ts` — The monthly dictionary-review notification (phase C).

- **`notifyDictionaryReview / claimReviewMonth / reviewMonth`** `notifyDictionaryReview(now?): Promise<number>; claimReviewMonth(month: string): Promise<boolean>; reviewMonth(now: Date, timeZone = 'Asia/Tashkent'): 'YYYY-MM'` — Silent when total stale is 0 (month NOT claimed); claim = settings upsert on key `calc_review_notified_month` with setWhere value IS DISTINCT FROM month; recipients usersWithPermission('ved.docs'); type 'CalcDictReview'. Scheduled by jobs.ts JOB_CALC_REVIEW = 'calc.dict-review' at '0 6 * * *'.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/warnings.ts` — Pure: what stood on screen at ✅ and the three counters the seal carries away.

- **`CalcWarningKind`** `'rate_off_dictionary' | 'baza_off_dictionary' | 'ai_low_confidence' | 'ai_rate_taken' | 'rate_noted' | 'baza_from_import'` — Stored into calc_groups.confirmed_warnings at confirm; read by warnedGroups.
- **`sealCounters / unchangedFromProposal / aiRateTaken`** `sealCounters(groups: { warnings: CalcWarningKind[]; blind: boolean }[]): { warnedGroups; aiBlindGroups; aiRateTakenGroups }; unchangedFromProposal(proposal: ProposalSnapshot | null, now: { tnvedCode; dutyPct; itemSeqs }): boolean; aiRateTaken(facts): boolean` — blind = ai_low_confidence warning AND unchangedFromProposal (code, aiDutyPct within 0.0005, item set equal). 'aiBlind is a STATISTIC and never a row on anybody's list.'

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/service.ts` — Phase A service; the parts this subsystem touches: CalcError, the rekey, the answer readers.

- **`CalcError`** `class CalcError extends Error { constructor(public readonly code: string, public readonly seq?: number) }` — Every calc action maps it to { error: code } and the UI prints `t.has('errors.'+code) ? t(...) : code`.
- **`rekeyLeadCalcRequests`** `rekeyLeadCalcRequests(leadId: string, dealId: string): Promise<number>` — UPDATE calc_requests SET entity_type='deal', entity_id=dealId WHERE lead (EVERY request — 'moves closed rows too'); then the same on calc_offers. Called from crm/service.ts convertLead (line 889-890) in a try/catch. Fenced by tests/unit/calc-rekey.test.ts (schema-derived).
- **`openCalcFor / lastCalcAnswerFor / finishCalcRequest / endRequest`** `openCalcFor(entityType, entityId); lastCalcAnswerFor(entityType, entityId) /* completed_via='task' only */; finishCalcRequest(...) throws 'seal_instead' when canSeal(workspace); endRequest(id, { via, actorId, answerAmount?, answerCurrency? })` — endRequest writes completed_via, answer_amount, answer_currency (the Готово anchor's source); finishCalcRequest sets answer_currency to `currency || 'USD'` when an amount is given.

#### `src/modules/wms/crm/service.ts` — The quote LOCK (law 2) and the lead form's refusal.

- **`quoteLockedFor`** `quoteLockedFor(entityType: 'lead' | 'deal', entityId: string): Promise<number | null>` — Dynamic import of calc/workspace. null if no seal. offered = releasedPriceFor; if offered.at >= seal.sealedAt → offered.price else seal.totalUsd ('LATER WRITER WINS, by the clock, because that is exactly how the card column was written'). isServerBehind → null (lock is a safeguard, not a gate).
- **`updateLead (refusal)`** `updateLead(id, input: LeadInput, ctx) → throws CrmError('quote_sealed')` — Line 546-549: sealedTotal !== null && !sameMoney(sealedTotal, values.quotedAmount) → 'quote_sealed'. A CHANGE check (#171: the locked form re-posts the value as hidden inputs). i18n crm.errors.quote_sealed.
- **`convertLead → rekey`** `(inside convertLead) await rekeyLeadCalcRequests(id, dealId)` — After createDeal, before the move to won; errors logged '[calc] rekey on win failed'.

#### `src/modules/wms/deals/service.ts` — Deal writers, the deal form's lock, and linkReceipt's link handling.

- **`DEAL_WRITE_PERMISSIONS / canWriteDeal`** `DEAL_WRITE_PERMISSIONS = ['crm.leads', 'ved.docs', 'clients.manage'] as const; canWriteDeal(permissions): boolean` — 'Both sides work a deal: sales quoted it, VED recalculated it (DEALS.md #2).' Because it carries ved.docs, every price-facing door adds upsaleScopeFor/mayOffer on top.
- **`updateDeal (refusal)`** `updateDeal(id, input: DealInput, ctx) → throws DealError('quote_sealed')` — Lines 195-199: only when amountChanged (compared as NUMBERS) AND quoteLockedFor('deal', id) !== null. i18n deals.errors.quote_sealed.
- **`linkReceipt`** `linkReceipt(receiptId: string, dealId: string | null, ctx: AuditContext): Promise<void>` — 'client_mismatch' across clients. In tx: BOTH the re-file and the detach branch null calc_request_id/calc_link_source/calc_link_confirmed_at/by; then `if (dealId) await stampCalcLink(tx, receiptId, dealId)`.

#### `src/modules/wms/receipts/service.ts` — Second stampCalcLink caller.

- **`confirmReceipt (tail)`** `await stampCalcLink(tx, receipt!.id, receipt!.dealId) at line 416, inside the confirm transaction` — The code comment names `createReceipt` ('writes `deal_id` on its own INSERT and never goes through `linkReceipt`'), but the enclosing exported function is `confirmReceipt` (line 122; next export is class VoidError at 458).

#### `src/modules/wms/accounting/service.ts` — voidExpense re-opens paid upsale offers.

- **`voidExpense`** `voidExpense(id: string, reason: string, ctx: AuditContext)` — Lines 260-261: `const { reopenUpsaleForExpense } = await import('../calc/upsale-service'); await reopenUpsaleForExpense(id).catch(() => undefined);` — best-effort, after the void.

#### `src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/actions.ts` — Server actions for the seal, the correction, the offer and the price book.

- **`sealAction`** `sealAction(id: string, input: { discountUsd: number; discountReason: string; bandOverrideMin: number | null; bandOverrideReason: string }): Promise<CalcFormState>` — run('ved.docs', …) → sealCalc; CalcError → { error: code }; isServerBehind → 'server_behind'; revalidates /hisoblash/[id] and /hisoblash.
- **`recalcAction`** `recalcAction(id: string): Promise<CalcFormState & { newId?: string }>` — authorize('admin.settings.manage') — 'a sealed price is what the client was told, so re-opening the question is the owner's call'; returns newId on success.
- **`makeOfferAction`** `makeOfferAction(anchor: { versionId } | { requestId }, input: { clientPriceUsd; locale: 'uz'|'ru'|'en'; clientName: string|null; belowFloorReason?; entityType; entityId; revalidate }): Promise<OfferFormState /* { ok?, error?, id?, text?, pending?, belowFloor?, delivered? } */>` — 'unauthenticated'; 'forbidden' unless canWriteDeal AND mayOffer AND (lead → crm.leads); 'validation' on bad entityType/locale; passes mayApprove = mayApproveBelowFloor(actor) and expect = card; CalcError → code; server behind → 'server_behind' (logs '[calc] server behind — migration 0087 not applied').
- **`savePriceBookAction / saveTariffAction`** `savePriceBookAction(input): run('ved.docs', savePriceBook, '/hisoblash/lugatlar'); saveTariffAction(input: { zone; minDensity; maxDensity: number|null; priceUsd; perKg; effectiveDate }): revalidates /admin/tarif and /hisoblash/lugatlar` — Price book is the VED's (ved.docs); the tariff stays admin.dictionaries.manage (checked in the action body, not shown here).
