#### `docs/VED.md` — The agreed spec; laws 1-11 at lines 47-98; phases D/E1/E2 status at 213-262.

- **`Laws quoted`** `Law 2: 'The price the VED sends lands on the card LOCKED: neither seller nor VED can change it. Only admin can; a change is audited AND notified to the owner. Corrections are new VERSIONS — nothing is overwritten.' Law 3: 'Quote validity = 1 month.' Law 4: 'Client price is entered by the seller on the card; upsale = client price − VED price, computed, never typed. Floor: client price ≥ VED price (below-floor is admin-only). ANY discount kills the upsale right … Visibility: owner + accountant + the seller (own only). VED never sees upsale. … the cash screen must show both figures side by side.' Law 10: 'Sellers and VED can read the price history (sellers: prices only, no cost breakdown).'` — E2 precondition: '≥20 sealed versions with confirmed links and settled cargo, and a non-empty calc_price_book'.

#### `docs/DEALS.md` — Deal writers.

- **`Decision 2`** `'| 2 | Who may re-price after reality differs | **Both** the sales manager and the VED manager |'` — Basis for DEAL_WRITE_PERMISSIONS carrying ved.docs.

### Flows


**Seal → locked floor on the card**

1. UI: hisoblash/[id]/calc-workspace.tsx seal form (calc-discount, calc-discount-reason, calc-band-override, calc-band-reason, calc-do-seal) → sealAction(id, input) [ved.docs via run()]
2. workspace.ts sealCalc: mustBeNumber; discount<0 → amount_range; reason rules; loadWorkspace(requestId, { overrideDensity, discountUsd }); canSeal else not_ready; freight zone/freight present; validUntil = now + settings.quote_valid_days (30)
3. db.transaction: lockRequestInTx CAS on calc_requests.rev (conflict) → UPDATE calc_requests completed_at/completed_by/completed_via='sealed'/current_version_no+1/rev+1 WHERE completed_at IS NULL (already_closed) → INSERT calc_versions (money cols, freight cols, band override, discount, ai counters, warned_groups/ai_blind_groups/ai_rate_taken_groups, breakdown jsonb)
4. if supersedes_request_id: UPDATE receipts SET calc_request_id = new WHERE calc_request_id = superseded (correction adopts cargo)
5. UPDATE leads (quoted_amount/currency/volume/weight) or deals (+ quoted_at/quoted_by) with the FLOOR
6. writeAudit calc_request update; announceSeal → notifyStaffTelegram 'CalcSealed' to requestedBy; if discount>0 or band override → 'CalcDiscounted' to discountAudience (super_admin+admin roles, accountant role, requestedBy, superseded request's requestedBy)
7. Later: crm/service.ts quoteLockedFor + updateLead / deals/service.ts updateDeal refuse a CHANGED quoted_amount with 'quote_sealed'

**Offer on a sealed version → Telegram text → client price on the card**

1. components/calc-panel.tsx renders CalcOfferForm({ anchor: { versionId: seal.id }, floorUsd: seal.totalUsd, discountUsd: seal.discountUsd, mayApprove: mayApproveBelowFloor(actor) }) when !seal.expired && upsaleScopeFor(actor)!=='none'
2. components/calc-offer.tsx → makeOfferAction(anchor, { clientPriceUsd, locale, clientName, belowFloorReason, entityType, entityId, revalidate }) [canWriteDeal + mayOffer + crm.leads on lead]
3. workspace.ts recordOffer version branch: load calc_versions+calc_requests by version id; expect mismatch → not_found; discount>0 && price>floor → discounted_no_upsale; offerText(...) from offer.ts
4. belowFloor = price < floor−0.009; reason required; approved = belowFloor ? mayApprove : true; INSERT calc_offers(version_id, entity_type/id, client_price_usd, below_floor, below_floor_reason, approved_at/by, locale, text, offered_by); audit calc_offer create
5. approved: applyOfferToCard → leads/deals.quoted_amount = CLIENT price, quoted_currency='USD'; hasLinkedChat(actor) → notifyStaffTelegram type 'CalcOffer' text to the seller; return { id, text, delivered, pending:false }
6. UI shows offer-text / offer-copy / offer-pdf (/api/calc/offer/{id}/pdf?til=); panel lists calc-offers
7. quoteLockedFor now returns releasedPriceFor().price when its `at` >= seal.sealedAt, so the ✏️ form's re-posted value is the client price

**Below-floor promise → pending → release (law 4)**

1. recordOffer with price < floor − 0.009 and mayApprove=false: row inserted with below_floor=true, approved_at NULL; notifyApprovers → notifyStaffTelegram 'CalcBelowFloor' to approverIds() (finance.reports holders) exceptUserId actor; returns { text: null, pending: true }
2. calc-panel shows calc-offer-pending on the row and NO PDF link; /api/calc/offer/[id]/pdf 404s (releasedOfferWhere in the fetch); releasedPriceFor ignores it; payableOffersSql excludes it (NOT below_floor OR approved_at NOT NULL)
3. /upsale page (mayApproveBelowFloor): pendingBelowFloor() list → ReleaseButton (upsale-allow) → releaseOfferAction → workspace.ts releaseOffer: UPDATE … WHERE id AND below_floor AND approved_at IS NULL AND offerStandsSql() RETURNING (single-shot claim); no row → 'superseded' | 'not_pending'
4. releaseOffer: resolve request from the offer's own anchor → applyOfferToCard; audit calc_offer update; 'CalcOffer' push to offered_by if linked; return text
5. An admin/accountant pressing below floor themselves: approved in one step (approved_at=now(), approved_by=actor)

**Готово answer as an offer anchor (phase 4)**

1. calc/service.ts finishCalcRequest → endRequest writes calc_requests.completed_at, completed_via='task', answer_amount, answer_currency (throws 'seal_instead' if canSeal(workspace))
2. calc-panel: lastAnswerAnchorFor(entityType, entityId) → { requestId, amountUsd, currency, stands (answerFloorStandsSql), expired (completed_at + quote_valid_days) }; rendered as calc-answer-door ONLY when !seal (any seal outranks it): calc-answer-not-usd / calc-answer-expired / CalcOfferForm({ anchor: { requestId }, floorUsd: amountUsd, discountUsd: 0 })
3. recordOffer answer branch: plain read of calc_requests; answer_missing / answer_not_usd / superseded (answerFloorStandsSql false) / answer_expired; floor = answer_amount; text from request volume/weight/section (coalesce 'podklyuch')/cities; INSERT calc_offers(request_id, version_id NULL) — CHECK calc_offers_one_anchor_check
4. payableOffersSql answer branch pays it (total_usd = r.answer_amount) until a NEWER USD answer or a later SEAL on the same card exists; /api/calc/offer/[id]/pdf recomputes validUntil

**Upsale payout (the accountant's Friday)**

1. /upsale page: upsaleRows(scope, actor.id, { from, to, sellerId }) → payableOffersSql + users + deals + clients + LATERAL Σ charges + balancesForClients → state per row; payable rows → PayForm (finance.expenses && scope 'all' && accounts)
2. CategoryForm → setUpsaleCategoryAction [admin.settings.manage] → setUpsaleCategory (active expense_categories check → category_not_found) → settings.upsale_expense_category_id + audit settings/SETTINGS_AUDIT_ID
3. PayForm (upsale-pick ticks, upsale-account, upsale-date, note; total derived) → payUpsaleAction(offerIds, { accountId, currency, expenseDate, note }) [finance.expenses AND upsaleScopeFor==='all']
4. upsale-service.ts payUpsale: setting → upsale_category_unset; rateFor(currency, date) → fx_missing; account currency → account_currency_mismatch; SELECT from payableOffersSql WHERE id IN ids AND payout_expense_id IS NULL → offer_not_payable; one seller → one_seller_at_a_time; Σ>0 → nothing_to_pay; amount = paidUsd/rate
5. db.transaction: addExpenseTx(categoryId, amount, currency, expenseDate, accountId, employeeId, note 'Upsale · N') → UPDATE calc_offers SET payout_expense_id, payout_at=now(), payout_by, payout_usd = p.upsale_usd FROM (payableOffersSql()) p WHERE … AND payout_expense_id IS NULL RETURNING → offer_already_paid / amount_moved → rollback; audit expense update
6. accounting/service.ts voidExpense → reopenUpsaleForExpense(expenseId) nulls all four payout columns (#528 pair); bySeller scoreboard + upsaleLiability (payable only) for the balance sheet

**Correction chain (Qayta hisoblash → V2)**

1. calc-workspace.tsx calc-recalc (canRecalc = admin.settings.manage) → recalcAction(id) → recalcFromSealed: not_closed / not_sealed / recalc_open / recalc_superseded; INSERT new calc_requests with supersedes_request_id=old, due_at now+2h; copy groups (no confirmed_*), items (incl. import_row_id, measure), extras; audit create {supersedes}; router.push(/hisoblash/{newId})
2. While the child is open: notSupersededSql already false for the parent → payableOffersSql stops paying its offers; offerStandsSql false → releasedPriceFor ignores its released offer; releaseOffer refuses 'superseded'; measurableRequestSql still TRUE (no version on the child) so actuals keep measuring the old price; ChainStateChip shows chain-recalc-open
3. Child seals (sealCalc): receipts re-pointed from parent to child; card floor rewritten; quoteLockedFor's later-writer-wins picks the new seal over the old released offer
4. chain.ts chainOf/quoteNoFor: quote_no = rank among sealed versions under the root by sealed_at → calc-panel calc-seal-version 'V2', calc-seal-prev 'Oldingi: V1 · $ (registry audience only) · date'; /hisoblash/tarix lists both versions with counts jobs/versions

**Won lead → deal rekey**

1. crm/service.ts convertLead (via winLead): createDeal(...) → rekeyLeadCalcRequests(id, dealId) in try/catch
2. calc/service.ts rekeyLeadCalcRequests: UPDATE calc_requests SET entity_type='deal', entity_id=dealId WHERE lead (all rows incl. sealed) → UPDATE calc_offers likewise
3. Consequence: currentSealFor('deal', dealId) finds the version → the deal is LOCKED; offersFor('deal', …) lists the seller's promise; tests/unit/calc-rekey.test.ts derives the table set from schema/wms.ts and asserts .update(calcRequests) and .update(calcOffers) with entityType:'deal'/entityId: dealId

**Cargo link → control screen → actuals (phase E1)**

1. receipts/service.ts confirmReceipt tx tail: stampCalcLink(tx, receipt.id, receipt.dealId); deals/service.ts linkReceipt tx: null the four calc_link columns (re-file AND detach) then stampCalcLink when dealId
2. link.ts stampCalcLink: exactly one sealed non-superseded request on the deal AND receipt.confirmed_at within [requested_at, valid_until] → UPDATE receipts SET calc_request_id, calc_link_source='auto' WHERE calc_request_id IS NULL
3. /hisoblash/nazorat (calcControlScopeFor≠none): linkSuggestions → CalcLinkRow link-confirm → confirmLinkAction → confirmCalcLink(receiptId, scope) (request_foreign re-proof, assertMine → not_mine) sets source 'person' + calc_link_confirmed_at/by; link-drop → setCalcLink(receiptId, null, scope)
4. receipts/[id]/page.tsx CalcLink picker (calcLinkOptions(dealId)) → setCalcLinkAction(receiptId, requestId) → setCalcLink (assertMine both ends, no_deal, request_foreign)
5. actuals.ts calcActuals(who, { settledOnly: true }): linked via measurableLinkSql → cargo Σ → arrival via box_movements into country='UZ' with ARRIVED_ON_A_TRUCK causes → settledFilter (arrived_at <= now − calc_actual_settle_days) → entries/scoped/money over cost types in calc_customs_cost_type_codes → bucketRefusal ladder → customsPct vs calc_customs_deviation_pct; freightBandCheck with bandsAsOf(tariffHistory, sealed date)
6. hisoblash/[id]/page.tsx shows linkedReceipts(id) with ✓/? per prixod; calcCoverage prints sealed/linked on the sealed-at clock

**Price history and price book (law 10)**

1. /hisoblash/lugatlar PriceBookForm → savePriceBookAction [ved.docs] → dictionaries.ts savePriceBook UPSERT (tnved_code, effective_date); audit 'calc_price'
2. hisoblash/[id]/last-quotes.tsx: lastQuotesByCode(codes, 5) + priceBookForCodes(codes, today) beside the group being priced; link to /hisoblash/narxlar?kod=
3. /hisoblash/narxlar: quoteHistoryFor(code, { scope: upsaleScopeFor(actor), limit: 10 }) + priceBookAt(code, today); VED (scope none) sees totals/customs but no client price; seller (own) sees client price/belowFloor but no totals/per-unit/customs and no card link
4. jobs.ts JOB_CALC_REVIEW '0 6 * * *' → review.ts notifyDictionaryReview → staleDictionaryCounts (bazas, rates, prices) → claimReviewMonth (settings key calc_review_notified_month) → 'CalcDictReview' to ved.docs holders

### Data model

- **`calc_versions`** (migration 0086_calc_pricing.sql (lines 221-266); 0088 adds index calc_versions_request_no_idx (request_id, version_no DESC); 0089 adds warned_groups, ai_blind_groups, ai_rate_taken_groups): id uuid PK; request_id uuid NOT NULL → calc_requests ON DELETE CASCADE; version_no int NOT NULL; sealed_at timestamptz NOT NULL default now(); sealed_by uuid NOT NULL → users; valid_until timestamptz NOT NULL; section text NOT NULL; weight_kg numeric(12,3); volume_m3 numeric(12,4); density numeric(12,4); customs_usd numeric(14,2) NOT NULL default 0; freight_usd numeric(14,2) NOT NULL default 0; extras_usd numeric(14,2) NOT NULL default 0; total_usd numeric(14,2) NOT NULL; per_m3_usd numeric(14,2); per_kg_usd numeric(14,4); freight_zone text; freight_band_min numeric(10,2); freight_rate numeric(10,4); freight_per_kg boolean; freight_list_usd numeric(14,2); band_override_min numeric(10,2); band_override_reason text; discount_usd numeric(14,2) NOT NULL default 0; discount_reason text; ai_groups_sealed int NOT NULL default 0; low_confidence_sealed int NOT NULL default 0; breakdown jsonb NOT NULL default '{}'; warned_groups int NOT NULL default 0; ai_blind_groups int NOT NULL default 0; ai_rate_taken_groups int NOT NULL default 0 — CHECKs: calc_versions_section_check IN ('yolkira','rastamojka','podklyuch'); calc_versions_total_check total_usd >= 0 AND <> 'NaN'; calc_versions_discount_check discount_usd >= 0 AND <> 'NaN'; calc_versions_parts_check customs/freight/extras <> 'NaN'; calc_versions_e_counts_check all three counters >= 0; UNIQUE calc_versions_request_no_unique (request_id, version_no); INDEX calc_versions_sealed_idx (sealed_at). Never UPDATEd after insert (immutable by convention).
- **`calc_requests (columns this subsystem uses)`** (migration 0086 adds current_version_no int NOT NULL default 0, supersedes_request_id uuid → calc_requests ON DELETE SET NULL, freight_zone, ai_proposal_started_at; widens completed_via CHECK to ('lines','task','returned','sealed'); 0088 index calc_requests_supersedes_idx (supersedes_request_id) WHERE NOT NULL; 0089 index calc_requests_entity_idx (entity_type, entity_id); 0093 CHECK calc_requests_answer_amount_check (answer_amount IS NULL OR (> 0 AND <> 'NaN')) NOT VALID; 0092 adds rev int clock): entity_type ('deal'|'lead'), entity_id, requested_by, assignee_id, section, from_city, to_city, weight_kg, volume_m3, completed_at, completed_by, completed_via, answer_amount, answer_currency, current_version_no, supersedes_request_id, rev, has_certificate, fee_override_usd, due_at — CHECKs: calc_requests_via_check; calc_requests_answer_amount_check (NOT VALID — pre-existing rows exempt)
- **`calc_offers`** (migration 0087_calc_price_book.sql (create); 0088_calc_upsale.sql (approval + payout columns, CHECKs, indexes); 0093_calc_offer_answer.sql (version_id nullable, request_id, one-anchor CHECK)): id uuid PK; version_id uuid NULL → calc_versions ON DELETE CASCADE; request_id uuid NULL → calc_requests ON DELETE CASCADE; entity_type text NOT NULL; entity_id uuid NOT NULL; client_price_usd numeric(14,2) NOT NULL; below_floor boolean NOT NULL default false; below_floor_reason text; approved_at timestamptz; approved_by uuid → users; locale text NOT NULL; text text NOT NULL; offered_by uuid NOT NULL → users; offered_at timestamptz NOT NULL default now(); payout_expense_id uuid → expenses; payout_at timestamptz; payout_by uuid → users; payout_usd numeric(14,2) — CHECKs: calc_offers_entity_check IN ('deal','lead'); calc_offers_locale_check IN ('uz','ru','en'); calc_offers_price_check > 0 AND <> 'NaN'; calc_offers_below_floor_reason_check (NULL or btrim <> ''); calc_offers_below_floor_only_check (below_floor OR (reason NULL AND approved_at NULL)); calc_offers_approval_pair_check (approved_at NULL) = (approved_by NULL); calc_offers_payout_amount_check (NULL or > 0 AND <> 'NaN'); calc_offers_payout_pair_check all four payout cols NULL together; calc_offers_one_anchor_check (version_id IS NULL) <> (request_id IS NULL). Indexes: calc_offers_version_idx, calc_offers_entity_idx (entity_type, entity_id, offered_at), calc_offers_pending_idx (offered_at) WHERE below_floor AND approved_at IS NULL, calc_offers_unpaid_idx (offered_by, offered_at) WHERE payout_expense_id IS NULL, calc_offers_payout_expense_idx, calc_offers_request_idx WHERE request_id NOT NULL. NOT granted to gsr_ai_reader.
- **`calc_price_book`** (migration 0087_calc_price_book.sql): id uuid PK; tnved_code text NOT NULL; label text NOT NULL; price_usd numeric(14,4) NOT NULL; unit text NOT NULL default 'm3'; effective_date date NOT NULL; note text; entered_by uuid → users; created_at timestamptz — CHECKs: calc_price_book_unit_check IN ('m3','kg'); calc_price_book_value_check price_usd > 0 AND <> 'NaN'; UNIQUE calc_price_book_code_date_unique (tnved_code, effective_date). Read: newest effective_date <= day, no fallback.
- **`calc_groups (E1 columns) + index`** (migration 0089 adds ai_proposal jsonb, confirmed_warnings jsonb, confirm_via text; 0087 adds calc_groups_code_idx (tnved_code) WHERE NOT NULL): ai_proposal (ProposalSnapshot: tnvedCode, aiDutyPct, itemSeqs), confirmed_warnings (CalcWarningKind[]), confirm_via ('single'|'bulk'), note, confirmed_at, tnved_code, label, seq — CHECKs: calc_groups_confirm_via_check IN ('single','bulk'); calc_groups_confirm_pair_check (confirm_via IS NULL OR confirmed_at IS NOT NULL) — 'TWO writers that clear it: unconfirm() and setGroupRates'
- **`receipts (calc link columns)`** (migration 0089_calc_actuals.sql): calc_request_id uuid → calc_requests ON DELETE SET NULL; calc_link_source text; calc_link_confirmed_at timestamptz; calc_link_confirmed_by uuid → users — CHECKs: receipts_calc_link_source_check IN ('auto','person') or NULL; INDEX receipts_calc_request_idx WHERE calc_request_id NOT NULL. DELIBERATELY no CHECK tying confirmation to the link ('ON DELETE SET NULL cannot coexist with a CHECK spanning the FK column' — measured twice); readers ask both columns via measurableLinkSql. Backfill: 'auto' where the deal has exactly one sealed non-superseded request.
- **`client_transactions (index)`** (migration 0088): deal_id — CHECKs: INDEX client_transactions_deal_live_idx (deal_id) WHERE deal_id NOT NULL AND voided_at IS NULL — upsaleRows' 'has this deal been invoiced' LATERAL
- **`settings (keys)`** (migration defaults in src/modules/platform/settings/service.ts): quote_valid_days (30); calc_actual_settle_days (7); calc_customs_deviation_pct (15); calc_customs_cost_type_codes ('["customs"]'); upsale_expense_category_id (''); deal_deviation_threshold_pct (10); calc_review_notified_month (claim, no default); company_name/company_address/company_phone — CHECKs: upsale_expense_category_id re-checked against active expense_categories on write; empty = refused at pay time with upsale_category_unset
- **`leads / deals (card columns written by this subsystem)`** (migration 0062 (leads.quoted_*), phase 5 (deals.quoted_at/quoted_by)): quoted_amount numeric(14,2), quoted_currency, quoted_volume_m3 numeric(12,3), quoted_weight_kg numeric(12,3), deals.quoted_at, deals.quoted_by, updated_at — CHECKs: No DB lock; the lock is quoteLockedFor + 'quote_sealed' in updateLead/updateDeal. Written by sealCalc (floor) and applyOfferToCard (client price) — last writer wins.

### Invariants (and the fence that enforces each)

- Law 2 (VED.md:52-55): 'The price the VED sends lands on the card LOCKED: neither seller nor VED can change it.' Fence: quoteLockedFor (crm/service.ts:356) + updateLead throws CrmError('quote_sealed') on a CHANGED amount (line 546-549) + updateDeal throws DealError('quote_sealed') when amountChanged (deals/service.ts:195-199); tests/integration/upsale.integration.test.ts 'and the card can still be SAVED afterwards — the lock follows the card' and 'the lock follows the card onto the new floor'.
- Law 2 'Corrections are new VERSIONS — nothing is overwritten': calc_versions is never UPDATEd; recalcFromSealed inserts a NEW calc_requests row (supersedes_request_id); there is no re-open. Fence: calc-chain.integration.test.ts 'a correction reads V2 and the old price keeps V1'; UNIQUE (request_id, version_no).
- Law 3 quote validity: valid_until = now + settings.quote_valid_days (default QUOTE_VALID_DAYS_DEFAULT=30) decided at seal; expiry decided at READ (SealedVersion.expired, ChainVersion.expired) — no sweep. An expired seal shows sealExpiredHint and no offer box (calc-panel line 192-194). The same clock gates the answer anchor ('answer_expired'). Fence: calc-offer-answer.integration.test.ts 'an old answer is answer_expired'.
- Law 4 'upsale = client price − VED price, computed, never typed': nothing stores the difference; payableOffersSql computes round(client_price_usd − total_usd, 2) at read; payUpsale derives paidUsd from the same fragment and PayForm has no amount input. Fence: tests/unit/upsale-door.test.ts 'the form posts WHICH jobs, never how much' / 'the service derives it from the payable rule itself'; e2e m9zr-upsale 'the accountant's total is computed from the ticks, never typed'.
- Law 4 'ANY discount kills the upsale right': upsaleEligible + payableOffersSql clauses (discount_usd <= 0.009; band override below density). Fence: tests/unit/upsale-eligibility.test.ts; upsale.integration 'a discounted job carries NONE, however small'. Round 112 extension: quoting ABOVE a discounted floor is refused ('discounted_no_upsale', workspace.ts recordOffer version branch; calc-offer.tsx disables the button). Fence: upsale.integration 'above a discounted floor is REFUSED'.
- Law 4 'below-floor is admin-only': the ROW is always written; the PROMISE waits on approved_at (mayApproveBelowFloor = finance.reports). Fences: releasedOfferWhere in releasedPriceFor/bothFiguresForDeals/both PDF routes; payableOffersSql (NOT below_floor OR approved_at NOT NULL); DB CHECKs calc_offers_below_floor_only_check + calc_offers_approval_pair_check; tests calc-offer-wire 'the PDF route serves RELEASED offers only', upsale.integration 'a seller's below-floor price is RECORDED and sends nothing' / 'releasing is single-shot'.
- Law 4 'VED never sees upsale': upsaleScopeFor(ved_manager) === 'none' because it keys on finance.reports and NOT seesAllMoney (ved_manager holds finance.manage). Fences: tests/unit/upsale-scope.test.ts enumerates every ROLE_MATRIX role (EXPECTED table: super_admin/admin/accountant 'all'; sales_manager/logist 'own'; ved_manager/warehouse_*/viewer 'none'); calc-offer-wire 'the action refuses the VED'; e2e m9zr-upsale.desktop 'the VED is REDIRECTED'; /upsale page redirect('/') on 'none'; both PDF routes 403 on 'none'; nav entry permissions ['finance.reports','crm.leads'].
- Law 4 accountant half: 'the cash screen must show both figures side by side' — finance/[clientId]/page.tsx renders both-figures only when upsaleScopeFor==='all' via bothFiguresForDeals (newest released standing offer, floor COALESCE(version total, answer_amount)).
- Law 10 two views of one row: quoteHistoryFor(scope required) — 'own' nulls the WHOLE cost family (totalUsd, perM3Usd, perKgUsd, groupCustoms*, cardReadable=false); 'none' nulls clientPriceUsd/belowFloor. Fence: calc-offer.integration 'hides the client price from the VED', 'hides the cost side from a seller — the WHOLE derived family'. /upsale hides floor/seller columns unless scope 'all' (upsale-scope.test 'the /upsale page prints the floor only under scope all').
- One payable per JOB (request), on the current version, not superseded, released, eligible+positive — all five in payableOffersSql() and nowhere else; payUpsale's claim embeds the same fragment. Fences: upsale.integration 'one sale, one commission' (re-quote replaces; corrected job pays nothing), 'pays the DERIVED amount once, and refuses the second press'; DB calc_offers_payout_pair_check (all four payout columns together) — 'caught the first version of this function writing them in two steps'.
- One-anchor rule: an offer stands on exactly one of version_id / request_id — DB CHECK calc_offers_one_anchor_check; recordOffer's anchor union; every version-only clause in payableOffersSql guarded by `o.version_id IS NOT NULL`. Fences: calc-offer-answer.integration (7 tests incl. 'a hand-posted OPEN request is refused — a NULL answer must never read as a $0 floor', 'a NEWER USD answer outranks', 'a version SEALED after the answer kills the answer payable').
- Money is never NaN: mustBeNumber → 'bad_number' in recordOffer/savePriceBook/sealCalc; DB `<> 'NaN'::numeric` on client_price_usd, payout_usd, price_usd, total_usd, discount_usd, answer_amount (0093, NOT VALID). Fence: calc-offer.integration 'refuses a price that is not a number rather than storing NaN'.
- The offer never prints or decomposes the sealed total and carries no emoji: offer.ts rules 1-3; PDF goods table has no price/TNVED (OfferSheetItem has no money field). Fences: tests/unit/calc-offer.test.ts ('gives ONE number and never decomposes', 'no offer label carries an emoji'), tests/unit/offer-sheet.test.ts (structural), calc-offer-wire 'renders the OFFER's price and 404s without one — never the sealed floor'.
- An `auto` cargo link never scores anybody: measurableLinkSql requires calc_link_confirmed_at; stampCalcLink writes only WHERE calc_request_id IS NULL and only with exactly one sealed non-superseded request inside the quote window. Fences: calc-actuals.integration 'an unconfirmed link is offered in the queue and scores nothing', 'suggests nothing once the deal carries two sealed calculations', 'refuses a prixod confirmed outside the quote window'.
- Link buttons are scoped, not just lists: assertMine (sealed_by must be the actor under scope 'own') on confirmCalcLink and BOTH ends of setCalcLink → 'not_mine'; scope is a REQUIRED argument. Fence: calc-actuals.integration 'a VED cannot touch the link that measures a colleague'.
- Re-filing or detaching a prixod clears its calc link (linkReceipt both branches) and a correction ADOPTS the parent's receipts at SEAL time (sealCalc). Fences: calc-actuals.integration 'linkReceipt stamps it, and detaching the deal takes the link with it', 're-filing a prixod onto another deal takes its calc link with it', 'moves confirmed links onto the new request when the correction seals'.
- Actuals compare CUSTOMS ONLY; a bucket that cannot be compared is never scored (nine ActualRefusal words, ⚠ + reason, never $0); settle gate in SQL before LIMIT; band check reads the tariff in force at the seal (bandsAsOf). Fences: tests/unit/calc-actuals.test.ts (ladder, band check, completeness by volume with weight fallback, settle clock), calc-actuals.integration ('the SQL gate keeps an unsettled row out', 'sees a BATCH-scope rastamojka that has no FX rate yet', 'refuses «unallocated»').
- «V2» is DERIVED (rank among sealed versions in the supersedes chain by sealed_at), never stored; 'stands' is decided by the EDGE. recalcFromSealed refuses a second child ('recalc_open' / 'recalc_superseded'). Fences: calc-chain.integration (5 tests) and the registry counting jobs (DISTINCT root_id) apart from versions.
- Winning a lead moves EVERY calc_* row carrying (entity_type, entity_id) — calc_requests (all, incl. sealed) and calc_offers. Fence: tests/unit/calc-rekey.test.ts derives the table set from schema/wms.ts and asserts `.update(calcRequests)`/`.update(calcOffers)` with entityType:'deal', entityId: dealId and the absence of `openRequests`; calc-offer.integration 'MEASURED: the offer is orphaned on the dead lead when only the request moves'.
- A correction / recalc is the owner's call: recalcAction gates on admin.settings.manage; the button is absent (canRecalc) for seller and VED. The seal itself is ved.docs (run()). The client price is the seller's: makeOfferAction requires canWriteDeal AND mayOffer (ved.docs alone is refused). Fence: calc-offer-wire 'the action refuses the VED, who may work the card but not quote it'.
- Every /api route carries its own door (no middleware): both PDF routes gate on upsaleScopeFor, lead+own needs crm.leads, own reprints only offeredBy===actor. Fence: calc-offer-wire 'the PDF route carries its own door' block (8 tests).
- Every post-seal screen/panel catches isServerBehind on deploy morning (calc-panel returns null, /upsale sets `behind`, narxlar/nazorat/tarix/lugatlar log and render empty, actions return 'server_behind'). Fence: calc-offer-wire 'survives deploy morning: 0087's tables are caught', upsale-door 'catches its own reads'.
- Pool-vs-transaction rule (#714): payUpsale reads setting/rate/account/payable rows on the pool BEFORE db.transaction; stampCalcLink is tx-only; sealCalc reads getSetting before the tx. Fence: tests/unit/tx-pool.test.ts (module-wide).

### Vocabulary (codes, keys, testids, settings, env, jobs, constants)

- CalcError codes thrown in this subsystem (src/modules/wms/calc/service.ts class; thrown in workspace.ts/upsale-service.ts/dictionaries.ts): bad_number, price_positive, unauthenticated, not_found, discounted_no_upsale, answer_missing, answer_not_usd, superseded, answer_expired, below_floor_reason_required, not_pending, no_offers, upsale_category_unset, fx_missing, account_currency_mismatch, offer_not_payable, one_seller_at_a_time, nothing_to_pay, offer_already_paid, amount_moved, category_not_found, not_closed, not_sealed, recalc_open, recalc_superseded, amount_range, discount_reason_required, band_reason_required, already_closed, not_ready, section_required, freight_zone_required, freight_missing, conflict, seal_instead, code_required, label_required
- CalcLinkError codes (src/modules/wms/calc/link.ts:25): receipt_not_found, request_not_found, request_foreign, no_deal, not_mine
- Action-level refusal words (not CalcError): 'forbidden', 'validation', 'server_behind', 'unauthenticated' — hisoblash/actions.ts, upsale/actions.ts, nazorat/actions.ts
- CrmError 'quote_sealed' (src/modules/wms/crm/service.ts:548) → i18n crm.errors.quote_sealed; DealError 'quote_sealed' (src/modules/wms/deals/service.ts:198) → deals.errors.quote_sealed; DealError 'client_mismatch' (linkReceipt)
- ActualRefusal union (src/modules/wms/calc/actuals.ts): customs_by_client, section_has_no_customs, unconverted, unallocated, no_actual_yet, no_actual_cost, cargo_incomplete, link_implausible, not_linked; FreightBandCheck.refusal: band_missing | band_ambiguous
- CalcWarningKind (src/modules/wms/calc/warnings.ts): rate_off_dictionary, baza_off_dictionary, ai_low_confidence, ai_rate_taken, rate_noted, baza_from_import; calc_groups.confirm_via: 'single' | 'bulk'
- UpsaleState (upsale-service.ts): paid | payable | awaiting_payment | no_invoice | no_deal; UpsaleScope / CalcControlScope: 'all' | 'own' | 'none'
- calc_offers.locale / ClientLocale: 'uz' | 'ru' | 'en' (offerLocaleFor defaults to 'uz'); calc_versions.section: yolkira | rastamojka | podklyuch; receipts.calc_link_source: 'auto' | 'person'; calc_requests.completed_via: lines | task | returned | sealed
- Constants: MONEY_EPSILON = 0.009 (upsale.ts); UPSALE_CAP = 300 (upsale-service.ts); REGISTRY_CAP = 200 (chain.ts); LINK_IMPLAUSIBLE_FACTOR = 3 (link.ts); QUOTE_VALID_DAYS_DEFAULT = 30 (workspace.ts); OFFICE_TIME_ZONE = 'Asia/Tashkent' (offer.ts); CLAIM_KEY = 'calc_review_notified_month' (review.ts); chain depth cap 64 (chain.ts treeSql); offersFor LIMIT 10; quoteHistoryFor default limit 5 (narxlar passes 10); lastQuotesByCode perCode default 3 (last-quotes passes 5); linkSuggestions limit 50; warnedGroups limit 100; calcActuals since default 90 days, limit 200; recalc due_at +2h
- Settings keys (src/modules/platform/settings/service.ts): quote_valid_days=30, calc_actual_settle_days=7, calc_customs_deviation_pct=15, calc_customs_cost_type_codes='["customs"]', upsale_expense_category_id='', deal_deviation_threshold_pct=10, calc_review_notified_month (claim), company_name / company_address / company_phone (PDF), SETTINGS_AUDIT_ID='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'

### Gaps (not built / cut / owed / suspect)

- `CalcSealed` and `CalcDiscounted` are emitted by announceSeal (src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts announceSeal) but appear in NO MUTE_GROUPS list (src/modules/platform/notifications/mutes.ts lists CalcOverdue, CalcBelowFloor in alerts; CalcDictReview, CalcOffer in operations; CalcRequested/CalcReturned/CalcPrefilled at the top) — they cannot be muted per user and are not in the muteable vocabulary.
- Untranslated refusal codes: components print `t.has('errors.'+code) ? t(...) : code` from the `calc` namespace, and messages/ru.json `calc.errors.*` has NO key for `superseded` (thrown by recordOffer answer branch and releaseOffer), `category_not_found` (setUpsaleCategory), `fx_missing` and `account_currency_mismatch` (payUpsale — `fx_missing` exists only under deals.errors); the raw code prints on /upsale and the card panel. Also nazorat's CalcLinkRow prints the CalcLinkError code raw (`<span>{error}</span>`, calc-link-row.tsx:47) and receipts/[id]/calc-link.tsx prints a generic tc('error') — none of receipt_not_found/request_not_found/request_foreign/no_deal/not_mine has words.
- `UpsaleRow.floorUsd` is populated for scope 'own' (upsale-service.ts upsaleRows maps money(r.total_usd) unconditionally); only the page hides it (`scope === 'all'`). The seller REPORT's own return TYPE 'cannot carry a cost-derived figure at all' (VED.md) — the upsale row type does not have that property; the floor reaches the server component render even though it is not drawn.
- E2 (VED.md 'E2 — AI control') is NOT built: history-dependent suspicious rules, per-worker accuracy ranking, the workspace sidebar's ACTUALS column, the Telegram digest. Precondition stated: ≥20 sealed versions with confirmed links and settled cargo, non-empty calc_price_book. Per-TNVED calc-vs-actual and the nightly AI pass are CUT ('stated to the owner').
- Law 4's 'Visibility: owner + accountant + the seller (own only)': in code upsaleScopeFor gives 'own' to ANY holder of crm.leads or clients.manage — so the seeded `logist` role (crm.leads + clients.manage) reads /upsale as a seller (tests/unit/upsale-scope.test.ts EXPECTED logist: 'own', with the comment 'they are a seller here by the owner's own matrix').
- `setCalcLinkAction` on the prixod card is gated on calcControlScopeFor (nazorat/actions.ts comment: 'STATED to the owner as a default and not a law: he is being asked whether the SELLER should be able to answer this too') — the seller cannot link cargo to a calculation today; owner's answer not recorded in code.
- The card `/bitimlar/[id]` prints the sealed floor with no ownership gate of its own (CLAUDE.md 'STATED, not fixed'; history.ts QuoteHistoryRow.cardReadable comment 'A card link is a door to the floor: `/bitimlar/<id>` prints it') — quoteHistoryFor hides the link for scope 'own' but the URL itself is open to any canWriteDeal holder.
- `/api/calc/[versionId]/offer.pdf` and `/api/calc/offer/[offerId]/pdf` differ in one header spelling (lowercase `content-disposition: inline; filename="offer-<8>.pdf"` vs `Content-Disposition: inline; filename="taklif.pdf"`) — harmless but two files claim to 'print the same paper'.
- recordOffer's answer branch computes validUntil from `quote_valid_days` at offer time and the by-id PDF route recomputes it the same way at render time; if the setting changes between the two, the Telegram text and the later PDF print different validity dates (no stored valid_until on an answer-anchored offer).
- reopenUpsaleForExpense is called `.catch(() => undefined)` from voidExpense — a failure there leaves offers reading paid with no alarm (best-effort by design, but silent).
- upsaleLiability() is exported but its consumer was not verified in this read (company balance sheet) — check `companyBalance` wiring before relying on it.
- Owner still owes production data: the VED must fill /hisoblash/lugatlar (bazas, price book) and pick the «sotuvchi ulushi» expense category on /upsale before payUpsale works (CLAUDE.md 'Still owed after that deploy').

### Docs-vs-code disagreements

- CLAUDE.md:1185 (VED 2.0 phase 4 paragraph) says `offerStandsSql` has FIVE consumers '(releaseOffer's CLAIM, releasedPriceFor, currentOfferFor, bothFiguresForDeals, the PDF)'. In the tree: `currentOfferFor` does not exist anywhere (grep of src/, tests/, docs/ returns nothing outside CLAUDE.md), and neither PDF route uses offerStandsSql — both use `releasedOfferWhere()` only (src/app/api/calc/[versionId]/offer.pdf/route.ts:88, src/app/api/calc/offer/[offerId]/pdf/route.ts:63) and the by-id route's comment says 'A SUPERSEDED offer's sheet deliberately still prints'. Actual consumers of offerStandsSql: releaseOffer's claim (workspace.ts ~3617), releasedPriceFor (workspace.ts ~3760), bothFiguresForDeals (upsale-service.ts:442). The task prompt's own list inherits the same phantom name.
- docs/VED.md:226-229 (phase E1) and CLAUDE.md (E1 paragraph) say the join has 'ONE writer (`stampCalcLink`, called from `createReceipt`, `linkReceipt` and `sealCalculation`)'. In code: `stampCalcLink` is called from src/modules/wms/receipts/service.ts:416 inside `confirmReceipt` (exported at line 122; the next export is `class VoidError` at 458 — there is no `createReceipt` function in that file, though the comment at lines 409-412 calls it that) and from src/modules/wms/deals/service.ts:469 inside `linkReceipt`. There is no function named `sealCalculation`; `sealCalc` (workspace.ts:1398) does NOT call stampCalcLink — it only re-points existing links from a superseded request (workspace.ts ~1631-1636).
- docs/VED.md law 4 (line 66): 'below-floor is admin-only'. Code: `mayApproveBelowFloor` = `finance.reports` (src/modules/wms/calc/upsale-scope.ts:313-317), which the seeded matrix gives to super_admin, admin AND accountant (catalog.ts:184-194); the file's own comment names 'the owner, the admins and the accountant'. Fenced as such by tests/unit/upsale-scope.test.ts (accountant 'all').
- docs/VED.md law 2 (line 52-53): 'Only admin can [change]; a change is audited AND notified to the owner.' Code: the change door is `recalcAction` gated on `admin.settings.manage` (hisoblash/actions.ts:457-462) and the NOTIFICATION on a correction is only the ordinary seal announce (`CalcSealed` to the requester; `CalcDiscounted` to owner/accountant only when a concession is present) — a plain re-seal at a different price notifies the owner nothing beyond the audit row.
- docs/VED.md law 4 (line 70-71): 'Visibility: owner + accountant + the seller (own only)'. Code: `upsaleScopeFor` returns 'own' for `crm.leads || clients.manage`, so the `logist` role (catalog.ts:123-142) also reads /upsale as 'own' — documented in tests/unit/upsale-scope.test.ts EXPECTED but not in VED.md.
- CLAUDE.md (round 112 item 5) says 'The phase-D «carries NONE» fixture moved to quote AT the floor' and (phase C) 'quoting below the sealed floor is ALLOWED and FLAGGED' — consistent with code; but CLAUDE.md phase C also says the offer 'records what a seller actually told a customer: quoting below the sealed floor is ALLOWED', whereas since 0088 a below-floor offer by a non-approver sends NOTHING (recordOffer returns text:null, pending:true) — the row is recorded, the customer is not told. Both sentences are true of different layers; read the code's 'PROMISE, not the record' framing (workspace.ts recordOffer comment).
- docs/VED.md 'Screens and flows' (line 185-186): 'Card (lead/deal): sealed VED price + versions + validity; seller's client-price field → upsale computed; one-tap client offer sheet'. Code: the card panel (calc-panel.tsx) shows the seal, the chain (V{n} + previous), the offer form and the offer list — but the previous version's MONEY prints only for mayReadCalcRegistry readers (line 175), and 'versions' as a list is on the workspace (calc-chain), not the card.


## docs-decisions-deploy

### Summary
This is the WRITTEN RECORD of the Telegram/AI VED calculation line: what the owner fixed, the laws, the migration ledger, the AI configuration, what is cut or owed, and how a change is verified, shipped and deployed. The truth lives in four documents plus DECISIONS.md: docs/VED.md (agreed 2026-08-13/22; laws 1-11, the freight tariff, the four dictionaries, phases A-E2), docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md (agreed 2026-09-04; the owner's verbatim request and SEVEN fixed answers for the quarterly customs import + the staff-bot AI VED hodimi, sub-rounds A and B both SHIPPED), docs/DEPLOY.md (deploy + the migration-count rule), CLAUDE.md (hard rules, commands, ritual, footguns, the State paragraphs) and DECISIONS.md #748-#925 (phase A #748-751, B #767-779, C #780-792, D #793-803, E1 #804-825, whole-module audit #826-830, VED 2.0 law engine #854-860, table workspace #861-867, measure row #868-873, round 112 #874-882, phase 4 #883-888, import sub-round A #889-896, AI VED hodimi #897-910, post-merge audit #911-914, heartbeat #915-918, second audit #919-920, picker #921-922, tarix/V2 chain #923-925). The chain of custody the record enforces: the bot (staff-handlers.ts, 🧮 Hisoblatish) COLLECTS and lands a job via landIntake → openCalcRequest into the ONE queue (calc_requests, 0085); the landing enqueues JOB_CALC_PREFILL ('calc.prefill') with the request's rev; the worker (calc/jobs.ts registerCalcPrefillWorker) runs aiPrefill with actorId:null after prefillStanding says 'ok', the model proposes codes/rows only (proposeGroups → priceProposedGroups → saveTable; pickImportRows answers an INDEX into real declarations), rates come from PP-3818 (calc_rates, 0091 seed), bazas from the newest READY customs_import_batches (0094/0095), and the reply (prefill-reply.ts prefillReplyText) goes back through notifyStaffTelegram type 'CalcPrefilled' with «⚠️ Rasmiy emas — VED xodimi tasdiqlaydi» and never a $0; the request STAYS in the VED queue, every group unconfirmed, until the VED's ✅ (confirmGroup, recording confirmed_warnings/confirm_via) and seal (sealCalc → calc_versions). Production: the owner's server is at ledger 95 (confirmed «deploy qildim 95 chiqdi», 2026-09-04 evening) carrying PRs #71/#72; main's journal is at 96 (0095 heartbeat merged via PR #73, plus #74-#76 merged, not yet deployed); PR #77 (the /hisoblash/tarix registry + V2 chain, no migration) is OPEN as a DRAFT on branch claude/gsr-logistics-wms-phase1-o8h4en, 4 commits ahead of origin/main. AI configuration is one env var (ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) with the model ids as constants in code; the assistant's daily cap is the setting ai_daily_limit (40) reserved atomically in ai_questions, and it does NOT cover the calc intake/prefill calls (v1 logs usage instead). Several statements in CLAUDE.md, DEPLOY.md and VED-IMPORT-AI.md are stale against the code and the ledger; they are listed with line numbers under disagreements.


### Files and exports


#### `docs/VED.md` — The agreed VED specification (2026-08-13/22): the owner's problem statement, the «what already exists — REUSE» table, the 11 numbered laws, the freight tariff table (with the 2026-08-23 correction to 701-999), the four dictionaries, screens/flows, the three error-control moments, build phases A-E2 with shipped notes for D (0088) and E1 (0089), and the house rules that bite.

- **`laws 1-11`** `prose` — Binding product rules; every one has an enforcing fence in code (see invariants).
- **`phase E2 precondition`** `prose` — «≥20 sealed versions with confirmed links and settled cargo, and a non-empty calc_price_book» — E2 is not started until then; per-TNVED comparison and the nightly AI pass are CUT.

#### `docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md` — The customs-IMPORT baza + AI VED hodimi spec (agreed 2026-09-04): §0 the owner's verbatim request and his SEVEN fixed answers; §1 the measured file (24 headers verbatim, units, quarter); §2 sub-round A (migration 0094, admin upload route, streaming pg-boss job, suggestImportBaza, workspace wiring, tests) with a SHIPPED note listing what differs; §3 sub-round B (the staff-bot AI pass) with its SHIPPED note; §4 the binding laws; §5 the verbatim build ritual; §6 open items / stated cuts.

- **`§5 Build discipline`** `prose` — The ritual: pg_ctl start line, DATABASE_URL postgres://postgres@127.0.0.1:5432/gsr_ci, CI-order pipeline, ${PIPESTATUS[0]} per gate, typecheck before push, rm -rf .next/cache/eslint, string-edit red proofs, pgboss maintained_on fix, fetch main before minting a migration/DECISIONS number, ship pattern push → draft PR → subscribe_pr_activity → green → merge → Uzbek deploy commands ending with the LEDGER COUNT.
- **`§6 Open items / stated cuts`** `prose` — See gaps.

#### `docs/DEPLOY.md` — Owner-facing deploy runbook in Uzbek: bootstrap, create-admin, tg-listen, the migration-count rule (`SELECT count(*) FROM drizzle.__drizzle_migrations` must equal the journal length; fix with `docker compose run --rm migrate`), one-off scripts through the `migrate` image never `app`, the VPS move, the second-disk procedure, HTTPS-only.

- **`migration-count rule`** `psql -tAc "select count(*) from drizzle.__drizzle_migrations"` — Must equal the journal length after every deploy; `migrate` is restart:'no' so a failed migration is silent; a restored dump carries the OLD ledger so the step after a restore is mandatory.

#### `docs/TELEGRAM-CRM.md` — Client-conversation import/listen/reply doc (Uzbek). Staff-bot content is NOT here — it holds only the manager-account listener (`tg-listen`, TELEGRAM_API_ID/HASH, TG_SESSION_KEY) and the rule «Avtomatik javob — hech qanday robot mijozga o'zi yozmaydi» (line 315), which is the client-facing-AI refusal restated.


#### `CLAUDE.md` — The map: Hard rules (lines 27-39), Stack (41-52), Commands (54-59), Verification ritual (61-74), Footguns (76-121), Where the truth lives (123-134), then the State paragraphs (VED phases A-E1 at 136-200ff, rounds 100-112, VED 2.0 phases 1-4, sub-rounds A/B, heartbeat, audits, tarix; production ledger line 1478 «HIS SERVER IS AT 95»).

- **`Hard rules`** `prose` — Never put an API key/bot token in chat/repo/commit; never `docker compose down -v`; never write the model identifier into a commit message, PR, code comment or pushed artifact; verified non-zero backup before every production update; work on the branch told; commits end with Co-Authored-By + Claude-Session trailers; no AskUserQuestion dialogs, ask in plain Uzbek.

#### `DECISIONS.md` — The numbered decision log, one entry per line, newest last; tail is #925 (line 1560). Entries are `NNN. **title** body`.

- **`#748-751`** `lines 1202-1208` — Phase A: the request becomes a JOB; 0052's one-open-per-card index DROPPED; queue owns assignment (fewest-open ved.docs holder); claim IS the UPDATE; clock ends 'lines'|'task'|'returned'; speed filters completed_via <> 'returned'; #749 lead-note attachment gate widened for ved.docs ONLY when calc_requests.note_id points at it, note_taken/note_foreign refusals, ved.docs stated as a four-power bundle; #750 isServerBehind learns 42703; #751 db.execute returns timestamps as STRINGS.
- **`#767-779`** `lines 1241-1266` — Phase B: draft vs FACT, the seal the one door, no earliest-row fallback, engine never invents a number, tariff seeded verbatim with band_missing/band_ambiguous refusals, seal one tx no re-open, lock is real, model proposes words never a number, «Bajarildi» stays, #776 tariff contiguous, #777 NaN through three layers, #778 sealed request follows a won lead.
- **`#780-792`** `lines 1269-1292` — Phase C: price book keyed on TNVED CODE; #781 offer = seller's price, seal = floor, below-floor allowed+flagged, no total/decomposition/emoji in offer text; #788 PDF font lacks Uzbek Cyrillic; #789 offers must re-key on won lead; #790 phase C leaked client price to the VED via canWriteDeal (ved.docs is in DEAL_WRITE_PERMISSIONS); #791 upsaleScopeFor keys on finance.reports not seesAllMoney.
- **`#793-803`** `lines 1295-1316` — Phase D: upsale DERIVED never stored; payableOffersSql one home; below-floor locks the PROMISE; released offer writes client price onto the card; payout = expenses row in a mandatory category; #803 PROCESS: chained gates, read only the second.
- **`#804-825`** `lines 1317-1360` — Phase E1: money comparison CUSTOMS ONLY (freight is margin, +78% forever); join at RECEIPT grain with ONE writer stampCalcLink; auto link never scores; ✅ records confirmed_warnings/confirm_via; six refusals; two clocks; #816 arrival CTE could never settle (ready_for_pickup not in_stock); #820 calc_customs_deviation_pct had no reader.
- **`#854-860`** `lines 1417-1430` — VED 2.0 phase 1: duty in four shapes (duty_mode advalor|specific|max|plus), 1,489 PP-3818 rows seeded ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING with vat_pct 12 and fee_usd 0, longest-prefix lookup, additional duty inclusive-HIGH bands with lgota killing it, BHM fee per DECLARATION inside customs_usd with fee_fx_missing, engine refuses litr/juft/sm3/m2 (retired by 0092), rate_off_dictionary compares VALUES.
- **`#889-910`** `lines 1487-1531` — Sub-round A (import) and B (AI VED hodimi): see invariants/gaps; #906 the central fix never ran (ai_running claim), #907 checklist unsatisfiable + invented refusal words, #908 rev-clock stand-down, #909 pick reason unshown/usage logged, #910 itemMeasure one field.
- **`#911-925`** `lines 1532-1560` — Post-merge audits (#911 yolkira $0 + customs_on_yolkira, #912 pick overwrote a typed baza / machine wrote as the seller / measure never closed, #913 subset must name subset, #914 empty groups = nothing priced), heartbeat (#915-918), audit (#919 race leak 1391→796 MB, #920 two clocks + 'failed' terminal), picker (#921-922), tarix (#923 V-rank DERIVED, #924 registry door boolean, #925 recalc from newest link).

#### `src/modules/platform/db/migrations/meta/_journal.json` — Drizzle journal; 96 entries; tail idx 95 tag 0095_customs_import_heartbeat when 1785190000074. Entries 0085..0095 use whens 1785190000055-59, 60, 70-74.


#### `src/modules/platform/settings/service.ts` — SETTING_DEFAULTS — the settings keys the calc/AI line reads, each with its owner-facing rationale comment.

- **`getSetting`** `getSetting<K extends SettingKey>(key: K): Promise<SettingValue<K>>` — Runs on the POOL — never call inside a transaction (#714 freeze); calc's client_code_next is read via tx.execute for that reason.

#### `src/modules/platform/ai/model.ts` — The injectable model call + the AI config predicate and tier constants.

- **`aiConfigured`** `aiConfigured(): boolean` — Boolean(process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) — the ONE config check; absent key = honest «sozlanmagan», never a crash.
- **`ANALYST_MODEL / STAFF_MODEL`** `const ANALYST_MODEL = 'claude-opus-5'; const STAFF_MODEL = 'claude-sonnet-5'` — Tier 2 (super_admin/admin analyst with run_sql) vs tier 1 (staff lookup).
- **`realCallModel`** `CallModel = (req: ModelRequest) => Promise<ModelResponse>` — new Anthropic({ timeout: 60_000, maxRetries: 1 }) — CALL_TIMEOUT_MS 60 s; the loop's wall clock is 120 s.

#### `src/modules/platform/ai/assistant.ts` — The tier-1/2 assistant loop; reserves the daily cap by INSERTing into ai_questions before the model is called.

- **`reserveQuestion (internal)`** `INSERT INTO ai_questions (user_id, surface, question) … WHERE (SELECT count(*) FROM ai_questions …) < ${limit}` — Atomic cap — limit = Number(getSetting('ai_daily_limit')); status 'limit' when refused. Applies to bot text fallback + /ai only, NOT to intake/prefill.

#### `src/modules/platform/ai/vet.ts` — Gate in front of model-written SQL (tier 2).

- **`vetAnalystSql`** `vetAnalystSql(raw: string): { ok: true; sql } | { ok: false; reason: VetRefusal }` — VetRefusal = 'empty'|'too_long'|'multi_statement'|'comment'|'not_select'; MAX_ANALYST_SQL 4000; the confidentiality boundary is the gsr_ai_reader role (0080), not this vet.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/jobs.ts` — The three calc pg-boss jobs.

- **`JOB_CALC_OVERDUE / JOB_CALC_REVIEW / JOB_CALC_PREFILL`** `'calc.overdue' | 'calc.dict-review' | 'calc.prefill'` — overdue: schedule '*/5 * * * *' → notifyOverdueCalcs (claims rows in the UPDATE, 0082's rule); review: '0 6 * * *' daily, claims the MONTH via setting calc_review_notified_month → notifyDictionaryReview; prefill: no schedule, work<CalcPrefillJob>.
- **`CalcPrefillJob`** `{ requestId: string; staffId: string; rev?: number | null }` — rev = the revision at queue time (prefillTicket); the worker calls prefillStanding first and stands down on anything but 'ok'; aiPrefill(requestId, { actorId: null }); answer via notifyStaffTelegram({ userIds:[staffId], type:'CalcPrefilled', text }); failure logged, NOT rethrown.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/prefill.ts` — The AI VED hodimi pass — every write through proposeGroups/saveTable; writes no provenance of its own.

- **`prefillStanding`** `prefillStanding(requestId: string, expectRev: number | null): Promise<PrefillStand>` — PrefillStand = 'ok'|'touched'|'confirmed'|'closed'|'not_found'; 'touched' when calc_requests.rev moved; 'confirmed' when any calc_groups.confirmed_at is set.
- **`prefillTicket`** `prefillTicket(requestId: string): Promise<number | null>` — Reads calc_requests.rev to stamp on the job.
- **`aiPrefill`** `aiPrefill(requestId: string, ctx: AuditContext, deps: PrefillDeps = {}): Promise<PrefillOutcome>` — PrefillDeps { propose?: typeof proposeGroups; pick?: (rows: PickRequest[]) => Promise<PickAnswer[]|null>; configured?: boolean }. PrefillOutcome { text, customsUsd, freightUsd, codesStamped, ratesPulled, importFilled, picked, aiUsed }. MAX_PICK_ROWS = 40 (capped rows named in the reply). Skips the whole customs half on a yolkira section (#911); treats zero groups as nothing priced (#914); drops picks over rows a person filled meanwhile (#912a).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/prefill-ai.ts` — The model's pick over import candidates (structured output).

- **`pickImportRows`** `pickImportRows(rows: PickRequest[], opts: { timeoutMs?: number } = {}): Promise<PickAnswer[] | null>` — null when !process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY; model 'claude-opus-5', timeout 60_000, maxRetries 1; answer keys are exactly seq/candidate/reason — no price-shaped key (pinned by ai-advisory.test.ts); ONE call per request; logs usage.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/prefill-reply.ts` — Pure reply shaper for the staff chat (Uzbek strings, no bundle).

- **`blockerText`** `blockerText(b: SealBlocker): string` — Literal maps keyed on the engine's unions: CUSTOMS_REASON: Record<CustomsRefusal,string> (baza_missing→'baza yo‘q', measure_missing, rates_missing→'bu kodga stavka topilmadi', group_empty, not_a_number); FREIGHT_REASON: Record<FreightRefusal,string> (zone_required→'yo‘nalish (zona) tanlanmagan', measure_missing, band_missing, band_ambiguous, not_a_number); FEE_REASON (fee_fx_missing→'so‘m kursi yo‘q'); TOTALS_REASON (discount_exceeds_total); customs_on_yolkira→'bu bo‘limda rastamojka hisoblanmaydi'.
- **`prefillReplyText`** `prefillReplyText(input: PrefillReplyInput): string` — PrefillReplyInput { customsUsd, freightUsd, hasFreight, hasCustoms (REQUIRED), blockers, codesStamped, ratesPulled, importFilled, link, aiConfigured, pickCapped?, pickRefused?, pickOvertaken? }. Lines: '🧮 Tahminiy: rastamojka ~$X (N ta tovarsiz) · yo‘lkira ~$Y' + '⚠️ Rasmiy emas — VED xodimi tasdiqlaydi.' | '🧮 Hozircha hisoblab bo‘lmadi.'; 'Yo‘lkira hali hisoblanmadi.'; 'AI qo‘ydi: N ta kod · N ta stavka · N ta baza (import)'; 'AI sozlanmagan — faqat yozilganidan o‘qildi.'; 'Bazasi qo‘yilmadi: …'; 'Yetishmayapti: …' capped at six + '; …'; the card link. Never a $0.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/intake.ts` — Pure decisions of the bot's «Hisoblatish» collection.

- **`REQUIRED_FIELDS`** `Record<CalcSection, CalcField[]>` — yolkira: fromCity,toCity,weightKg,volumeM3,goods; rastamojka: weightKg,volumeM3,goods,itemMeasure; podklyuch: all six. CalcSection = 'yolkira'|'rastamojka'|'podklyuch'; SECTION_LABEL '🚚 Yo‘lkira' / '🛃 Rastamojka' / '🔑 Podklyuch (yo‘lkira + rastamojka)'; FIELD_LABEL itemMeasure = 'tovarning soni yoki og‘irligi'.
- **`loneWeightKg / itemFacts / missingFields / isComplete`** `loneWeightKg(itemCount, totalKg); itemFacts(facts: CalcFacts): CalcItemFact[]; missingFields(section, facts): CalcField[]; isComplete(section, facts): boolean` — A single line's weight IS the shipment's (derived, not asked); itemMeasure is satisfied by a count OR a weight OR the 0092 measure pair (#910/#912c). MATERIAL_NOTE_CAP = 20_000.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/intake-ai.ts` — The intake's model extraction (never prices).

- **`analyzeIntake`** `analyzeIntake(input: {…; timeoutMs?: number}): Promise<AiIntakeResult | null>` — null when !ANTHROPIC_API_KEY; new Anthropic({ timeout: input.timeoutMs ?? 60_000, maxRetries: 1 }); model 'claude-opus-5'; dispatched OFF grammy's sequential poller (bot-ai-dispatch.test.ts).

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/staff-handlers.ts` — grammy shell of the staff bot; the 🧮 flow and the prefill enqueue.

- **`HISOBLATISH (const)`** `'🧮 Hisoblatish'` — Keyboard label; '✅ Tasdiqlash' callback 'c:save'; «Bo'ldi» is one live edited-in-place control; stale flow → 'Bu so‘rov eskirgan. «🧮 Hisoblatish» tugmasidan qaytadan boshlang.'
- **`queuePrefill (internal)`** `queuePrefill(requestId: string, staffId: string): Promise<void>` — rev = await prefillTicket(requestId); enqueue(JOB_CALC_PREFILL, { requestId, staffId, rev }); failure only warns — the seller's confirmation must not be lost. Called when target.queued && target.requestId after landIntake; the reply reads '✅ Saqlandi — bitim|lead: <label>\n<APP_URL>/bitimlar|crm/leads/<id>\n\n🧮 Hisoblash navbatiga tushdi — VED xodimi javob beradi.' or '⚠️ Hisoblash navbatiga tushmadi — kartadan qo‘lda yuboring.'

#### `src/modules/platform/telegram/calc-intake.ts` — In-memory collection state (30-min TTL; a bot restart loses typed text — stated).

- **`mayCollect`** `mayCollect(chatId: bigint): Promise<boolean>` — «Everyone is (owner's answer 3) — the only question is whether they are staff at all»: staffForChat(chatId) !== null. MAX_INTAKE_IMAGES = 6; IntakeStage = 'section'|'client'|'material'|'review'.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/pricing.ts` — The pure engine; cannot name an ai identifier (fenced).

- **`refusal unions`** `CustomsRefusal = 'group_empty'|'rates_missing'|'not_a_number'|'baza_missing'|'measure_missing' (+itemSeq/itemLabel); BandRefusal = 'band_missing'|'band_ambiguous'; FreightRefusal = 'zone_required'|'measure_missing'|'not_a_number'|BandRefusal; FeeRefusal = 'fee_fx_missing'|'not_a_number'; TotalsRefusal = 'discount_exceeds_total'|'not_a_number'` — Every entry point answers {ok} or {ok:false, reason}; BazaSource = 'dictionary'|'typed'|'import'|null; DutyMode = 'advalor'|'specific'|'max'|'plus'; DutyUnit = 'kg'|'dona'|'litr'|'juft'|'1000_dona'|'sm3'|'m2'; MeasureUnit = 'juft'|'litr'|'m2'|'sm3'; SMALL_FREIGHT_USD = 50; FEE_TIERS table; bandDensityOf = Math.round.

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts` — The workspace, seal, offer and the two AI doors.

- **`SealBlocker`** `{kind:'section_missing'}|{kind:'no_groups'}|{kind:'ungrouped_items';count}|{kind:'groups_unconfirmed';count}|{kind:'customs';groupSeq;groupLabel;reason;itemLabel?}|{kind:'freight';reason}|{kind:'fee';reason}|{kind:'totals';reason}|{kind:'customs_on_yolkira'}` — reason is typed string — hence the Record<Union,string> maps in prefill-reply.ts.
- **`proposeGroups / priceProposedGroups / applyProposal / saveTable / sealCalc / canSeal / recalcFromSealed`** `proposeGroups(...); priceProposedGroups(...); applyProposal(requestId, drafts: DraftGroup[], ctx); saveTable(...); sealCalc(...); canSeal(workspace): boolean; recalcFromSealed(...)` — AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS = 10*60_000 (the ai_proposal_started_at claim heals on the same window as lockRequestInTx); the claim is released after the model call and BEFORE the pricing tail (#906); applyProposal writes aiDutyPct/aiProposal and never dutyPct/vatPct/feeUsd/bazaUsd/rateSource; QUOTE_VALID_DAYS_DEFAULT = 30; recalcFromSealed refuses not_sealed/recalc_open/recalc_superseded (#925).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/warnings.ts` — Pure warning predicates shared by workspace, confirm, seal counters and control screen.

- **`CalcWarningKind`** `'rate_off_dictionary'|'baza_off_dictionary'|'ai_low_confidence'|'ai_rate_taken'|'rate_noted'|'baza_from_import'` — baza_from_import carries NO dictionary clause on purpose — it means nobody stated the price. Label key: labels.ts baza_from_import → 'warnings.bazaFromImport'.

- **`mayReadCalcRegistry`** `mayReadCalcRegistry(actor): boolean` — finance.reports || ved.docs — owner's answer 2A (owner + accountant + VED, never sellers).

#### `src/modules/wms/calc/upsale-scope.ts` — Law 4's who-sees-prices predicates.

- **`upsaleScopeFor / mayOffer / mayApproveBelowFloor`** `upsaleScopeFor(actor): 'all'|'own'|'none'; mayOffer(actor): boolean; mayApproveBelowFloor(actor): boolean` — finance.reports → all; crm.leads||clients.manage → own; else (the VED) none. mayApproveBelowFloor = finance.reports (NOT finance.debt_override).

- **`JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT / JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT_SWEEP`** `'customs.import' | 'customs.import-sweep'` — work<CustomsImportJob>({includeMetadata:true}); CustomsImportJob { batchId, storageKey, fileName }; CustomsImportError reasons 'missing_columns'|'no_rows'|'unreadable'|'in_use' → importFailureText sentences; BatchGoneError stands down; LAST attempt writes 'Xatolik: …'; enqueue per-job options give the import 2 h / 2 attempts (#916).

#### `src/modules/wms/customs/import-service.ts` — Streaming parse, heartbeat, sweep, batch lifecycle.

#### `src/modules/wms/customs/import-baza.ts` — The suggestion service (docs said calc/; lives under customs/).

- **`suggestImportBaza / unitsForRow / importRowForCode`** `suggestImportBaza(input, opts?): Promise<ImportBazaSuggestion>; unitsForRow(input): ImportUnit[]; importRowForCode(...)` — Newest READY batch only; exact code; word_similarity with MIN_NEEDLE 4; opts.picker lists 50 with nameSim 0 (auto never fires); BASIS_FOR_UNIT / UNIT_FOR_BASIS maps; mentions no ai identifier (fenced).

#### `src/modules/platform/db/migrations/0080_ai_assistant.sql` — The AI round's schema: ai_questions ledger, gsr_ai_reader role, v_client_balance_usd view.

- **`gsr_ai_reader`** `CREATE ROLE gsr_ai_reader NOLOGIN (guarded IF NOT EXISTS; cluster-wide); GRANT SELECT ON <allowlist> …; column grants on users/lead_sources minus password/secret columns; RAISE EXCEPTION if a dblink/fdw extension is installed` — Default-deny; 0088 deliberately grants NOTHING on calc_offers/calc_versions (every margin in the company). ai_questions(user_id NOT NULL, surface CHECK IN ('bot','web'), question, answer, ok, tool_log, created_at) + index (user_id, created_at).

#### `.github/workflows/ci.yml` — CI = the verification oracle: typecheck → lint → db:migrate → db:seed → db:seed:demo → test → build → playwright install → `CI=1 pnpm e2e` on ONE postgres:16 db gsr_test; timeout 90 min; env DATABASE_URL, SESSION_SECRET, STORAGE_DRIVER=local, APP_URL — NO ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, so every model path is proven with the model mocked.


### Flows


**Verification ritual (every change, before push)**

1. Start postgres if dead: `su postgres -c "/usr/lib/postgresql/16/bin/pg_ctl -D /var/local/pg/data -l /var/local/pg/log -o '-k /tmp' start"` (CLAUDE.md ritual 1; VED-IMPORT-AI §5 writes the log path as /var/local/pg/log/pg.log).
2. Use a THROWAWAY database: DATABASE_URL=postgres://postgres@127.0.0.1:5432/gsr_ci (TCP; `?host=/tmp` breaks postgres.js). gsr_dev is the owner's real imported data — never test on it. A long-lived local db is a different oracle from CI (#653, #910).
3. Rebuild in CI's order: drop/create gsr_ci → `pnpm db:migrate` → `pnpm db:seed` → `SEED_DEMO=1 pnpm db:seed:demo` → FULL `pnpm vitest run` → `pnpm build` → `fuser -k 3000/tcp` → `pnpm e2e` WITHOUT CI=1 (CI=1 selects a headless shell this container lacks). Playwright runs WITHOUT re-seeding — state a spec leaves behind is the next spec's input (#154/#183).
4. Gate discipline (#738/#803/#918): run gates with `&&`, read each gate's OWN failure marker; `pnpm lint | tail` and `cmd | tail && echo $?` report the PIPELINE's code — use `${PIPESTATUS[0]}`. `pnpm typecheck` BEFORE THE COMMIT (not only before push): vitest transpiles without checking and `next build` types src/ only, so a test-file type error is green locally and red in CI (#591, #905).
5. When `next build` lint contradicts `pnpm lint`: `rm -rf .next/cache/eslint` (#871 — the cache serves pre-edit diagnostics).
6. Red proofs: every fix must be SHOWN red without it. Undo a red proof by a STRING EDIT, never `git checkout` (#430 — broken five times; #905: COMMIT the surgery before running any proof; use a one-occurrence swap helper that refuses on 0 or 2 matches). A red proof that will not go red is evidence about the fixture (#166). Fences must strip comments first (#725/#920) and assert callers EXIST before checking them (#494/#902).
7. Never run two vitest runs against one database (#911 — contention produced a red that was not the code's; the tell is a stack line number that does not match the file).
8. Playwright webServer timing out at 60 s locally = stale `pgboss.version.maintained_on` → `UPDATE pgboss.version SET maintained_on = now()` (or a fresh db).
9. Look at the screen: screenshots at 360×800 AND 1280×900, document width must equal the viewport (#400 page-rescale); a permission fix is half-verified until a NON-admin role opens it in a browser (#792); a PDF is verified by rendering it (pypdfium2, #881).
10. Before minting a migration or DECISIONS number: `git fetch origin main`, read the tail of `src/modules/platform/db/migrations/meta/_journal.json` (idx 95 / when 1785190000074 / 0095_customs_import_heartbeat) AND the tail of DECISIONS.md (#925) — fourteen collisions so far; a collision needs the `when` moved too, not only the name.
11. Record: DECISIONS entries (newest last), CHANGELOG entry in Uzbek (newest first, with the test counts, red-proof count and the ledger number), CLAUDE.md State paragraph — committed as «Yozuvlar: DECISIONS #…, CHANGELOG, holat».

**Ship: commit → PR → merge**

1. Branch: `claude/gsr-logistics-wms-phase1-o8h4en` (all VED PRs #68-#77 came from this one branch; work on the branch you were told to).
2. Commit subject in Uzbek, body says why; every commit ends with the two trailers `Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>` and `Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01GKDEroAfbcbPjTyydp62pL`. Never the model identifier in a commit message, PR, code comment or pushed artifact; never a key or token.
3. Push → open a DRAFT PR with an Uzbek title (PR #77 is `draft: true`; merged ones show draft:false after being marked ready), body ending with `🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)` + the session URL → `subscribe_pr_activity` → drive CI to green (CI runs ~35 min; a PR that CONFLICTS runs NO check at all — a PR with no check is the tell of a stale branch, so merge main first).
4. Merge on green: merge commits are authored «Bekzod Kodirov» with message `Merge pull request #NN: <Uzbek title>` — merged via the API on his behalf (CLAUDE.md: «verified merged: false via the API, merged it for him»). Post-merge adversarial audits are the house pattern (#911, #919) — survivable because merging is not deploying.
5. Renumber on collision: the OTHER session merges first → DECISIONS numbers and migration idx/when move (0079→0080 etc.).

**Deploy (owner does it by hand; instructions in Uzbek ending with the LEDGER COUNT)**

1. Take a verified non-zero backup: `docker compose exec -T postgres pg_dump -U gsr -d gsr -Fc --no-owner > gsr.dump && ls -lh gsr.dump` (0 bytes = stop).
2. MERGE FIRST — `git pull` on production takes `main`; telling him to deploy is telling him to merge first (trap 1, hit four times; `git pull` answering «up to date» with the count unchanged = the PR is still open).
3. `git pull` → `docker compose build migrate app` (NEVER `build app` — `migrate` is a separate image target `build` and carries the migration files; trap 2) → `docker compose up -d`.
4. Count the ledger: `docker compose exec -T postgres psql -U gsr -d gsr -tAc "select count(*) from drizzle.__drizzle_migrations"` — must equal the journal length (96 today; his server reads 95). Short → `docker compose run --rm migrate` and count again. `migrate` is restart:'no', so a failure leaves the app on the old schema with nothing on screen; read that container's own output.
5. Restart the listener if its code changed: `docker compose --profile telegram up -d` / `logs -f tg-listen`. One-off scripts go through `docker compose run --rm migrate pnpm …`, never `app` (the standalone image has no pnpm/tsx/source).
6. Server `.env` must carry ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (the NEW key; the old one is burned), TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN, APP_URL=https://gsrwms.uz; without the key every AI surface says «sozlanmagan» and the prefill degrades to the TNVED memory.
7. After deploy, watch the first real bot pass in `docker compose logs` ([calc-prefill] lines) — no Telegram is reachable from the build container, so the live halves are proven only in production.

**The recorded AI VED hodimi flow (what the record says must happen)**

1. Staff presses «🧮 Hisoblatish» in the staff bot (staff-handlers.ts) → section → client hint → materials → «Bo'ldi» → analyzeIntake (model, off the poller, 60 s) → review → «✅ Tasdiqlash».
2. landIntake (wms/calc/intake-land.ts) lands on the newest OPEN deal (coded client) or a lead (stranger; owner = the sender), opens the request via openCalcRequest (calc_requests, source 'bot', items via itemFacts/loneWeightKg), moves the card to crm_calc_stage/deal_calc_stage if set.
3. Bot reply «✅ Saqlandi … 🧮 Hisoblash navbatiga tushdi — VED xodimi javob beradi.» then queuePrefill → enqueue(JOB_CALC_PREFILL, {requestId, staffId, rev}).
4. Worker: prefillStanding(requestId, rev) must be 'ok' → aiPrefill(requestId, {actorId:null}): (skip customs half on yolkira) proposeGroups (model groups + codes, claim released before the tail) → priceProposedGroups (pullRatesFromDictionary per coded group, then saveTable stamps codes) → saveTable SWEEP + 0094 import fill (newest READY batch, word_similarity ≥ import_baza_min_sim 0.45, unit gate) → pickImportRows over ≤40 still-empty rows (index only) → applied via TableItemEdit.importRowId → loadWorkspace → prefillReplyText.
5. notifyStaffTelegram type 'CalcPrefilled' (MUTE_GROUPS entry) to the seller; the request stays in /hisoblash queue, groups unconfirmed.
6. VED opens the pre-filled workspace, reviews 📥 chips (`calc-baza-import`), ✅ per group (confirmGroup records confirmed_warnings incl. baza_from_import, confirm_via) or confirm-all, seals (sealCalc → calc_versions; canSeal gates «Готово» with seal_instead). Official price = the seal or the typed «Готово» answer; offers/upsale/E1 read from there.

### Data model

- **`calc_requests`** (migration 0085_calc_queue / 0086 / 0091 / 0092 / 0093): 0052 base + 0085: section text CHECK IN ('yolkira','rastamojka','podklyuch') NULLABLE; source CHECK IN ('card','bot'); from_city, to_city, weight_kg numeric(12,3), volume_m3 numeric(12,3), note_id uuid (indexed calc_requests_note_idx), taken_at, return_reason, answer_amount numeric(14,2), answer_currency, answer_note, updated_at; item_count CHECK BETWEEN 0 AND 1000; completed_via CHECK IN ('lines','task','returned'); assignee_id/task_id DROP NOT NULL; DROP INDEX calc_requests_open_entity_idx. 0086: current_version_no int DEFAULT 0, supersedes_request_id uuid, freight_zone text, ai_proposal_started_at timestamptz. 0091: has_certificate, fee_override_usd. 0092: rev integer clock. 0093: answer_amount CHECK (>0 AND <> 'NaN') NOT VALID. — CHECKs: see columns; overdue_notified_at stamped on pre-0085 open rows so the sweep does not telegraph year-old requests
- **`calc_request_items`** (migration 0085 / 0086 / 0092 / 0094): 0085: request_id, seq (UNIQUE calc_request_items_seq_idx), label/name, quantity, weight_kg, tnved_code … ; 0086: group_id, baza_usd numeric(14,4), baza_basis, baza_source; 0092: measure_unit CHECK IN ('juft','litr','m2','sm3'), measure_qty CHECK (>0 AND <> 'NaN'); 0094: import_row_id bigint REFERENCES customs_import_rows(id) ON DELETE SET NULL (NO CHECK may mention it, #809) — CHECKs: calc_items_baza_source_check: baza_source IN ('dictionary','typed','import') — asserted WHOLE by ai-advisory.test.ts; no 'ai' ever
- **`calc_bazas, calc_rates, calc_freight_tariffs`** (migration 0086 / 0091): The three phase-B dictionaries, versioned by effective_date, read like fx_rates WITHOUT earliest-row fallback. calc_rates 0091: duty_mode CHECK IN ('advalor','specific','max','plus'), duty_specific numeric(12,4), duty_unit; seeded 1,489 PP-3818 rows source 'pp3818', vat_pct 12, fee_usd 0, ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING on (code, fixed date). — CHECKs: two-directional pair CHECKs on duty_mode/duty_specific/duty_unit
- **`calc_groups`** (migration 0086 / 0089 / 0091): the groups a calculation is made of; rate_source CHECK IN ('dictionary','typed'); ai_duty_pct; 0089: ai_proposal jsonb, confirmed_warnings jsonb, confirm_via CHECK IN ('single','bulk'); 0091: duty_mode/duty_specific/duty_unit snapshot (NULL = advalor for pre-0091 seals) — CHECKs: calc_groups_rate_source_check has no 'ai'
- **`calc_versions`** (migration 0086 (+0089 counters)): immutable seal snapshot: total_usd, freight_usd, freight_list_usd, customs_usd, breakdown jsonb, tariff row, sealed_at, valid_until, aiGroupsSealed/aiBlindGroups/aiRateTakenGroups counters (the ONLY ai-named values the seal writes) — CHECKs: money columns <> 'NaN'::numeric (#777); UNIQUE (request, version_no)
- **`calc_extras, calc_price_book, calc_offers`** (migration 0086 / 0087 / 0088 / 0093): calc_extras → existing cost-type dictionary; calc_price_book (tnved_code, effective_date) UNIQUE, CLIENT price; calc_offers: version_id (nullable since 0093), request_id (0093), client_price_usd, locale, text, below_floor, approved_at (0088), payout_expense_id (0088) — CHECKs: calc_offers_one_anchor_check: (version_id IS NULL) <> (request_id IS NULL)
- **`receipts (E1 join)`** (migration 0089_calc_actuals): calc_request_id uuid ON DELETE SET NULL, calc_link_source CHECK IN ('auto','person'), calc_link_confirmed_at, calc_link_confirmed_by — CHECKs: no CHECK spanning the FK column (#809)
- **`customs_import_batches`** (migration 0094 / 0095): id uuid, file_name, uploaded_by FK users, uploaded_at, status CHECK IN ('processing','ready','failed'), row_count, skipped_rows, period_from/to date, error text; 0095: heartbeat_at timestamptz (+ partial index for the sweep) — CHECKs: first beat is the CLAIM (`… AND status='processing' RETURNING`); 'failed' is terminal
- **`customs_import_rows`** (migration 0094_customs_import): id bigserial, batch_id FK ON DELETE CASCADE, tnved_code, name, name_norm (GIN gin_trgm_ops), unit CHECK IN ('kg','dona','m2','juft','litr'), price_per_unit_usd numeric(14,4) CHECK (>0 AND <> 'NaN'), weight_per_unit_kg numeric(12,4) CHECK (NULL OR (>0 AND <> 'NaN')), netto_kg, customs_value_usd, declared_at, sender, origin_country; indexes (batch_id), (batch_id, tnved_code) — CHECKs: customs_import_rows_weight_check — the constraint his own file tripped (#918, fitNumeric)
- **`ai_questions`** (migration 0080_ai_assistant): user_id NOT NULL, surface CHECK IN ('bot','web'), question, answer, ok, tool_log jsonb, created_at; index (user_id, created_at) — CHECKs: the audit AND the daily cap counter (ai_daily_limit)

- OWNER FIXED ANSWERS 1-7 (2026-09-04): 1(b) columns by HEADER NAME never position — fence customs-import-parse.test.ts header matrix, CustomsImportError 'missing_columns' names the column; 2(b) imports ACCUMULATE, newest READY answers — newestReadyBatchId(); 3(a) AUTO-FILL above the threshold marked «📥 importdan, taxmin», below it EMPTY + picker — import_baza_min_sim 0.45, testid calc-baza-import, warning baza_from_import; 4 ADMIN uploads («admin yuklab bersin fileni sistemamiz tez ishlashi kerak») — /admin/bojxona-import gated admin.dictionaries.manage, route handler /api/admin/customs-import, JOB_CUSTOMS_IMPORT; 5(a) AI hisobchi in the STAFF bot only, NO client-facing AI — mayCollect = staff only; 6 «sen aytgandek bolsin»: AI never official, «Tahminiy … (rasmiy emas)» AND the request lands in the VED queue; official price only after ✅ + seal — prefillReplyText's '⚠️ Rasmiy emas — VED xodimi tasdiqlaydi.' line, groups left unconfirmed, canSeal; 7 «sen aytgandek bolsin»: ONLY the BAZA comes from the file; duty/VAT/BHM from OUR engine — prefill.ts writes no rate; rates via pullRatesFromDictionary.
- OWNER ANSWERS ON THE BOT INTAKE (rounds 33-37, CLAUDE.md): «endi telegram botni mukammal qilishimiz kerak hodimlar ishlashi uchun»; AI intake REDESIGNED by him («hato ishlash extimoli yuqori» on reading whole chats): staff opens the BOT, «Hisoblatish» mode, forwards files/photos/texts + client info; three sections yo'lkira / rastamojka / podklyuch; rastamojka+podklyuch: AI checks completeness (kg, kub, tovar nomi); yo'lkira: from-city/to-city/kub/kg/tovar; bot entry = separate STAFF and CLIENT buttons; podklyuch = rastamojka + yo'lkira combined; «the bot COLLECTS, staff quote» (answer 2); hisoblatish for ALL staff (answer 3 → mayCollect); client button = cabinet only. - OWNER ANSWER (round 100, the AI round): «sistemamizga AI ulay olamizmi» → both tiers, both surfaces (staff bot text fallback + /ai), NO client-facing AI, read-only v1; tier 2 under gsr_ai_reader; ai_daily_limit 40. He confirmed the assistant answers live («ishlayabti») with the NEW key.

Fence: tests/unit/calc-pricing.test.ts walks every whole density 1..1500 in both zones; bandFor still refuses band_missing/band_ambiguous.

- OWNER ANSWERS (VED 2.0 phase 3, 2026-09-01): «tenved kodni olchov birligiga qarab kg dona m2 chiqsin va bazani shu yerda sorasin … yonida rastamojka summasi chiqadgan», answers 1a/2/3a/4a/5a — 1a = same code, different prices = SEPARATE ROWS (baza moved to the ROW); 0092 measure pair; requestCustomsFor live figure.
