- OWNER ANSWERS (VED 2.0 phase 4, 2026-09-04, after ledger 93/94): six post-deploy items — «Готово» answer became an offer ANCHOR (0093), defaultBasisFor in four sites, footer prints «База $2/kg», Итоговая цена big, bot GS777 full cargo picture.
- OWNER ANSWERS (tarix round, 2026-09-04): «hisoblangan narsalarning tarixini hohlayabman» → 1A sealed only (bot «Готово» answers excluded and the screen says so), 2A owner + accountant + VED, never sellers → mayReadCalcRegistry = finance.reports || ved.docs; «qayta hisoblaganda V1 turibti, V2 bo'lib chiqishi kerak emasmi» → chain.ts derives the rank by sealed_at, never stored, never backfilled.
- OWNER ANSWERS (whole-module audit, 2026-08-25): «Hisoblatishga yuborish» from a thread — «yaxshi, qilib bersang kerak» (#829; text lands verbatim on the note, files counted not copied); «tannarx korinmasin sotuvchiga» (#830 — the seller's view is a return TYPE that cannot carry a cost figure).
- OWNER (round 28, superseded in part): measure request→SAVED, seller picks the VED person, deadline by item count → calcDueMinutes = min(30×items, 120); phase A moved assignment from the seller to the QUEUE (nextVedAssignee fewest-open, #748).
- LAW 1 (VED.md verbatim): «AI is advisory, never load-bearing. AI fills the first draft and flags; no key / no balance / refusal → the same screens work manually. Nothing AI writes reaches a client without the VED's explicit confirm. Every AI-filled cell carries a visible marker until the VED touches or confirms it, and BOTH the AI draft and the confirmed values are stored.» Fence: tests/unit/ai-advisory.test.ts (pricing.ts names no ai identifier; the seal writes only aiGroupsSealed/aiBlindGroups/aiRateTakenGroups; applyProposal writes no dutyPct/vatPct/feeUsd/bazaUsd/rateSource, does write aiDutyPct + aiProposal; pickImportRows schema = seq/candidate/reason only; prefill.ts writes no bazaSource/rateSource; 0086 + 0094 CHECKs have no 'ai'; import-baza.ts names no ai). rate_source ∈ {dictionary,typed}; baza_source ∈ {dictionary,typed,import}.
- LAW 2: «The sealed price. The price the VED sends lands on the card LOCKED: neither seller nor VED can change it. Only admin can; a change is audited AND notified to the owner. Corrections are new VERSIONS — nothing is overwritten.» Fence: sealCalc one tx, no re-open, recalcFromSealed mints a NEW request via supersedes_request_id; quoteLockedFor + hidden re-post (#171); updateLead/updateDeal refuse a changed quote; calc-seal.integration.test.ts; #778 rekeyLeadCalcRequests moves sealed requests too.
- LAW 3: «Quote validity = 1 month. After that the card says 'expired — recalculate'.» Fence: quote_valid_days 30 frozen at seal; testid calc-seal-expired; chain.ts expired compares Date (pinned after #923's FORMATTING_ERROR).
- LAW 4: «Upsale. Client price is entered by the seller on the card; upsale = client price − VED price, computed, never typed. Floor: client price ≥ VED price (below-floor is admin-only). ANY discount kills the upsale right — a freight discount included (owner: «yolkiradan tushirganda ham upsale o'chsin»). Visibility: owner + accountant + the seller (own only). VED never sees upsale. Money flow: client pays the FULL sum (VED price + upsale) to the kassa; the accountant pays the upsale out to the seller — the cash screen must show both figures side by side.» Fence: upsaleScopeFor (finance.reports→all, crm.leads|clients.manage→own, else none), mayApproveBelowFloor = finance.reports, payableOffersSql() one home embedded in payUpsale, approved_at/payout_expense_id, upsale_expense_category_id mandatory, discounted_no_upsale, role-matrix behavioural test over every seeded role.
- LAW 5: «Baza is per-PRODUCT, not per-code, versioned by validity period … values older than 3 months wear a stale ⚠; old calcs keep their period's value for ever. A monthly task opens for the VED to review the baza dictionary (owner: «har oyda»).» Fence: calc_bazas effective_date, testid calc-baza-stale, JOB_CALC_REVIEW daily claim of the month via calc_review_notified_month (#785: a NOTIFICATION on a daily clock, not a monthly cron).
- LAW 6: «Rates live in our dictionary and learn from corrections. Entering a TNVED code auto-fills its rates; when the VED checks AIC calculator and corrects a rate, the correction is REMEMBERED. AIC calculator stays the external verification source — never scraped, never called.» Fence: calc_rates + saveRates (carries duty shape forward, #856), pullRatesFromDictionary server-side, rate_off_dictionary compares VALUES (#860). (VED-IMPORT-AI §4 restates «Law 6» as «never $0 — every refusal is a worded sentence» — the numbering there is the import doc's own.)
- LAW 7: «Lgota (exemption) is per-CALC, decided by the VED each time (duty exemption and tax exemption are separate flags); the dictionary remembers the last state as the offered default.» Fence: testid calc-lgota-last; a duty-free lgota zeroes the additional duty (#857).
- LAW 8: «Freight: density = TOTAL kg ÷ TOTAL m³ of the whole request; band from the tariff table; price per m³, except density ≥1000 kg/m³ which is per kg. YW and GZ share one tariff; cargo arriving directly at Kashgar uses the Kashgar column. Client price is always TO TASHKENT. No minimum charge — very small cargo gets only a warning. The VED may discount freight, but a below-tariff figure is FLAGGED and notified to the owner (and kills upsale, law 4).» Fence: densityOf, bandDensityOf = Math.round (whole kg/m³), bandFor, SMALL_FREIGHT_USD 50, freight_usd vs freight_list_usd, discountAudience (#878), tariff-seed.ts is seed AND fence.
- LAW 9: «Request types: full (podklyuch = rastamojka + yo'lkira + expenses), rastamojka-only, yo'lkira-only … map 1:1 onto the bot's existing sections.» Fence: CalcSectionName, sectionParts(section), CHECK section IN ('yolkira','rastamojka','podklyuch'), customs_on_yolkira blocker, aiPrefill skips the customs half on yolkira (#911).
- LAW 10: «Price book guard. The current selling price per product is hand-maintained (admin/VED) and shown IN the workspace beside the last 5 real quotes … Sellers and VED can read the price history (sellers: prices only, no cost breakdown).» Fence: calc_price_book keyed on TNVED CODE (#780), quoteHistoryFor EXISTS-shape, lastQuotesByCode row_number per code (#784), /hisoblash/narxlar.
- LAW 11: «Everything the seller submitted is shown to the VED AS-IS — files, photos, forwarded messages, unabridged, beside the AI draft.» Fence: #749 attachment gate — a lead note opens for ved.docs ONLY when calc_requests.note_id points at it; note_taken / note_foreign refusals in openCalcRequest.
- VED-IMPORT-AI §4 binding laws: the model proposes WORDS/ROWS never a number a customer pays; never $0 (every refusal a worded sentence — Record<Union,string> maps, #907); #171 what a control renders is what the save posts; #432 no per-row queries (one pick call per request, fill batched per distinct (code, units, name, weight)); #714 nothing pooled inside a transaction (tx-pool.test.ts derives the pooled set transitively); #291 files through route handlers; platform never imports wms; round 101: model calls off the bot poller with explicit 60 s timeouts (bot-ai-dispatch.test.ts); round 36: the bot may know only what the person already knows — the AI answers into the STAFF chat that asked.
- THE MACHINE STANDS DOWN: the pass carries the rev it was queued at; prefillStanding returns touched/confirmed/closed → no write (#908); a pass killed half-way is NOT retried (deliberate, logged); the machine acts as actorId:null in audit_log, never as the seller (#912b); a pick never overwrites a baza a person typed meanwhile (#912a, named as pickOvertaken).
- A FIGURE DERIVED FROM A SUBSET MUST NAME THE SUBSET (#913): '(N ta tovarsiz)' on a partial customs headline; zero groups = nothing priced (#914); reconcile warning only when every item is grouped and measured (#904).
- QUEUE/CLOCK RULES (#748): every speed figure filters completed_via <> 'returned'; the claim IS the UPDATE (WHERE assignee_id IS DISTINCT FROM me RETURNING); MAX_CALC_ITEMS 1000; MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER 20 (too_many_open); overdue sweep every 5 min claims rows in the UPDATE; no money on the queue screen (round 91 fence — ved_manager holds finance.manage).
- THE WHO-SEES-PRICES MATRIX: no new permission codes (#170). ved.docs = deal write + company-wide Telegram supervision + customs papers + the calc queue (a four-power bundle, #749, owner's call to split). finance.reports = owner/admins/accountant → all money; ved.docs → own calculations / cost side, never a client price (#790); sellers (crm.leads|clients.manage) → prices, never the floor; calcControlScopeFor(seller) = 'none'.
- HARD RULES (CLAUDE.md): never a key/token in chat/repo/commit; never `docker compose down -v`; never the model identifier in a commit/PR/comment/artifact; verified non-zero backup before every production update; commits carry the Co-Authored-By + Claude-Session trailers; reply to the owner in Uzbek in business terms, numbered questions, no AskUserQuestion dialogs.

### Gaps (not built / cut / owed / suspect)

- CUT (VED.md E2): per-TNVED calc-vs-actual — «a group is a CODE, a receipt lot is a NAME in zh/ru — they do not join, and names do not normalise»; and the nightly AI pass (gsr_ai_reader's allowlist cannot reach the calc tables, ai_questions.user_id is NOT NULL so a scheduled call is unauditable, and it is subtraction the arithmetic already does for ~$320/month). Stated to the owner as an explicit «yo'q».
- GATED, NOT STARTED (VED.md E2): the history-dependent suspicious rules, the per-worker ranking, the workspace sidebar's actuals column and the Telegram digest — precondition «≥20 sealed versions with confirmed links and settled cargo, and a non-empty calc_price_book». Dictionaries ship EMPTY, so today every rule would be true of 100 % of groups. Per-VED accuracy report = «Aziz: 23, 2 %» with one VED person.
- OWED (VED-IMPORT-AI §6, #909): the model's REASON for a pick is logged and shown nowhere — no column, sub-round B minted no migration — so a model pick and the deterministic ≥0.45 auto-fill land identically (baza_source='import' + import_row_id, one 📥 chip, one baza_from_import warning). Owed: a column and a chip that separate «the file matched this name» from «the model chose between five candidates», in a round that may mint one.
- OWED / NOT BUILT (VED-IMPORT-AI §6): the per-code history across quarters («bu kod avvalgi chorakda qancha edi») was designed and is NOT built — owed as a «yo'q» or a later round. Prefix fallback for prices CUT in v1 (exact code only). Auto-fill threshold 0.45 is a first guess to be tuned on the real 500k file and the measurement recorded. The AI hisobchi does NOT quote freight without a known zone (bot-landed requests carry none → «Yo‘lkira hali hisoblanmadi.»). Client-facing AI: refused (standing decision).

- docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md:263 «add fact + warning kind `baza_import`» — the shipped kind is `baza_from_import` (src/modules/wms/calc/warnings.ts CalcWarningKind; labels.ts:49 → 'warnings.bazaFromImport').
- docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md:204 «Update `row_count` every ~20k» and :211 «delete button for failed batches only» — superseded in code: the parse beats every 10 s on a WALL CLOCK carrying row_count (0095, #916), and a READY batch is deletable while importBatchUsage is 0 (`in_use` otherwise, #895; deleteImportBatch in import-service.ts).
- docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md:320 (§3 shipped note) «The checklist extension is `itemQuantity` + `itemWeight`» and :339 «per-item `quantity` and (for dona-shaped goods) per-item weight» — the shipped checklist is ONE field `itemMeasure` (intake.ts REQUIRED_FIELDS lines 39-42, CalcField comment; #910 — two fields made every multi-line card-form podklyuch permanently incomplete).
- docs/VED.md:40 «Calc request + SLA clock … door closed — REOPEN, do not re-invent» — reopened in phase A (0085; calc/service.ts openCalcRequest/takeCalcRequest/notifyOverdueCalcs; jobs.ts JOB_CALC_OVERDUE); the table row reads as still closed.
- CLAUDE.md:63 ritual pg_ctl `-l /var/local/pg/log` vs docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md:399 `-l /var/local/pg/log/pg.log` — two spellings of the postgres log path.
- CLAUDE.md:31-32 hard rule «Never write the model identifier into a commit message, PR, code comment or any pushed artifact» vs the mandated commit trailer `Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>` present on every commit (git log), and the API model ids 'claude-opus-5'/'claude-sonnet-5' as string constants in src/ (platform/ai/model.ts, intake-ai.ts:104, prefill-ai.ts:95, tnved/service.ts:129,208). The rule is read in practice as «the assistant's own model name is chat-only»; the API model strings are load-bearing and the trailer is harness-mandated — a successor should not «fix» either.
- CLAUDE.md:1478 «HIS SERVER IS AT 95 … Everything in this file is in production» — not true of PRs #73-#76 (0095 heartbeat, /tmp spool, 595 MB race, picker dialog — merged 2026-09-04 17:45-21:46Z, ledger 96) nor of open draft PR #77; CHANGELOG.md:3 itself says «ledger 96 ligicha» for the tarix round.
- VED-IMPORT-AI.md §4 cites «Law 6: never $0 — every refusal is a worded sentence», while docs/VED.md's law 6 is the rates dictionary; the «never a $0» rule is docs/VED.md's engine rule from phase B (#768) and the import doc's numbering is its own — cite by content, not number.


## Audit findings on this path (7-lens audit of 2026-09-04/05, verification status as recorded)


### A1. F63 — Upsale paid TWICE on one job: a re-offer after the payout is payable again in full (blocker)
- lens: seal-chain · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran — pass 2 died at F9; the lens measured it on gsr_ci through the real functions)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/upsale.ts:137`
- mechanism: payableOffersSql ranks a request's offers by offered_at DESC and pays rn=1; the only pay-twice fence is payout_expense_id IS NULL on THAT row. Re-offering is the designed workflow (pick a language, press again), so after the accountant pays offer A the later offer B becomes rn=1 with payout NULL and is payable in full; A drops to rn=2 and vanishes from /upsale together with its paid sum. Measured: seal floor 3300 → offer 3800 → payUpsale 500 → re-offer 4000 → upsaleRows shows ONE row {upsale 700, no paid row} → payUpsale 700 → $1,200 paid on a job whose rightful maximum is $700. #794's red proof never tested a re-quote AFTER a payout.
- fix: Make «one payable per job» hold across payouts: in the ranked CTE compute paid_on_request = sum(payout_usd) over the request's paid offers and expose upsale − paid as the payable delta (refuse ≤ 0), or treat a request with any paid offer as settled («avval to'langan»); keep the PAID row visible so bySeller.paidUsd stops losing money that left the till. Integration test: pay → re-offer → payUpsale refuses or pays only the delta.
- owner decision: 1) Qayta taklifdan keyin faqat FARQI to'lansin ($700 − $500 = $200). 2) Bir ish uchun bir marta to'langan bo'lsa keyingi takliflar umuman to'lanmasin («avval to'langan»). 3) To'langan ishga qayta taklif yozish taqiqlansin.

### A2. F40 — ⚙ «Сбор $» per group is ADDED on top of the automatic BHM declaration fee — the fee lands twice (or N times) in the sealed client price
- lens: pricing-law · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; the lens reproduced it in a real browser as VED Demo: customs $288.16 → $338.16 after typing 50, fee line unchanged, group footer parts explain $232 of $282)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/items-table.tsx:1480 (engine: src/modules/wms/calc/pricing.ts:274; dictionary form: hisoblash/lugatlar/dict-forms.tsx:176)`
- mechanism: customsFor adds group.feeUsd into each group's customs figure and requestCustomsFor adds the per-declaration VMQ-55 BHM fee on top of the sum. Since VED 2.0 (#858) the declaration fee is automatic and the dictionary pull leaves the group fee empty, but the group ⚙ form and the lugatlar rates form still offer a box labelled «Сбор $ / Yig‘im $» — the word the VED knows for the bojxona yig'imi — so a number typed there is added per group while the totals line keeps printing only the automatic «1 BHM ≈ $32.96». Nothing in the group footer prints the typed fee, so the footer's parts no longer add up, and the inflated figure seals onto the client card.
- fix: Remove the per-group «Сбор $» box from the ⚙ form and from the lugatlar rates form (column may stay, never written by a screen) and make the request-level setFeeOverride the ONE fee door (see F41). If the box must stay, relabel it «Qo‘shimcha yig‘im (BHM ustiga)» and print it in the group footer so the parts add up.
- owner decision: 1) Guruhdagi «Yig‘im $» katakchasini olib tashlash — yig‘im faqat avtomat BHM shkalasi bo‘yicha (tavsiya). 2) Katakcha qolsin, nomi «Qo‘shimcha yig‘im (BHM ustiga)» va guruh satrida ko‘rinsin.

### A3. F66 — Готово amount typed «1 000» closes the job with NO amount (Telegram says «💵 NaN USD»); «1,000» becomes a $1 floor so the whole client price is upsale
- lens: seal-chain · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/calc-actions.tsx:202 (server: src/modules/wms/calc/service.ts:549 finishCalcRequest)`
- mechanism: The finish form parses Number(amount.replace(',', '.')). «1 000» → NaN; finishCalcRequest has no mustBeNumber, num(NaN) stores answer_amount NULL while answer_currency stores 'USD' (NaN != null is true) and the request is CLOSED via 'task' — a currency with no amount, the noise #778 forbids; the seller's Telegram prints «💵 NaN USD». «1,000» becomes 1.000 = $1.00, a valid positive answer: the Готово anchor's floor is $1, every client price clears it and the upsale is the whole price minus a dollar. The 0093 answer-amount CHECK catches neither NULL nor 1.
- fix: Parse like the rest of the money inputs (strip spaces/NBSP, treat comma+exactly three digits as a thousands separator or refuse ambiguous input); in finishCalcRequest call mustBeNumber(answer.amount) and refuse bad_number with a sentence BEFORE endRequest; never write answer_currency without an amount. Unit-test «1 000», «1,000», «1 000,50».

### A4. F32 — Two jobs on one deal: ONE invoice makes BOTH sellers' shares «payable»; the cash screen and the card show only the newest job's price
- lens: money · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; lens probe S3 on gsr_ci)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/upsale-service.ts:145 (bothFiguresForDeals :449; applyOfferToCard src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts:3507)`
- mechanism: upsaleRows judges every offer of a deal against the deal's TOTAL charges (sum of client_transactions.charge with deal_id), not against that job's price or the sum of the deal's standing offers. 0085 deliberately allows several requests per deal (a rastamojka job beside a yolkira job is normal), so a deal with two sealed jobs gets two payable rows and one charge of the first job's price satisfies both. bothFiguresForDeals overwrites per deal, so the accountant's «tannarx · mijoz narxi» pair shows only the newest job; applyOfferToCard writes the LAST offer's price onto deals.quoted_amount, so the deal «Summa», the board and every revenue report read one job's price for a two-job deal. Measured: floors 4000+2500, offers 5000+3000, one charge 5000 → both rows 'payable', card quoted_amount 3000.
- fix: (a) Compare the deal's charges against the SUM of client_price_usd over its standing released offers (one grouped query, same payableOffersSql CTE) so no job is payable until the whole promised sum is invoiced — or track charges per request (client_transactions.calc_request_id + a job picker on the ledger). (b) bothFiguresForDeals returns every standing pair per deal and /finance/[clientId] prints them all with the section name. (c) The card writes the SUM of standing offers or prints «N ta hisob» beside the amount.
- owner decision: 1) Bitta bitim = bitta hisob (ikkinchi hisob so'ralganda yangi bitim) — sodda, lekin 0085 ni qaytaradi. 2) Bitimda bir nechta hisob qoladi; ulush faqat BARCHA aytilgan narxlar hisob-fakturaga tushganda «tayyor», kassa ekrani va karta hamma hisoblarni ko'rsatadi. 3) Har bir hisob-faktura qaysi hisobga tegishli ekanini buxgalter tanlaydi.

### A5. F33 + F64 — An offer on a SUPERSEDED or EXPIRED seal is accepted server-side: the stale client price overwrites the card, is fully payable, and the locked ✏️ form refuses every later save
- lens: money + seal-chain (two lenses found it independently) · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; lens probes S2 and E2/E3 on gsr_ci both ACCEPTED the offer)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts:3339-3392 (recordOffer, version branch)`
- mechanism: recordOffer's VERSION branch checks only existence, the card's identity and discounted_no_upsale — never currentVersionSql/notSupersededSql (the clause releaseOffer, releasedPriceFor and payableOffersSql all carry) and never v.valid_until; the ANSWER branch refuses answer_expired and superseded, the version branch — the one every seal uses — does not. The panel merely hides the form, so a tab opened before the correction/expiry (or any hand post) still carries the old version id. Measured: V1 3300 superseded by V2 3900 → recordOffer(V1, 3400) ACCEPTED: card 3400, lock 3900, releasedPrice null → the locked ✏️ re-posts 3400 and updateDeal refuses quote_sealed on every later save (#827's loop in the other direction). Expired version (40 days back) → ACCEPTED, sheet says «действует до 27.07.2026», card overwritten, /upsale state 'payable' (expiry is deliberately not a payable clause).
- fix: In the version branch re-derive both admissions server-side, mirroring the answer branch: refuse quote_expired (i18n ×4, or reuse answer_expired's sentence) when valid_until < now(), and refuse superseded unless the version satisfies currentVersionSql() AND notSupersededSql() in one SELECT with the fragments offerStandsSql uses. Integration tests: expired version → refused; offer on V1 after V2 sealed → refused and the card untouched.

### A6. F34 — A Готово-answer client price is NEVER locked on the card: ✏️ changes it freely while /upsale and the cash screen keep the old figure
- lens: money · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; lens probe S4 on gsr_ci)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/crm/service.ts:363 (quoteLockedFor)`
- mechanism: quoteLockedFor returns null when currentSealFor finds no calc_versions row, BEFORE ever asking releasedPriceFor. A Готово-anchored offer (0093) has no version, so the lock is null: both ✏️ forms render the editable quote branch and updateDeal/updateLead accept any amount. #884 wired releasedPriceFor into the anchor union exactly so the lock would follow the released answer price — the early return makes it unreachable. Today the dictionaries are empty in production, so EVERY price is a Готово answer (#883). Measured: answer 3000, offer 3500 → card 3500, lock null; updateDeal(2000) → OK, card 2000; upsaleRows still {price 3500, floor 3000}.
- fix: quoteLockedFor asks releasedPriceFor FIRST; a standing released offer locks at its price regardless of a seal; only then fall back to the seal's total (keeping the clock rule between an offer and a NEWER seal). Red proof: S4 as an integration test in calc-offer-answer.integration.test.ts.

### A7. F22 + F45 — The rates dictionary can only hold a PERCENT duty: «lug'atga yozish» on a code priced off a MAX heading writes an advalor exact-code row that then OUT-RANKS the law's floor — every later calc on that code is under-priced
- lens: ui-desktop (F22) + pricing-law (F45, same defect from the engine side) · status: **F22 confirmed 3/3 (a verifier found the money consequence: the workspace's «lug'atga yozish» calls saveRates with no dutyMode, so a new exact code under a «20 %, min $3/juft» heading is remembered as plain 20 % and longest-prefix lookup prefers it); F45 unverified** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/lugatlar/dict-forms.tsx:158 (saveRates src/modules/wms/calc/dictionaries.ts:304-323; workspace «teach» button items-table.tsx:1355-1365)`
- mechanism: RatesForm posts tnvedCode/dutyPct/vatPct/feeUsd/effectiveDate only and saveRatesAction's input type does not even declare dutyMode/dutySpecific/dutyUnit; the group ⚙ editor is the same. saveRates carries a standing EXACT-code shape forward but a NEW 10-digit code with no standing row is forced to 'advalor' (#856 deliberately does not carry the heading's MAX shape). 239 of 1,489 seeded rows are max/plus; 157 of the 198 MAX rows are 4/6-digit headings that exact codes fall back to — the common case, not an edge. Nothing on any screen can mint or correct a max/plus/specific rate, and the screen does not show which shape a row has when the VED types over it.
- fix: Add a duty-shape select (advalor / max / plus / specific) with «$ за» + unit inputs to RatesForm (and pass the shape from the workspace teach button), prefilled from the in-force row when an exact code is typed, posting dutyMode/dutySpecific/dutyUnit which saveRates already accepts; until then the teach button must carry the heading's shape or refuse.

### A8. F44 — Excise (aksiz) is modelled by the engine but no screen or dictionary can set it — an excisable good is silently priced without excise and without VAT on it
- lens: pricing-law · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/items-table.tsx:1462 (engine pricing.ts:121/247; setGroupRates accepts excisePct)`
- mechanism: pricing.ts carries excisePct and puts excise into the VAT base (#857), setGroupRates accepts it and calc_groups has the column with a CHECK — but the ⚙ GroupForm offers duty/VAT/fee/two lgota boxes only, calc_rates has no excise column, the lugatlar form no box, and grep 'excise' over hisoblash/ returns nothing. excisePct is null on every group a person can make, so the line is always $0 with no warning that the code is excisable; the price told to the client is below the real payment and the gap is the company's loss.
- fix: Either add an «Aksiz %» box to the ⚙ form (setGroupRates already takes it; it must unconfirm the group like a rate edit), or record in DECISIONS that excise goods are out of scope and have the engine and the bot reply say so — never a silent $0.
- owner decision: 1) ⚙ oynasiga «Aksiz %» katakcha (VED o‘zi yozadi). 2) Aksiz tovarlar bu modulda hisoblanmaydi deb qayd etish — ekranda «aksiz tovar — qo‘lda» ogohlantirish bilan.

### A9. F27 — The freight band-override box is offered on a rastamojka-only seal: a typed value is stored, fires a false «chegirma» alarm, and STRIPS the seller's upsale on that job
- lens: ui-desktop · status: **confirmed 3/3 (one verifier raised the severity from minor: traced that sealCalc writes band_override_min, notifies owner/seller/accountant about a tariff band that does not exist on the quote, and payableOffersSql treats it as a concession — a rastamojka quote usually has density NULL, which fails closed)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/calc-workspace.tsx:400 (seal write workspace.ts:1582; upsale.ts:66-70, 153-156)`
- mechanism: SealPanel's «Скидка / смена строки» fold renders the band-override pair unconditionally; only the sealed panel and the totals/zone blocks ask workspace.parts.freight. sectionParts('rastamojka').freight is false, so the override changes no price, but the seal still stores it, the Telegram concession alert fires, and the payable predicate reads «band override set, density NULL» as a concession. DECISIONS #770(a) states the opposite rule («the band must not appear on the sheet at all»), so the panel breaches the recorded design.
- fix: Wrap the band pair in workspace.parts.freight ? … : null, AND have sealCalc refuse or null bandOverrideMin when !parts.freight (#531 — a screen gate alone leaves the action accepting it).

### A10. F47 — Any staff member with canWriteDeal can re-offer on a colleague's deal: the commission silently moves to the last presser and the card price changes under the original seller
- lens: access · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; the lens drove it in a browser on gsr_ci: after an admin's $4,700 re-offer Dilnoza's /upsale rows went to 0, her card still showed her $4,500 PDF, deals.quoted_amount = 4700)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/upsale.ts:138 (recordOffer src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts:3295; seller's own-offer filter on the card)`
- mechanism: recordOffer checks only «is this version this card's» — never whether the presser is the deal's owner / the client's manager / the previous offerer; anyone with canWriteDeal + mayOffer (crm.leads or clients.manage: sellers, logists, admins) may offer. payableOffersSql pays the NEWEST offer per request regardless of who made it, so another person's re-offer erases the earlier seller's commission and books it to themselves, while the seller's card lists only their OWN offers and so shows neither the new price nor the lost share — they can still forward a stale PDF to the customer.
- fix: Tie the offer door to the card's owner (deal owner_id / client sales_manager_id / previous offered_by) with a refusal sentence naming who offered, plus an explicit admin «o'z zimmasiga olish» door (audited, Telegram to the previous seller); show other people's offers on the seller's card («boshqa hodim qayta narx berdi»).
- owner decision: 1) Faqat bitim egasi (yoki mijoz menejeri) va adminlar taklif qila oladi. 2) Har kim taklif qiladi, lekin ulush BIRINCHI taklif qilganniki bo'lib qoladi. 3) Hozirgidek (oxirgi olgan) + oldingi sotuvchiga xabar. Qo'shimcha: logist mijozga narx aytib ulush olishi kerakmi — ha/yo'q.

### A11. F67 — Two simultaneous «Qayta hisoblash» presses spawn TWO corrections off one seal; both later stand, both pay a commission, the prixod link can no longer choose
- lens: seal-chain · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts:1827 (recalcFromSealed)`
- mechanism: recalcFromSealed checks «no child yet» with a plain SELECT on the pool and INSERTs the child in a separate transaction; no FOR UPDATE on the parent and 0088's calc_requests_supersedes_idx is non-unique. Two concurrent callers both pass and both insert; the function's own comment names the consequence: two children off one parent both stand (notSupersededSql sees no child on either), payableOffersSql pays both, stampCalcLink's «exactly one sealed request» fails, and the chain shows two V2s.
- fix: Partial UNIQUE index on calc_requests(supersedes_request_id) WHERE NOT NULL (new migration; count doubles first — production should have none), catch 23505 in recalcFromSealed as recalc_open so the loser gets the existing sentence and the child's link; optionally FOR UPDATE on the parent inside the insert tx.

### A12. F38 — A band override BELOW the real density is a concession (#795) but recordOffer's discounted_no_upsale gate only reads discountUsd: the seller may quote above a band-lowered floor and the difference is paid to nobody
- lens: money · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts:3348 (panel calc-panel.tsx:157; upsale.ts:154-158)`
- mechanism: The promise gate of #879 («above a discounted floor the seller may not sell») is implemented as `Number(v.discountUsd) > 0` only, and the panel passes discountUsd alone. payableOffersSql already pays nothing on a band-lowered offer, so the customer does not get the concession and the seller gets no share — the margin above the conceded floor belongs to no one.
- fix: Compute «conceded» once (an upsaleEligible over the version's discount/band/density in upsale.ts) and use it in both the recordOffer gate and the form's hint; keep the discounted_no_upsale sentence.
- owner decision: 1) Band tushirilganda ham sotuvchi tannarxdan yuqori ayta olmasin (#879 bilan bir xil). 2) Band tushirish chegirma emas — unda payable ham to'lasin (ikkala joyda bir qoida).

### A13. F52 — The calc queue counts the owner and every admin as a VED: a new request is auto-assigned to the OWNER first (blocker)
- lens: workflow-queue · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; lens evidence from gsr_ci tasks table and the queue screenshot «Взял: Bekzod (Super admin)»)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/service.ts:103 (nextVedAssignee; ROLE_MATRIX rbac/catalog.ts:120-121)`
- mechanism: nextVedAssignee's pool is usersWithPermission('ved.docs') and super_admin/admin hold ALL, so every admin is in it; ordering is «fewest open, never-had-one FIRST», so the owner (0 open) wins every fresh bot/card request; openCalcRequest mints the timed task on him, and the overdue sweep for an ASSIGNED request tells only requester+owners — the real VED never hears the job exists. Same shape #660 corrected for the lead rota.
- fix: Exclude super_admin/admin roles from the auto-assign pool (they keep «Olaman»), or a per-person «hisoblash navbatida» flag like /admin/taqsimot, or no auto-assign at all with the overdue sweep alerting every ved.docs holder.
- owner decision: 1) Admin/egani navbatdan avtomatik chiqarish. 2) /admin/taqsimot kabi ro‘yxat: kim navbatda — siz belgilaysiz. 3) Avto-biriktirmaslik: «hech kim olmagan», kechiksa hamma VED ga xabar.

### A14. F53 + F65 — Saving the deal's «Позиции» (seller's goods panel / goods import) silently CLOSES the VED's open workspace: no price, no seal, no notification, every later VED write refused already_closed (blocker)
- lens: workflow-queue + seal-chain (found independently) · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; the lens reproduced it in a real browser: seller saved one line → calc_requests.completed_via='lines', completed_by=seller, task done, VED page shows «Готово» with an empty answer card and no save button)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/deals/service.ts:409-410 (completeCalcForDeal src/modules/wms/calc/service.ts:601-611)`
- mechanism: Phase A's 'lines' clock-stop (#748) survives into VED 2.0: saveLines calls completeCalcForDeal after its tx, which endRequest()s the OLDEST open request on the deal with no canSeal check, no answer and no notification. The request is now a workspace with groups/rates/✅, a deal may carry several jobs (0085), so the closed request may not even be the consignment the lines describe; lockRequestInTx then throws already_closed on saveTable/confirm/seal, drafts are lost, only an admin can recalc.
- fix: Retire the 'lines' ending for workspace jobs: delete the saveLines hook, or narrow completeCalcForDeal to requests with NO workspace state (no groups/items/rev movement) opened without a section; keep completed_via 'lines' readable. Test: a request with a group survives saveLines.
- owner decision: 1) Bitim tovarlari saqlanganda hisoblash so'rovi umuman yopilmasin (faqat Muhr yoki Готово yopadi). 2) Faqat VED hali hech narsa kiritmagan (guruhsiz, kodsiz) eski uslubdagi so'rovlar yopilsin.

### A15. F31 — On a LEAD, the moment «Qayta hisoblash» is pressed the locked ✏️ form refuses every save («Narx muhrlangan») — for ever if the correction is returned/abandoned (blocker)
- lens: money · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; lens probe S1 on gsr_ci: after recalcFromSealed card=5000, lock=4000, updateLead(name changed) → REFUSED quote_sealed)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/crm/service.ts:383 (quoteLockedFor) and :547 (updateLead)`
- mechanism: Once a superseding request EXISTS, offerStandsSql stops the released offer from standing, releasedPriceFor returns null and the lock falls back to the seal's FLOOR. The lead card still carries the CLIENT price applyOfferToCard wrote, the locked lead form re-posts that card value as a hidden input (#171), and updateLead compares the posted amount against the LOCK's number — floor ≠ client price → quote_sealed on every save. updateDeal is immune because it compares posted vs before.quotedAmount.
- fix: Make updateLead decide like updateDeal: the lock says only WHETHER the quote is locked; the change test compares the posted amount against the card's own before.quotedAmount (sameMoney), so a form re-posting what it renders never refuses. Red proof: offer → recalc pressed → ordinary lead save, in upsale.integration.test.ts.

### A16. F43 — The tariff screen can only ADD rows: a mistyped or moved band overlaps for ever, every quote in that density refuses «ikki bandda», and the band override that exists to rescue it cannot reach the server
- lens: pricing-law · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; lens inserted a second cn row 200–260 dated today → freight «⚠ плотность попадает в две строки», calc-do-seal disabled even with an override typed; row deleted afterwards)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/dictionaries.ts:482 (saveTariffBand; bandFor pricing.ts:350; seal gate calc-workspace.tsx:446; /admin/tarif page)`
- mechanism: saveTariffBand upserts on (zone, min_density, effective_date) and tariffFor keeps the newest row per (zone, min_density), so a row is superseded ONLY by another with the same floor; /admin/tarif has no delete/retire/end-date and the save checks neither gaps nor overlaps. bandFor then answers band_ambiguous (or band_missing for a hole) for the whole range — by design, but with no door to repair it. The seal button is disabled by workspace.blockers.length > 0 computed WITHOUT the override, so the override cannot be submitted.
- fix: (a) Give a tariff row an end (ends_on, or «retire» writing a superseding row with the same floor) and a delete for future-dated rows; (b) refuse at save a row that overlaps or leaves a hole against the in-force bands of that zone, naming the conflict; (c) compute seal blockers from the typed override (loadWorkspace(id,{overrideDensity})) so a band refusal can actually be overridden.
- owner decision: 1) Tarif satrini «bekor qilish» tugmasi + saqlashda ustma-ust/teshik tekshiruvi (tavsiya). 2) Faqat tekshiruv, bekor qilish yo‘q — xato satr bazadan o‘chiriladi.

### A17. F15 — A podklyuch/rastamojka job with no coded goods prints «Растаможка $0.00» and a bold «Итоговая цена» equal to the freight alone (law 6)
- lens: ui-desktop · status: **confirmed 3/3 (a verifier notes DECISIONS #914 already recorded this exact vacuous-every branch, fixed only the BOT's reading and deferred the engine/screen fix — an acknowledged deferral, not an accepted trade-off)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/pricing.ts:572 (requestCustomsFor; workspace.ts:513-523; TotalsPanel calc-workspace.tsx:302-346)`
- mechanism: requestCustomsFor computes allOk = customs.every(ok), vacuously TRUE over an EMPTY group list, fee null, and returns customsUsd: 0; loadWorkspace then calls totalsFor with customs 0 as soon as the freight prices, so totals.ok is true while the seal is blocked by no_groups. The panel and the sticky bar print a false total next to «Без группы: 1». This is the ordinary starting state of every customs job (zero groups until a code is typed).
- fix: In requestCustomsFor (or at the workspace call site) treat an empty group list on a customs-bearing section as not priced (customsUsd null, reason no_groups) so totals stays null and the panel keeps saying «Расчёт ещё не полный»; keep the freight line visible on its own.

### A18. F1 — The phone ✅ records «warnings seen» (confirmed_warnings, confirm_via='single') for a card that never showed the duty %, VAT %, ✨ or off-dictionary chips — E1's «ko'rmasdan tasdiqlagan» record is blind at the phone door
- lens: ui-phone · status: **confirmed 3/3 in pass 1 AND 3/3 again in pass 2 (6 confirming verdicts, 0 refuted; verifiers reproduced with a typed 7 % against the book's 5 %: the phone card showed no rate, one tap wrote confirmed_warnings=['rate_off_dictionary','ai_rate_taken'])** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/items-table.tsx:964 (confirmGroup src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts:1144-1177)`
- mechanism: The md:hidden phone card prints only the TNVED code, the baza, the confirm chip, the customs $ and the item names; the desktop BlockFooter prints what the ✅ is about (duty/VAT, dictionary rates, ✨ low-confidence, «⚠ rate noted», «📥 taxmin»). Both doors call the same confirmGroup, which stamps confirm_via='single' and confirmed_warnings = warningsNow() «what stood on the screen» (#807) — false at the phone door; nazorat's only via-marker is the 'bulk' chip, so a blind phone ✅ is indistinguishable from a deliberate desktop one. The screen itself advertises «взять, подтвердить и запечатать можно с телефона».
- fix: Print the same rates line and chips on the phone card (one muted 11 px row, same as BlockFooter) so the ✅ records what the person saw; and stamp confirm_via='phone' from the phone door so E1 can tell the two apart.
- owner decision: 1) Telefon kartasida stavkalar va ogohlantirish chiplari ko'rsatilsin, tasdiqlash qoladi; 2) telefonda tasdiqlash butunlay o'chirilsin; 3) telefonda tasdiqlash qolsin, lekin «telefondan» belgisi bilan alohida yozilsin.

### A19. F14 — After the seller's offer the VED reads the CLIENT price on the deal card («Сумма 4500 USD 🔒») two blocks above their own seal — law 4, the upsale is one subtraction away
- lens: ui-desktop · status: **confirmed 3/3 (independently reproduced as VED Demo: deal-compare «Сумма 4500 USD», deal-quote-locked, calc-seal-total $3796.96 on one screen)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/bitimlar/[id]/page.tsx:187 (deal-form.tsx:81; applyOfferToCard workspace.ts:3513-3536)`
- mechanism: A released offer writes the client price onto deals.quoted_amount (#800, for the revenue surfaces) and the card prints that column in the «Договорено · Сумма» row and the locked ✏️ block to anyone who can open it; canWriteDeal includes ved.docs and /bitimlar/[id] has no ownership gate (stated open in #790). The 🧮 panel correctly hides the offers for the VED but prints the floor, so the VED reads both numbers. #790-792 verified only the four phase-C surfaces; #800 never stated the VED's card view would then carry the client price.
- fix: Render the «Сумма» fact and the locked ✏️ amount only when upsaleScopeFor(actor) !== 'none' (or the actor is the deal's owner); for the VED print «narx berildi» without the figure — the seal in the panel is already their number. Same on the lead card.
- owner decision: 1) VED bitim kartasida mijoz narxini ko'rmasin (faqat o'z muhri); 2) hozirgidek qolsin — VED mijoz narxini ham bilsin (qonun 4 ni yumshatish).

### A20. F54 — The timed task of a SEALED request stays open and «late» on the VED's /bugun, the home count and the owner's tasks report
- lens: workflow-queue · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; lens counted 5 such rows on gsr_ci, all completed_via='sealed', all past due)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts:1547 (sealCalc UPDATE)`
- mechanism: sealCalc sets completedVia:'sealed' and never touches tasks; only endRequest (Готово/Qaytarish) closes the task minted by openCalcRequest/takeCalcRequest. So every seal leaves a red priority-1 task until the VED presses «✅ Bajarildi» by hand — and completeCalcForTask then finds no open request.
- fix: Close the task inside sealCalc (or right after) the way endRequest does («Muhrlandi»); one-off script closes the open tasks of already-sealed requests.

### A21. F55 + F68 — «Qayta hisoblash» opens a silent correction (no task, no Telegram to the assignee) and the correction's seal notice goes to the admin who pressed recalc, never to the seller whose card price changed
- lens: workflow-queue + seal-chain · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; lens browser walk: after an admin recalc the new request shows «Взял/Просит: Bekzod», no calc-sealed notice to the original seller)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts:1856 (recalcFromSealed) and :1678 (announceSeal)`
- mechanism: recalcFromSealed inserts the correction with assigneeId=old.assigneeId, dueAt now+2h, requestedBy=the presser (always an admin — recalcAction is gated on admin.settings.manage) and only writeAudit — no createTask, no notifyStaffTelegram; the overdue sweep for an ASSIGNED request reaches requester+owners only. When sealed, announceSeal sends CalcSealed to [requestedBy] = the admin, exceptUserId the sealer; discountAudience (#878) learned the superseded request's seller, the seal notice did not — so the seller keeps quoting $3,300 while the card already says $3,900, and if the admin seals it themself nobody is told at all.
- fix: Recalc mints a task and notifies the assignee like takeCalcRequest; announceSeal's audience = {requestedBy} ∪ the superseded chain's original requestedBy (walk supersedes_request_id to the root as discountAudience does one step), minus the sealer. Test: a correction's seal queues CalcSealed for the original requester.

### A22. F42 — A discount or band override typed in the seal panel is previewed NOWHERE — the VED first sees the discounted/re-banded figure after the irreversible seal
- lens: pricing-law · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; lens browser: with «100» in calc-discount the panel and bar read $504.16, the seal wrote total_usd 404.16)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/page.tsx:80 (loadWorkspace(id) with no opts; SealPanel inputs calc-workspace.tsx:404/424)`
- mechanism: The page calls loadWorkspace(id) without opts, so TotalsPanel and the sticky bar always show the undiscounted total at the un-overridden band; the SealPanel's discount and band inputs are local state that feeds only sealAction. sealCalc's own comment claims «the screen previews with the same call the seal validates with» — it does not. The seal is one-way (a correction is a NEW request), so the first sight of the number is on the locked card.
- fix: Recompute the preview from the typed concessions (totalsFor is pure and runs in the browser; freight needs a client-side bandFor over workspace.tariff rows) or debounce a server preview via loadWorkspace(id,{discountUsd, overrideDensity}); print «Muhrlanadi: $404.16 (−$100)» above the 🔒 and surface discount_exceeds_total before the press.

### A23. F56 — A RETURNED calculation's reason never reaches the seller's card: the 🧮 panel is empty and the history prints English keys and bare UUIDs
- lens: workflow-queue · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; lens browser: seller's calc-panel innerText «🧮 Расчёт», history rows «calcRequest: → 01a06e89-…»)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/service.ts:1126 (lastCalcAnswerFor; labels src/modules/platform/audit/fields.ts:15)`

The reason survives only in the audit history, where AUDIT_FIELD_LABELS knows none of calcRequest/calcTakenBy/calcReturned/calcRequested/section/items (law 6). The card also stays on the hisoblatish stage the bot moved it to.
- fix: CalcPanel prints «↩ Qaytarildi (sana): <sabab>» for the last returned request (a 'returned' branch in lastCalcAnswerFor); add the calc keys to AUDIT_FIELD_LABELS ×4 and the request/user ids to AUDIT_FIELD_REFS.
- owner decision: Qaytarilganda karta bosqichi: 1) joyida qolsin (hozirgidek), 2) oldingi bosqichga qaytsin — sizning tanlovingiz.

Add a «Rad etish» door (rejected_at/by/reason on calc_offers, additive migration; releasedOfferWhere unchanged), notify the seller, panel chip «rad etildi».

### A25. F17 — Without an API key the ✨ «Предложить группы (ИИ)» button is drawn and answers «ИИ не ответил» instead of «ИИ не настроен» — the VED retries a permanent state
- lens: ui-desktop · status: **confirmed 3/3 (reproduced on a keyless server: proposeGroups throws ai_failed while the batch log says ai_not_configured)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts:2107 (tnved/service.ts:195; button items-table.tsx:688-696)`
- mechanism: proposeGoodsGrouping throws TnvedError('ai_not_configured') when ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is absent; proposeGroups catches every per-batch error as a partial failure and, when all batches failed, throws CalcError('ai_failed') → «ИИ не ответил»; the ai_not_configured sentence exists two lines away in every bundle and is unreachable. The button renders regardless of isAiConfigured(), unlike the /ai menu entry and the batch TNVED editor, and contrary to #897's recorded norm for the bot half (honest «sozlanmagan»). Production's key is documented as burned/pending.
- fix: In proposeGroups rethrow ai_not_configured unchanged when the first batch reports it, and hide (or disable with the sentence) the ✨ button when isAiConfigured() is false.

### A27. F58 — «✅ Bajarildi» on the calc task (bot button, /bugun, card) closes a SEALABLE job with no price and no way back for the VED

- mechanism: completeTask → completeCalcForTask → endRequest({via:'task'}) with no answer and no canSeal check; the Telegram TaskAssigned for the calc task carries «✅ Bajarildi». #880 left this door deliberately untouched, but its consequence is a dead end: lockRequestInTx refuses already_closed on every mutator and recalcAction needs admin.settings.manage, so the VED cannot reopen or correct; the seller gets no «hisob tayyor».

### A38. F61 — Bot: «Hisoblash navbatiga tushmadi — kartadan qo‘lda yuboring» names no cause, and the realistic cause (20 open requests per requester) makes the card refuse the same way
- lens: workflow-queue · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/platform/telegram/staff-handlers.ts:660 (intake-land.ts:246-249; cap calc/service.ts:70/189)`
- mechanism: landIntake swallows openCalcRequest's error into a log and returns queued:false; the bot prints one constant sentence for every cause. With too_many_open the card form refuses too («Ochiq so‘rovlar juda ko‘p»), so the advice sends the seller to a door that also closes.
- fix: landIntake returns the code; the bot says «Sizda 20 ta ochiq so‘rov — VED javob bergach qayta yuboring» / «karta topilmadi» etc.

### A39. F18 — After the seal the customs breakdown disappears: baza, duty %, VAT %, the BHM fee and per-group customs are rendered nowhere on the sealed screen
- lens: ui-desktop · status: **confirmed 3/3** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/calc-workspace.tsx:470 (page.tsx:229-262 read-only fallback)`
- mechanism: On a completed request CalcWorkspace renders only SealedPanel (total, customs, freight, extras, discount, band, per-unit, valid-until) and the page falls back to the read-only goods table (name · TNVED · kg · m³); the per-group figures snapshotted in calc_versions.breakdown are shown nowhere, so the VED cannot answer «nega buncha?» nor start a correction from the old numbers.
- fix: Render the sealed version's breakdown under SealedPanel as a read-only block per group (code · baza · duty · VAT · customs) plus the fee line — from the snapshot, not today's dictionaries.

### A40. F5 + F20 — Queue rows: every lead job is titled «Расчёт» with no goods, the deadline is a bare clock time («Срок: 22:01» for a three-day-old job), and on the phone the only door into a job is a 63×24 px word
- lens: ui-phone (F5) + ui-desktop (F20) · status: **F5 confirmed 3/3 in pass 1 and 3/3 in pass 2; F20 confirmed 3/3** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/page.tsx:86-92 and :126 (calcQueue calc/service.ts:792-818)`
- mechanism: The card is a plain <li>; only the title <Link> navigates, and for a lead the title is the constant t('title') (the lead NAME is fenced on purpose, #514); calcQueue reads no goods, so nothing else identifies the job; dueAt is formatted {hour, minute} only; three header buttons push the first card to y=246 at 360×800.
- fix: Make the whole card the link (or a ≥44 px «Ochish» button), print the first goods names on the row (one grouped query over calc_request_items — goods, not the lead's name), format dueAt with the date when not today (or «2 дня назад»), collapse the header buttons on phones.
- owner decision: 1) Lid so'rovida mol nomlari chiqsin (mijoz ismi baribir yopiq); 2) lid nomi ham chiqsin; 3) hozirgidek qolsin.

### A41. F21 — The workspace header names the deal number only — no client code or name, one extra jump per job
- lens: ui-desktop · status: **confirmed 3/3** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/page.tsx:90 (requestLabel calc/service.ts:180)`
- mechanism: title = deal.code, subtitle = itemCount · requester; the client code (GS…) and name are on the deal card behind «Карточка». The company addresses cargo by client CODE (#579).
- fix: Extend requestLabel/calcRequestDetail for deals to carry clients.client_code (+ name) and print «B-000067 · GS323 · Tez mijoz» in the header and on the queue row.

### A42. F8 — Seller's price history: a sealed quote with no released offer is a card with no number and no reason
- lens: ui-phone · status: **confirmed 3/3 in pass 1 and 3/3 in pass 2 (law 4 correct in both directions; a real shortcoming)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/narxlar/page.tsx:130 (quoteHistoryFor history.ts:138-173)`
- mechanism: For scope 'own' hideCost nulls totalUsd/perM3/perKg/groupCustoms and the only money line is clientPriceUsd; with no offer released for that seal the row prints section · date · code · m³ · kg and nothing about money.
- fix: For scope 'own' print «mijozga narx berilmagan» on rows without a released offer, or drop them from the seller's list.

### A43. F7 + F49 — On «Narx tarixi» the back link «← Очередь расчётов» and the «Карточка →» links bounce sellers, the accountant and the VED (lead rows) to the home page
- lens: ui-phone (F7) + access (F49) · status: **F7 confirmed 3/3 in pass 1 and 3/3 in pass 2; F49 unverified** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/narxlar/page.tsx:70 (cardReadable src/modules/wms/calc/history.ts:173)`
- mechanism: PageHeader back = /hisoblash for every viewer, and /hisoblash redirects on !ved.docs — the seller and the accountant are exactly who /narxlar was opened for (#790). cardReadable = !hideCost is true for scope 'all' (accountant) and 'none' (VED), but /bitimlar/[id] needs canWriteDeal and /crm/leads/[id] needs crm.leads, so 4 of 5 doors on the accountant's screen lead to «Здравствуйте». The accountant reaches nazorat only by hand-typed URL.
- fix: Show the back link only for ved.docs holders; derive cardReadable from the real doors (canWriteDeal for deals, crm.leads for leads) and print the code as text otherwise; give the accountant menu entries for «Narx tarixi» and «Hisob vs haqiqat».

### A44. F48 — The seller reads the VED floor openly on the deal card (calc-seal-total, offer form prefilled with it) while /upsale hides the same number from them — two screens answer one person oppositely, and the card has no ownership gate
- lens: access · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; lens browser as sales: card shows $3796.96, /upsale shows $703.04 upsale and $4500 client price with no floor)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/components/calc-panel.tsx:126 (floorUsd :156; upsale-scope.ts; #830)`
- mechanism: CalcPanel draws seal.totalUsd for anyone with canWriteDeal and passes floorUsd to CalcOfferForm; #830 removed «Себестоимость» from the seller's /upsale rows and upsale-scope.ts says «the seller never sees the floor» — yet /upsale prints upsale and client price side by side, so the floor is one subtraction away there too; the card is open by URL to a seller who does not own the deal (#790 «STATED, not fixed»).
- fix: One rule from the owner's answer: (a) seller sees the floor → restore it on /upsale own rows and update #830; (b) seller must not → hide calc-seal-total/floorUsd for scope 'own', the form learns «poldan past» from the server after the press, /upsale own rows show state only, and the deal card gets an ownership gate.
- owner decision: 1) Sotuvchi polni ko'rsin — o'z bitimida; /upsale ham ko'rsatadi. 2) Sotuvchi polni ko'rmasin — kartada ham, /upsale da ham. 3) Ko'rsin, lekin faqat O'Z bitimida — boshqa sotuvchining bitim kartasi unga ochilmasin.

### A45. F19 — The deal card's history prints raw keys and a bare 36-char request id when a calculation is requested («calcRequested: → 01a06e82-…», «section: → podklyuch», ISO due date)
- lens: ui-desktop · status: **confirmed 3/3 (writer is openCalcRequest, not requestCalc — naming slip only)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/service.ts:273-282 (labels src/modules/platform/audit/fields.ts:95)`
- mechanism: openCalcRequest writes after: {calcRequested: uuid, section, items, dueAt: ISO}; AUDIT_FIELD_LABELS knows dueAt only and #690's resolver has no entry for calc requests, so the History block prints column names, the internal section code, an ISO timestamp and an unlinked uuid (law 6).
- fix: Add calcRequested/section/items to AUDIT_FIELD_LABELS ×4, print section through SECTION_LABELS and dueAt through the formatter, resolve calcRequested to «Расчёт →» (/hisoblash/<id>) in resolveAuditRefs — or write one human line.

### A46. F6 + F26 + F57 — A sealed request page draws an EMPTY bordered card at the bottom and a «Готово» chip that cannot be told from a typed-answer or a lines-closed job
- lens: ui-phone (F6) + ui-desktop (F26) + workflow-queue (F57) · status: **F6 confirmed 3/3 in pass 1 and 3/3 in pass 2; F26 confirmed 3/3; F57 unverified** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/page.tsx:305-327 (chip :112-115)`
- mechanism: When closed the page renders <section class="card" data-testid="calc-answer"> unconditionally and fills it only for completedVia==='returned' or an answerAmount; a sealed request has neither. The chip prints t('finish') for every non-returned closing, so sealed, task-answered and 'lines' all read «Готово».
- fix: Render the answer card only when it has content (or put the sealed summary there); chip by completedVia: 'sealed' → «Muhrlangan vN», 'task' → «Готово», 'returned' → «Qaytarildi», 'lines' → «Pozitsiya saqlandi».

### A47. F10 — «Последние цены» prints a code with no prices when the only quote for that code is this request's own
- lens: ui-phone · status: **confirmed 1/3 — one confirming verdict; the other two verifiers never returned** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/last-quotes.tsx:51`
- mechanism: shown keeps a code when lastQuotesByCode returned any row, but the rows are filtered q.requestId !== requestId only at line 63 — the request's own sealed version keeps the code in the list and is then removed, leaving a heading, the code and a link with nothing under it.
- fix: Apply the own-request filter before computing shown (or exclude requestId in lastQuotesByCode).

### A48. F24 — The toolbar chip says «без ставки: N» / «无税率» about groups that HAVE a rate and lack a BAZA — three sentences about one row, one naming the wrong missing thing
- lens: ui-desktop · status: **confirmed 3/3** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/items-table.tsx:615 (messages/ru.json:2359, zh-CN.json:2359)`
- mechanism: unpriced counts groups whose live customs is not ok for ANY reason; the engine checks rates first and baza second, so the commonest state (rates auto-pulled from PP-3818, baza not) refuses baza_missing and is announced with the rates-missing word in ru/zh; en/uz are reason-neutral.
- fix: Reword table.unpriced in ru/zh to «не посчитано» / «未计价», or split the count by reason («нет базы: N»).

### A49. F23 — The TNVED cell clips the tenth digit of a 10-digit code («852852000(») — cause is the <datalist> picker indicator stealing ~19 px inside the input, not the column width
- lens: ui-desktop · status: **confirmed 3/3 — all three verifiers reproduced the clip but corrected the mechanism: the lens's 101.8 px measurement was the body font (a getComputedStyle(el).font artefact under tabular-nums); removing the list attribute alone drops scrollWidth 119 → 114** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/items-table.tsx:1103 (list={`codes-${id}`} ~:1073)`
- mechanism: Chromium reserves its ::-webkit-calendar-picker-indicator box (~1.34em) inside an <input list>, leaving ~79 px of text room for ten monospace digits (84 px on Mac/Linux Chrome; Windows Consolas fits; Firefox never clips). Only the last digit, desktop only, code also visible in the footer — minor.
- fix: .input-cell[list]::-webkit-calendar-picker-indicator { display:none } (or drop the datalist arrow) rather than widening the column, which would mask the same 19 px stolen from every other datalist input.

### A50. F25 — A refused «Вернуть» leaves «Укажите причину» standing under the «Готово» fold
- lens: ui-desktop · status: **confirmed 3/3** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/calc-actions.tsx:100-116`
- mechanism: The two mode toggles touch only mode; error is cleared solely inside settle() on a successful action, so the <p role="alert"> renders the last refusal whatever fold is open.
- fix: setError(null) inside the two mode toggles.

### A51. F28 — The workspace facts block prints «вес 1500 / объём 30» with no unit, unlike the queue row's «1500 kg · 30 m³»

- mechanism: <dd className="num">{row.weightKg}</dd> / {row.volumeM3} are printed bare under lowercase labels; the cargo-facts inputs carry no unit hint either.
- fix: Print «{weightKg} kg» / «{volumeM3} m³» (or put the unit in the label as the deal card does) and capitalise the labels.

### A52. F29 — «Расчёт против факта» shows the VED their OWN seals only without saying so (#913)
- lens: ui-desktop · status: **confirmed 3/3** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/nazorat/page.tsx:96 (control-scope.ts:38-42; actuals.ts ownFilter :305/735/787)`
- mechanism: calcControlScopeFor gives ved.docs scope 'own' and every nazorat query adds sealed_by = actor; the coverage line «Запечатано: N · с привязанным приходом: M» carries no «только мои» qualifier, so a personal subset reads as a company count.
- fix: When scope === 'own' append «· только мои» to the coverage header and to both lists' headings.

### A53. F30 — The import-baza picker tells the VED to go to «Администрирование → Таможенная база», a screen that bounces them home

- mechanism: importNoBatch is shown to whoever opens the picker when no READY import exists; the admin uploads (his answer 4) and the VED, who reads it, is redirected from /admin to /.
- fix: Reword for the reader («popросите администратора загрузить квартальный файл») and keep the path only when the actor holds the admin permission.

### A54. F4 — The «без ставки: N» chip is a dead tap on the phone (its anchor targets the hidden desktop row)
- lens: ui-phone · status: **confirmed 3/3 in pass 1 and 3/3 in pass 2** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/items-table.tsx:643 (id=calc-g-N on the BlockFooter <tr> :1275 inside hidden md:block)`
- mechanism: The sticky bar renders on every viewport and the chip is <a href="#calc-g-N">; the only element with that id is the desktop table row (0×0 on the phone), so the tap scrolls nowhere.
- fix: Give the phone group card id=calc-g-N below md (and the desktop row above md), or render the chip as a plain span on phones.

### A55. F11 — The freight zone hint reads as a three-city chain «Yiwu → Иу / Гуанчжоу → Ташкент?» and cannot be accepted with a tap
- lens: ui-phone · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/app/(protected)/hisoblash/[id]/calc-workspace.tsx:125`
- mechanism: The hint is «{fromCity} → {zone label}?» and the zone label already contains «→ Ташкент», so two arrows chain; on the phone it wraps under the select as an 11 px line with no button.
- fix: Word it «{fromCity}: {zone}?» and make the hint a button that applies the guessed zone (setZoneAction) — the person still decides, one tap instead of a select.

### A56. F60 — Four refusal codes reach the screen as raw words: superseded (offer release on a corrected seal), fx_missing, category_not_found, account_currency_mismatch (payout)
- lens: workflow-queue · status: **unverified (verifiers never ran; node check of calc.errors in uz.json: all four absent)** · `/home/user/fullprompt/src/modules/wms/calc/workspace.ts:3607 (recordOffer :3396; upsale-service.ts:245/252/383; calc-offer.tsx:164; upsale/pay-form.tsx:181,218)`
- mechanism: releaseOffer throws CalcError('superseded') («need different sentences on the screen»), the payout throws the other three; none exists under calc.errors in any bundle, so the components fall through t.has() to the raw code (law 6).
- fix: Add the four keys ×4 locales and a derived fence test CalcError codes ↔ calc.errors keys.

### contradictions

- docs/VED.md:173-175 «The ANSWER returns to the seller's bot chat: price, breakdown, validity — plus a ready-to-forward client offer text» — the code sends none of that: workspace.ts:1676-1681 announceSeal → notifyStaffTelegram type 'CalcSealed' with text `🧮 Hisob tayyor: $${totals.totalUsd.toFixed(2)}\n${link}` only (no breakdown, no valid_until); the client offer text is a separate SELLER press (recordOffer, workspace.ts:3295, delivered as 'CalcOffer' at :3504/:3661). The queue-lifecycle map's vocab «🧮 Hisob tayyor: $total» is the correct statement.
- docs/VED.md:160 «asks ONLY for what is missing («Shahar qaysi?»)» and docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md:333-336 «the AI asks follow-ups in the same chat until complete or the user says «Bo'ldi»» — not built: staff-handlers.ts:365-386 analyseIntakeAndReply prints ONE summary with the whole `⚠️ Yetishmayapti:` list (intake.ts:231-234) plus the confirm keyboard («✅ Tasdiqlash / ➕ Yana ma’lumot / ✖️ Bekor qilish», :100-106); no per-field question loop exists anywhere in src/modules/platform/telegram.
- docs/VED-IMPORT-AI.md:214 names the suggestion service `src/modules/wms/calc/import-baza.ts`; it lives at `src/modules/wms/customs/import-baza.ts` (prefill.ts:10 imports '../customs/import-baza'; workspace.ts and the API route import '@/modules/wms/customs/import-baza'). Same doc :228 says the rank is `similarity(name_norm, …)`; the code is `word_similarity(${needle}, r.name_norm)` (import-baza.ts:208, with the comment explaining why plain similarity scores a short name inside a 500-char paragraph near zero). Same doc :320 says the checklist extension is `itemQuantity` + `itemWeight`; the code has ONE field `itemMeasure` (intake.ts:41-42,70 — #910 collapsed the pair). Same doc :353 «give the model the TOP-5 rows»; prefill.ts:305-316 passes every candidate suggestImportBaza returns (CANDIDATE_LIMIT = 10, import-baza.ts:106). Same doc :342,346 says codes come from `suggestTnved`; the prefill's code source is `proposeGoodsGrouping` via `proposeGroups` (workspace.ts:2100-2110) — suggestTnved is not on the prefill path.
- CLAUDE.md:49 «`platform` must never import `wms`» is broken STATICALLY on this very path: src/modules/platform/telegram/bot.ts:6 `import { clientsForChat } from '../../wms/client-cabinet/service'` and src/modules/platform/telegram/client-cabinet.ts:17 `… from '../../wms/client-cabinet/service'`. No eslint no-restricted-imports rule and no unit fence enforces it (grep of eslint.config.* for 'wms' is empty; tests/unit mentions the rule only in comments of batch-read-door.test.ts:47 and cargo-stages.test.ts:124). keyboards.ts:21 does the same read by dynamic import and says why. The bot-intake map's claim «every staff handler calls next() for chats it does not own» is right; its silence on this import is the gap.
- bot-intake map line citations drift ~20 lines from the file: «photo … while stage === 'client' falls through (staff-handlers.ts:210)» → the check is :208 (`if (!state || state.stage === 'client') return next();`; :210 is the no-staff next()); «'crm_activity' (entityId = noteId) — staff-handlers.ts:457» → :478-479; «'application/octet-stream' (staff-handlers.ts:460)» → :483; «MODEL_IMAGE_PX = 1568 and the 4_500_000-byte skip (staff-handlers.ts:470-483)» → :503-513; «saveIntakeFile's fetch(...) (staff-handlers.ts:482)» → :468; «EVERY text except the literal '🧮 Hisoblatish' is swallowed (staff-handlers.ts:256)» → :253. Substance of each claim is correct.
- queue-lifecycle map: «SealBlocker.kind (workspace.ts L268)» → the union is at L249-258 (L268 is `export function guessZone`). «sealCalc's UPDATE (workspace.ts:1541-1560) touches calc_requests only» → the closing UPDATE is at :1558-1575 (the tx opens at :1544); the inference itself is CONFIRMED by grep: no `tasks` identifier appears anywhere in workspace.ts, so the seal never closes the VED's task — only service.ts endRequest (:462-515) does, and the 'sealed' via never calls it.
- queue-lifecycle map's summary says a request is «one row per consignment … ONE writer `openCalcRequest`» and the bot-intake map says the card path «note written inside openCalcRequest» — both correct, but the THIRD door writes `source: 'card'` (from-thread.ts:283) because 0085's `calc_requests_source_check` admits only ('card','bot'): `calc_requests.source` cannot tell the thread door from the card form, and any map/handoff sentence «source names the door» is false for the thread.
- pricing-law-engine map lists «CalcError codes thrown by service.ts: … » but the codes `empty`, `too_many` (>200 ids), `not_yours`, `bad_section` are thrown by from-thread.ts (:61, :62, :105, :227) and rendered from `calc.threadErrors.*`, not `calc.errors.*` (thread-calc.tsx:208) — the bot-intake map has the codes but names no namespace for them.
- bot-intake map «calc-intake.integration.test.ts never asserts landIntake's queued/requestId» — CONFIRMED (grep: only the afterAll cleanup at :62-68 touches calc_requests); and «MATERIAL_NOTE_CAP … activitySchema.note.max(4000) (crm/service.ts:1152)» → the schema is at :1151 and addActivity (:1158) takes `z.infer<typeof activitySchema>` as a TYPE only — no `.parse` runs, confirmed.
- queue-lifecycle map says the mutes tripwire «walks buildRecipients cases only» — stronger than stated: `grep "case 'Calc"` in src/modules/platform/notifications/service.ts returns NOTHING, so none of the ten Calc* types (CalcRequested/Taken/Done/Returned/Prefilled/Overdue/Sealed/Discounted/DictReview/Offer/BelowFloor) is routed through buildRecipients; every one is a direct notifyStaffTelegram caller, and only calc-prefill-wire.test.ts:24 (`MUTE_GROUPS … toContain('CalcPrefilled')`) pins any of them to a mute group.
- pricing-law-engine map's claim that WARNING_LABELS/REFUSAL_LABELS/BAND_REFUSAL_LABELS are not walked by any test is CONFIRMED against labels.ts:10-13 and :36-41 which claim tests/unit/locales.test.ts anchors them: locales.test.ts imports only BRIDGE_LABELS and FEED_LABELS (:4-5); tests/unit/calc-queue.test.ts:79-84 walks SECTION_LABELS and FIELD_LABELS only. The comment in labels.ts is the false claim.

### i18n

- WEB BUNDLES — `calc` namespace in messages/{ru,uz,en,zh-CN}.json: 244 top-level keys / 378 leaf keys, identical in all four locales (measured). Groups: root 232 keys; `calc.errors.*` 65; `calc.table.*` 28; `calc.refusals.*` 11 (engine CustomsRefusal/FreightRefusal/FeeRefusal/TotalsRefusal words, incl. the stale `unit_unsupported`); `calc.refusal.*` 9 (E1 control-screen refusals via labels.ts REFUSAL_LABELS); `calc.threadErrors.*` 9 = empty, too_many, not_yours, bad_section, forbidden, not_found, note_taken, server_behind, unauthenticated (read ONLY by src/components/thread-calc.tsx:208 `t(`threadErrors.${error}`)` with no t.has guard — a new CalcError thrown from from-thread.ts needs a key HERE, not in calc.errors); `calc.warnings.*` 6 (rateOffDictionary, bazaOffDictionary, aiLowConfidence, aiRateTaken, rateNoted, bazaFromImport); `calc.fields.*` 6 (fromCity, toCity, weightKg, volumeM3, goods, itemMeasure — the checklist chips on /hisoblash and /hisoblash/[id] via labels.ts FIELD_LABELS); `calc.blockers.*` 3; `calc.sections.*` 3 (uz: Faqat yo‘lkira / Faqat rastamojka / Podklyuch); `calc.bandRefusal.*` 2; `calc.source.*` 2; `calc.zones.*` 2 (cn, kashgar). Thread-island root keys: threadStart, threadPickHint, threadRead, threadMissing, threadPicked, threadReading, threadAnalyze, threadSendBtn, threadCancel, threadLands, threadSent, threadOpenCard.
- WEB BUNDLES — `customsImport` namespace (×4 locales, 17 top-level / 25 leaf keys): title, hint, file, period, rows, skipped, status, ready, processing, failed, empty, upload, uploading, queued, deleteConfirm, lastBeat («yangilandi {time}» — ICU arg), errors.{not_xlsx, too_large, empty, no_file, forbidden, enqueue_failed, in_use, network, server_behind} — the route's JSON `error` codes map 1:1 onto errors.*. `home.*` VED-flow keys (flowCalcOpen, flowCalcLate, flowDocsPending, flowTnvedMissing) as the queue map lists.
