service.ts (queue notifications :324 CalcRequested, :414 CalcTaken, :542 CalcReturned, :586 CalcDone, :782-784 CalcOverdue; task title `Hisoblash: ${label} (${n})` :293/:382; task results 'Hisoblandi' | 'Qaytarildi' :509, 'Navbatga qaytarildi' :446); workspace.ts announceSeal (:1680 «🧮 Hisob tayyor: $…», :1696-1705 «⚠️ Hisobda chegirma: …», «Chegirma: $… — …», «Tarif bandi o'zgartirildi: … kg/m³ — …», «Yakuniy: $…»); review.ts :70-72; from-thread.ts (`Mijoz:`/`Biz:` speaker labels :127, «(N ta fayl suhbatda — kartadagi chat panelida)» :130, via 'suhbatdan' :260); intake-land.ts (lead note «Telegram bot: hisoblatish (<section>)» :166, deal title `Hisoblatish — ${section}` :87); customs/jobs.ts importFailureText :30-47 and import-service.ts sweep text :551 (Uzbek sentences the ADMIN reads on /admin/bojxona-import — via the `error` column, not a bundle).
- MODEL PROMPTS ARE RUSSIAN: intake-ai.ts SYSTEM :36-57 (asks `steps` «короткие строки на узбекском»), prefill-ai.ts SYSTEM :46-61 (asks `reason` «одна короткая строка на узбекском»), the user turn labels «Раздел: / Прикреплено файлов: / Фотографий для чтения: / Материалы: / Текста нет — читай фото.» (intake-ai.ts:159-162) and «Строка N: «…» (код …)» + «(единица не совпадает)» (prefill-ai.ts:76-88). tnved/service.ts prompts likewise Russian (not read in full).
- CABINET dictionary: src/modules/platform/telegram/client-labels.ts DICT with CLIENT_LOCALES = ['uz','ru','en'] (no zh-CN); bothKeyboard (staff-handlers.ts:69-80) reads it for the client rows under the Uzbek staff row; isCabinetText (staff-bot.ts:125) derives from allLabelVariants so a new locale joins both sides in one edit (bot-both-modes.test.ts:32 pins «not a hardcoded three»).
- ANCHOR RULE (CLAUDE.md footgun 1 / #163): a missing key throws at RENDER in every locale; runtime keys must go through literal maps — labels.ts SECTION_LABELS/FIELD_LABELS (walked by tests/unit/calc-queue.test.ts:79-84), WARNING_LABELS/REFUSAL_LABELS/BAND_REFUSAL_LABELS (NOT walked by any test despite labels.ts:10-13/:36-41 saying so). Guarded readers: calc-offer.tsx:164 uses `t.has(...)`; unguarded readers: calc-actions.tsx:219 `t(`errors.${error}`)` and thread-calc.tsx:208 `t(`threadErrors.${error}`)`. Known holes: `superseded` (thrown workspace.ts:3419, :3630) has no calc.errors key; stale keys with no thrower: calc.errors.stale_baza, group_gone, save_failed, ai_not_configured, calc.refusals.unit_unsupported.

### testCommands

- ENVIRONMENT FIRST (CLAUDE.md ritual): start Postgres — `su postgres -c "/usr/lib/postgresql/16/bin/pg_ctl -D /var/local/pg/data -l /var/local/pg/log -o '-k /tmp' start"`; never point tests at gsr_dev (db/client.ts:12 defaults DATABASE_URL to postgres://postgres@127.0.0.1:5432/gsr_dev = the owner's real data; integration files `import 'dotenv/config'` so .env decides). Fresh throwaway db in CI's order (.github/workflows/ci.yml:43-56): `export DATABASE_URL=postgres://postgres@127.0.0.1:5432/gsr_ci && pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm db:migrate && pnpm db:seed && pnpm db:seed:demo && pnpm test && pnpm build` — chain with `&&`, grep each gate's OWN failure marker (#803). `pnpm typecheck` is the only thing that types tests/.
- UNIT (no database) for this subsystem: `pnpm vitest run tests/unit/calc-pricing.test.ts tests/unit/calc-law.test.ts tests/unit/pricing-pure.test.ts tests/unit/calc-basis.test.ts tests/unit/calc-warnings.test.ts tests/unit/calc-grouping.test.ts tests/unit/calc-clock.test.ts tests/unit/calc-queue.test.ts tests/unit/calc-rekey.test.ts tests/unit/calc-control-scope.test.ts tests/unit/calc-history.test.ts tests/unit/calc-actuals.test.ts tests/unit/calc-offer.test.ts tests/unit/calc-offer-wire.test.ts tests/unit/offer-sheet.test.ts tests/unit/proposal-wire.test.ts tests/unit/calc-prefill-wire.test.ts tests/unit/prefill-reply.test.ts tests/unit/ai-advisory.test.ts tests/unit/intake-facts.test.ts tests/unit/intake-images.test.ts tests/unit/intake-items-wire.test.ts tests/unit/thread-calc-wire.test.ts tests/unit/bot-ai-dispatch.test.ts tests/unit/bot-both-modes.test.ts tests/unit/ai-route-text.test.ts tests/unit/customs-import-parse.test.ts tests/unit/customs-import-wire.test.ts tests/unit/notification-mutes.test.ts tests/unit/locales.test.ts tests/unit/i18n-keys.test.ts tests/unit/tx-pool.test.ts tests/unit/upsale-scope.test.ts tests/unit/upsale-door.test.ts tests/unit/ved-audit-wire.test.ts` (vitest.config.ts: include src/**/*.test.ts + tests/unit/** + tests/integration/**, fileParallelism false, testTimeout 20 s).
- INTEGRATION (real Postgres, run AFTER migrate+seed+seed:demo on gsr_ci/gsr_test): `pnpm vitest run tests/integration/calc-queue.integration.test.ts tests/integration/calc-intake.integration.test.ts tests/integration/calc-prefill.integration.test.ts tests/integration/calc-table.integration.test.ts tests/integration/calc-seal.integration.test.ts tests/integration/calc-finish.integration.test.ts tests/integration/calc-clock.integration.test.ts tests/integration/calc-chain.integration.test.ts tests/integration/calc-offer.integration.test.ts tests/integration/calc-offer-answer.integration.test.ts tests/integration/calc-actuals.integration.test.ts tests/integration/thread-calc.integration.test.ts tests/integration/staff-bot.integration.test.ts tests/integration/bot-lookup.integration.test.ts tests/integration/customs-import.integration.test.ts tests/integration/upsale.integration.test.ts`. What they prove: calc-queue (assign-on-open, untaken row, take race, counters == rows, bounce needs reason, finish records answer, overdue claim, rekey on win, note mint/adopt refusal); calc-intake (REQUIRED_FIELDS, summary/note text, deal-vs-lead landing, second request joins, forward-only stage move, ambiguous phone) — does NOT assert `queued`/`requestId`; calc-prefill (model INJECTED via PrefillDeps {propose, pick, configured}: codes/rates/baza, stays unconfirmed, no-model sweep, row-not-number pick, unit fence, none-of-these, stands down on touched, yolkira left alone, overtaken baza kept, never «~$0.00», model failure = sentence); calc-finish (seal_instead vs typed answer #775); thread-calc (viewer fence on ids, supervision names speakers, lands on the deal on screen, files-only submittable, lead thread on the lead, client → open deal, note_taken on retry); staff-bot (decisions only: parseCallback shapes e:/t:/a:, linkStaffChat chat_taken, buttonsFor, task/approval from bot); customs-import (his sample parsed whole, READY with period, re-run replaces rows, delete refusal in_use, exact code + name rank, unit fence, short-name-in-paragraph, MIN_NEEDLE, newest ready batch, importRowForCode, saveTable fill/never-overwrite/picked-from-file/refuse-foreign-row/SET NULL keeps price, heartbeat sweep two clocks, swept batch never resurrects, one refused row keeps the quarter, no spooled /tmp sheet, first beat before a byte).
- E2E (Playwright, ONE worker, one shared database, lexical file order; each spec is `serial` and ENDS with a cleanup TEST): `fuser -k 3000/tcp; pnpm build && pnpm e2e --project desktop-chromium tests/e2e/m9zo-hisoblash.desktop.spec.ts tests/e2e/m9zp-hisoblash-seal.desktop.spec.ts tests/e2e/m9zq-hisoblash-offer.desktop.spec.ts tests/e2e/m9zr-hisoblash-olchov.desktop.spec.ts tests/e2e/m9zs-hisoblash-nazorat.desktop.spec.ts tests/e2e/m9zt-gotovo-offer.desktop.spec.ts tests/e2e/m9zu-bojxona-import.desktop.spec.ts tests/e2e/m9zv-hisoblash-tarix.desktop.spec.ts` (+ `tests/e2e/m9zr-upsale.spec.ts` on mobile-chromium and `m9zr-upsale.desktop.spec.ts`). Run WITHOUT `CI=1` locally (CI=1 selects a headless shell this container lacks); do NOT re-seed between vitest and Playwright (CI uses one database for both). playwright.config.ts: testDir tests/e2e, projects mobile-chromium (testIgnore /\.desktop\.spec\.ts$/) and desktop-chromium (testMatch /\.desktop\.spec\.ts$/), baseURL APP_URL ?? http://localhost:3000, webServer `pnpm start` (the STANDALONE server — build first) with env TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN=<fake> and TELEGRAM_POLLING='0', health url /api/health (a stale pgboss.version.maintained_on makes it 503), reuseExistingServer when not CI. Logins in the specs: super_admin +998900000001, ved_manager +998900000004, sales_manager +998900000009, accountant +998900000010, password demo1234 (m9zo:20-21, m9zs, m9zv). Coverage by spec: m9zo card form → queue → cargo facts → take → finish (types 480; passes only while canSeal is false, i.e. empty dictionaries) → cleanup; m9zp workspace refusals → zone → table+baza+confirm → seal → locked card; m9zq sealed price → offer → price history; m9zr-olchov m² measure → live 248 → seal 280.96; m9zs nazorat four roles; m9zt Готово → offer → PDF; m9zu upload → auto-fill → picker → cleanup deletes the import; m9zv registry V1 + filter + roles.
- NOT COVERED BY ANY E2E: the Telegram bot conversation (🧮 → section → client → material → Bo‘ldi → Tasdiqlash), the JOB_CALC_PREFILL worker end-to-end, and the CalcPrefilled Telegram delivery — grammy needs a live token/network; the only behavioural proofs are calc-prefill.integration.test.ts (aiPrefill with injected deps) and staff-bot.integration.test.ts (decisions). Source-shape pins stand in: intake-images.test.ts, bot-ai-dispatch.test.ts, calc-prefill-wire.test.ts, bot-both-modes.test.ts. First real run must be watched in `docker compose logs -f app` for '[calc-prefill] pass finished' / 'pass failed' / 'stood down' and '[calc intake] model tokens'.

### footguns

- A FOURTH DOOR into the queue must clear three derived fences before it compiles/passes: tests/unit/intake-items-wire.test.ts walks every file containing `openCalcRequest(` (excluding calc/service.ts), requires the three known callers by path (intake-land.ts, from-thread.ts, hisoblash/actions.ts) and asserts the call's arguments build items through `itemFacts(`/`loneWeightKg(` — never straight off a goods array; 0085's `calc_requests_source_check` admits only 'card'|'bot' (the thread door reuses 'card', from-thread.ts:283) so a new `source` value needs a migration widening the CHECK; `openCalcRequest` refuses `note_taken` when the posted note id already exists and `note_foreign` when the note sits on another card (service.ts:199-227) — mint the uuid in the browser, upload files against it FIRST (`/api/files/upload`, entityType 'crm_activity'), then post it once.
- `CalcItemInput` (service.ts:122-132) has NO measureQty/measureUnit: no intake door can land the law's own unit (m²/juft/litr); it is written only through `saveTable`'s `TableItemEdit.measureQty`, and the UNIT is never posted — the server stamps it from the group's dutyUnit (workspace.ts:2300-2305). A door that adds `measureUnit` to its payload will be a forged-post hole, not a feature.
- EVERY workspace write must go through `mutateRequest`/`lockRequestInTx` (workspace.ts:2396-2450): `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on calc_requests, throws not_found / already_closed / ai_running (while ai_proposal_started_at is younger than AI_CLAIM_STALE_MS = 10 min), then `rev + 1`. tests/unit/calc-clock.test.ts classifies EVERY export of workspace.ts — an unclassified new export fails «an unclassified door is a decision nobody made». `proposeGroups` HOLDS the AI claim, so any pricing tail it calls must run after `releaseAiClaim` (workspace.ts:2158-2160, pinned by proposal-wire.test.ts «the claim is RELEASED before the tail») — the shipped-dead bug of #906 was exactly this.
- #714 pool deadlock: nothing pooled inside a transaction — openCalcRequest reads the note, the TNVED memory (`tnvedFor`) and the rota (`nextVedAssignee`) BEFORE its tx and mints the task AFTER it (service.ts:159-166, 287-315); sealCalc reads getSetting/loadWorkspace before its tx (:1394-1397); saveTable reads dictionaries and import suggestions pre-tx; the AI prefill sweep is `saveTable(requestId, {items:[], adds:[]}, ctx)`. tests/unit/tx-pool.test.ts derives the pooled set transitively and goes red on `getSetting`/`db.` inside a `tx` body.
- The AI prefill has ONE enqueuer: the bot's `c:save` handler (staff-handlers.ts:397-411 queuePrefill, called at :648-650 only when `target.queued && target.requestId`). `submitCalcAction` (card form) and `threadCalcSend` (thread door) never enqueue JOB_CALC_PREFILL — a seller sending from a card gets no AI pass and no CalcPrefilled. calc-prefill-wire.test.ts pins the bot source to contain `enqueue(JOB_CALC_PREFILL, {…rev…})` and to NOT contain `aiPrefill`; a second enqueuer must pass `rev` from `prefillTicket(requestId)` or `prefillStanding` skips the rev check (jobs.ts:14-17 «Absent on a job queued before this field existed»).
- prefillStanding stands the worker down on 'touched' when `calc_requests.rev` moved — and aiPrefill's own first half (proposeGroups/saveTable) moves it, so a re-delivered job after a mid-pass crash is NOT retried (prefill.ts:53-57 «COST, stated»); a confirmed group or a closed request also stands it down. The worker never rethrows (jobs.ts:129-131), so pg-boss's `retryLimit: 5` default only re-delivers on process death. `aiPrefill(requestId, {actorId: null})` — the machine writes audit rows as NOBODY; `requested_by` keeps who asked.
- sectionParts('yolkira').customs === false: the prefill skips grouping and the pick on freight-only jobs (prefill.ts:143-149 — before this guard the model committed customs groups onto a freight quote and `customs_on_yolkira` made it permanently unsealable: no screen deletes groups). `loadWorkspace` answers customsUsd: 0 for a section with no customs half and `requestCustomsFor([])` answers 0 vacuously for a customs section with no groups — the reply forces null (prefill.ts:204-221) and prefillReplyText never prints «~$0.00»; do NOT «fix» requestCustomsFor's empty case: the live browser bar and the seal gate consume it verbatim.
- THE SEAL DOES NOT CLOSE THE VED'S TASK — no `tasks` write exists in workspace.ts; only service.ts `endRequest` (:462-515) closes the task, on via 'task'|'returned'|'lines'. `completeCalcForDeal` ends only the OLDEST open request on the deal (:601-612); `recalcFromSealed` copies assignee_id, mints NO task, sets due_at = now + 2 h regardless of items, and re-stamps requested_by = the admin pressing recalc (:1844-1859) — the overdue sweep flags every correction after 2 h.
- In-memory bot state dies with the process: `collections` Map in calc-intake.ts (TTL_MS 30 min, refreshed on every updateIntake; expiry checked on READ in activeIntake), `pendingResults`/`staffEntryIntents` in staff-bot.ts (PENDING_TTL_MS 10 min). The bot polls INSIDE the Next.js process (src/instrumentation-node.ts → startTelegramBot) — the container the owner restarts on every deploy; only one getUpdates poller per token (bot.ts:47-51, TELEGRAM_POLLING=0 elsewhere; the e2e server pins it off). grammy's poller is SEQUENTIAL: never `await` a model call in a handler — `void analyseIntakeAndReply(...)` (:618) and `void answerWithAssistant(...)` (:355) are the idiom, pinned by bot-ai-dispatch.test.ts.
- During a live collection EVERY text except the literal '🧮 Hisoblatish' is swallowed as material (staff-handlers.ts:253-269): '/bugun', cabinet buttons, codes and '/hisoblatish' all become material until ✖️ Bekor or the 30-min TTL; pressing a section button while a collection is live calls startIntake and silently REPLACES the state (calc-intake.ts:90) — files already stored against the old noteId become orphan attachments (nothing deletes them on cancel/TTL; grep for deleteAttachment in the two bot files is empty). A photo/document sent while stage === 'client' falls through to the cabinet via next() with no reply (:208).
- saveIntakeFile (staff-handlers.ts:456-491) fetches `https://api.telegram.org/file/bot${TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN}/${file_path}` with NO timeout/AbortSignal, inside the sequential poller; returns null without the token; a document with no Telegram mime_type is stored as 'application/octet-stream' (:483) which files/service.ts refuses → «Faylni saqlab bo‘lmadi». Only PHOTOS reach the model (:224-232): at most MAX_INTAKE_IMAGES = 6, downscaled by sharp to 1568 px JPEG q80 (lazy import — never trace sharp into the standalone bundle), dropped if the reduced copy exceeds 4_500_000 bytes; documents are stored, counted («Прикреплено файлов: N»), never read. The thread door copies NO files (from-thread.ts:32-36 «STATED CUT»).
- Four independent «is AI configured» checks that must keep agreeing: `aiConfigured()` (platform/ai/model.ts:42 — used by prefill.ts:121 and the bot text router) vs raw `process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` in intake-ai.ts:90, prefill-ai.ts:72, tnved/service.ts:104 and :195 (TnvedError 'ai_not_configured', which proposeGroups surfaces as 'ai_failed'). Model ids are string literals: 'claude-opus-5' in intake-ai.ts:104, prefill-ai.ts:95 and tnved/service.ts; ANALYST_MODEL/STAFF_MODEL in model.ts:51-52; every client is `new Anthropic({ timeout, maxRetries: 1 })` (60 s default, 20 s for the thread door) and structured output rides `output_config.format.type: 'json_schema'` (SDK-version specific — check the pinned @anthropic-ai/sdk before copying).
- Client resolution traps: parseClientHint treats ≥7 digits as a PHONE and /^[A-Za-z]{1,4}\d{1,8}$/ as a CODE (intake.ts:309-315); resolveIntakeClient requires `clients.active = true` (intake-land.ts:48) and a phone must match EXACTLY ONE active client (activeClientsByPhone) — ambiguity or an inactive code → nobody → a LEAD named by the raw hint (staff-bot.ts:343 `state.clientHintRaw.trim() || 'Hisoblatish (nomsiz)'`); openLeadForPhone scans only the newest 500 leads (intake-land.ts:283-287) matching the last 9 digits, so an older lead on the same phone gets a duplicate; dealFor takes the client's newest OPEN deal by created_at or mints `Hisoblatish — ${section}` owned by the collector (:74-97).
- landIntake writes the note FIRST (addActivity with the pre-minted id) and only then openCalcRequest with `noteId` (intake-land.ts:173-232); a refused queue open (too_many_open — MAX_OPEN_PER_REQUESTER 20 counted by requested_by = the collecting STAFF member; note_foreign; server_behind) leaves the note and files on the card, logs '[calc-intake] queue open failed' with the CalcError, and the bot only says «⚠️ Hisoblash navbatiga tushmadi — kartadan qo‘lda yuboring.» (staff-handlers.ts:660) — the code is never shown. The stage move (crm_calc_stage/deal_calc_stage settings, forward-only by sort_order through moveLead/moveDeal) runs after and is fenced separately (:239-243).
- Unit vocabulary is THREE-way and mapped, never shared: file `ImportUnit` kg|dona|m2|juft|litr (import-parse.ts:16), item `BazaBasis` unit|kg|juft|litr|m2 (pricing.ts:22), law `DutyUnit` kg|dona|litr|juft|1000_dona|sm3|m2 (pricing.ts:106); bridges are BASIS_FOR_UNIT / UNIT_FOR_BASIS / unitsForRow (import-baza.ts:23-73) and defaultBasisFor (basis.ts:14). sm3 never appears in the file; the pick's basis fence is `BASIS_FOR_UNIT[chosen.unit] !== BASIS_FOR_UNIT[unit]` → refused and COUNTED (prefill.ts:345-347). `pullRatesFromDictionary` erases a group's typed feeUsd on purpose. `MeasureUnit`'s CHECK admits only juft|litr|m2|sm3 — kg lives on weight_kg, dona/1000_dona on quantity (0092).
- Deploy-morning schema lag: every action maps `isServerBehind` → 'server_behind' (hisoblash/actions.ts run/runTable/submit/thread), `/api/calc/import-baza` answers `state:'behind'`, the [id] page catches its workspace/link/chain reads; the ledger must read 96 (journal has 96 entries, last 0095 when 1785190000074 — a new migration must be 0096 with a larger `when`, after `git fetch origin main` and reading the tail: fourteen number collisions so far); drizzle applies all pending migrations in ONE transaction (no CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY). A CHECK that spans an ON DELETE SET NULL column cannot coexist with it (#809) — 0094 says so for import_row_id.
- Dictionaries: seedFreightTariff writes ONLY into an empty calc_freight_tariffs (scripts/seed.ts:334-368) and seedPp3818Rates is row-idempotent on (code, '2026-01-01') with vat_pct 12 on every row (:369+); calc_bazas and calc_price_book ship EMPTY, so nothing seals until the VED fills /hisoblash/lugatlar; dictionary reads have NO earliest-row fallback; the freight tariff is edited only at /admin/tarif under admin.dictionaries.manage (never ved.docs); the band is looked up by a WHOLE kg/m³ (bandDensityOf = Math.round) and refuses band_missing/band_ambiguous. Whether m9zo's «finish with 480» passes depends on the local database's dictionaries (seal_instead when canSeal) — a long-lived gsr_dev is a different oracle.
- Notifications: CalcSealed and CalcDiscounted are in NO MUTE_GROUPS list (only 'all' silences them); no Calc* type is routed through buildRecipients, so notification-mutes.test.ts's derived fence does not see them — a new Calc* type must be added to MUTE_GROUPS by hand (calc-prefill-wire.test.ts:24 is the only pin). notifyStaffTelegram queues a row whether or not the person has a linked chat (settled 'muted' by the drain) — a CalcPrefilled for a staff member with no linked Telegram is silently muted; buttonsFor attaches inline buttons only for TaskAssigned and DebtApprovalRequested (staff-bot.ts:181-197) — the prefill reply carries no button and `link: null` (prefill.ts:238).
- Telegram callback_data is capped at 64 bytes; the grammar is `e:s`|`e:c`, `c:<yolkira|rastamojka|podklyuch|done|save|more|cancel>` (CALC_STEPS), `t:<uuid>`, `a:[01]:<uuid>` (staff-bot.ts:154-174) — a new step must be added to CALC_STEPS or parseCallback returns null and the press falls through to next(). IntakeStage 'section' is dead (startIntake begins at 'client'); after review, a further text/photo drops stage to 'material' but keeps the previous facts/steps, and the old «✅ Tasdiqlash» still lands them (staff-handlers.ts:263-268, 622-664); analyseIntakeAndReply has no claim, so two «Bo‘ldi» presses start two Opus calls.
- Registration order matters: bot.ts registers /start (:56), then registerStaffBot (:158), then registerClientCabinet (:159); every staff handler must `return next()` for chats it does not own or the cabinet dies; cabinet button texts are let through BEFORE the task-result capture (staff-handlers.ts:283, pinned by bot-both-modes.test.ts:53); every keyboard-bearing reply must call `replyKeyboardFor(chatId)` rather than name a keyboard (:663, pinned at bot-both-modes.test.ts:69) or a staff+client chat loses half its buttons.
- i18n render-time throw: a CalcError thrown from from-thread.ts needs a `calc.threadErrors.<code>` key (thread-calc.tsx:208, unguarded); one thrown from queue/workspace actions needs `calc.errors.<code>` (calc-actions.tsx:219 unguarded; calc-offer.tsx:164 guarded with t.has); engine refusals need `calc.refusals.<reason>` AND a sentence in prefill-reply.ts's `Record<Union,string>` maps (compile error otherwise); the warning/refusal label maps in labels.ts are NOT walked by any test — add the bundle key in all four files by hand and run tests/unit/i18n-keys.test.ts + locales.test.ts.
- The layer rule «platform must never import wms» is unenforced and already broken statically at platform/telegram/bot.ts:6 and client-cabinet.ts:17; the house idiom on this path is a dynamic `await import('../../wms/...')` (keyboards.ts:21, staff-bot.ts:285/:325, staff-handlers.ts:373/:399-401, jobs/boss.ts registrations) — copy that, and expect no test to catch a static one.
